### [1] [AI's Capability in Assisting Scientific Research in Physics, Astrophysics, and Cosmology II: Project Planning and Proposal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25881)
*Jia Liu,Veena Krishnaraj,Kateryna Vovk,Kosuke Aizawa,Adrian E. Bayer,Linda Blot,Jessica Cowell,Suyog Garg,Jonathan Grée,Anamaria Hell,Ben Horowitz,Masaya Ichikawa,Kanyuni Iemoto,Keigo Kondo,Zacharie Lorsin,Kevin McCarthy,Jamie Robinson,Miguel Ruiz-Granda,Leander Thiele,Ievgen Vovk,Mingshen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过人类研究者与三种大型语言模型（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek）为物理学、天体物理学和宇宙学中的八个专家设想的科研项目分别生成一页项目计划，并由人类评审和两种AI评审进行双盲评估。结果显示，人类评审对AI生成与人类撰写的计划评分总体相似，但AI评审对AI生成的计划存在系统性偏好（评分高出约1分/5分制）；此外，AI评审能100%正确区分计划来源，而人类评审正确识别率约为72%–79%。研究提示在项目申请撰写与评审中广泛应用LLM需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（large language models, LLMs）在科研辅助方面的能力日益增强，但其在科研项目规划与提案评审中的表现尚未得到系统检验。本研究旨在评估当前LLMs能否生成与人类研究者水平相当的项目计划，并考察AI评审在评估过程中是否存在偏好。

Method: 针对八个由专家构思的物理学、天体物理学和宇宙学研究项目，分别由人类研究者和三种当代LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek；2025年中期模型，使用默认工具访问权限）独立生成一页项目计划，共获得32份提案。随后由四名人类评审和两个较新的前沿LLM（Claude Opus 4.8和ChatGPT Pro 5.5）使用四项评估标准进行盲评，并要求评审识别每份提案的作者是“人类”还是“AI”。

Result: 人类评审对AI生成和人类撰写的提案评分总体相似；而两种AI评审对AI撰写提案的评分均比人类撰写提案高出约1分（五分量表）。人类评审正确识别人类撰写和AI生成提案的比例分别为72%和79%，而两种AI评审均100%正确分类全部32份提案。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs能够生成在人类评审眼中与人类水平相当的项目计划，但AI评审对AI生成的提案表现出系统性偏好。该结果表明，在项目申请撰写和评审中大规模部署LLM需要谨慎对待。

Abstract: We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can assist scientific project planning and proposal evaluation. One-page project plans were independently generated for eight expert-conceived research projects in physics, astrophysics, and cosmology by human researchers and three contemporary LLMs (ChatGPT, Claude, and DeepSeek; mid-2025 models, used with their default tool access). The resulting 32 proposals were blindly evaluated by four human reviewers and two newer frontier LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8 and ChatGPT Pro 5.5) using a four-aspect evaluation rubric. Reviewers were also asked to identify whether each proposal was written by a human or an AI. Human reviewers rated human- and AI-written proposals similarly overall, whereas both AI reviewers scored AI-written proposals about one point higher (on a five-point scale) than human-written proposals. Human reviewers correctly identified human- and AI-written proposals 72% and 79% of the time, respectively, while both AI reviewers correctly classified all 32 proposals (100%). These results suggest that current LLMs can produce project plans comparable to human-written ones in the eyes of human reviewers, but that AI reviewers show a systematic preference for AI-generated proposals. Our results suggest caution when deploying LLMs widely in proposal preparation and evaluation.

</details>

### [2] [Variational approach to Yukawa fluids. I. Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25908)
*S. A. Khrapak,A. G. Khrapak*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用以硬球流体为参考系的变分方法，计算了强耦合汤川流体（Yukawa fluid）的过量能量、熵和压力。研究表明，Percus-Yevick 维里熵（virial entropy）适用于此类计算，并与分子动力学模拟结果高度吻合，尤其在强耦合区域表现突出。该方法在数值数据稀缺时具有预测价值，并成功应用于估算强屏蔽条件下的熔化曲线位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为强耦合汤川流体的热力学性质（过量能量、熵、压力）提供一种无需大量数值模拟的计算方法，特别是在分子动力学数据稀缺或尚未获得的情况下，发展一种有效的预测工具。

Method: 采用变分方法（variational approach），以硬球流体（hard spheres）作为参考系统，计算强耦合汤川流体的过量热力学量。其中熵的计算使用了 Percus-Yevick 近似下的维里熵（Percus-Yevick virial entropy）。

Result: Percus-Yevick 维里熵适用于变分计算，所得结果与分子动力学模拟结果高度一致；在强耦合区域一致性尤为显著。该方法还成功估算了强屏蔽条件下熔化曲线（melting curve）的位置。

Conclusion: 基于硬球参考系的变分方法结合 Percus-Yevick 维里熵，可准确描述强耦合汤川流体的热力学性质，并在数值数据不足时作为有效的预测工具；该方法可进一步用于估算熔化曲线。

Abstract: The excess energy, entropy, and pressure of a strongly coupled Yukawa fluid are calculated from the variational approach using the fluid of hard spheres as a reference system. As in the case of the one-component plasma, the Percus-Yevick virial entropy is appropriate for such calculations and delivers remarkable agreement with available results from molecular dynamics simulations. The agreement with the molecular-dynamics results is particularly impressive in the strongly coupled regime, making this approach a useful predictive tool when numerical data are scarce or not yet available. As an application of the variational approach, we estimate the location of the melting curve in the regime of sufficiently strong screening.

</details>


### [3] [Variational approach to Yukawa fluids. II. Instantaneous elastic moduli and sound velocities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25923)
*S. A. Khrapak,A. G. Khrapak*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Bogoliubov不等式的变分方法，以硬球流体为参考体系，计算了Yukawa流体的瞬时剪切模量、体模量和纵向模量及相关声速，并验证了该方法的准确性；同时从状态方程推导了绝热声速，分析了其与瞬时弹性模量所得声速的关系，详细讨论了弱耦合与强耦合区域之间的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yukawa流体的弹性性质（elastic properties）和声速（sound velocities）在不同耦合强度下的系统表征尚不充分，尤其是瞬时弹性模量与绝热声速之间的对应关系有待厘清。

Method: 采用基于Bogoliubov不等式（Bogoliubov inequality）的变分方法，以硬球流体（hard spheres）作为参考体系，计算Yukawa流体（Yukawa fluids）的瞬时剪切模量（instantaneous shear modulus）、体模量（bulk modulus）和纵向模量（longitudinal modulus）及其相关声速。同时，从相应的状态方程（equation of state）评估绝热声速（adiabatic sound velocity）。

Result: 文档记录了该方法显著的准确性，并详细分析了弱耦合（weakly coupled）与强耦合（strongly coupled）区域之间的转变。

Conclusion: 基于Bogoliubov不等式的变分方法能够高精度地评估Yukawa流体的瞬时弹性模量和声速；绝热声速与瞬时弹性模量导出的纵向和体声速之间存在可讨论的关联，且系统从弱耦合到强耦合的过渡行为可以被清晰地刻画。

Abstract: The variational approach based on the Bogoliubov inequality using the fluid of hard spheres as a reference system is implemented to evaluate instantaneous shear, bulk and longitudinal elastic moduli, as well as related sound velocities of Yukawa fluids. The remarkable accuracy of this method is documented. In addition, we evaluate the adiabatic sound velocity from an appropriate equation of state and discuss its relation to the longitudinal and bulk sound velocities obtained from the corresponding instantaneous elastic moduli. The transition between weakly coupled and strongly coupled regimes is analyzed in detail.

</details>

### [4] [Momentum-Selective Two-Component Excitations in Electron-Doped Mott Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24936)
*Zeyu Han,Can Cui,Jia-Xin Zhang,Zheng-Yu Weng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过建立$t$-$t'$-$J$模型基态波函数的两分量结构（相干准粒子与不相干复合成分），揭示了电子掺杂铜氧化物中低能单粒子激发的不对称性：空穴掺杂时共振主导节点区激发，电子掺杂时相干准粒子在反节点区增强，从而统一解释了电子与空穴掺杂铜氧化物的迥异行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现电子掺杂与空穴掺杂铜氧化物（cuprates）在低能单粒子激发（single-particle excitations）上存在显著不对称性：电子掺杂体系同时表现出费米液体行为（Fermi-liquid behavior）和关联驱动的典型特征，这种二分性（dichotomy）对掺杂Mott绝缘体（doped Mott insulator）的统一理论描述构成了挑战。

Method: 论文首先从理论上建立$t$-$t'$-$J$模型基态波函数的两分量结构，然后基于单空穴水平的变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）方法对两种掺杂情况进行计算分析，并在此基础上提出有限掺杂下的唯象格林函数（phenomenological Green's function）。

Result: 对于空穴掺杂（$t'<0$），两分量间的共振占主导地位，并在低能节点区（nodal region）集中；对于电子掺杂（$t'>0$），相干准粒子传播在低能反节点区（antinodal region）选择性增强，形成动量空间分离：低能反节点区谱权重由相干准粒子主导，节点区则由不相干复合成分主导。

Conclusion: 两分量共振可物理解释为来自分数化自由度（fractionalized degrees of freedom）的复合，驱动了强关联相关现象；基于该结构和实验观测提出的有限掺杂唯象格林函数可产生与实验一致的谱特征，为统一描述电子与空穴掺杂铜氧化物提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Experimental studies reveal a striking asymmetry in low-energy single-particle excitations between electron- and hole-doped cuprates. Electron-doped cuprates display a nontrivial dichotomy: Fermi-liquid-like behavior (suggesting weaker electronic correlations) coexists with correlation-driven features typical of hole-doped systems. This dual nature challenges a unified description within a doped Mott insulator framework. The present work addresses this issue by establishing that the ground-state wave function of the $t$-$t'$-$J$ model generically possesses a two-component structure, comprising a coherent quasiparticle and an incoherent composite component. The kinetic energy arises from both the intrinsic propagation of the coherent quasiparticle and the resonance between these components. Using variational Monte Carlo at the level of a single hole, we show that for hole doping ($t'<0$), this resonance between components dominates and concentrates in the nodal region at low energies. This emergent propagation induced by resonance can be physically interpreted as originating from the recombination of fractionalized degrees of freedom, which drives various phenomena associated with strong correlations. Conversely, for electron doping ($t'>0$), the coherent quasiparticle propagation, which exhibits conventional properties of a Fermi liquid, is selectively enhanced in the antinodal region at low energies. This produces a separation in momentum space for systems with electron doping: the antinodal spectral weight at low energies is governed by the coherent quasiparticle, fundamentally differing from the nodal region, which remains dominated by the incoherent composite component. Motivated by such a structure and guided by experimental observations, we propose a phenomenological Green's function at finite doping, yielding spectral features consistent with experiments.

</details>


### [5] [Chiral Magnons: Mechanisms and Research Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24963)
*Wanxing Lin,Hanchen Deng,Bao-Tian Wang,Dao-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了手性磁子（chiral magnons）的统一框架，涵盖对称性破缺机制、材料实现、实验表征、输运响应及多体非厄米动力学，并评估了面向室温与器件平台的实现路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性磁子因其打破动量反转对称性（momentum-inversion symmetry）而具有本征非互易自旋波传播特性，在自旋信息传递、热-自旋转换及低耗散非互易微波器件方面具有重要潜力。近年来交变磁性的提出拓宽了手性磁子的物理起源与研究框架，使其成为凝聚态物理的研究前沿，因此亟需一篇系统综述来统一梳理其机制、材料与实验进展。

Method: 基于对称性分析（symmetry analysis）、模型哈密顿量（model Hamiltonians）和自旋波理论（spin-wave theory），结合第一性原理计算以及非弹性与极化中子散射、布里渊光散射等谱学测量和输运测量。

Result: 综述了由体带隙与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）诱导的手性磁子边缘态、通过手性自旋泵浦和腔-磁子杂化增强非互易性，以及由多粒子阻尼与增益-损耗竞争产生的非厄米特征。

Conclusion: 本综述为阐明手性磁子的微观机制、推进新型材料的合成与实验表征以及指导下一代非互易磁子器件设计提供了全面的参考。

Abstract: Chiral magnons are distinctive collective spin excitations in magnetic ordered systems, whose dispersion relations break momentum-inversion symmetry, $ω(\boldsymbol{k}) \neq ω(-\boldsymbol{k})$, resulting in essential non-reciprocal spin-wave propagation. This built-in directionality provides new opportunities for spin information transfer, thermal-spin interconversion, and low-dissipation non-reciprocal microwave devices, which complement but differ from topological magnonics. In recent years, the proposal and rapid development of altermagnetism have broadened the physical origin and research framework of chiral magnons, making them a research frontier in condensed matter physics. This review presents a unified framework for chiral magnons, covering symmetry-breaking mechanisms, material implementation, experimental characterization, transport response, and many-body non-Hermitian dynamics, and evaluates routes toward room-temperature and device-related platforms. The discussion is based on symmetry analysis, model Hamiltonians, and spin-wave theory, combined with first-principles calculations as well as recent spectroscopic (e.g., inelastic and polarized neutron scattering, Brillouin light scattering) and transport measurements. This review further summarizes bulk-gap and Berry-curvature induced chiral magnon edge states, the enhancement of non-reciprocity via chiral spin pumping and cavity-magnon hybrids, as well as non-Hermitian features arising from multiparticle damping and gain-loss competition. This review provides a comprehensive reference for elucidating the underlying mechanisms of chiral magnons, advancing the synthesis and experimental characterization of novel materials, and also guiding the design of next-generation non-reciprocal magnonic devices.

</details>


### [6] [Stacking-dependent anisotropic altermagnetism in V$_{1/3}$NbS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25213)
*Chris J. Lygouras,Nathan Prouse,Jack H. Drouin,Youzhe Chen,Laura Garcia-Gassull,Zili Feng,Mingxuan Fu,Lü Fang,Alexander I. Kolesnikov,Christina Hoffman,Yiqing Hao,Huibo Cao,Maxime A. Siegler,Robert J. Birgeneau,Roser Valentí,Satoru Nakatsuji,Collin L. Broholm*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过单晶X射线、中子衍射、输运和磁化测量，发现V₁/₃NbS₂的两种不同多型体（polytypes）具有迥异的易磁化轴和反常霍尔响应。非弹性中子散射数据分析表明，振荡型RKKY相互作用在两种多型体中均稳定了准共线A型交变磁性（altermagnetic order），但易轴相互垂直。层状堆叠顺序对宏观磁性有显著调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究范德瓦尔斯材料NbS₂层间三角晶格磁性过渡金属离子的堆叠序列（stacking sequence）如何影响材料的宏观磁性和电输运性质。

Method: 使用单晶X射线和中子衍射（single crystal x-ray and neutron diffraction）进行结构表征；结合输运测量（transport measurements）和磁化测量（magnetization measurements）研究宏观物性；通过非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）数据的自洽分析（self-consistent analysis）揭示微观磁相互作用机制。

Result: 发现V₁/₃NbS₂存在两种不同的多型体（polytypes），分别具有不同的易磁化轴（easy axes of magnetization）和反常霍尔响应（anomalous Hall responses）。非弹性中子散射证据表明，振荡型RKKY相互作用（oscillatory RKKY interactions）延伸至1 nm尺度，在两种多型体中均稳定了准共线A型交变磁性（quasi-collinear A-type altermagnetic orders），但易轴相互垂直。

Conclusion: 层状体材料的多型体堆叠序列（stacking sequence）对宏观反常霍尔响应和磁性有显著影响，这为工程化调控层状三维固体的体性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: We report profound impacts of the stacking sequence of triangular lattices of magnetic transition metal ions intercalated between the layers of the van der Waals material NbS$_2$. Using single crystal x-ray and neutron diffraction, and transport and magnetization measurements, we show there are two distinct polytypes of $\rm V_{1/3}NbS_2$ with disparate easy axes of magnetization and different anomalous Hall responses. Self-consistent analysis of inelastic neutron scattering data provides evidence for oscillatory RKKY interactions that extend to 1 nm and stabilize quasi-collinear A-type altermagnetic orders in both polytypes though with perpendicular easy axes. The detailed stacking sequence of a bulk polytype crystal dramatically impact its macroscopic anomalous Hall response and magnetism, which suggests a new path to engineer the bulk properties of a layered three dimensional solid.

</details>


### [7] [Covariance Geometry of Basis-Resolved Low-Energy Wavefunctions in the Spin-$1/2$ Kitaev--Heisenberg Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25462)
*Sk Saniur Rahaman,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基分辨协方差框架（basis-resolved covariance framework），用于研究自旋-1/2 Kitaev-Heisenberg模型中低能多体波函数的组织结构。通过主成分分析（PCA）识别主导协方差模，揭示了协方差几何（covariance geometry）在相图中的系统性演化：磁有序相表现为一维协方差流形，常规磁相边界存在协方差模竞争，Kitaev区域呈现内在多维协方差几何。该框架为刻画阻挫量子多体系统提供了超越常规序参量的互补统计视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对阻挫量子多体系统中低能波函数的组织方式缺乏有效的统计刻画手段，传统的序参量（order parameters）难以捕捉波函数在不同算符表示下的复杂关联结构。本文旨在建立一种基分辨协方差几何方法，从统计视角系统揭示波函数的内部关联结构及其在相图上的演化规律。

Method: 从低能态的局域自旋（local-spin）、键关联（bond-correlation）和面通量（plaquette-flux）三种算符表示中构造协方差矩阵，利用主成分分析（Principal Component Analysis, PCA）提取主导集体协方差模（collective covariance modes），并结合香农熵（Shannon entropy）与参与比（participation ratio）进行定量度量。

Result: 磁有序相表现为本质上的一维协方差流形；常规磁相边界处存在主导协方差模之间的竞争；Kitaev区域呈现出内在的多维协方差几何。同一多体波函数在不同算符表示下产生不同的协方差几何，表明协方差几何由量子态和探测所用的物理可观测量共同决定。

Method: 提出了一种普适的动力学机制：受运动约束的激发通过与局域序参量场耦合而获得迁移能力，序参量的凝聚使系统得以大幅降低动能，从而稳定长程有序相。该机制在两种典型体系（具有磁化偶极子守恒律的自旋链和 Kitaev-Kondo 模型）中进行了实例化说明。

Conclusion: 在热激活条件下，提高温度反而可能促进有序化，因为热能激活更多受约束的激发，从而增大其耦合序参量后获得的动能增益。这一反直觉结论为理解强关联体系中温度与有序之间的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: A fascinating feature of strongly correlated systems is that excitations may carry nontrivial quantum numbers under emergent symmetries which, in some cases, may strongly constrain their motion. This mechanism becomes operative whenever a finite density of mobility-constrained excitations is present, either through non-equilibrium preparation or thermal activation. In the latter case, we argue that increasing temperature can, counterintuitively, promote order by enhancing the kinetic-energy gain of thermally activated excitations.

Abstract: In this work, we show that Fermi Sets---a provably universal neural network architecture for fermionic wavefunctions---can be systematically scaled up to find interacting ground states through energy minimization in a variational Monte Carlo framework. Abstract: We propose doping $SU(3)$-symmetric spin liquids as a route toward charge-$6e$ superconductivity. As a concrete platform, we study a bilayer triangular-lattice Hubbard model with $SU(3)$ spin symmetry and interlayer antiferromagnetic exchange. Pairing these trions gives a time-reversal-symmetric charge-$6e$ superconductor. Doping Abelian $SU(3)_1$ and $SU(6)_1$ chiral spin liquids yields chiral charge-$6e$ superconductors with and without residual Abelian topological order, respectively. Doping a non-Abelian $SU(3)_2$ chiral spin liquid leads to a non-Abelian chiral charge-$6e$ superconductor intertwined with $SO(3)_{-3}$ topological order and supporting non-Abelian $h/(6e)$ superconducting vortices. Our results identify doped $SU(3)$ spin liquids as a natural setting where symmetry, fractionalization, and topology cooperate to produce charge-$6e$ superconductivity.

Method: 设计8个由专家构思的研究项目（涵盖物理、天体物理和宇宙学），让人类专家与AI提示者（AI prompters）并行执行相同的文献综述任务。AI端使用了2025年中的模型：ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT Deep Research和Gemini，并比较人类与AI所选文献的重叠情况。

Result: 人类与AI所选参考文献的重叠率低于6%；AI生成的参考文献中，3%为完全捏造的文献（fabricated references），64%为存在至少一个字段错误的真实论文（real papers with incorrect fields），包括标题、作者、年份、期刊、DOI或链接错误。2026年模型ChatGPT Pro 5.5的单项目测试中未发现任何捏造或元数据不匹配。

Conclusion: 截至2025年中期，大语言模型尚无法独立复现称职的专家文献检索，但具备辅助人类进行文献搜索的潜力；AI生成参考文献的可靠性仍需系统验证。较新的2026年模型在文献准确性上表现出显著改进。

Abstract: We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can assist with literature reviews for scientific research. We perform a controlled study of eight expert-conceived research projects across the areas of physics, astrophysics, and cosmology. Each project has a defined background and goal, and human experts and AI prompters are asked to perform identical literature review tasks in parallel. We compare the relevant literature selected by humans with that selected by mid-2025 LLMs (ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT Deep Research, and Gemini). We find the overlap between human- and AI-selected references to be small ($<$6\%), indicating that AI models do not yet reproduce a competent expert search on their own, though they have the potential to complement literature searches by humans. We then assess the reliability and completeness of AI-generated candidate references, distinguishing two types of hallucination: fabrications (references to nonexistent papers) and metadata mismatches (real papers with one or more incorrect fields). We find that while fabricated references make up 3\% of the AI-generated references, 64\% are real papers with at least one incorrect field (title, author, year, journal, DOI, or link), indicating that the mid-2025 models require systematic verification. However, the performance is significantly improved for the 2026 model ChatGPT Pro 5.5, with a single-project test showing zero fabrication or metadata mismatches.

</details>

Abstract: Providing a practical and hadron-level definition of multiple jet flavors has been a long-standing challenge in collider physics. Previous work has introduced a data-driven, operational definition of quark and gluon jets, but no robust generalization beyond two jet categories presently exists. To address this, we introduce a machine-learning framework called "simplex demixing'' to extract $T$ jet flavors (or topics in the statistics literature) from $M$ data samples (or mixtures) with minimal constraints. Intuitively, our procedure identifies the maximally separable categories in the data, translating a multi-category classifier on the $M$ mixtures into a bounded geometric object with $T$ vertices. We first demonstrate our procedure on a toy problem to infer the truth-level fractions of down-quark, up-quark, and gluon jets from synthetic mixtures of the three pure samples. We then propose a tag-and-probe strategy to extract multiple light-flavor categories in a more realistic collider setting involving dijet production. As expected, the identifiability of jet flavors depends on their relative abundance in the samples and the hadron-level information available to the classifier architecture. Our work opens the door to data-driven extractions of multiple jet flavor properties at the Large Hadron Collider.

Abstract: Bound-state $β$-decay of tritium, the process, in which the final-state electron is created in a bound atomic state of the produced ${\rm ^3He}$ atom instead of freely flying away, is predicted by the standard theory of weak interactions but has not been observed so far. We also propose a novel approach to direct neutrino mass measurement and sterile neutrino search based on accurate determination of the speed of the produced ${\rm ^3He}$ atoms through Doppler broadening of the emitted photon lines.

The momentum conservation constraint becomes less important as more phonons are produced, yielding the transition to incoherent scattering. Using numerical calculations of the 1D structure factor, we also obtain quantitative validation of using an incoherent approximation to compute sub-GeV dark matter scattering in realistic 3D crystals.

</details>

Abstract: We present a next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) QCD analysis of polarized parton distribution functions (PDFs) based on the world data set of inclusive polarized deep-inelastic scattering (DIS). Abstract: We investigate how possible new CP-violating top-quark interactions are encoded in the quantum state of a produced $t\bar t$ pair. We derive analytic expressions for the production density matrix in several benchmark channels relevant to hadron, lepton and photon colliders. In a common spin basis, we identify two characteristic CP-odd structures in the Fano--Bloch decomposition: a difference between the top and antitop polarisation vectors and an antisymmetric component of the spin-correlation matrix. We construct observables that directly probe these structures and study how quantum information measures, including discord, concurrence, magic and trace distance, respond to CP-even and CP-odd SMEFT contributions. Finally, using current measurements and future collider projections, we assess the sensitivity of these observables to possible new sources of CP violation in top-quark production. Abstract: We develop a quantum-tomographic framework for determining whether possible CP-odd effects in $t\bar t$ events originate in production, in decay, or in both. In the narrow-width approximation, the process $I\to t\bar t\to b\ell^+ν\,\bar b\ell^-\barν$ factorises into a production density matrix and top and antitop decay density matrices. We extend the standard tomography procedure to a general anomalous $Wtb$ vertex and derive the corresponding angular distributions. Polar-angle distributions retain their usual tomographic form, up to modifications of the spin-analysing powers, and can therefore be used to reconstruct the production density matrix and test its CP properties. By contrast, dedicated azimuthal observables involving the $b$--lepton decay planes contain characteristic sine modulations that provide linear probes of possible new CP-violating interactions in the decay vertex. Combining the two classes of observables gives a systematic strategy for separating sources of CP violation in production and in decay. We illustrate the resulting angular signatures for representative $t\bar t$ production scenarios at hadron and lepton colliders.

Both resummation results are obtained from the factorization theorems in Soft-Collinear Effective Theory. In particular, we study the Sudakov shoulders in the trijet region, originating from the incomplete cancellation of infrared singularities between final states with different parton multiplicities, and resum the induced large logarithms to all orders. The shoulder resummation yields sizable corrections and improves the perturbative stability of the distribution. Our results provide state-of-the-art predictions for heavy jet mass in $H \to gg$ and $H\to q\bar{q}$ and can be applied to precision Higgs measurements at future $e^+e^-$ colliders.

Motivation: 探索双重狭义相对论（doubly special relativity）框架下光子气体的热力学行为，旨在揭示普朗克能量标度作为观察者无关的不变量对光子气体热力学性质的影响，并检验该理论在高温极限下与标准狭义相对论结果的偏离程度。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamics of a photon gas within the Magueijo-Smolin formulation of doubly special relativity, a framework that augments the speed of light with an observer-independent energy scale of the order of the Planck energy. We derive the logarithmic grand partition function, and the complete set of thermodynamic quantities for the photon gas, including the Helmholtz free energy, internal energy, entropy, pressure, and heat capacity, and perform their numerical evaluation. For temperatures approaching the Planck scale, the finite cutoff induces a systematic suppression of all thermodynamic functions relative to their standard special-relativistic counterparts.

</details>

Method: 以Berry相位（Berry phase）和Dirac哈密顿量（Dirac Hamiltonian）为起点，将绕数（winding）的概念推广至石墨烯、拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）、Weyl半金属（Weyl semimetals）和磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmions）等多个体系，系统分析对称性保护下波函数连续性（wavefunction continuity）对物理响应的约束。

Result: 在斯格明子中，绕数划分磁位形空间并稳定超小信息载体；在石墨烯中，赝自旋匹配（pseudospin matching）调控Klein隧穿（Klein tunneling），实现栅控传输间隙；在拓扑绝缘体与Weyl半金属中，自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）与Berry曲率工程（Berry-curvature engineering）产生电可选自旋流，螺旋性依赖的光跃迁产生圆光电流（circular photogalvanic response）。

Conclusion: 拓扑不仅是量子物质的一种分类工具，更是一种可工程化的设计语言（design language），利用对称性保护的波函数连续性来实现存储器、开关、驱动与传感等实际功能。

Abstract: Spin, pseudospin, valley, polarization, and other two-component degrees of freedom share the geometry of SU(2), yet their topological manifestations are usually discussed as separate phenomena. This review develops a common geometric language for their winding in momentum and real space,beginning with Berry phase and the Dirac Hamiltonian and extending to graphene, topological insulators, Weyl semimetals, and magnetic skyrmions. We argue that the common thread is not merely topology itself, but the continuity constraints imposed on two-component wavefunctions. Whenever the relevant symmetry is preserved, winding determines which states can continuously connect across an interface or deformation, thereby governing transmission, torque generation, optical selection rules, and other physical responses. We then ask a practical question: what does topology buy an engineer? In skyrmions, winding partitions magnetic configuration space and stabilizes ultrasmall information carriers with tunable dynamics. In graphene, pseudospin matching governs Klein tunneling, enabling a gate-controlled transmission gap without sacrificing the massless Dirac dispersion. In topological insulators and Weyl semimetals, spin-momentum locking and Berry-curvature engineering generate electrically selectable spin currents, while helicity-dependent optical transitions produce circular photogalvanic responses. Together, these examples suggest that topology is not merely a classification of quantum matter, but a design language in which symmetry-protected wavefunction continuity can be engineered for memory, switching, actuation, and sensing.

</details>

Abstract: Entanglement carries universal content that labels phases and critical points. We study the entanglement entropy of the steady states of critical non-Hermitian free-fermion chains. Remarkably, weak real onsite disorder leaves this logarithmic scaling intact and enhances the entanglement. </details>

Conclusion: 这些发现表明能斯特效应可作为莫尔材料中费米面拓扑的灵敏探针，并揭示了范霍夫奇点的普适热电特征。

Abstract: This thesis investigates how superperiodicity, quasiperiodicity, and disorder shape electronic and spin transport in graphene-based systems, with an emphasis on experimentally relevant length scales and realistic atomistic modeling. This disorder-induced delocalization is further linked to changes in the quantum metric extracted from optical conductivity, revealing a direct connection between transport, electronic geometry, and the real-space extent of the underlying states. The study then turns to graphene quasicrystal approximants and hybrid multilayer stacks, identifying sub-ballistic transport and self-similar localization patterns as signatures of quasicrystalline order, while also demonstrating their strong fragility against disorder and interlayer proximity effects. Finally, the thesis addresses spin transport in suspended monolayer graphene, showing that atomic-scale corrugations generate short-range fluctuating Rashba fields that can limit spin lifetimes to the nanosecond range even when charge transport remains close to ballistic. Taken together, these results provide a unified picture of how geometry, disorder, and structural complexity govern transport phenomena in twisted and corrugated graphene systems.

We show that these contributions significantly modify the angular velocity profile and can lead to features not accessible within existing models. Furthermore, we demonstrate significant back-action effects through modeling and experiment; specifically, the dynamics of the nuclear spin bath depends upon the electron spin occupation schedule. Finally, our modeling quantitatively predicts the benefits from compensating for the slow drift of Overhauser fields in qubit operations. Our findings indicate that compensating for an Overhauser rotation measured $Δt$ in the past results in an effective $T_2^*$, which we denote $\tilde{T}_2^*(Δt)$ for clarity, under certain scenarios of interest, can be one or two orders of magnitude larger than the ergodic $T_2^*$ if the Overhauser rotation is re-characterized every 100 milliseconds; that is, $\tilde{T}_2^*(Δt = 100~{\rm ms})$ can be $10$ to $100$ times larger than $T_2^*$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 有效场论揭示了算符扩散与混沌增长的对称性机制，Lyapunov指数的正性由实时间路径积分的收敛性保证，并在Brownian SYK链中通过鞍点展开验证了该构造。

Conclusion: 本文发现了scrambling动力学的对称性起源，将算符大小水动力学纳入有效场论框架，并为理解混沌系统提供了一致性的对称性描述。

Abstract: Operator scrambling is commonly diagnosed by the growth of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs), yet a general symmetry principle underlying their effective dynamics has remained elusive. For Brownian or short-time-correlated large-$N$ Majorana systems, we develop a symmetry-based effective field theory for operator scrambling, organized by a strong-to-weak U(1) symmetry breaking in operator space. The key observation is that, in the noninteracting fermion limit, the four-fold Keldysh contour representation of an OTOC admits an emergent strong U(1) symmetry in a doubled Hilbert-space description, even when the original system has no ordinary conserved quantity. The associated slow mode is the phase of the strong-charge creation operator, whose conjugate density is identified with the local operator size. Generic interactions explicitly break the strong symmetry and generate a mass term at lowest order for the would-be Goldstone mode, thereby converting diffusive operator spreading into chaotic growth. We further show that higher-order symmetry breaking terms are tightly constrained by an emergent duality that combines time reversal with contour permutation. This duality fixes the effective action up to quadratic order in the response field, relates the multiplicative noise strength directly to the Lyapunov exponent, and makes the positivity of the Lyapunov exponent a consequence of convergence of the real-time path integral. The resulting OTOC dynamics is governed by a noisy FKPP equation, which captures within a unified framework the early-time exponential growth, ballistic propagation, nonlinear saturation, and stochastic front broadening of operator scrambling. We verify this construction in a Brownian SYK chain, where a direct saddle-point expansion reproduces the symmetry-based effective action. Our results reveal a symmetry origin of operator-size hydrodynamics and scrambling.

</details>

We find that the critical behavior is in a distinctly different universality class from that of traditional undirected percolation, but that all bond arrangements appear to fall in the same universality class.

Result: 新构建的电路族产生的影响矩阵是非马尔可夫的，展现出非平凡的时间关联（temporal correlations）。与之前可解实例（如双幺正电路）不同，这些电路生成的关联模式与典型多体系统（typical many-body systems）的关联模式非常相似。

Here we propose a new systematic approach to generating quantum circuits with complex dynamics for which the influence matrices can be written down exactly. In contrast to previous frameworks of this kind, such as dual-unitary circuits, the resulting influence matrices are non-Markovian, exhibiting nontrivial temporal correlations. We explicitly construct a broad family of circuits of this kind, based on dressing free-fermion (matchgate) circuits with appropriately chosen interaction terms. We show that, contrary to previous solvable instances, these circuits produce patterns of correlations that closely resemble that of typical many-body systems. Our approach can be directly interpreted in terms of an error correction scheme where the terms breaking the solvability of the influence matrices play the role of errors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多老化材料（aging materials）中观测到对数时间依赖性，但无论是对数松弛律（$\ln t$ relaxation law）还是$1/t$事件率都无法唯一确定背后的随机机制。本文旨在构造一个具有明确事件级构造的对数老化过程，并系统刻画其统计特征，以提供更严格的检验标准。

Method: 定义一种通过对数比率$U_n=\ln(T_{n+1}/T_n)$为独立同分布（i.i.d.）且具有显式非指数密度的事件时间乘法过程，该过程基于每次事件后迭代年龄条件前向更新律（age-conditioned forward-recurrence law）构建。

Result: 事件计数（event count）的均值和方差均随$\ln(t/t_0)$线性增长；第$n$个事件时间的密度在对数正态中心区之外具有固定$n$的代数远尾（algebraic far tail）；时钟统计量产生对数漂移与扩散、局域细致平衡下的爱因斯坦关系（Einstein relation），以及超慢输运和靶存活律；时间平均均方位移的相对涨落按$1/\ln(T/t_0)$衰减，但其均值不收敛到系综滞后均方位移。

Conclusion: 该对数时钟模型应通过乘子（multiplier）、计数、输运、首达时间（first-passage）和有限窗口可观测量的联合无再拟合自洽性来检验，而非单条对数曲线。该事件级构造与其扩散极限下的广义福克-普朗克方程（generalized Fokker-Planck）和随机从属化（random-clock subordination）表示存在本质区别，且广义朗之万闭合（generalized-Langevin closure）虽能匹配部分响应和协方差，但无法复现事件计数或稀有持续时间统计。

Abstract: Logarithmic time dependences occur in many aging materials, but neither a $\ln t$ relaxation law nor a $1/t$ event rate uniquely identifies the underlying stochastic mechanism. We examine a specific log-aging process defined by iterating the age-conditioned forward-recurrence law after every event. This rule makes the event times multiplicative: the logarithmic ratios $U_n=\ln(T_{n+1}/T_n)$ are independent and identically distributed with an explicit non-exponential density. Consequently, both the mean and the variance of the event count grow linearly with $\ln(t/t_0)$, while the density of the $n$th event time has a log-normal central sector and a fixed-$n$ algebraic far tail. These clock statistics generate logarithmic drift and spreading, an Einstein relation under local detailed balance, and ultraslow transit and target-survival laws. They also separate trajectory reproducibility from ensemble--time equivalence: the relative scatter of the time-averaged mean-square displacement decays as $1/\ln(T/t_0)$, although its mean does not converge to the ensemble lag MSD. We distinguish the exact event-level construction from its diffusion-limit generalized Fokker--Planck and random-clock subordination representations, and from a generalized-Langevin closure that can match selected responses and covariances but need not reproduce event counts or rare-duration statistics. The proposed clock is therefore tested not by a single logarithmic curve, but by the joint, no-refitting consistency of multiplier, count, transport, first-passage, and finite-window observables.

</details>


### [53] [Don't truncate, decompose: mean-field dynamics of long-range quantum systems from strongly correlated states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25434)
*Federico Carollo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文挑战了平均场理论（mean-field theory）无法描述长程开放量子系统（long-range open quantum systems）的普遍共识，证明在强长程区域（strong long-range regime）中，平均场动力学可以精确重构整个演化过程，并首次识别出累积量展开（cumulant expansion）能精确预测低阶累积量的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍认为，在对称性破缺（symmetry breaking）或从强关联态（如宏观叠加态）出发时，平均场理论无法描述长程开放量子系统，因此依赖累积量展开方法；然而累积量展开依赖于缺乏明确理由的截断近似。本文旨在澄清这一方法在强长程区域的概念有效性，并重新确立平均场理论在这类系统中的精确性。

Abstract: We study the out-of-equilibrium dynamics of the spherical Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model with non-reciprocal asymmetric couplings.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该框架定量描述了主动驱动和被动非平衡驱动两类系统，识别了非单调输运的共同机制，建立了主动与非平衡被动驱动下输运的统一描述。

Abstract: The stochastic dynamics of interacting particles far from equilibrium remains a fundamental challenge in statistical physics. While reciprocal interactions often permit effective one-body descriptions, such reductions generally fail for nonreciprocal interactions, which are ubiquitous in driven and active systems. We develop a unified theoretical framework for interacting particles with reciprocal and nonreciprocal couplings, applicable to the principal classes of active matter, including run-and-tumble, active Brownian, and active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particles. As a minimal example, we study a passive particle driven by an active particle. On a periodic ring, we show analytically that the driven particle is always diffusive at long times, independent of the microscopic driving mechanism. Analytical predictions and numerical simulations reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of the effective diffusivity on the driving activity, giving rise to both enhanced and suppressed transport. Remarkably, the same behavior occurs in an equilibrium system driven out of equilibrium by coupling the driving particle to a higher local temperature. Our framework quantitatively captures both systems, identifies the common mechanism underlying the nonmonotonic transport, and establishes a unified description of transport under active and passive nonequilibrium driving.

</details>

We construct the relevant diffusive eigenoperators explicitly and show that, in the quartic sector, they can be interpreted as interacting pairs of bilinear hydrodynamic modes. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般线性群（general linear group）稳定上同调（stable cohomology）的生成元可由不变微分形式表示，将其沿热带Torelli映射（tropical Torelli map）拉回后，可得到与图关联的规范收敛积分。这些图积分与GL_n上同调及图复形（graph complexes）上同调密切相关，因此一个自然的问题是确定这些图积分的数值。

Method: 论文通过证明原始规范图积分与形变量子化中的复位置空间积分相等来回答上述问题。推导过程涉及图复形的上同调性质以及积分表示之间的对应关系。

Abstract: The Borel classes generating the stable cohomology of the general linear group can be represented by invariant differential forms. It is known that pulling these forms back along a tropical Torelli map yields canonical convergent integrals associated to graphs, which are closely connected to the cohomology of $\mathrm{GL}_n$ and of graph complexes. A natural question is what numbers these graph integrals are. We answer this for primitive canonical integrals by showing that they coincide with a family of complex position-space integrals arising in deformation quantisation. As a consequence, canonical integrals of graphs evaluate to single-valued multiple zeta values. We further deduce that every single-valued multiple zeta value occurs as a rational linear combination of Feynman periods of graphs with massless propagators. Finally, in the commutative graph complex, our result implies that the two associated cocycles agree.

</details>


### [61] [Calabi-Yau Deformation Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25438)
*Jakob Ulmer*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文将Kontsevich的形式性态射（formality morphism）从形变量子化（deformation quantization）扩展到Calabi-Yau版本，并发现任何单位模全纯Poisson Calabi-Yau（unimodular holomorphic Poisson Calabi-Yau）都具有其解析函数代数的典范闭形变量子化。更广泛的动机是通过Sen-Zwiebach的弦顶点（string vertices）在Calabi-Yau范畴的框架下为Kontsevich形变量子化提供解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是在Calabi-Yau范畴（Calabi-Yau categories）的设定下，借助Sen-Zwiebach的弦顶点（string vertices）为Kontsevich的形变量子化（deformation quantization）提供一种新的解释路径。

Method: 记录了Kontsevich形式性态射（formality morphism）的Calabi-Yau版本，将其从标准形变量子化框架推广到Calabi-Yau几何语境中。

Result: 发现任何单位模全纯Poisson Calabi-Yau（unimodular holomorphic Poisson Calabi-Yau）都自然地存在一个解析函数代数的典范闭形变量子化（canonical closed deformation quantization）。

Conclusion: Calabi-Yau版本的形式性态射为理解Kontsevich形变量子化在Calabi-Yau范畴中的推广提供了基础，并与弦理论中的弦顶点构造建立了联系。

Abstract: We record the Calabi-Yau version of Kontsevich's formality morphism from deformation quantization. As a special case we find that any unimodular holomorphic Poisson Calabi-Yau has a canonical closed deformation quantization of its resolved algebra of functions. A broader motivation is to find an explanation of Kontsevich's deformation quantization in the setting of Calabi-Yau categories via Sen-Zwiebach's string vertices.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次搜索稀有衰变过程 $B^0 \to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-$，基于 Belle 和 Belle~II 探测器收集的 11.6 亿 $\Upsilon(4S)$ 事例数据，未发现信号，并在 90\% 置信水平上设定分支比上限为 $8.3 \times 10^{-4}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该衰变过程 $B^0 \to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-$ 是标准模型（Standard Model）中高度压低的味改变中性流（flavor-changing neutral current, FCNC）过程，对新物理（new physics）贡献敏感。此前实验上尚未针对该过程进行过搜索。

Method: 研究使用 Belle 和 Belle~II 探测器采集的 11.6 亿 $\Upsilon(4S)$ 事例。在 $\Upsilon(4S)\to B^0\bar{B}^0$ 衰变中，非信号的 $\bar{B}^0$ 介子通过强子道（hadronic channel）完全重建；信号侧 $B^0$ 介子衰变中，$\tau$ 轻子选取仅含一个带电粒子的末态。采用多变量分类器（multivariate classifier）将多个区分性输入合并为单一拟合观测量。

Result: 未观测到信号，在 90\% 置信水平（confidence level）下设定分支比（branching fraction）上限 $\mathcal{B}(B^0\to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-) < 8.3 \times 10^{-4}$。结合同旋伙伴（isospin-partner）衰变 $B^+\to K^+τ^+τ^-$ 的测量结果，得到 $\mathcal{B}(B\to Kτ^+τ^-) < 5.4\times10^{-4}$（90\% 置信水平）。

Conclusion: 这是对 $B^0 \to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-$ 衰变的首次实验搜索，所设上限对标准模型预测及新物理模型提供了实验约束。

Abstract: We present the first search for $B^0 \to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-$ decays. We look for signal decays in $B^0\bar B^0$ events produced in asymmetric-energy electron-positron collisions. This work uses samples from the Belle and Belle~II detectors, comprising 1.16 billion $Υ(4S)$ events. In $Υ(4S)\to B^0\bar{B}^0$ decays, the non-signal $\bar{B}^0$ meson is fully reconstructed in a hadronic channel. For the signal $B^0$ meson, $τ$-lepton decays into final states with a single charged particle are selected. A multivariate classifier is used to combine several discriminating inputs into a single fit observable. We observe no evidence for the signal and set an upper limit on the branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(B^0\to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-) < 8.3 \times 10^{-4}$ at the 90\% confidence level. Combining this with the recent measurement of the isospin-partner decay $B^+\to K^+τ^+τ^-$, we determine an upper limit $\mathcal{B}(B\to Kτ^+τ^-) < 5.4\times10^{-4}$ at the 90\% confidence level.

</details>


### [63] [Measurement of the fragmentation properties of jets containing $Υ$(nS) mesons in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25653)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用质心能量为13 TeV、积分亮度为138 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，测量了包含Υ(nS)介子的喷注（jet）的碎裂性质。通过重建Υ(nS)介子衰变产生的正负缪子对，分析了Υ(nS)介子动量沿喷注动量方向的纵向与横向投影。将结果与包含部分子簇射中夸克偶素产生最新模型的蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）预言进行比较，发现现有模型对数据的描述不令人满意，表明此类过程的建模仍有改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重夸克偶素（quarkonia）在强子对撞中的产生机制仍是量子色动力学（QCD）的重要课题。对含有Υ(nS)介子的喷注进行碎裂测量，可为检验和优化部分子簇射（parton shower）中夸克偶素产生的理论模型提供关键的实验约束。

Method: 基于质心能量13 TeV、积分亮度138 fb⁻¹的质子-质子碰撞数据，通过重建Υ(nS)介子衰变至正负缪子对来识别与喷注关联的Υ(nS)信号。进一步研究Υ(nS)介子动量沿对应喷流动量方向的纵向与横向投影分布。

Result: 将实验测得的纵向与横向动量投影分布与包含最新夸克偶素产生模型的蒙特卡罗预言进行比较，发现现有模型对数据的描述不够理想。

Conclusion: 现有蒙特卡罗模型在描述含Υ(nS)喷注的碎裂性质方面存在不足，该类过程的建模仍有待进一步改进。

Abstract: A measurement of the fragmentation properties of jets containing $Υ$(nS) mesons using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, is presented. The $Υ$(nS) mesons associated with jets are reconstructed via their decays producing pairs of oppositely charged muons. The longitudinal and transverse projections of the momenta of the $Υ$(nS) mesons along the momenta of the corresponding jets are studied. The results are compared with Monte Carlo predictions, including recent developments in the modelling of quarkonia production in parton showers. The description of the data by these Monte Carlo predictions is found to be unsatisfactory, leaving room for improvements in the modelling of such processes.

</details>


### [64] [Observation of the electroweak production of $γγjj$ at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV in $140\ \text{fb}^{-1}$ of $pp$ collision data with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25869)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道在ATLAS实验中对双孤立光子与双喷子伴随产生的弱电产生（electroweak production）的观测，并测量了相应的面元微分截面（fiducial and differential cross sections），同时基于有效场论（effective field theory）对反常四规范玻色子耦合（anomalous quartic gauge couplings）设定了上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证标准模型（Standard Model）中双光子加双喷子弱电产生过程的预测，并通过微分截面测量和有效场论解释，探索可能超出标准模型（beyond Standard Model）的新物理信号，特别是反常四规范玻色子耦合。

Method: 基于大型强子对撞机（LHC）上ATLAS探测器采集的质子-质子对撞数据（质心能量√s = 13 TeV，积分亮度140 fb⁻¹），对双孤立光子伴随双喷子产生的弱电过程进行事件选择，通过统计显著性检验观测信号，并测量面元截面和微分截面；同时利用有效场论对维数为8的算符（dimension-8 operators）的Wilson系数设定限制。

Result: 双孤立光子与双喷子弱电产生过程以6.2σ的显著性被观测到，观测到的面元截面为σ^{EW}_{obs}= 13.8^{+3.0}_{-2.6} fb，与标准模型预测值σ^{EW}_{obs}= 17.1^{+2.4}_{-2.4} fb一致。微分截面针对多种运动学变量进行了测量，并与理论预言进行了比较。此外，还报道了结合QCD与弱电产生过程的截面结果，并对反常四规范玻色子耦合的参数化设定了上限。

Conclusion: 该测量结果与标准模型预测在误差范围内一致，未发现超出标准模型的新物理迹象；通过有效场论方法设定的Wilson系数上限为标准模型以外的反常四规范玻色子耦合提供了实验约束。

Abstract: This Letter presents the observation of the electroweak production of two isolated photons in association with two jets together with measurements of the corresponding fiducial and differential cross sections. The analysis is performed using proton-proton collision data collected by ATLAS at the LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV and with an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The electroweak production of two isolated photons in association with two jets is observed with a significance of $6.2 σ$ and with a fiducial cross-section of $σ^{\mathrm{EW}}_{\mathrm{obs}}= 13.8^{+3.0}_{-2.6}\, \mathrm{fb}$, in agreement with the Standard Model prediction of $σ^{\mathrm{EW}}_{\mathrm{obs}}= 17.1^{+2.4}_{-2.4}\, \mathrm{fb}$. Differential cross-sections are measured as a function of various kinematical variables probing photon and jet properties and compared with predictions. In addition, fiducial and differential cross-sections are reported for the combined QCD and electroweak production of two isolated photons and two jets. The measurement is also interpreted in the context of an effective field theory, and limits are set on the Wilson coefficients of several dimension-8 operators parameterising anomalous quartic gauge couplings.

</details>


### [65] [The calibration of large-radius jets using the Run 2 dataset with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25893)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于蒙特卡洛模拟和质子-质子对撞数据，建立了大半径喷注（large-radius jets）的校准流程，涵盖了Run 2的Soft Drop算法修正与现场验证，最终将喷注能量与质量标度的残余不确定性控制在1%（横动量≤1 TeV）和2–3%（≤2 TeV），从而支持超越标准模型（Standard Model）的高灵敏度搜索和标准模型精确测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机（LHC）上的高能量对撞会产生高横动量的“增强型对象”（boosted objects），其衰变产物在大半径喷注中难以直接区分。ATLAS实验需要精确校准大半径喷注的能量和质标度，以保证超越标准模型搜索的灵敏度和标准模型测量的精度。

Method: 采用基于蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）的方法进行初始修正；随后利用质子-质子对撞数据（$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV）实施原位方法（in situ methods），验证喷注能量与质量标度及其分辨率。喷注重建基于统一流对象（Unified Flow Objects），并使用Soft Drop算法进行修饰（grooming）。

Result: 喷注能量和质量标度的残余不确定性在横动量（transverse momentum）不超过1 TeV时约为1%，在不超过2 TeV时为2–3%。该结果显著降低了系统误差，为增强型对象的物理分析提供了可靠校准。

Conclusion: 本文建立的校准流程有效控制了Run 2大半径喷注的能量和质量标度不确定性，使得ATLAS实验能够开展对超越标准模型粒子的高灵敏度搜索以及标准模型框架下的精确测量。

Abstract: The ATLAS experiment has developed an extensive procedure based on Monte Carlo simulation and collision data to calibrate the response of large-radius jets. This paper describes the Run 2 calibration of large-radius jets reconstructed from Unified Flow Objects and groomed with the Soft Drop algorithm, using data produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. Results are presented for the Monte Carlo-based correction and for the in situ methods that are used to validate the jet energy and mass scales and their resolutions in collision data. The residual uncertainty on the jet energy and mass scales is close to 1% for transverse momenta up to 1 TeV and is 2-3% up to 2 TeV, enabling sensitive searches to beyond the Standard Model particles and precise Standard Model measurements with boosted objects.

</details>

### [66] [Bosonic Condensed Phase Real-time Dynamics from Ring Polymer Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25892)
*Yotam M. Y. Feldman,Sourav Karmakar,Sutirtha Paul,Jacob Higer,Adrian Del Maestro,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种玻色子环聚合物分子动力学（bosonic RPMD）方法，用于近似计算玻色凝聚相中的实时关联函数和量子输运系数。该方法在谐振、短时间和高温极限下是精确的，并首次实现了使用RPMD直接模拟有限温度下大型玻色凝聚相的实时动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于严重的动力学符号问题（dynamical sign problem），路径积分表述中量子实时关联函数的直接计算是不可能的；而通过虚时关联函数进行解析延拓的方法又是不适定的（ill-posed）。现有的环聚合物分子动力学（RPMD）虽然能准确近似凝聚相实时关联函数，但忽略了交换效应（exchange effects），无法处理玻色子体系。

Method: 作者开发了一种玻色子环聚合物分子动力学（bosonic RPMD）方法。该方法的关键发现是：玻色子的Kubo变换关联函数（Kubo-transformed correlation function）仅在关联可观测量（correlated observables）在交换下对称时才是实数。该方法在谐振、短时间和高温极限下严格精确。

Result: 在谐振和非谐振模型系统上对方法进行了基准测试，随后直接获得了Lieb-Liniger气体在不同温度和动量转移下的密度-密度实时关联函数（density-density real-time correlation functions）。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次实现了使用RPMD方法直接模拟有限温度下大型玻色凝聚相的实时动力学，克服了此前RPMD方法无法处理玻色子交换效应的局限性。

Abstract: We present a method for approximating real-time correlation functions and quantum transport coefficients of bosonic condensed phases. The direct evaluation of quantum real-time correlation functions in the path integral formulation is impossible due to a severe dynamical sign problem. Evaluating imaginary-time correlation functions and inverting them using analytic continuation is famously ill-posed. Ring polymer molecular dynamics (RPMD) provides an alternative approach resulting in accurate approximations for real-time correlation functions of condensed phases but neglects exchange effects. In this work, we develop a bosonic RPMD method, which is exact in the harmonic, short time, and high temperature limits. A critical enabling observation is that the Kubo-transformed correlation function for bosons is real only when the correlated observables are symmetric under exchange. We benchmark the method on harmonic and anharmonic model systems and then use it to directly obtain the Lieb-Liniger gas density-density real-time correlation functions at various temperatures and momentum transfers. This work enables the direct simulation of finite-temperature, real-time dynamics of large bosonic condensed phases using RPMD for the first time.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于格点动力学蒙特卡洛（KMC）框架，用于模拟GaN(0001)的分子束外延生长过程，能够捕捉温度依赖的表面扩散、Ehrlich–Schwoebel台阶边缘势垒、Ostwald熟化及解吸附等微观机制，并支持基于机器学习势函数的自适应势垒评估，揭示了高温解吸附驱动的岛“行走”行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaN(0001)的分子束外延生长涉及多种微观过程，现有模拟工具难以同时高效、系统地探索实验相关生长条件下的原子尺度动力学；因此需要开发一个兼具可扩展性、微观精度和计算效率的模拟框架。

Method: 采用格点动力学蒙特卡洛（kinetic Monte Carlo, KMC）方法构建框架，集成温度依赖的表面扩散、流量驱动的沉积、Ehrlich–Schwoebel (ES) 台阶边缘势垒、Ostwald熟化以及物种选择性解吸附（desorption）等过程。除预定义的激活能目录（activation-energy catalog）外，还支持基于机器学习原子间势能（machine-learned interatomic potential）的自适应按需势垒评估；当遇到未见过的局域原子构型时，通过微动弹性带（nudged elastic band, NEB）、势能扫描（potential energy scan）或Brønsted–Evans–Polanyi (BEP) 方法计算激活势垒并缓存复用。

Result: 预设势垒的模拟成功再现了紧致三角形岛的形成、生长中断期间的Ostwald熟化以及ES势垒诱导的多层成核（multilayer nucleation）。在高温条件下，解吸附驱动了一种岛“行走”（walking）行为：N–Ga交换在岛后缘生成弱束缚的Ga吸附原子（AdGa），后者优先解吸附导致岛边缘不对称后退和净平移。

Conclusion: 该KMC框架为原子尺度GaN外延的预测性模拟提供了灵活平台，并可推广应用于化合物半导体的非平衡生长（non-equilibrium growth）研究。

Abstract: We present a lattice-based kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) framework for simulating GaN(0001) growth by molecular beam epitaxy. The framework captures the key microscopic processes governing epitaxial growth, including temperature-dependent surface diffusion, flux-driven deposition, Ehrlich--Schwoebel (ES) step-edge barriers, Ostwald ripening, and species-specific desorption, within a scalable architecture that enables systematic exploration of experimentally relevant growth conditions. In addition to predefined activation-energy catalogs, the framework supports adaptive on-the-fly barrier evaluation using machine-learned interatomic potentials. When previously unencountered local atomic configurations arise, activation barriers are computed via nudged elastic band, potential energy scans, or Brønsted--Evans--Polanyi methods, and cached for reuse. Predefined-barrier simulations reproduce compact triangular island formation, and further capture Ostwald ripening during growth interruptions and ES barrier-induced multilayer nucleation. At elevated temperatures, desorption drives an island ``walking'' regime, in which N--Ga exchange generates weakly bound Ga adatoms (AdGa) at trailing edges; preferential desorption of AdGa leads to asymmetric edge retreat and net island translation. Our KMC framework provides a flexible platform for predictive simulations of GaN epitaxy at the atomic scale and, more broadly, non-equilibrium growth of compound semiconductors.

</details>


### [68] [Organizing Principles for Moiré Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24944)
*Qiaoling Xu,Yifan Gao,Tao Zhang,Ammon Fischer,Yi Jiang,Hanqi Pi,Zike Fan,Dongdong An,Kun Zhou,Yingjian Li,Yongqing Li,Yuhao Fu,Lei Wang,Lijun Zhang,B. Andrei Bernevig,Dante M. Kennes,Enge Wang,Angel Rubio,Lede Xian*

TL;DR: 本文指出传统仅基于Γ谷和K谷的莫尔平带（moiré flat bands）机制是不完整的，通过大规模第一性原理计算与对称性分析，识别出六种以上基于单层能带边（band edge）的动量空间位置、有效局域轨道（orbital）特征及莫尔对称性的广义组织变量，建立了连接本征材料电子结构与涌现低能莫尔哈密顿量（moiré Hamiltonian）的谷-轨道-对称性（valley-orbital-symmetry）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对范德华双层（van der Waals bilayers）中莫尔平带的讨论局限于Γ谷（Γ valley）和K谷（K valley）以及近期关注的M谷（M valley）机制。本文认为这一视角是不完整的，需建立更一般的理论框架。

Method: 采用完全弛豫的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）、能带展开（band unfolding）与对称性表示分析（symmetry-representation analysis），对涵盖所有二维晶格类（2D lattice classes）的600余个公度扭曲双层体系（commensurate twisted bilayers）进行系统研究。

Result: 识别出超越传统单轨道（single-orbital）范式的多条形成莫尔量子物质（moiré quantum matter）的路径，实现了三角、蜂窝、四方、棋盘及类Kagome（kagome-like）Hubbard模型；发现自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）多轨道平带具有超越K谷背景的对称性指示拓扑（symmetry-indicated topology）；非对称性莫尔对称性（nonsymmorphic moiré symmetries）导致半金属平带连接性；在M谷六角与X谷四方/矩形体系中发现准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）平带结构；在多谷流形（coupled multi-valley manifolds）中识别出类Kagome连接性。

Conclusion: 建立了谷-轨道-对称性框架，将本征材料电子结构与涌现的莫尔哈密顿量联系起来，为关联（correlated）、拓扑（topological）及对称性强制（symmetry-enforced）的莫尔相提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Moiré flat bands in van der Waals bilayers are usually discussed through a small set of mechanisms associated with the $Γ$ and $K$ valleys of hexagonal crystals, and more recently with $M$-valleys systems. Here we show that this view is incomplete. The momentum-space location and effective local orbital character of the monolayer's band edge, in conjunction with the moiré symmetry and the symmetry representations of the resulting bands, provide a general set of organizing variables for the emergent low-energy moiré Hamiltonian. Applying fully relaxed first-principles calculations, band unfolding and symmetry-representation analysis to more than 600 commensurate twisted bilayers spanning all 2D lattice classes, we identify several routes to moiré quantum matter beyond the conventional single-orbital paradigm. The resulting flat bands realize trigonal, honeycomb, square, checkerboard and kagome-like Hubbard models with single-orbital, multi-orbital and multi-site Hilbert spaces; spin-orbit-coupled multi-orbital flat bands exhibit symmetry-indicated topology beyond the conventional $K$-valley setting; and nonsymmorphic moiré symmetries enforce semimetallic flat-band connectivity. Analogous quasi-one-dimensional flat-band structures are found in $M$-valley hexagonal systems and $X$-valley square or rectangular systems resulting from emergent momentum-space nonsymmorphic symmetries. Separately, coupled multi-valley manifolds with kagome-like connectivity are identified in several systems whose parent band edges lie at non-high-symmetry points. These results establish a valley-orbital-symmetry framework for connecting parent-material electronic structure to emergent moiré Hamiltonians relevant to correlated, topological and symmetry-enforced moiré phases.

</details>


### [69] [MANDALA: An E(3)-Equivariant Graph Neural Network Framework for Learning Electronic-Structure Operators with Observable Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24997)
*Bartosz Brzoza,Wiktoria Szopa,Zakaria Elabid,Vincent Martinetto,Varadarajan Rengaraj,Mani Lokamani,Thomas D. Kühne,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Mandala 是一个模块化软件框架，利用 E(3)等变图神经网络（E(3)-equivariant graph neural networks）学习块稀疏电子结构矩阵（block-sparse electronic-structure matrices），从而在保持量子力学算符表示的同时，重建能带结构、态密度等电子可观测量，填补了传统机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）缺乏电子结构信息的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham 密度泛函理论（density functional theory）的计算成本限制了可模拟的体系大小和时间尺度；传统机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）仅预测能量和力，无法提供能带结构、态密度等电子结构层次的量子算符信息。

Method: Mandala 采用模块化软件框架设计，以原子分辨的哈密顿量矩阵（Hamiltonian matrix）、重叠矩阵（overlap matrix）和密度矩阵（density matrix）的统一表示为核心，集成基组转换、稀疏块处理、不可约表示映射（irreducible representation mapping）、图构建（graph construction）、模型定义与训练等可复用抽象层，并使用 E(3)等变图神经网络（E(3)-equivariant graph neural networks）学习块稀疏电子结构矩阵。

Result: Mandala 能够从预测的算符直接评估能带能量（band energy）、电子数（electron count）、态密度（density of states）和能带结构（band structure）等可观测量，支持异质化学成分和多种神经架构变体，并兼容多个电子结构后端。

Conclusion: Mandala 通过将电子结构学习与可观测量引导的建模相结合，在单一可扩展实现中补充了原子间势工作流，能够同时解析电子结构及算符导出的可观测量。

Abstract: Electronic-structure calculations based on Kohn-Sham density functional theory remain indispensable in computational materials science and chemistry. Their computational cost, however, limits accessible system sizes and simulation times. At the same time, conventional machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs), which are becoming the workhorse of large-scale materials modeling, usually target only energies and forces. They therefore leave out the quantum-operator-level information required to reconstruct band structures, densities of states, spatial charge distributions, and other electronic observables. \texttt{Mandala} fills this methodological gap. It is a modular software framework for learning block-sparse electronic-structure matrices with E(3)-equivariant graph neural networks. The framework is built around a unified representation of atom-resolved Hamiltonian, overlap, and density matrices, together with reusable abstractions for basis conversion, sparse block handling, irreducible representation mapping, graph construction, model definition, and training. This design allows \texttt{Mandala} to support heterogeneous chemical compositions, a wide range of neural architecture variants within one workflow, and multiple electronic-structure backends. \texttt{Mandala} evaluates selected observables directly from the predicted operators, including band energy, electron count, density of states, and band structure. This connects electronic-structure learning and observable-guided modeling while retaining a representation tied to quantum-mechanical operators rather than only scalar or vector targets as in MLIPs. In this form, \texttt{Mandala} is intended to complement atomistic interatomic potential workflows by resolving electronic structure and operator-derived observables within one scalable implementation.

</details>


### [70] [Machine-Learning Potentials for sodium-potassium chloride mixtures: Predicting thermophysical properties and phase behavior of multicomponent salts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25022)
*Karim Zongo,Hao Sun,Zijian Meng,Christopher Maxwell,Edmanuel Torres,Laurent Karim Béland*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于密度泛函理论（DFT）数据训练的矩张量势（moment tensor potential），用于NaCl、KCl、NaCl-KCl混合物及NaK合金的多组分熔盐体系，实现了大规模分子动力学模拟，预测了多种热物理性质和相图，并指出系统偏差的存在支持一种将第一性原理驱动的机器学习势与实验数据校准结合的混合建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用密度泛函理论（DFT）预测多组分熔盐（multicomponent molten salts）的性质在计算上难以实现，因为评估输运性质和相行为所需的空间与时间尺度在计算上代价过高。

Method: 基于NaCl、KCl、NaCl-KCl混合物及NaK合金的DFT数据集，训练了一个矩张量势（moment tensor potential），并系统评估了D3色散修正（D3 dispersion corrections）的影响，随后利用该势函数进行大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟。

Result: 模型成功再现了液体密度、扩散系数、径向分布函数、热容、热导率以及NaCl-KCl相图中随温度和组成变化的趋势，但在多个绝对性质上仍存在系统偏差（systematic deviations）。

Conclusion: 第一性原理驱动的机器学习势虽具有可迁移的预测能力和机理洞察力，但需要通过实验数据的校准来提高定量精度，因此支持一种混合建模框架（hybrid modeling framework）。

Abstract: Predicting the properties of multicomponent molten salts using density functional theory (DFT) remains challenging because the spatial and temporal scales required to evaluate transport properties and phase behavior are computationally prohibitive. In this work, we develop a moment tensor potential trained using a a DFT dataset of NaCl, KCl, NaCl-KCl mixtures, and the NaK alloy, enabling large-scale molecular dynamics simulations across wide ranges of temperatures and compositions. We systematically evaluate the effect of D3 dispersion corrections and apply the resulting potential to predict liquid densities, diffusion coefficients, radial distribution functions, heat capacities, thermal conductivities, and the NaCl-KCl phase diagram. The model successfully reproduces many temperature- and composition-dependent trends. However, systematic deviations in several absolute properties persist, highlighting the importance of experimental validation and calibration. These findings support a hybrid modeling framework in which first-principles-informed machine-learning potentials provide transferable predictive capability and mechanistic insight, while experimental data incorporated during model development or subsequent engineering assessments is necessary to improve quantitative accuracy.

</details>


### [71] [Citrine Informatics: Chemical & Materials Development Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25039)
*Maxwell C. Venetos,Steven J. Brown,Kenneth Kroenlein,Steven K. Kauwe,James E. Saal,Marco Musto,Matthew D. Gerboth,Kyle D. Miller,Gregory J. Mulholland*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出Citrine平台，通过四阶段闭环学习循环（数据摄取与特征化、不确定性量化建模、设计空间约束、序贯学习优化）系统解决数据驱动材料发现中数据稀缺、精度过度估计和设计空间受限三大障碍，实验表明可将实验工作量降低至随机搜索的1/9到1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动材料发现虽有望缩短材料从发明到部署的周期，但三个反复出现的障碍阻碍了其从个例成功向可持续工业发现项目的转化：实验数据稀缺且难以复用、传统精度指标在外推条件下高估模型性能、以及受物理、可制造性、供应链和成本约束的现实设计空间未被充分考虑。

Method: Citrine平台由四个合作阶段构成闭环序贯学习循环（sequential learning loop）：Stage 1通过材料数据图形化表达（GEMD）模型摄取和特征化数据，将工艺历史、测量不确定性和溯源作为一等特征（first-class features）；Stage 2构建具有良好校准不确定性（well-calibrated uncertainty）的机器学习模型，包括面向相关目标的多变量预测区间（multivariate prediction intervals），并采用外推交叉验证（extrapolative cross-validation）和动态发现指标代替随机留出划分；Stage 3将成分、物理、加工和经济约束直接编码到设计空间中；Stage 4应用FUELS序贯学习框架（sequential learning framework），结合不确定性感知的采集函数（uncertainty-aware acquisition functions）在严格评估预算下导航大规模约束空间。

Result: 在有机半导体、自主纳米颗粒合成和基准优化任务等已发表案例研究中，Citrine平台相比随机搜索实现了实验工作量两到九倍的减少，展示了数据层、建模层和设计空间层持续共同演化的技术栈效果。

Conclusion: 通过将数据、建模和设计空间约束整合为闭环协同演化的系统化技术栈，能够显著降低实验成本，为可持续的工业级材料发现项目提供可行路径。

Abstract: Data-driven materials discovery promises to compress the historically decades-long path from invention to deployment, yet translating individual successes into sustained industrial discovery programs remains difficult. Three obstacles recur: experimental data are scarce, costly, and published in formats that resist reuse; conventional accuracy metrics overstate model performance under the extrapolative conditions that define discovery; and realistic design spaces are bounded by physics, manufacturability, supply, and cost. We present the Citrine Platform, developed over more than a decade as an integrated response to these obstacles, and organize it as four cooperating stages within a closed sequential learning loop. Stage 1 ingests and featurizes data through the Graphical Expression of Materials Data (GEMD) model, which treats process history, measurement uncertainty, and provenance as first-class features. Stage 2 builds machine learning models with well-calibrated uncertainty, including multivariate prediction intervals for correlated objectives, and validates them with extrapolative cross-validation and dynamic discovery metrics rather than random held-out splits. Stage 3 encodes compositional, physical, processing, and economic constraints directly into the design space, and Stage 4 applies the FUELS sequential learning framework with uncertainty-aware acquisition functions to navigate large constrained spaces under tight evaluation budgets. Published case studies spanning organic semiconductors, autonomous nanoparticle synthesis, and benchmark optimization tasks demonstrate two- to nine-fold reductions in experimental effort relative to random search, illustrating a stack in which data, modeling, and design-space layers continuously co-evolve.

</details>


### [72] [First-Principles Origins of Charge Transport in Molecular Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25089)
*Tong Jiang,Joonho Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无参数框架，从第一性原理电子-声子哈密顿量出发，通过非微扰Green-Kubo动力学在全声子谱和数百分子尺度上计算电荷传输，并推翻了对DNTT的微观机制的传统认知，揭示了基于声子关联的瞬态局域化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷传输决定有机晶体管和光伏器件的性能，但仅依据原子结构预测电荷传输仍然困难。现有方法只能通过假设某种机制或将电子-声子耦合简化为少数模式来获得可处理性，缺乏真正无先验假设的预测能力。

Method: 构建一个无参数（parameter-free）框架，基于第一性原理（ab initio）的电子-声子哈密顿量进行计算，采用完整声子谱（full phonon spectrum）在数百分子尺度上传播载流子，通过非微扰Green-Kubo动力学（nonperturbative Green–Kubo dynamics）让传输机制和瓶颈自然涌现。

Result: 在五种代表性有机晶体上，该框架成功复现了实验测量的迁移率、温度指数和光学导纳指纹。具体针对DNTT，结果推翻了先前主流的微观机制，将其瞬态局域化归因于来自声学声子的关联在位无序（correlated on-site disorder），而非独立的跳跃涨落（independent hopping fluctuations）。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了一个双轴传输图（two-axis transport map），提供了设计原则；并突显了尚未被充分研究的phenacene家族（以高迁移率的picene为代表）作为有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Charge transport governs organic transistors and photovoltaics, yet predicting it from atomic structure remains challenging. Electron--phonon interactions span disparate frequencies, strengths and spatial ranges, and collectively generate nonperturbative carrier dynamics. Existing methods regain tractability only by assuming a mechanism or reducing electron--phonon coupling to a few modes. We introduce a parameter-free framework that instead computes transport from ab initio electron--phonon Hamiltonians, propagating carriers across hundreds-of-molecule domains with the full phonon spectrum and letting transport regimes and bottlenecks emerge from nonperturbative Green--Kubo dynamics. Across five representative crystals, it captures measured mobilities, temperature exponents, and optical-conductivity fingerprints. Our results overturn the prevailing microscopic mechanism for DNTT, tracing its transient localization to correlated on-site disorder from acoustic phonons rather than independent hopping fluctuations. The resulting two-axis transport map provides design principles and highlights the underexplored phenacene family, exemplified by the high-mobility picene, as a promising direction.

</details>


### [73] [Identifying Contact Barrier Types in Few-Layer MoS2 Devices Using Correlative IV, LBIC, and Bias-Dependent KPFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25102)
*Ariane Ufer,Zeinab Eftekhari,Benjamin Mayer,Hendrik Lambers,Hubert J. Krenner,Rebecca Saive,Ursula Wurstbauer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成电流-电压(IV)表征、激光束诱导电流(LBIC)绘图和偏压依赖的开尔文探针力显微镜(KPFM)的实验框架，用于全面解析基于少层二硫化钼(MoS2)的二维半导体-金属接触特性，可明确区分肖特基势垒与隧穿势垒的类型及不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属与二维半导体（如MoS2）之间的电接触对器件性能至关重要，但单一表征技术难以揭示其微观本质。因此需要一种综合实验框架来全面解析接触特性。

Method: 集成三种表征手段：IV测量提供宏观输运特性；LBIC以微米级空间分辨率绘制局域光电流响应，揭示MoS2-金属界面内建电场的位置与性质；偏压依赖的KPFM（非传统功函数模式）提供纳米级分辨的势垒分布，量化接触势垒的相对大小和空间位置。

Result: 对三种代表性器件（一种欧姆接触型、两种二极管型）的分析表明，该框架可明确识别主导势垒是肖特基型还是隧穿型，并判定两接触端的不对称性。热退火显著降低总电阻，但接触势垒仍是电阻的主要来源。

Conclusion: 该方法论可直接推广至其他二维半导体-金属体系，为定量理解二维器件接触的微观机制提供了一条实用且全面的途径。

Abstract: Electrical contacts between metals and two-dimensional (2D) semiconductors such as molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) critically govern device performance, yet their microscopic nature remains difficult to disentangle using any single characterization technique. The closeness of the energies of the ferroelectric, antiferroelectric, and mixed states indicates the emergence of a multi-minimum potential with an infinite number of wells separated by potential barriers in the configuration space. ### [77] [Transformer Atomic Cluster Expansion: TRACE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25652)

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer原子簇展开（TRACE），一种结合原子簇展开密度关联与局部多头交叉注意力的能量守恒架构，在笔记本电脑上即可高效训练，并在多相碘化铯铅、液态水和分子内甲基迁移等多种基准测试中实现了与实验高度一致的预测结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 一个统一的TRACE架构能够在多样化的基准体系中成功捕获多物种结晶、液体结构、相图和化学反应活性，展示了其在跨学科原子模拟中的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Designing machine-learning interatomic potentials involves achieving the precise representation of complex many-body interactions alongside the efficiency required for scalable molecular dynamics. We introduce Transformer Atomic Cluster Expansion (TRACE), an energy-conserving architecture that combines atomic cluster expansion density correlations with local multihead cross-attention. The correlations form an O(3)-equivariant state for each center, which queries tensorial neighbor features that remain fixed functions of species and geometry. No learned state is passed between atoms. On a laptop MacBook-M1, we train and test TRACE for polymorphic cesium lead iodide, liquid water, and intramolecular methyl migration against experiments. For cesium lead iodide, TRACE reproduces the r$^2$SCAN+rVV10 ordering of four polymorphs and gives a classical edge-sharing hexagonal non-perovskite($δ$) to corner-sharing cubic perovskite($α$) Gibbs-free-energy crossing $\simeq$580K near the experimental observations of $\simeq$600K. By employing enhanced sampling to cross high energy barriers, the same TRACE potential successfully captures the $δ$-to-$α$ perovskite transformation without any reinforcement learning. A water potential trained on a reduced set of CCSD(T) configurations places the first oxygen--oxygen maximum at 2.85~Å, compared to the experimental value of 2.80~Å. For the gas-phase methyl migration in 2,2-dimethylisoindene, umbrella sampling yields an activation free energy of $27.92\pm0.03$~kcal~mol$^{-1}$, in close agreement with the experimental measurement of $29.2\pm1.1$~kcal~mol$^{-1}$. Across these diverse benchmarks, a single unified architecture successfully captures multi-species crystallization, liquid structures, phase diagrams, and chemical reactivity.

*M. Zhezhu,A. Vasilev,M. Sukiasyan,A. Kutuzyan,N. Anosov,M. Yaprintsev,D. A. Ghazaryan,H. Gharagulyan*

### [79] [Integrating moment tensor potentials with finite-element modeling for heat transfer prediction in FLiBe-based molten salt systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25803)
*Mikhail Polovinkin,Ksenia Abramova,Oksana Rahmanova,Farit Valiev,Andrey Isakov,Andrey Goryachikh,Alexander Galashev,Yurii Zaikov,Dmitrii Maksimov,Alexander Shapeev,Nikita Rybin*

The FE model with literature transport properties reproduces the experimental heat-transfer behavior of pure FLiBe to within 10% in the laminar regime and 18% in the transitional and turbulent regimes, validating the end-to-end pipeline for this composition. The same model with MTP-MD-derived transport properties systematically overestimates the heat-transfer coefficient by 25-28%, an offset consistent with the MTP-MD biases on thermal conductivity and viscosity.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 捕获完整晶格非简谐性（full lattice anharmonicity）对于预测锡的相稳定性至关重要；绝对转变温度仍然受限于底层 0 K 能量学（0 K energetics）的精度。

Abstract: Predicting the $α\to β$ (grey-to-white) transition temperature in tin presents a longstanding challenge for atomistic simulations, with existing theoretical approaches over- or underestimating the experimental boundary (286 K) by up to several hundred Kelvin. In this work, we construct an Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) potential trained on density functional theory data to evaluate the finite-temperature free energies of both phases. ### [84] [Singular geometry and eigenframe topology in local rank-2 tensor observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26008)
*I. C. J. Yap,B. Doerschel,S. Q. Jin,T. T. Dang,P. M. Scott,H. C. Hofsaess,D. C. Lupascu,A. Krawczuk,J. H. Schell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对称二阶张量（symmetric second-rank tensor）在按主值大小排序表示时，张量空间被折叠，导致参数出现尖点（cusps）和标签交换；并发现沿主轴的箭头环绕简并点（degeneracy）一周后可能反转取向，定义了一种二值返回宇称（return parity）。以电场梯度（EFG）张量为例，结合第一性原理计算和应变调控，在 TiO2、SnO2 和 MgO 中识别了不同拓扑特征的简并结构，区分了局域报告奇点（reporting singularities）与全局本征框架拓扑（global eigenframe topology）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称二阶张量通常通过按大小排序的主值和主轴来报告，但这种表示方式隐含了拓扑效应：当主值穿越零或两主值简并时，参数空间会出现奇点，且沿闭合环路环绕简并可能改变主轴的取向。本文旨在揭示这种局域报告奇点与全局本征框架拓扑之间的区别，并利用电场梯度张量作为原型，通过应变调控从实验上探测这一全局拓扑效应。

Method: 采用电场梯度（electric-field-gradient, EFG）张量作为对称无迹张量的清洁原型；通过外加应变作为外部控制坐标（control coordinates），结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）确定局域 EFG 在张量空间中的演化；在金红石 TiO2、SnO2 和立方 MgO 中分析控制空间中的简并点结构，并计算返回宇称（return parity）。

Result: 在 TiO2 中发现一个具有非平庸宇称（nontrivial parity）的孤立控制空间简并点；SnO2 中简并表现为点状或线状，取决于控制切片（control slice）；立方 MgO 中应变可局域控制全部五个 EFG 分量。这些结果区分了局域报告奇点与全局本征框架拓扑。

Conclusion: 所揭示的谱几何（spectral geometry）同样适用于空间张量场；应变调控的、取向分辨的超精细谱学（strain-tuned, orientation-resolved hyperfine spectroscopy）为局域重构返回宇称提供了实验路径。

Abstract: Symmetric second-rank tensors are reported through magnitude-ordered principal values and axes. This representation folds tensor space: although the physical tensor remains smooth, the reported parameters develop cusps and exchange labels when one principal value crosses zero or two become degenerate. It conceals a global effect: an arrow chosen along a principal axis and transported continuously around a closed loop encircling a degeneracy can return with opposite orientation, even though the tensor returns to itself. This reversal defines a binary return parity that is invariant under smooth loop deformations that avoid degeneracy. We use the electric-field-gradient (EFG) tensor as a clean prototype because it is symmetric, traceless, and locally measurable at a probe site. Applied strain provides external control coordinates that steer the local EFG through tensor space, as determined from first-principles calculations. In rutile TiO2, we identify an isolated control-space degeneracy with nontrivial parity; SnO2 exhibits point- or line-like degeneracies depending on the control slice; and in cubic MgO, strain locally controls all five EFG components. These results distinguish local reporting singularities from global eigenframe topology. The underlying spectral geometry also applies to spatial tensor fields, and strain-tuned, orientation-resolved hyperfine spectroscopy offers a route to reconstruct return parity locally.

</details>


### [85] [Extracting Atomic Environments for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26018)
*Jared C. Stimac,Fei Zhou,Kyle Bushick,Bo Lei,Sebastien Hamel,Amit Samanta,Vincenzo Lordi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模原子模拟中从大体系提取局部原子环境用于DFT计算的方法进行了系统基准测试，提出并验证了一种名为“deletions”的简单方法，在非晶SiO₂、含螺位错Ta和熔融C等多种材料体系中表现优于其他提取方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模分子动力学（Molecular Dynamics, MD）模拟中，力场模型通常基于密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）参考数据训练，而DFT计算仅适用于数百至数千个原子的小体系。为了在感兴趣区域计算DFT力（例如用于主动学习或即时训练原子间势），需要从大模拟盒子中提取一小部分原子，并适配周期性边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）。然而，提取原子集的形状、大小以及必要钝化包络的生成方法一直缺乏系统分析。

Method: 本文对多种从大块体构型中提取原子环境并将其嵌入适合周期性DFT计算的小构型的技术进行了基准测试。测试材料体系包括非晶SiO₂、含螺位错（screw dislocation）的Ta以及熔融C。文章中提出并重点考察了一种称为“deletions”的简单提取流程。

Result: 在所测试的多种提取方法中，名为“deletions”的方法表现出优越的性能，优于其他一系列备选提取方案。

Conclusion: 系统分析表明，在非晶、缺陷晶体及熔融等多样化的材料体系中，deletions方法能够有效提取适用于DFT计算的局部原子环境，为主动学习和势函数训练提供了可靠的技术基础。

Abstract: In order to appropriately capture large-scale material features and emergent phenomena via atomistic simulations, such as Molecular Dynamics (MD), the system scale can range up to hundreds of millions of atoms. However, the force-field models that drive those simulations are generally trained with Density Functional Theory (DFT) reference data, limited to relatively small configurations on the order of 100s or 1000s of atoms. To compute DFT forces on atoms in regions of interest, for example for active-learning or on-the-fly training of interatomic potentials, one needs to extract a small set of atoms from the larger simulation box, and typical work with periodic boundary conditions for DFT; however, methods to select the shape and size of this extracted set of atoms, as well as to generate a potentially necessary passivating envelope, have not been systematically analyzed. In this work, we present a benchmark of various techniques to extract atomic environments from large, bulk configurations and embed them into smaller configurations suitable for DFT calculations with periodic boundary conditions. We test with a diverse set of material systems, which includes amorphous $\mathrm{SiO_2}$, Ta with screw dislocations, and molten C. We demonstrated a notably simple procedure, a method we refer to as deletions, yields superior performance over an array of alternative extraction methods.

</details>


### [86] [The interplay of crystal-field transitions and exchange spin dynamics in a ferrimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26026)
*Arpita Dutta,Pratyay Mukherjee,Ritwik Mondal,Shovon Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用温度依赖的太赫兹时域光谱（THz time-domain spectroscopy），本研究在钆镱铋铁石榴石（Gd₃/₂Yb₁/₂BiFe₅O₁₂）中发现了Yb离子的晶场（CEF）激发与Yb-Fe交换模之间的杂化现象（hybridization），该耦合导致随温度降低时Yb-Fe交换模出现反常红移（anomalous redshift），揭示了CEF介导的交换耦合（CEF-mediated exchange coupling）在低能自旋动力学中的关键作用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土铁石榴石（rare-earth iron garnets）为探索低能激发与复杂温度依赖的磁化动力学（magnetization dynamics）之间的相互作用提供了理想平台。尽管已有广泛研究，但晶场激发（CEF excitations）与交换模（exchange modes）之间的相互作用在很大程度上仍未得到解析（largely unmapped）。

Conclusion: 该反常红移现象源于Fe交换场与Yb CEF激发相互作用所导致的Yb-Fe交换各向异性（exchange anisotropy）的修正。这些发现凸显了CEF介导的交换耦合在塑造低能自旋动力学中的关键作用，将稀土铁石榴石定位为未来太赫兹自旋电子学（THz spintronic technologies）的核心材料。

Abstract: Rare-earth iron garnets offer an ideal platform for exploring the interplay of low-energy excitations and the complex temperature-dependent magnetization dynamics. In these systems, exchange coupling between rare-earth and iron sublattices generates high-frequency collective spin excitations. In addition, the robust spin-orbit coupling of localized 4$f$ electrons triggers the crystal-electric-field (CEF) transitions at THz frequencies. Using temperature-dependent THz time-domain spectroscopy, we demonstrate a hybridization between the Yb-ion CEF excitation and the Yb-Fe exchange mode in Gd$_{3/2}$Yb$_{1/2}$BiFe$_{5}$O$_{12}$. This coupling is characterized by a significant redistribution of spectral and temporal weights as the material approaches its magnetization compensation temperature. Notably, the Yb-Fe exchange mode exhibits an anomalous redshift upon cooling -- a reversal of the conventional blue shift typically driven by increased exchange coupling. We trace this phenomenon to a modification of Yb-Fe exchange anisotropy, driven by the interplay of the Fe exchange field and Yb CEF excitations. These findings highlight the critical role of CEF-mediated exchange coupling in shaping low-energy spin dynamics, positioning rare-earth garnets as a cornerstone for future THz spintronic technologies.

</details>


### [87] [Soft-mode nonlinearities away from ferroelectric phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26030)
*Payel Shee,Ipek Efe,Jingwen Li,Kshitij V. Goyal,Morgan Trassin,Shovon Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过线性与二维太赫兹（THz）光谱实验，结合局域场介导的电子-晶格耦合微观模型，揭示了在顺电相钛酸锶（SrTiO₃, STO）中，软光学声子模的非线性行为主要由电子极化率驱动；局域场虽能启动非微扰晶格动力学，但仅凭其自身不足以诱发非线性，自发极化才是决定极性强关联介电体软模非线性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极性介电体的结构相变中，离子子系统与电子子系统之间的相互作用（由软光学声子模介导）决定了相变行为。在钛酸锶（SrTiO₃, STO）等孵化型铁电体中，强局域场效应（local-field effects）可在相边界附近将晶格驱动至非微扰（non-perturbative）区域。然而，从实验上区分局域场与自发宏观极化（spontaneous macroscopic polarization）各自的贡献仍然是一个挑战。
