Conclusion: 局域场虽对启动非微扰晶格动力学是必要的，但仅凭局域场自身并不足以引发非线性行为。这表明自发极化在决定强关联极性介电体中软模的非线性（soft-mode nonlinearities）方面起决定性作用。

Abstract: The interplay between ionic and electronic subsystems dictates the behavior of structural phase transitions in polar dielectrics, a coupling mediated by soft optical phonon modes. In incipient ferroelectrics such as SrTiO$_3$ (STO), strong local-field effects can drive the lattice into a non-perturbative regime near the phase boundary. However, disentangling the distinct contributions of local fields from those of spontaneous macroscopic polarization remains an experimental challenge. Here, we isolate these mechanisms by probing paraelectric STO deep within its symmetric phase, where macroscopic spontaneous polarization is suppressed. Linear terahertz (THz) spectroscopy reveals that the soft mode exhibits a hybrid character, predominantly driven by electronic polarizability. Utilizing two-dimensional THz spectroscopy, we map the underlying nonlinear signals, demonstrating that the system persists in a perturbative regime characterized by robust local-field coherence. By implementing a microscopic model of coupled electronic and lattice degrees of freedom mediated by local fields, we qualitatively reproduce these multidimensional coherent signatures. Our findings highlight that while local fields are necessary to initiate non-perturbative lattice dynamics, they are insufficient on their own. This reveals that spontaneous polarization plays a deterministic role in dictating soft-mode nonlinearities in strongly correlated polar dielectrics.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于12年IceCube数据，通过改进的冰模型、校准和重建方法，首次以5.7σ显著性在银河系平面（Galactic plane）上探测到高能中微子（high-energy neutrino）发射，标志着银河系多信使天文学（multi-messenger astronomy）新时代的到来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中存在能够将宇宙线（cosmic rays）加速到远超地面粒子加速器所能达到能量的天体物理源，但长期以来一直难以定位这些强大的银河系引擎（Galactic engines）的具体位置，并理解宇宙线在银河系中的传播机制及其导致的高能中微子产生过程。

Method: 结合三种中微子味型（neutrino flavours）的特征事件形态，应用近期在冰模型（ice modelling）、校准（calibration）和重建（reconstruction）方面的改进，对12年IceCube数据进行全局、预定义的分析（predefined, global analysis）。

Result: 以5.7σ显著性确立了银河系平面的高能中微子发射。进一步研究表明银河系内部区域是一个突出的中微子源，在可见能量高于5 TeV的217个簇射事件（shower events）中，预期背景仅为154.4 ± 4.1。

Conclusion: 这些结果开启了银河系多信使天文学的新纪元，为研究宇宙线传播以及在千秒差距（kiloparsec）尺度上探测中微子性质（neutrino properties）创造了新的机遇。

Abstract: The Milky Way hosts astrophysical objects that accelerate cosmic rays to energies beyond the reach of terrestrial particle accelerators. It remains a longstanding goal to locate the sites of these powerful Galactic engines and understand how cosmic rays propagate through the Galaxy, leading to the production of high-energy neutrinos. In this paper, we combine event morphologies characteristic of all three neutrino flavours and apply recent improve- ments in ice modelling, calibration and reconstruction to 12 years of IceCube data. With a predefined, global analysis we establish high-energy neutrino emission from the Galactic plane at 5.7 $σ$ significance. A further study shows that the inner region of the Galaxy is a prominent neutrino source, with 217 shower events with visible energy above 5 TeV com- pared with an expected background of 154.4 $\pm$ 4.1. These results herald a new era of Galactic multi-messenger astronomy, creating new opportunities to study cosmic-ray propagation and probe neutrino properties over kiloparsec distances.

</details>


### [89] [Gravitational Wave Modeling of White-Dwarf--Compact-Object Binaries and Observational Outlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24951)
*Tristan S. Weaver,David Radice,Donghui Jeong,Victor Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过发展一种结合质量转移与2.5阶后牛顿运动学的新型积分器，研究了白矮星-致密天体双星系统的末期演化。系统经历弱引力辐射主导的缓慢旋近（inspiral）和质量转移主导的快速“外旋”（outspiral）两个阶段，产生独特引力波波形，其最大截止频率强烈依赖于白矮星质量与致密性。该系统对LISA及地面探测器不可见，但将成为未来分赫兹探测器（如DECIGO和LGWA）的重要源。双星最终命运多样（共有包层、并合或不接触），最高频率10–20 Hz，无法模拟亚太阳质量致密天体信号或解释S251112cm类信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白矮星-致密天体双星系统的末期演化阶段尚缺乏系统、精确的数值研究，特别是在同时考虑质量转移与高阶后牛顿（Post-Newtonian）动力学的情况下。理解该类系统的引力波特征及其最终命运，对于未来引力波探测器的源识别与科学目标设定具有重要意义。

Method: 发展了一种新型数值积分器，同时实现了质量转移（mass transfer）与2.5阶后牛顿运动学（2.5-order Post-Newtonian kinematics）的耦合计算。

Result: 系统经历弱引力辐射反作用主导的缓慢旋近与质量转移主导的快速外旋两阶段；引力波信号在由旋近-外旋转变设定的最大截止频率附近累积。最大截止频率强烈依赖于白矮星质量与致密性。该类双星无法被LISA及当前/未来地面探测器观测到，但数十个白矮星-中子星双星末期旋近将被LGWA每年探测到，DECIGO可探测数百万旋近与数千外旋事件。白矮星与低/高质量伴星最终进入共有包层（common envelope）或并合（merger），而与中等质量伴星不发生接触。最高频率为10–20 Hz。

Conclusion: 白矮星-致密天体双星末期是未来分赫兹引力波探测器（如DECIGO和LGWA）的关键源。该类系统在10–20 Hz频段的信号无法模拟亚太阳质量致密天体信号或解释S251112cm类事件，这对相关误判具有排除意义。

Abstract: We investigate the end stages of circular white-dwarf--compact-object binaries by developing a novel integrator implementing mass transfer and 2.5-order Post-Newtonian kinematics. White-dwarf--compact-object binaries are subject to two phases: a slow inspiral during which binary evolution is dominated by weak-gravity radiation reaction and a quicker ``outspiral" dominated by mass transfer. These systems have unique gravitational waveforms with several characteristic features including an accumulation of signal near the maximum cutoff frequency set by the transition between in- and outspiral. We investigate trends in gravitational wave parameters based on component masses. In particular, maximum cutoff frequency depends strongly on white dwarf mass and compactness. It is unlikely that extragalactic binaries will be measured by LISA or current or future terrestrial detectors. However, white-dwarf--compact-object binary end stages will be an important source for future decihertz detectors such as DECIGO and LGWA: The final inspiral of tens of white-dwarf--neutron-star binaries will be detected annually by LGWA, and millions of inspirals along with thousands of outspirals will be detected by DECIGO. The ultimate fates of these binaries are diverse and dependent upon the intricacies of angular momentum loss. In particular, we show that white dwarfs with partners of sufficiently low/high mass can end their lives in common envelopes/mergers, while white dwarfs do not enter contact with partners of intermediate mass. The highest frequencies produced by white-dwarf--compact-object binaries in our tests are 10--20 Hz---incapable of mimicking sub-solar-mass compact object signals in terrestrial detectors or explaining S251112cm-like signals.

</details>

### [90] [Cumulative suspicion and absorption dynamics in an agent-based Mafia game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24980)
*Eduardo Velasco Stock,Roberto da Silva*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于智能体（agent-based）的黑手党（Mafia/Werewolf）博弈模型，用累积怀疑分数（suspicion scores）替代均匀随机投票，形成历史依赖的随机过程，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）考察了人口规模、初始黑手党人数及侦探角色对博弈吸收结局（absorbing outcomes）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑手党博弈模型中，白天投票阶段通常被简化为均匀随机投票，缺乏微观交互机制。本文旨在建立一个更贴近现实交互过程的智能体模型，揭示微观指控历史与宏观吸收统计之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用智能体建模（agent-based modeling），随机配对玩家依据角色修改公开怀疑分数，以与累积怀疑分数成正比的概率淘汰玩家。通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）研究人口规模、初始黑手党人数和侦探角色对博弈结果的影响，并引入随机处决近似（random-execution approximation）进行理论分析。

Result: 无侦探时，在稀疏黑手党（dilute-mafia）和早期时间区域，黑手党灭绝的累积概率满足 $F(τ)\sim (τ/N)^{N_m}$，与模拟结果一致。胜率曲线呈现经验性的有限尺寸交叉（finite-size crossover），特征人口尺度 $N_c$ 可将不同初始黑手党人数的曲线近似坍塌（collapse）。完美信息的侦探显著缩短黑手党生存时间，并引入对人口组成的额外依赖，单参数重整化不再充分。

Conclusion: 该模型建立了隐藏角色博弈中微观指控历史与宏观吸收统计之间的最小化联系（minimal connection），为理解协调少数与未知情多数之间的竞争动态提供了定量框架。

Abstract: The Mafia game, also known as Werewolf, describes a competition between a coordinated minority whose identities are concealed and a larger good faction composed primarily of uninformed civilians attempting to identify and eliminate them. We introduce an agent-based formulation in which the daytime decision is not represented by uniform random voting. Instead, randomly paired agents modify player-specific public suspicion scores according to their roles, and one surviving player is subsequently eliminated with probability proportional to their accumulated suspicion. These scores persist throughout the game, producing a history-dependent stochastic process with two competing absorbing outcomes: elimination of the mafia or numerical parity between the mafia and the good faction. We investigate the effects of population size, initial mafia size, and the presence of perfectly informed detectives through extensive Monte Carlo simulations. In the absence of detectives, a random-execution approximation predicts that, in the dilute-mafia and early-time regime, the cumulative probability of mafia extinction behaves as $F(τ)\sim (τ/N)^{N_m}$, in agreement with simulations as $N_m/N$ decreases. The winning-probability curves also exhibit an empirical finite-size crossover characterized by a population scale $N_c$. Rescaling the population by $N_c$ approximately collapses the curves obtained for different initial mafia populations. Perfectly informed detectives substantially shorten mafia-survival times and introduce an additional dependence on population composition for which the same one-parameter rescaling is insufficient. The model provides a minimal connection between microscopic histories of accusation and the macroscopic absorption statistics of hidden-role games.

</details>


### [91] [Open-ended innovation in zero-sum games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25677)
*Matteo Marsili*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了开放性创新（open-ended innovation）下的零和博弈（zero-sum games），其中每位玩家可引入新策略。创新过程被建模为从某个分布中抽取新策略。文章认为，在一般条件下，该设定会引发永无止境的创新军备竞赛，因为一方引入新技术会提高对手进行技术创新的边际效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在分析在零和博弈框架下，当玩家能够持续引入新策略（即开放性创新）时，博弈的长期动态行为，特别是探讨创新是否会导致永久的军备竞赛。

Method: 将创新过程形式化为从某分布中抽取新策略的随机过程，并在零和博弈的框架下分析策略引入对双方边际效用（marginal utility）的影响。

Result: 在一般条件下，一方引入新技术会增加对手进行技术创新的边际效用，从而导致一种永无止境的创新军备竞赛（everlasting innovation arm race）。

Conclusion: 开放性创新零和博弈在一般条件下不会收敛到均衡，而是会驱动双方持续进行技术创新，形成长期军备竞赛的动态。

Abstract: This note discusses zero-sum games with open-ended innovation, whereby each player may introduce new strategies. The innovation process is modelled as a draw of new strategies form a distribution. It is argued that, under generic conditions, this setting can lead to an everlasting innovation arm race, because the introduction of new technologies of one player increases the marginal utility for technological innovation of the opponent.

</details>

### [92] [Magnetic pressure anisotropy in ultra-dense white dwarfs: Landau quantization and neutronization effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25104)
*Edson Otoniel,Juan M. Z. Pretel,Victor B. T. Alves,César O. V. Flores,Clésio E. Mota*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文研究了强磁场通过朗道能级（Landau levels）量化简并电子横向运动、分裂压力为平行与垂直分量而对白矮星（white dwarf）物质热力学产生的影响。在状态方程（EoS）中一致处理库仑晶格贡献、电子俘获阈值、中子滴线及核组成后，利用各向异性扩展的Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) 方程求解恒星平衡结构。结果表明，压力各向异性使白矮星序列向更小半径偏移，最大质量变化较小，低质量白矮星半径显著减小，与蒙特利尔白矮星数据库（Montreal White Dwarf Database）的观测数据更为吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场会通过朗道能级（Landau levels）量化简并电子的横向运动，导致白矮星内部压力分裂为平行和垂直于磁场的分量，产生微观压力各向异性。现有研究尚未在状态方程（EoS）中一致处理库仑晶格贡献、电子俘获阈值、中子滴限和核组成，以系统评估这种各向异性对超致密白矮星序列结构与观测特征的影响。

Method: 研究在状态方程（EoS）框架内一致纳入库仑晶格贡献、电子俘获阈值、中子滴限和核组成，并求解各向异性扩展的Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) 方程，其中径向压力$P_r = P_\parallel$，切向压力$P_t = P_\perp$。比较了纯$^{12}\rm{C}$、$^{16}\rm{O}$、$^{20}\rm{Ne}$、$^{24}\rm{Mg}$以及固定50/50 C/O、Ne/O和Mg/Ne混合物在电子俘获前后序列的差异。

Result: 在仅有少数朗道能级被占据的密度范围内，状态方程（EoS）预测$P_\perp < P_\parallel$。压力各向异性使白矮星序列向更小半径偏移，但对最大质量的改变较小。电子俘获后的构型无法仅从质量-半径平面解释，因其$M(ρ_c)$曲线在达到最大质量后出现不稳定部分。强磁场显著减小了低质量白矮星的半径，与蒙特利尔白矮星数据库（Montreal White Dwarf Database）的观测数据更吻合。

Conclusion: 强磁场产生的压力各向异性对白矮星结构有显著影响，尤其能减小低质量白矮星的半径，使其更符合现有观测数据。未来对高质量磁白矮星的高精度观测有望帮助评估磁压力各向异性在确定其质量和半径中的作用。

Abstract: Strong magnetic fields modify the thermodynamics of white dwarf (WD) matter by quantizing the transverse motion of degenerate electrons into Landau levels and splitting the pressure into components parallel and perpendicular to the magnetic field. We investigate how this microscopic pressure anisotropy affects ultra-dense WD sequences when Coulomb lattice contributions, electron capture thresholds, neutron drip limits, and nuclear composition are treated consistently in the equation of state (EoS). The stellar equilibrium structure is obtained by solving an anisotropic extension of the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff (TOV) equations, where $P_r = P_\parallel$ and $P_t = P_\perp$ denote the radial and tangential pressures, respectively. We compare the post-electron-capture branches with their corresponding pre-capture sequences for pure $^{12}\rm{C}$, $^{16}\rm{O}$, $^{20}\rm{Ne}$, and $^{24}\rm{Mg}$ compositions, as well as for fixed 50/50 C/O, Ne/O, and Mg/Ne mixtures. The EoS predicts $P_\perp < P_\parallel$ in density ranges where only a few Landau levels are occupied. Consequently, pressure anisotropy shifts the stellar sequences toward smaller radii, with only minor changes in the maximum masses. We also find that post-capture configurations cannot be interpreted from the mass--radius plane alone, since the corresponding $M(ρ_c)$ curves reveal unstable portions after the maximum mass is reached. Our results show that strong magnetic fields significantly reduce the radii of low-mass WDs, yielding better agreement with the observational data available in the Montreal White Dwarf Database and suggesting that future high-precision observations of massive magnetic WDs could help assess the role of magnetic pressure anisotropy in determining their masses and radii.

</details>

### [93] [The D'Alembert-Lagrange Principle and Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24825)
*Oleg Zubelevich*

Main category: math.HO

TL;DR: 本文为教学性质（expository article），系统给出了达朗贝尔-拉格朗日原理（D'Alembert-Lagrange principle）的严格数学表述，并针对受理想约束的力学系统推导了拉格朗日方程（Lagrange equations），重点展示了从动力学普遍方程到第二类拉格朗日方程的几何化过渡，以及变分导数（variational derivative）在构型流形嵌入映射下的协变性（covariance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为教学目的提供一份方法性指南，给出达朗贝尔-拉格朗日原理的严格数学表述，并清晰建立扩展相空间（extended phase space）的几何框架，使虚位移空间与约束反力的定义独立于具体的分析表示形式。

Method: 采用几何框架对扩展相空间进行严格数学定义，通过嵌入映射（embedding mapping）下变分导数的协变性，从动力学普遍方程（general equation of dynamics）自然地推导出完整系统（holonomic systems）的第二类拉格朗日方程（Lagrange equations of the second kind）。

Result: 证明了拉格朗日方程可以通过构型流形（configuration manifold）嵌入映射下变分导数的协变性自然、优雅地导出，给出了达朗贝尔-拉格朗日原理与第二类拉格朗日方程之间的严格数学推导链条。

Conclusion: 该文为面向教学的方法性综述，通过几何化表述统一了经典分析力学的推导逻辑，阐明了虚位移、约束反力等核心概念的几何本质与协变性质。

Abstract: This expository article serves as a methodical guide, presenting a rigorous mathematical formulation of the D'Alembert-Lagrange principle and the derivation of the Lagrange equations for mechanical systems subject to ideal constraints. Designed for educational purposes, the paper establishes a clear geometric framework for the extended phase space, defining the space of virtual displacements and constraint reactions independently of their specific analytical representations. A central focus of this instructional text is the transition from the general equation of dynamics to the Lagrange equations of the second kind for holonomic systems. Specifically, we demonstrate that the Lagrange equations can be naturally and elegantly derived from considerations of the covariance of the variational derivative under the embedding mapping of the configuration manifold.

</details>

### [94] [Topological Classification of Non-Normalizable Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25848)
*Philipp Gessler,Alessandro Pignedoli,Alexander Neuhaus,Frank-J. Meyer zu Heringdorf,Maria Azhar,Karin Everschor-Sitte*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将不可归一化的 n 维矢量场通过嵌入映射转化为 (n+1) 维归一化矢量场的拓扑分类框架，从而利用同伦不变量对包含振幅零点（amplitude zeros）的场进行拓扑表征，并在一至三维系统中给出了具体的拓扑荷（topological charges）构造与稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理矢量场的拓扑分类依赖于场归一化（field normalization）和基于同伦（homotopy）的不变量，但当场振幅为零时归一化失去定义，导致无法直接进行拓扑表征。本文旨在建立一种适用于不可归一化矢量场的统一拓扑分类框架。

Method: 通过将 n 维不可归一化的矢量场转化为 (n+1) 维归一化矢量场，将目标空间（target space）提升一维，从而使得同伦分类方法能够适用于包含振幅零点的场。该构造扩展了传统的基于同伦的拓扑分类方案。

Result: 对一维、二维和三维不可归一化矢量场分别给出了具体的拓扑不变量（topological invariants）构造，并证明这些拓扑荷在连续变形下保持鲁棒，仅当嵌入结构（embedding structure）出现奇点时才会发生变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为不可归一化场的拓扑表征提供了统一路径，可推广至磁织构（magnetic textures）、铁电体（ferroelectrics）、电磁场（electromagnetic fields）及波动系统（wave systems）等多种物理系统中的拓扑现象研究。

Abstract: Topological classification of physical vector fields conventionally relies on field normalization and homotopy-based invariants. However, when field amplitudes vanish, normalization becomes ill-defined, preventing a direct topological characterization. Here, we introduce a general framework for the topological classification of non-normalizable $n$-dimensional vector fields with compactifiable base spaces by transforming them into $(n+1)$-dimensional normalized vector fields. This construction extends homotopy-based classification to fields containing amplitude zeros. We explicitly demonstrate the approach for one-, two-, and three-dimensional non-normalized vector fields and derive the corresponding topological invariants. The resulting topological charges are robust under continuous deformations and can change only when the embedding structure becomes singular. Our framework provides a unified route to the topological characterization of non-normalizable fields and opens the door to the study of topological phenomena in a broad range of systems, including magnetic textures, ferroelectrics, electromagnetic fields, and wave systems.

</details>


### [95] [Gibbs Phenomenon and Friedel Oscilations: Similarities, Differences, and the Educational Potential of their Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25469)
*Christos Mystilidis,Christos Tserkezis,George Fikioris*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过教学式对比，分析了不连续信号傅里叶表示中的吉布斯现象（Gibbs phenomenon）与固体中电子密度近邻异常处出现的弗里德尔振荡（Friedel oscillations）之间的异同。尽管两者在形式上相似，但研究表明弗里德尔振荡并不能归因于部分傅里叶级数无法描述不连续性。文章旨在为电气工程师与凝聚态物理学家之间建立共同语言，促进跨学科理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索吉布斯现象与弗里德尔振荡之间的相似性与差异性，并尝试从工程师的视角引入固体物理概念，为两个领域建立沟通桥梁。

Method: 采用教学式（educational style）分析，以方势垒约束的一维电子气（one-dimensional electronic gas）的密度为研究对象，从电气工程师的视角对两种现象进行对比研究。

Result: 尽管两种现象在形式上存在相似性，但弗里德尔振荡不能归因于部分傅里叶级数（partial Fourier series）无法描述不连续性（discontinuity）。

Conclusion: 两种现象确实存在可利用的相似性以培养直觉；通过建立共同语言，有望激励电气工程与凝聚态物理两个社区从相邻但不同的学科中寻求直觉与更深层次的理解。

Abstract: We explore the similarities and differences between the Gibbs phenomenon in partial Fourier representations of discontinuous signals and Friedel oscillations in the electron density of a solid near an anomaly. Inspired by the apparent similarities of the two phenomena, we perform a detailed exploration of both from the viewpoint of an engineer being introduced to a concept from solid-state physics. Focusing on the density of an one-dimensional electronic gas confined by a square potential, we show that, despite the similarities, Friedel oscillations cannot be attributed to the inability of a partial Fourier series to describe a discontinuity. Nevertheless, the two phenomena do exhibit similarities, which can be exploited to develop intuition. By adopting an educational style, we hope to establish some common language between electrical engineers and condensed-matter physicists, hoping that this can further inspire the two communities to seek intuition and further comprehension within neighbouring but different disciplines.

</details>


### [96] [Critical Ripples and Dirac Fermions in Crystalline Membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25767)
*Sebastián Bahamondes,Rodrigo Soto-Garrido,Enrique Muñoz,Vladimir Juričić*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该论文构建了狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermions）与弯曲声子（flexural phonons）耦合系统的低能场论（low-energy field theory），分析了从长波平坦相到有限波长失稳的两个不同标度区域的临界行为，指出过渡分为玻色型Wilson-Fisher不动点和手征XY Gross-Neveu-Yukawa普适类两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯（graphene）等晶体膜同时包含非相对论性弯曲声子和相对论性狄拉克准粒子两种低能激发，二者动力学性质迥异，但现有理论对它们之间的耦合机制及耦合导致的临界行为缺乏系统理解。

Method: 通过建立低能有效场论，利用转动对称性（rotational symmetry）约束重整化过程，分析标度依赖的弯曲刚度（bending rigidity）与标量应变-密度耦合（scalar strain-density coupling）的关联；对于失稳情形，则引入有序波矢对并考虑质量型狄拉克双线性（mass-type Dirac bilinear）的对称性匹配条件。

Result: 在平坦相中，弯曲声子变慢使得费米子反馈呈幂律消失，该相对论性耦合的强度对数衰减，平坦相稳定；在有限波长失稳时，若无公度相锁定，转变由Wilson-Fisher不动点主导；当对称性允许质量型狄拉克双线性与波纹共享动量及反射宇称时，转变由手征XY Gross-Neveu-Yukawa普适类描述。

Conclusion: 狄拉克费米子与弯曲声子的耦合在长波平坦相是边缘无关的，不会破坏平坦相稳定性；但在有限波长失稳条件下，可产生纯玻色或电子-结构混合临界点，后者的各向同性连续极限中费米子和玻色子速度锁定。

Abstract: Crystalline membranes hosting Dirac fermions, with graphene as the paradigmatic example, combine two low-energy sectors with sharply different dynamics: nonrelativistic flexural phonons and relativistic-like Dirac quasiparticles. We develop the low-energy field theory of this coupled system at charge neutrality and determine how this dynamical mismatch controls the coupling between the two sectors. In the long-wavelength flat phase, rotational symmetry ties the renormalization of the leading local scalar strain--density coupling to the scale-dependent bending rigidity, causing its dimensionless strength to decrease logarithmically. At the same time, flexural modes become parametrically slower than the Dirac fermions, so the resulting fermionic feedback vanishes as a power law.The flat phase is therefore stable against this perturbation. The physics changes when elastic interactions or electronic softening destabilize the membrane at a finite wavelength, selecting a ripple pattern formed by modes at $\pm\mathbf{Q}$. For an isolated pair of ordering wavevectors, provided that commensurability-induced phase pinning is irrelevant, the transition is governed by the bosonic Wilson--Fisher fixed point, while the Dirac fermions remain spectators. A genuinely hybrid electronic--structural critical point arises instead when symmetry permits a mass-type Dirac bilinear to share the ripple's momentum and quantum numbers, including horizontal-reflection parity. The transition is then described by the chiral-XY Gross--Neveu--Yukawa universality class. Using the known one-loop critical exponents, we characterize this transition, determine the induced secondary elastic distortion, and show that the fermionic and bosonic velocities lock in the isotropic continuum limit.

</details>

*Marco Ticse*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究平方晶格上随机环境中的约束度渗流模型，证明了若存在无限开簇，则其几乎必然唯一，并由此推出超临界区域中连通函数的正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析随机环境与度约束共同作用下的渗流行为，特别是无限开簇的唯一性这一基础但尚未被证明的问题。

Method: 基于平方晶格（square lattice），为每个顶点赋予随机度约束（degree constraint），边在随机时刻尝试开放，构建约束度渗流（constrained-degree percolation）模型。

Result: 证明只要存在无限开簇（infinite open cluster），则其几乎必然（almost surely, a.s.）唯一；进而推出在超临界区域（supercritical regime）中连通函数（connectivity function）为正。

Conclusion: 该唯一性结论为模型的超临界相行为提供了关键理论支撑，连通函数的正性意味着系统在该区域具备长程有序性。

Abstract: This note considers a constrained-degree percolation model in a random environment on the square lattice, where each vertex is assigned a random degree constraint, and edges attempt to open at random times. We prove that whenever an infinite open cluster exists, it is almost surely unique, implying the positivity of the connectivity function in the supercritical regime.

</details>

### [98] [A Rigorous Proof of Metric Blow-Up for Pseudospherical Metrics Associated with the Degasperis--Procesi Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25293)
*Zhenhua Shi,Mingyue Guo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Degasperis–Procesi方程柯西问题解诱导的伪球面度量（pseudospherical metric）在有限时间内爆破（finite-time blow-up）的现象。通过特征线法、动量传输公式、格林函数表示以及Riccati型微分不等式，证明了在临界时间前余标架（coframe）保持非退化，度量张量分量在波浪破碎（wave breaking）发生时发散至无穷大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Degasperis–Procesi方程解所诱导的伪球面几何结构在波浪破碎时刻的行为，特别是度量张量分量是否会发生爆破，从而揭示波浪破碎的几何意义。

Method: 采用特征线法（method of characteristics）、动量传输公式（momentum-transport formula）、格林函数表示（Green's-function representation）以及Riccati型微分不等式（Riccati-type differential inequalities），沿一条特定的特征线对伪球面度量进行分析。

Result: 在临界时间前，余标架保持非退化，伪球面度量定义良好；当波浪破碎发生时，度量分量 g₂₂ 发散至 +∞；当 μ ≠ 0 时，混合分量 g₁₂ 的绝对值也发生爆破。

Conclusion: Degasperis–Procesi方程的有限时间波浪破碎会导致相应伪球面度量的某些分量发生爆破。

Abstract: This paper studies finite-time blow-up of pseudospherical metrics induced by solutions of the Cauchy problem for the Degasperis--Procesi equation. For initial momentum profiles satisfying suitable left--right sign conditions, we use the method of characteristics, the momentum-transport formula, the Green's-function representation, and Riccati-type differential inequalities to analyze the metric along a distinguished characteristic. We prove that the associated coframe remains non-degenerate before the critical time, so that the induced pseudospherical metric is well defined in the precritical region. Moreover, as wave breaking is approached, the metric component \(g_{22}\) diverges to \(+\infty\); when \(μ\neq0\), the mixed component \(g_{12}\) also blows up in absolute value. Thus, finite-time wave breaking for the Degasperis--Procesi equation is shown to induce blow-up of certain components of the corresponding pseudospherical metric.

</details>


### [99] [Notes on a paper by F. Génot and B. Brogliato on the Painlevé paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25414)
*Stefano Pasquero*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了由F. Génot与B. Brogliato提出的经典Painlevé悖论分析，指出其关于动力学演化的某些结论不满足伽利略不变性（Galilean invariance），即运动状态的分类依赖于观测者的选择。论文追溯了该问题的根源在于摩擦约束的速度依赖性表征，并探讨了恢复观测者不变性（observer invariance）的模型重构方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验已有Painlevé悖论分析结果与伽利略不变性（Galilean invariance）的一致性，识别其中因观测者依赖而无法被视为系统内在性质（intrinsic properties）的结论。

Method: 通过伽利略观测者变换（change of Galilean observer）检验原有分类结论的协变性，并利用伽利略速度相加定理（Galilean velocity-addition theorem）追溯摩擦约束的速度依赖性（velocity-dependent characterization）与观测者依赖的关联。

Result: 运动状态的分类并非观测者不变（observer-invariant），其依赖于摩擦约束的速度依赖性表征，且该依赖性的根源可追溯到伽利略速度相加定理。

Conclusion: 现有分析结果不能直接视为系统内在属性，需要重新构建模型以实现在不同伽利略观测者下保持不变的描述。

Abstract: We reconsider the analysis of the Classical Painlevé Problem developed by F. Génot and B. Brogliato ([1] New results on Painlevé paradoxes. - European Journal of Mechanics-A/Solids, 18(4):653{677, 1999), focusing on the consistency of their results with Galilean invariance. We show that certain conclusions concerning the dynamical evolution of the mechanical system are not invariant under changes of Galilean observer and therefore cannot, in their present form, be interpreted as intrinsic properties of the system. In particular, we show that the classi?cation of motion states depends on the observer through the velocity{dependent characterization of the frictional constraint, and we trace the origin of this dependence to the Galilean velocity-addition theorem. We present and discuss possible reformulations of the model aimed at restoring observer-invariant descriptions of the problem.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum estimates for classical polynomial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25445)
*Oleg Evnin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受量子力学变分法（quantum-mechanical variational method）启发的全新策略，用于求解多元齐次多项式（multivariate homogeneous polynomial）的上下界，方法是将多项式替换为在大状态空间中作用的算子，通过对该算子（即大型矩阵）进行对角化来逼近系数张量（coefficient tensor）的最大和最小特征值，从而获得多项式的界限。该方法在张量特征值文献的标准测试示例及数学物理中的 Strichartz 型不等式等问题上得到了成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找多元齐次多项式的上下界在动态稳定性（dynamical stability）和数据分析等多个领域中具有重要应用，但标准迭代方法在高阶张量情形下因多项式目标函数地形复杂而收敛不可靠，亟需一种更稳健的策略。

Method: 受量子力学变分法启发，将原始多项式替换为在一个适当选取的大状态空间中作用的算子；在合适的“经典”极限下，该算子逼近由交换变量构成的多项式表达式。通过计算该量子算子（表示为大型矩阵）的最小和最大特征值，即可逼近系数张量的相应特征值，从而获得多项式的上下界。

Result: 该方法被成功应用于张量特征值文献中的标准测试示例，以及数学物理中的 Strichartz 型不等式（Strichartz-type inequalities）等其他问题，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 量子变分方法为求解多项式界限问题提供了一种有别于传统非线性迭代的新路径，在张量特征值计算及相关数学物理问题中展现出可行性。

Abstract: The problem of finding lower and upper bounds on multivariate homogeneous polynomials is both difficult and important given its applications to questions ranging from dynamical stability in complex potential landscapes to data analysis. From the standpoint of tensor eigenvalue theory, the question is equivalent to finding the smallest and the largest eigenvalues of the coefficient tensor corresponding to the given polynomial. Standard approaches outlined in the literature amount to running nonlinear iterations in search for the optimal rays along which the growth of the polynomial is fastest or slowest. Unlike the case of matrices (or their corresponding multivariate quadratic forms) convergence of such algorithms for higher-rank tensors is capricious due to the complex topography of polynomial objective functions. In this essay, a very different strategy, inspired by quantum-mechanical variational methods, is introduced for finding bounds on polynomials. The original polynomial is replaced by an operator acting in a suitably chosen (large) space of states, such that in an appropriate "classical" limit this operator approaches the original polynomial expression made of commutative variables. As a result, approximating the smallest and largest eigenvalues of the coefficient tensor, and thus finding bounds on polynomials, amounts to diagonalizing the resulting quantum operator, represented as a large matrix, and then inspecting the smallest and largest eigenvalues of this matrix. This approach is then successfully applied to standard test examples from tensor eigenvalue literature and other problems of interest in mathematical physics including Strichartz-type inequalities.

</details>


### [101] [Mori-Zwanzig formalism: An existence proof for weak solutions of the orthogonal dynamics equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25855)
*Christoph Widder*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Givon等人关于Zwanzig投影存在性的证明，从平稳哈密顿系统推广到由拟压缩半群（quasicontraction semigroup）描述的非平稳非哈密顿系统，建立了解的增长率界（growth bound）和唯一性条件，并以阻尼谐振子为例进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典统计物理中，Mori-Zwanzig投影算子技术用于推导广义朗之万方程（generalized Langevin equation）。标准推导隐含假设了正交动力学方程（orthogonal dynamics equation）解的存在性以及变分常数公式（Dyson identity）的有效性。Givon等人指出，对于Zwanzig投影这类无限秩投影（infinite-rank projection），解的存在性是一个微妙问题，其唯一性和正则性仍是开放问题。

Method: 本文将Givon等人针对平稳哈密顿系统（stationary Hamiltonian systems）的存在性证明进行推广，使其适用于由拟压缩半群（quasicontraction semigroup）描述的非平稳非哈密顿系统（nonstationary non-Hamiltonian systems），并建立了解的增长率界（growth bound）以及足够正则解（sufficiently regular solutions）的唯一性条件。

Result: 成功将无限秩投影的存在性证明从平稳哈密顿系统推广至非平稳非哈密顿系统；建立了解的增长率界；给出了足够正则解（若存在）的唯一性条件；以阻尼谐振子（damped harmonic oscillator）为例验证了Zwanzig投影的应用。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将Zwanzig投影弱解的存在性由平稳哈密顿系统拓展至更广泛的非平稳非哈密顿动力系统（由拟压缩半群描述），并补充了增长率界和唯一性结果，从而推进了Mori-Zwanzig投影算子理论的数学基础。

Abstract: In classical statistical physics, the Mori-Zwanzig projection operator technique is used to derive generalized Langevin equations for a random variable (observable). Standard derivations implicitly assume the existence of solutions to the so-called orthogonal dynamics equation as well as the validity of the variation of constants formula (Dyson identity). It was pointed out by Givon, Hald and Kupferman that the existence is a subtle issue for infinite-rank projections such as Zwanzig's projection [D. Givon, O. H. Hald, R. Kupferman, Israel Journal of Mathematics, 145 (221-241), 2005]. The authors proved the existence of weak solutions for Zwanzig's projection in the context of stationary Hamiltonian systems. To this date, this is the only existence proof that allows for an infinite-rank projection, whereas the uniqueness and regularity remain open problems.
  In this article, we generalize the existence proof by Givon et al. to nonstationary non-Hamiltonian systems whose time evolution is given by a quasicontraction semigroup. We establish growth bounds as well as the uniqueness for sufficiently regular solutions (if existent). Finally, we apply our results to Zwanzig's projection using the damped harmonic oscillator as an example.

</details>


### [102] [Accurate Computation of Activated Volume in Electromagnetic Heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25994)
*Hongyun Wang,Parthiv Seetharaman,Shannon E. Foley,Hong Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对三维矩形网格上温度分布的等值面（isosurface）所围体积的计算问题，提出了一种结合外推法（extrapolation）与三角剖分（triangulation）逼近的高精度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电磁加热等应用中，需要计算通过有限差分法（finite difference method）数值求解偏微分方程（partial differential equation）得到的三维温度分布中等值面所围的体积。现有三角剖分逼近方法的精度有限，亟待改进。

Method: 在现有三角剖分逼近（用三角形网格近似等值面、用四面体集近似所围区域并求和体积）的基础上，引入外推法（extrapolation）进行组合，从而构建更高精度的体积计算方法。

Result: 结合外推法与三角剖分逼近的新方法相比传统三角剖分逼近，在计算三维矩形网格温度分布等值面所围体积时具有更高的精度。

Conclusion: 外推法可有效提升三角剖分逼近在计算等值面所围体积时的精度，为电磁加热等应用提供更准确的数值计算手段。

Abstract: In electromagnetic heating and other applications, we need to compute the volume enclosed by an isosurface of the 3D temperature distribution that is numerically represented on a rectangular grid. This situation arises naturally when the temperature distribution is obtained by solving a partial differential equation numerically using a finite difference method. Given the temperature distribution on the 3D grid, the isosurface of a prescribed value is represented approximately by a triangulation, a collection of triangles with vertices on the grid lines. The vertices are determined by a linear interpolation to approximate the locations where the temperature is at the prescribed level. The region enclosed by the isosurface is approximated by that enclosed by the triangulation, which is a set of tetrahedrons. The enclosed volume is approximated by summing those of tetrahedrons. This volume approximation is analogous to approximating a curve using line segments and is limited in accuracy. In this study, we combine extrapolation with the triangulation approximation to develop a more accurate method for computing the volume enclosed by an isosurface of the 3D temperature distribution on a rectangular grid.

</details>

### [103] [Stripe-tuned superconductivity in single-flavor metals with nontrivial quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24905)
*Yi-Ting Tu,Yang-Zhi Chou,Yi Huang,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究非平凡量子几何与外加条纹势共同作用如何影响二维单味金属中的超导性。在强条纹势下，系统在准一维极限内存在两种配对态：传统的纵向p_y波序和奇异的横向p_x波序，二者竞争由电子密度和贝里曲率修饰的相互作用控制。横向p_x波序具有节线，而纵向p_y波序为全能隙态，条纹势可调控配对对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子几何（quantum geometry）与外加条纹势（stripe potential）的耦合效应对超导配对对称性的影响，以期通过条纹势实现超导对称性的人为调控。

Method: 假设弱接触吸引相互作用，在强条纹势下将系统限制到最低子带（lowest subband），在准一维极限（quasi-one-dimensional limit）内解析推导两种可能的配对态。

Result: 除常规纵向p_y波序（longitudinal p_y-wave order）外，还存在一种奇异的横向p_x波序（transverse p_x-wave order）。二者竞争由每条条纹的电子密度（electron density）和贝里曲率修饰的相互作用（Berry-curvature-dressed interaction）控制。横向p_x波序在k_x=0处形成节线（nodal line），纵向p_y波序则具有全能隙（fully gapped）。

Conclusion: 通过条纹势可调控超导配对对称性，预言了具有非平凡量子几何（nontrivial quantum geometry）的超导态，并讨论了区分两种配对序的实验探测手段。

Abstract: We study how the interplay between nontrivial quantum geometry and an applied stripe potential affects superconductivity in a two-dimensional single-flavor metal. Assuming a weak contact attractive interaction and focusing on the lowest subband in the presence of a strong stripe potential, we analytically derive two possible pairing states in the quasi-one-dimensional limit. In addition to the conventional longitudinal $p_y$-wave order (with the stripes along the $y$ direction), we find that an exotic transverse $p_x$-wave order can be stabilized. The competition between these two orders is controlled by the electron density of each stripe and the Berry-curvature-dressed interaction. Notably, the transverse $p_x$ wave order develops a nodal line at $k_x=0$, while the longitudinal $p_y$ order is fully gapped. We discuss the possible experimental probes distinguishing these orders. Our results establish a way of controlling the pairing symmetry through a stripe potential, predicting superconductivity with nontrivial quantum geometry.

</details>


### [104] [Probing nonlocal superconducting fluctuations with covariance noise magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24906)
*Gustav Romare,Ilya Esterlis,Shimon Kolkowitz,Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在含时金茨堡-朗道（time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau）框架内计算了非局域超导涨落对电导率张量 σ_{ij}(q,ω) 的修正，并系统分析了其在量子噪声磁力测量中的可观测效应，包括单氮空位（NV）传感器的弛豫率、双传感器协方差磁力测量中的涨落关联长度，以及非平衡电流噪声对涨落-耗散定理的违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超导临界点附近，超导涨落（superconducting fluctuations）会导致电导率等物性的修正（即paraconductivity）。此前的理论处理多为局域近似，且对于非局域涨落如何影响磁噪声测量（特别是基于NV色心的量子噪声磁力计）尚未有系统研究。

Method: 采用含时金茨堡-朗道（time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau, TDGL）框架，解析计算非局域超导涨落对电导率张量 σ_{ij}(q,ω) 的修正；推导单NV传感器弛豫率 1/T_1 对温度、样品-传感器距离和探测频率的依赖关系；研究双传感器协方差磁力测量中的两点场关联函数；分析Maki-Thompson修正和涨落抗磁性（fluctuation diamagnetism）；并严格求解非平衡电流噪声至直流电场的所有阶及所有波矢。

Result: 单NV弛豫率中，非局域性和库珀对（Cooper pair）涨落动力学在临界点附近截断（cut off）了临界增强效应；双传感器协方差磁力测量中，两点场关联函数出现额外空间结构，其范围直接测量涨落关联长度 ξ(T)；非平衡电流噪声与非线性paraconductivity退耦，在临界点处以普适因子违反涨落-耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem），并产生偏压诱导的空间各向异性。

Conclusion: 本文的理论结果与近期BSCCO薄膜电流噪声实验建立了联系，表明协方差磁力测量能够直接探测超导涨落的关联长度和非平衡噪声的空间结构，为理解临界涨落和非平衡统计力学提供了可实验检验的预言。

Abstract: The nonlocal superconducting fluctuation corrections to the conductivity tensor $σ_{ij}(\mathbf{q},ω)$ are calculated within the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau framework, and their observable consequences for quantum noise magnetometry are worked out. For a single nitrogen-vacancy (NV) sensor we obtain the relaxation rate $1/T_1$ as a function of temperature, sample-sensor distance, and probe frequency, identifying the scales at which the nonlocality and the dynamics of the pair fluctuations cut off the critical enhancement near $T_c$. For two-sensor covariance magnetometry we show that the two-point field correlator develops additional spatial structure whose range directly measures the fluctuation correlation length $ξ(T)$. We further analyze two channels that accompany the paraconductivity: the Maki-Thompson correction to the spin susceptibility, and the fluctuation diamagnetism. Finally, we solve exactly, to all orders in a dc electric field and at all wave vectors, for the nonequilibrium current noise of the fluctuating film: the noise decouples from the nonlinear paraconductivity, violating the fluctuation-dissipation theorem by universal factors at criticality and acquiring a bias-induced spatial anisotropy directly measurable by covariance magnetometry. The results are connected to a recent experiment measuring current noise near a thin film of BSCCO.

</details>


### [105] [Nonlocal Majorana polarization in non-Hermitian topological superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25424)
*Arjun S. Kumar,Jorge Cayao,Oladunjoye A. Awoga*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文将非局域马约拉纳极化（nonlocal Majorana polarization）扩展至非厄米（non-Hermitian）体系，利用双正交本征态（biorthogonal eigenstates）定义该拓扑指标，并展示其在区分非厄米超导体中的马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes）、平庸零能态（trivial zero-energy states）与奇异点（exceptional points）等方面的有效性。此外，引入非局域马约拉纳极化灵敏度（nonlocal Majorana polarization sensitivity）以表征非厄米性对极化的贡献，并发现非厄米性可增强马约拉纳零模的鲁棒性（robustness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的非局域马约拉纳极化在厄米（Hermitian）拓扑超导体系中是可靠的拓扑指示符，但尚未被推广至非厄米体系。本文旨在将该概念拓展至非厄米领域，并检验其在更广泛超导系统中的适用性。

Method: 通过引入双正交本征态，将非局域马约拉纳极化的定义推广到非厄米情形；同时提出非局域马约拉纳极化灵敏度，用于量化非厄米性对极化量的具体贡献。

Result: 该方法能清晰区分非厄米超导体中的马约拉纳零模、平庸零能态及奇异点。此外，非局域马约拉纳极化捕捉到非厄米性增强马约拉纳零模鲁棒性的现象。

Conclusion: 非局域马约拉纳极化可有效作为非厄米超导体系中马约拉纳零模的拓扑指示符，且非厄米性对其鲁棒性有增强作用。

Abstract: The nonlocal Majorana polarization, defined as the product of the expectation values of the particle-hole operator at opposite halves of the system, has been shown to be a reliable topological indicator that determines the presence and quality of Majorana zero modes in Hermitian topological superconducting setups. In this work, we extend the concept of nonlocal Majorana polarization to the non-Hermitian realm by taking into account the biorthogonal eigenstates and demonstrate its utility by exploring distinct non-Hermitian superconducting systems. In particular, we show that the Majorana polarization can distinguish between Majorana zero modes, trivial zero-energy states, and exceptional points in non-Hermitian superconductors. Also, we introduce the concept of nonlocal Majorana polarization sensitiviy for characterizing the contribution of non-Hermiticity to Majorana polarization. As a byproduct, we find that non-Hermiticity enhances Majorana zero modes robustness, a property captured by the nonlocal Majorana polarization.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Low-Temperature Magnetoresistance Hysteresis in Granular Cr1-xFexO2: Slow Relaxation of Transport-Active Magnetic Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25263)
*E. Yu. Beliayev,V. A. Horielyi,I. G. Mirzoiev,V. V. Andrievskii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过对CrO2和Fe掺杂Cr1-xFexO2压实粉末低温磁阻回线的再分析，发现当电输运由少量自旋依赖的晶粒间隧穿路径主导时，磁阻回线呈现强非单调性，并识别出一个约3秒的有效磁输运弛豫时间，归因于晶粒接触处局域磁矩和畴构型的缓慢演化，而非微观自旋翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有数据显示未掺杂CrO2和含Fe的Cr1-xFexO2在低温下出现非常规的磁阻回线分支交叉现象，但对其背后的物理机制尚不清晰。本文旨在通过重新分析这些数据，揭示非单调迟滞特征的形成机制。

Method: 对已报道的CrO2和Fe掺杂Cr1-xFexO2压实粉末的低温磁阻数据进行重新分析，重点关注常规低场迟滞之外的额外分支交叉现象。通过对三种最快扫描速率的数据进行数字化处理，提取电阻恢复场与扫描速率的关系。

Result: 在含Fe样品中，电阻先下降、经过最小值、在较宽场区间上升、再在高场下降；这种非单调迟滞特征在减慢磁场扫描时显著减弱。电阻开始恢复的磁场随扫描速率近似线性变化，对应有效磁输运弛豫时间约3秒。Fe掺杂增加了矫顽力和界面磁异质性，同时降低了整体负磁阻。

Conclusion: 观测结果表明，传统的负隧穿磁阻叠加一个与瞬态局域磁无序相关的较慢电阻增加贡献。约3秒的弛豫时间归因于主导电流的晶粒间接触处局域磁矩和畴构型的缓慢演化，而非微观自旋翻转。研究结果支持输运权重磁重构机制（transport-weighted magnetic-reconfiguration mechanism）解释异常迟滞形状。

Abstract: Low-temperature magnetoresistance loops of compacted CrO2 powders become strongly nonmonotonic when electrical transport is dominated by a small number of spin-dependent intergranular tunnelling paths. We reanalyse previously reported data for undoped CrO2 and Fe-containing Cr1-xFexO2, focusing on additional branch crossings beyond the conventional low-field hysteresis. In the Fe-containing specimen, the resistance decreases, passes through a minimum, rises over an extended field interval, and then decreases again at higher field. This nonmonotonic hysteretic feature is strongly reduced when the magnetic-field sweep is slowed. Digitization of the three fastest sweeps shows that the field at which the resistance starts to recover shifts approximately linearly with sweep rate, corresponding to an effective magnetotransport relaxation time of about 3 s. This scale is attributed not to microscopic spin flips but to the slow evolution of local moment and domain configurations at the intergranular contacts dominating the current. The observations are consistent with conventional negative tunnelling magnetoresistance superimposed on a slower resistance-increasing contribution associated with transient local magnetic disorder. Fe increases coercivity and interfacial magnetic heterogeneity while reducing the overall negative magnetoresistance. The results support a transport-weighted magnetic-reconfiguration mechanism for the unusual hysteresis shape.

</details>


### [107] [Relaxation of quenched structural glasses: descent in a stiffening caging potential over inflection-point 'speed bumps'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25360)
*Ting Qu,Deng Pan,Yuliang Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟结构玻璃形成体中的梯度下降动力学，揭示了淬火玻璃慢能量弛豫中幂律衰减的微观起源。研究表明，该幂律行为并非源于此前提出的鞍点减速、边缘稳定性、局域激发粗化或声子动力学等机制，而是来自一种简单的笼蔽效应（caging effect）：每个粒子经历由多体约束产生的有效硬化势（stiffening potential），该势能在特征笼尺寸处发散。这一机制解析地导出了观测到的幂律衰减，并可由一个最小化的单粒子笼模型定量再现。动力学过程中伴随能量景观拐点处的涨落，但这些涨落仅起“减速带”（speed bumps）作用而不影响整体幂律行为。与平均场自旋玻璃理论不同，本文未发现区分不同动力学区间的特征温度；只要系统足够接近其固有结构（inherent structure），所有初始温度下的玻璃盆地内态跟随（state following）均普遍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火玻璃中的慢能量弛豫是普遍存在但理解不足的现象，尽管已有广泛研究，观测到的幂律衰减（power-law decay）的微观起源仍存在争议。已有机制包括鞍点减速（saddle-point slowdown）、边缘稳定性（marginal stability）、局域激发粗化（coarsening of localized excitations）和声子动力学（phonon dynamics），但均缺乏共识。

Method: 作者对典型结构玻璃形成体（structural glass formers）进行梯度下降（gradient descent）模拟，并与上述已有机制对比。进一步构建了最小化的单粒子笼模型（minimal single-particle cage model），将相邻粒子固定以检验集体弛豫模式的必要性。

Result: 已有机制均无法解释模拟数据；幂律行为源于一种简单的笼蔽效应——每个粒子感受到的多体约束产生有效硬化势，其在特征笼尺寸处发散。这一机制解析地导出了幂律衰减。动力学中能量景观拐点处的涨落仅充当“减速带”，不影响整体幂律行为。未发现区分不同动力学区间的特征温度；态跟随在所有初始温度下普遍成立。

Conclusion: 本文为典型结构玻璃中的梯度下降动力学建立了完整的物理图像，表明集体弛豫模式并非必需，而单粒子笼效应足以解释观测到的幂律弛豫行为。该结果与平均场自旋玻璃理论（mean-field spin glass theory）形成对比，后者预言存在特征温度区分不同动力学区间。

Abstract: The slow energy relaxation in quenched glasses is a ubiquitous yet poorly understood phenomenon. Despite extensive study, the microscopic origin of the observed power-law decay remains debated, with proposed mechanisms ranging from saddle-point slowdown and marginal stability to coarsening of localized excitations and phonon dynamics. Here, by simulating gradient descent in archetypal structural glass formers, we show that none of these scenarios can account for our data. Instead, the power-law behavior emerges from a remarkably simple caging effect: each particle experiences an effective stiffening potential that arises from many-body confinement and diverges at a characteristic cage size. This mechanism analytically yields the observed power-law decay and is quantitatively reproduced by a minimal single-particle cage model with fixed neighbours, demonstrating that collective relaxation modes are not essential. The dynamics is punctuated by fluctuations as the system rolls through inflection points on the energy landscape, which act as `speed bumps' but do not affect the overall power-law behaviour. In contrast to mean-field spin glass theory, we find no characteristic temperature that separates distinct dynamical regimes; state following within a given glass basin occurs universally for all initial temperatures whenever the system is sufficiently close to the inherent structure. Our results establish a complete physical picture of gradient descent dynamics in typical structural glasses.

</details>

### [108] [Correlated signals of ultralight scalar dark matter in pulsar timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24912)
*Joshua W. Foster,Kimberly K. Boddy,Jeff A. Dror,Vincent S. H. Lee,Andrea Mitridate,Tristan L. Smith,Kelsie Taylor,Tanner Trickle*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）探测超轻暗物质（ULDM）时，现有方法仅在完全相关或完全不相关极限下分析的局限性，首次发展了一种自洽的高斯随机场（Gaussian random field）框架，通过有限空间相关性使振幅先验在两种极限之间连续插值，并在模拟数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTA对ULDM的搜索通常采用完全相关极限（fully correlated limit）或完全不相关极限（fully uncorrelated limit）这两种近似描述，而这两种极限之间的转变恰好发生在PTA敏感的质量范围内，导致投影灵敏度和数据约束依赖于所选择的极限假设。

Method: 将ULDM场视为具有有限空间相关性的高斯随机场，使PTA信号模型中的振幅先验（amplitude prior）在完全相关与完全不相关极限之间连续插值；针对线性和二次耦合标量ULDM分别应用该框架，并通过增强潜场先验（augmented latent-field prior）纳入脉冲星距离不确定性，使用归一化流（normalizing-flow）代理表示距离边缘化后的潜振幅分布。

Result: 在模拟PTA数据集（含盲法信号注入测试）上验证了该方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该自洽框架消除了此前因采用不同极限假设而产生的敏感性依赖，为未来PTA数据对ULDM的约束提供了更可靠的分析基础。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) are sensitive to ultralight dark matter (ULDM) in the $10^{-24}$-$10^{-20}\,\mathrm{eV}$ mass range, with existing datasets already probing otherwise open parameter space and future PTAs promising substantial improvements in reach. Thus far, however, PTA searches for ULDM have typically been formulated using limiting descriptions. Analyses are performed in either the fully correlated limit, in which the local ULDM amplitude is shared across the array, or the fully uncorrelated limit, in which each pulsar has an independent local amplitude. Because the transition between these regimes occurs within the PTA-sensitive mass range, projected sensitivities and data-derived constraints can depend on which limiting description is assumed. For the first time, we develop a self-consistent analysis that treats the ULDM field as a Gaussian random field with finite spatial correlations, allowing the amplitude prior used in PTA signal models to interpolate continuously between the fully correlated and fully uncorrelated limits. We apply the framework to both linearly and quadratically coupled scalar ULDM, the latter including the universal gravitational signal sourced by the oscillating ULDM pressure. Pulsar-distance uncertainties are incorporated through an augmented latent-field prior, and the resulting distance-marginalized latent-amplitude distribution is represented with a normalizing-flow surrogate. We validate the method on mock PTA datasets, including blinded signal injection tests.

</details>


### [109] [Primordial Physics in the Nonlinear Universe: Revealing the oscillating halo bias from cosmological collider models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24939)
*Dhayaa Anbajagane,Neal Dalal*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需传统模板基分解的新模拟方法，用于重现宇宙学对撞机（cosmological collider）模型在原初三点关联函数中产生的振荡信号，并在模拟中首次测量了振荡晕偏置（oscillating halo bias），发现其幅度、相位与晕质量、频率及成团偏置（assembly bias）密切相关，且可用峰值背景分裂理论（peak background-split theory）准确建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙的初始条件包含了极高能粒子物理的丰富信息。一类称为宇宙学对撞机的特征会在原初密度场的三点关联函数（或双谱）中产生振荡，并在晕偏置（halo bias）中留下尺度依赖的振荡印记。然而，传统基于模板基分解的模拟方法存在局限，需要发展新的模拟技术以更准确地重现并测量这些振荡信号。

Method: 作者提出了一种新的模拟方法，摒弃了传统的基于模板的基分解（template-based basis-decomposition）方式，能够以百分之一的精度重现输入模板中的尺度依赖信号。利用该方法，针对一类对撞机模型进行了模拟，并首次在模拟中测量了振荡晕偏置。

Result: 振荡的幅度和相位对晕质量有明显依赖性：晕质量变化十倍会导致振荡位置移动约两倍。提高原初双谱模型的频率会抑制晕偏置中的信号，因为振荡在晕的窗函数（window function）上被平均化。信号的相位对成团偏置（assembly bias）敏感。在所有情况下，尺度依赖的晕偏置都可以用简单的峰值背景分裂理论（peak background-split theory）准确建模。

Conclusion: 振荡及其质量和选择依赖的相位偏移是一种独特的信号特征，不易被已知观测系统误差（observational systematics）所模仿，因此是一个更为稳健的观测目标。模拟代码和初始条件代码均已公开发布。

Abstract: The initial conditions of our Universe contain a wealth of information about the particle physics of very high energies. One such class of signatures, called cosmological colliders, generates oscillations in the three-point correlations (or bispectra) of the primordial density field, and these imprint scale-dependent oscillations in the halo bias. We develop a new method for simulating cosmological collider models that foregoes traditional template-based basis-decomposition methods and can reproduce the scale-dependent signals of the input template at percent-level accuracy. Using this method, we produce simulations for one class of collider models and present the first measurements of oscillating halo bias in simulations. The amplitude and phase of the oscillations show a clear dependence on halo mass, with a factor of ten shift in halo mass causing a factor of two shift in the location of the oscillations. Increasing the frequency of the primordial bispectra model suppresses the signal in the halo bias, as the oscillations average down over the window function of the halo. The phase of the signal is also sensitive to assembly bias. In all cases, the scale-dependent halo bias can be accurately modeled using a simple peak background-split theory. The oscillations and their mass/selection-dependent phase offsets are a unique signature that is not easily mimicked by known observational systematics and is therefore a more robust target. Our simulations and underlying initial conditions code are both made publicly available.

</details>


### [110] [EFT-Ramses: a code to simulate the effective field theory of dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24940)
*Nathaniel Ota Woodcock,Sownak Bose,Yunhao Gao,Baojiu Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍EFT-RAMSES，一个基于有效场论框架（EFTofDE）拓展的宇宙学N体模拟代码。该代码通过统一的“主”Vainshtein方程整合了多种标量和矢量-张量引力理论，包括DGP模型、三次伽利略子（标量、矢量及广义协变形式）以及通用EFT参数化方案，能够在暗能量和修正引力场景下进行非线性结构形成的高精度模拟。与现有代码（ECOSMOG、HiCOLA）及线性理论的验证比较显示结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ΛCDM模型虽与当前多数观测高度吻合，但围绕宇宙学常数（$Λ$）的理论困难强烈推动了动力学暗能量（DE）和修正引力（MG）模型的探索。为利用第四阶段巡天（Stage-IV surveys）的高精度数据研究宇宙加速的物理本质，需要能够探测非线性结构增长的N体模拟工具。

Method: 将有效场论框架（EFTofDE）嵌入ECOSMOG模拟代码，利用$α$-basis参数化构建模型无关的通用计算引擎。将DGP模型（正常支和自加速支）、三次伽利略子（标量csG、矢量cvG及广义协变GCCG）以及通用EFT参数化等不同理论统一纳入单一“主”Vainshtein方程，无需针对特定模型开发求解器。

Result: 对正常支DGP（nDGP）、csG、GCCG及EFT模型进行了高分辨率N体模拟。将得到的物质功率谱与已有代码（ECOSMOG、HiCOLA）及线性理论进行比较，结果显示出高度的一致性。

Conclusion: EFT-RAMSES为利用即将开展的宇宙学巡天对暗能量和修正引力进行精确宇宙学检验提供了一个鲁棒且通用的计算工具。代码已在GitHub EFT-RAMSES仓库中开源下载。

Abstract: While the standard $Λ$CDM paradigm is in excellent agreement with most current cosmological observations, theoretical challenges surrounding the cosmological constant ($Λ$) have strongly motivated the exploration of dynamical dark energy (DE) and modified gravity (MG) models. Investigating the physical nature of the cosmic acceleration requires N-body simulations to probe the non-linear growth of cosmic structure and prepare for the high-precision data from Stage-IV surveys. In this paper, we present EFT-RAMSES, a comprehensive extension of the ECOSMOG cosmological simulation code designed to explore non-linear structure formation in DE and MG scenarios. We embed the effective field theory of dark energy (EFTofDE) framework into this new numerical pipeline, utilising the $α$-basis parameterisation to provide a versatile, model-agnostic, computational engine. By consolidating diverse scalar and vector-tensor theories---including the normal and self-accelerating Dvali-Gabadadze-Porrati (DGP) models, cubic Galileons (cubic scalar Galileon (csG), cubic vector Galileon (cvG) and generalised cubic covariant Galileon (GCCG)), and generic effective field theory (EFT) parameterisation---into a single "master" Vainshtein equation, this pipeline bypasses the need for model-specific solvers and easily specialises to any particular model. As validations, we perform high-resolution N-body simulations for the normal-branch DGP (nDGP), csG, GCCG, and EFT models, comparing the resulting matter power spectra against dependent and independent codes such as legacy ECOSMOG and HiCOLA, as well as linear theory, and find excellent agreement. EFT-RAMSES provides a robust and versatile computational tool for precision cosmological tests of DE and MG using upcoming cosmological surveys. The code is available for download from the GitHub EFT-RAMSES repository.

</details>


### [111] [Gravitational Waves from Superconducting Cosmic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25317)
*Jinyoung Jhun,Takashi Hiramatsu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究利用三维晶格场模拟在膨胀宇宙中演化超导宇宙弦网络，发现弦与载流子之间的耦合常数会影响引力波谱的小尺度特征，该特征有望被未来高频引力波观测探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导宇宙弦（superconducting cosmic strings）是早期宇宙中可能形成的一类拓扑缺陷（topological defect），其演化会辐射引力波（gravitational waves）。理解不同耦合强度下弦网络的演化及对应的引力波谱，对于预测未来高频引力波实验的可观测信号至关重要。

Method: 采用$U(1)_{\rm local}\times U(1)_{\rm global}$场论模型，通过三维晶格场模拟（three-dimensional lattice field simulations）在膨胀宇宙背景下演化超导宇宙弦网络，并计算由其产生的引力波功率谱（gravitational-wave power spectrum）。

Result: 研究发现引力波谱的形状依赖于弦形成场与载流子场之间的耦合常数（coupling constant）：随着相互作用强度增大，小尺度上的引力波谱受到抑制。

Conclusion: 这种耦合依赖的谱特征可作为未来高频引力波观测（high-frequency gravitational-wave observations）的潜在探测目标，为验证超导宇宙弦模型提供可检验的预言。

Abstract: We study the evolution of superconducting cosmic-string networks in a $U(1)_{\rm local}\times U(1)_{\rm global}$ field-theoretic model using three-dimensional lattice field simulations in an expanding universe. The scalar field charged under $U(1)_{\rm local}$ forms cosmic strings, while the scalar field associated with $U(1)_{\rm global}$ acts as a current carrier and condenses in their vicinity. We investigate the evolution of the string network for several values of the coupling constant between the string-forming and current-carrier fields and estimate the gravitational-wave power spectrum sourced by the resulting superconducting cosmic-string network. We find that the spectral shape depends on the coupling constant: as the interaction strength increases, the spectrum is suppressed on small scales. This feature may be probed by future high-frequency gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [112] [Neutrino-electron scattering kernels in isotropic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25436)
*Jens Chluba,Bryce Cyr,Michiru Uwabo-Niibo,Masahide Yamaguchi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文推导了各向同性介质中电子中微子-电子散射核（electron neutrino-electron scattering kernel）的通用解析表达式，将五维碰撞积分简化至二维，并与康普顿散射核进行对比，结果可用于大爆炸核合成（Big Bang Nucleosynthesis）等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙中微子与等离子体粒子（尤其是自由电子和正电子）的散射过程对宇宙演化具有重要意义，但现有的散射核表达式在高维积分上计算复杂，缺乏简洁的解析形式。

Method: 采用类似于康普顿散射核（Compton scattering kernel）推导的方法，对各向同性介质中的角积分进行化简，将五维碰撞积分降为二维，最终得到仅由初等函数（elementary functions）构成的紧凑解析表达式。

Result: 得到了电子中微子-电子散射核的解析形式，并计算了其前几阶矩（moments），揭示了能量再分布过程的动力学特征。通过与光子-电子散射核的对比，展示了二者的异同。此外，将该表达式推广至μ/τ中微子-电子以及中微子-正电子散射过程。

Conclusion: 本文给出的解析散射核易于数值求值，可直接应用于大爆炸核合成等宇宙学计算，并已作为扩展模块加入康普顿散射库 CSpack 中，供更广泛的应用场景使用。

Abstract: In the early universe, neutrinos undergo many interactions with the particles in the plasma. Key processes are the scattering of neutrinos by free electrons and positrons. In this paper, we derive general expressions for the electron neutrino-electron scattering kernel in isotropic media, analytically simplifying the 5D collision integral to two dimensions. We follow a procedure that is similar to the derivation of the Compton scattering kernel to reduce the angular integrals, yielding a compact analytic expression in terms of elementary functions that can be easily evaluated. We illustrate the properties of this kernel and also compute its first moments analytically, providing insights into the energetics of the redistribution process. For comparison, we consider the photon-electron scattering kernel, highlighting differences and similarities. We then explain how the obtained expressions can also be applied to the $ν_{μ/τ}$-electron and neutrino-positron scattering processes. The results presented here may be useful in the context of Big Bang Nucleosynthesis and were added as an extension to the Compton scattering library CSpack for more general applications.

</details>


### [113] [No preference for generalized emergent dark energy from current cosmological data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25539)
*Vipin Kumar Sharma,Himanshu Chaudhary,Salvatore Capozziello,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,G. Mustafa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过结合DESI DR2重子声学振荡、CMB（ACT、SPT、Planck）以及Ia型超新星（Pantheon⁺、DES-Dovekie、Union3）等多类观测数据，对广义涌现暗能量模型（GEDE）进行了检验。结果表明，GEDE模型与ΛCDM范式在统计上不可区分：关键参数Δ在2σ内与ΛCDM一致，且未能再现DESI DR2所暗示的幻象穿越（phantom crossing）行为。模型无法缓解H₀和S₈宇宙学张力，贝叶斯证据和显著性水平均不支持GEDE优于ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI DR2数据暗示可能存在动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）乃至Quintom-B型行为，即暗能量状态方程穿越w=-1的幻象穿越（phantom crossing）现象。本文旨在用多类观测数据系统检验广义涌现暗能量模型（generalized emergent dark energy, GEDE）是否能够描述这一行为，并评估其相对于ΛCDM模型的统计偏好。

Method: 将GEDE模型与DESI DR2重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）测量、ACT、SPT和Planck的联合CMB数据，以及Pantheon⁺、DES-Dovekie和Union3三类Ia型超新星样本进行联合拟合。通过对比关键模型参数Δ的约束、暗能量状态方程w(z)的演化行为、H₀和S₈参数以及贝叶斯证据（Bayesian evidence）和Gaussian显著性水平，评估GEDE相对于ΛCDM的统计偏好。

Result: GEDE模型与ΛCDM范式兼容，加入超新星数据后参数Δ在2σ内与ΛCDM值Δ=0一致。模型未呈现DESI DR2所暗示的幻象穿越，其w(z)表现为全幻象（phantom, w<-1）或全精粹（quintessence, w>-1），取决于数据集组合；包含Pantheon⁺时w≈-1接近ΛCDM。H₀约束为67.9–69.8 km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹，r_d和S₈与ΛCDM一致，未缓解H₀和S₈张力。Gaussian显著性水平<2σ，贝叶斯证据ln B_{i,j}<1，均不支持GEDE优于ΛCDM。

Conclusion: 尽管DESI DR2暗示了动力学暗能量，但GEDE模型在观测上与ΛCDM不可区分（observationally indistinguishable），不支持DESI DR2所提示的Quintom-B型行为。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the generalized emergent dark energy model by confronting it with DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation measurements, in combination with joint CMB data from ACT, SPT, and Planck, as well as Type Ia supernova samples including Pantheon$^+$, DES-Dovekie, and Union3. We find that the GEDE model remains compatible with the $Λ$CDM paradigm, with no statistically significant preference for deviations when all datasets are combined. In particular, the key model parameter $Δ$ is consistent with the $Λ$CDM value $Δ= 0$ within $2σ$ once SNe Ia data are included. Despite this overall agreement, the GEDE model does not exhibit the phantom crossing suggested by DESI DR2. Instead, the evolution of the dark energy equation of state $w(z)$ indicates that the model behaves either as a full phantom ($w < -1$) or quintessence ($w > -1$), depending on the dataset combination, without a clear transition across $w = -1$. When Pantheon$^+$ data are included, the model converges closely to $Λ$CDM with $w \simeq -1$. The GEDE model does not alleviate the existing cosmological tensions. The inferred values of $H_0$ remain in the range $67.9$-$69.8\ \mathrm{km,s^{-1},Mpc^{-1}}$, while the sound horizon $r_d$ and clustering parameter $S_8$ remain consistent with $Λ$CDM, failing to resolve the $H_0$ and $S_8$ tensions. Finally, both Gaussian significance levels ($<2σ$) and Bayesian evidence ($\ln B_{i,j} < 1$) indicate no statistically significant preference for GEDE over $Λ$CDM. We conclude that, although DESI DR2 hints at dynamical dark energy, the GEDE model remains observationally indistinguishable from $Λ$CDM and does not support the Quintom-B-type behavior suggested by DESI DR2.

</details>


### [114] [Minimally modified gravity with Laplacian auxiliary constraints and an inflationary realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26031)
*Jakkrit Sangtawee,Antonio De Felice,Khamphee Karwan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种最小修正引力理论（minimally modified gravity theory），该理论在空间平坦的FLRW背景上仅传播两个张量引力自由度，并具有可预测的宇宙学微扰论。通过将辅助约束与乘子的空间拉普拉斯项耦合，消除了原始四约束构造中拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multipliers）的歧义，使得乘子扇区不再影响均匀背景方程，同时继续约束非均匀标量扇区。在特定子类中，张量色散关系不含k⁴项，耦合标准暴胀场后唯一传播的标量模式是暴胀子涨落。对于二次势，引力修正改变了标量谱指数，且超光速张量速度cT>1会压低张量-标量比r=16εs/cT。满足观测约束的参数区域要求超光速张量速度，但极大的cT可能降低微扰截断能标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始四约束构造中拉格朗日乘子的均匀值未被完全确定，却出现在二次张量作用量中，阻碍了宇宙学可预测性。因此需要修正该构造以消除这一歧义，使得理论在FLRW背景上具有预测能力。

Method: 通过将辅助约束与乘子的空间拉普拉斯项（spatial Laplacians）耦合，使乘子扇区从均匀背景方程中消失，同时仍约束非均匀标量扇区。随后选取一个引力哈密顿量密度正比于lapse函数且包含三次动量不变量（cubic momentum invariants）的子类进行分析。

Result: 在修正后的理论中，张量色散关系（tensor dispersion relation）一般包含k²和k⁴贡献，但不存在传播的引力矢量或标量模式。在特定子类中k⁴项消失；耦合标准暴胀场后，唯一传播的标量模式是暴胀子涨落，声速为1。对于二次势，标量谱指数偏离广义相对论预言，张量-标量比被cT>1压低为r=16εs/cT。

Conclusion: 满足当前观测约束的参数区域要求超光速张量速度（superluminal tensor speed），但极大的cT同时会减小张量动能系数，可能降低微扰截断能标（perturbative cutoff）。确定实际的强耦合能标仍需非线性分析。

Abstract: We construct a minimally modified gravity theory that propagates only two tensorial gravitational degrees of freedom around a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker (FLRW) background and admits a predictive cosmological perturbation theory. We first show that, in the original four-constraint construction, the homogeneous values of the Lagrange multipliers are not fully determined and nevertheless enter the quadratic tensor action, thereby obstructing cosmological predictivity. We remove this ambiguity by coupling the auxiliary constraints to spatial Laplacians of the multipliers. The multiplier sector then drops out of the homogeneous background equations while continuing to constrain the inhomogeneous scalar sector. The tensor dispersion relation generically contains both $k^{2}$ and $k^{4}$ contributions, whereas no propagating gravitational vector or scalar mode is present on the generic branch for which the constraint reduction is nondegenerate. We subsequently study a subclass whose gravitational Hamiltonian density is proportional to the lapse and contains cubic momentum invariants. In this subclass the $k^{4}$ tensor term vanishes and the lapse can be absorbed into a time redefinition at the background level. After coupling a canonical inflaton, the only propagating scalar mode is the inflaton fluctuation, with unit sound speed. For a quadratic potential, departures from general relativity shift the scalar spectral index, while a tensor speed $c_{T}>1$ suppresses the tensor-to-scalar ratio according to $r=16ε_{s}/c_{T}$. The parameter regions compatible with the observational bounds adopted in this work require a superluminal tensor speed; however, very large $c_{T}$ simultaneously reduces the tensor kinetic coefficient and may lower the perturbative cutoff, although determining the actual strong-coupling scale requires a nonlinear analysis.

</details>


### [115] [$Λ$XCDM: a running vacuum strategy for crossing the phantom divide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26050)
*Joan Solà Peracaula,Adrià Gómez-Valent,Alex González-Fuentes*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过ΛXCDM模型——一种复合动力学暗能量（composite dynamical dark energy, DDE）模型——对DESI等宇宙学数据进行了拟合分析，发现该模型能够自然解释DESI观测到的幽灵分割线穿越现象（phantom divide crossing），并提供比标准w₀wₐCDM参数化更好的拟合结果，同时缓解了宇宙巧合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，复合动力学暗能量（composite DDE）有望缓解宇宙学中的张紧（tensions）问题。本文旨在通过ΛXCDM模型这一更基础的理论框架，检验其能否解释DESI数据中观测到的幽灵分割线穿越（phantom divide crossing）现象，并为该现象提供第一性原理的理论支持。

Method: 计算ΛXCDM模型的有效状态方程（effective equation of state），并利用最先进技术将模型拟合到两组标准宇宙学数据集：一组包含Pantheon+的Ia型超新星（SNIa）、DESI DR2的重子声学振荡（BAO）数据以及Planck PR4的宇宙微波背景（CMB）数据；另一组将Pantheon+替换为DES-Dovekie的SNIa数据。分析中未使用大尺度结构（LSS）形成数据，也未采用SH0ES对哈勃常数H₀的标定。

Result: ΛXCDM模型自然实现了DESI在红移z≈0.4附近观测到的幽灵分割线穿越现象，且该特征与现有模型无关分析一致。发现宇宙人（cosmon）X在近当前时期表现为“幽灵物质”（phantom matter, PM），与通常的幽灵暗能量不同，它满足强能量条件（strong energy condition）并产生正压（P_X > -ρ_X > 0），但代价是负能量密度（ρ_X < 0）。ΛXCDM模型比w₀wₐCDM参数化提供更好的拟合，并减轻了宇宙巧合问题（cosmic coincidence problem）。

Conclusion: 由于幽灵物质（PM）出现在弦论版本的运行真空模型（RVM）中，ΛXCDM作为一种复合动力学暗能量（DDE）模型，有希望从第一性原理（first principles）解释幽灵分割线穿越现象，从而为DESI基于通用暗能量参数化所得观测结果提供理论支持。

Abstract: Composite dynamical dark energy (DDE) has recently been explored as an efficient way to help cure cosmological tensions through the so-called $w$XCDM model {Gomez-Valent:2024tdb,Gomez-Valent:2024ejh}, a toy-model version of the $Λ$XCDM model {Grande:2006nn}. The latter is a composite running vacuum model (RVM) that involves a DE component $X$ (`cosmon') of generic nature. We compute the effective equation of state of $Λ$XCDM and use state-of-the-art techniques to fit this model to two standard sets of cosmological data, one involving SNIa from Pantheon$+$ and the other SNIa from DES-Dovekie, in addition to BAO data from DESI DR2 and the CMB data from Planck PR4. We do not use large scale structure formation data for this analysis nor the SH0ES calibration of $H_0$. We find that $Λ$XCDM naturally performs the crossing of the phantom divide as observed by DESI near $z\simeq 0.4$ using the $w_0w_a$CDM parameterization, a feature well favored by existing model-agnostic analyzes of the same data {Gonzalez-Fuentes:2025lei,Gonzalez-Fuentes:2026rgu}. It turns out that the cosmon $X$ behaves as `phantom matter' (PM) near the present, which in contrast to usual phantom DE satisfies the strong energy condition (as ordinary matter) and furnishes positive pressure ($P_X>-ρ_X>0$) at the expense of negative energy density ($ρ_X<0$). $Λ$XCDM provides a better fit than $w_0w_a$CDM and, as a bonus, alleviates the cosmic coincidence problem. Given that PM appears in stringy versions of the RVM, the $Λ$XCDM appears to be a composite DDE model with a good chance of explaining the crossing of the phantom divide from first principles, therefore providing theoretical support to the DESI observations inferred from generic parameterizations of the DE.

</details>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [A geometric approach to QNMs in optics: application to pseudospectrum and structural stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25133)
*Lamis Al Sheikh,José Luis Jaramillo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何-谱框架（geometric-spectral framework），用于计算紧支撑开放光学腔中的准正规模（QNMs）并分析其稳定性。该方法将来自引力物理的超曲面方法（hyperboloidal approach）引入电磁学，结合辅助场公式描述色散吸收介质，通过Chebyshev谱离散化求解非厄米谱问题，并扩展伪谱分析（pseudospectrum analysis）以评估光学共振在扰动下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放光学腔中的准正规模（QNMs）是描述光学共振的关键工具，但其计算和稳定性分析面临挑战——如何将外向边界条件直接纳入问题表述，并对色散吸收介质中的非厄米谱问题进行可靠的稳定性评估。

Method: 采用超曲面方法（hyperboloidal approach）将外向边界条件直接嵌入光学共振问题的表述；通过辅助场公式（auxiliary-field formulation）用Lorentz模型介电常数描述色散吸收介质；利用Chebyshev谱离散化（Chebyshev spectral discretization）求解非厄米谱问题；并将伪谱分析（pseudospectrum analysis）扩展至光学场景，比较不同标量积（scalar product）选择对稳定性评估的影响。

Result: 伪谱分析提供了仅靠准正规模（QNM）谱无法获得的共振敏感性信息；谱稳定性（spectral stability）的评估与测量扰动时所采用的函数设定（functional setting）密切相关；不同标量积选择会导致稳定性性质差异。

Conclusion: 将超曲面方法与伪谱分析相结合，可在计算光学准正规模（QNMs）的同时研究其谱稳定性。该方法将引力物理中用于共振问题的技术扩展至色散电磁学，为研究开放光学系统的稳定性和敏感性奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a geometric--spectral framework for the computation and stability analysis of quasi-normal modes (QNMs) in open optical cavities of compact support. The hyperboloidal approach, transferred from gravitational physics to electromagnetism [1], incorporates outgoing boundary conditions directly into the formulation of the optical resonance problem. Dispersive and absorbing media are described by a Lorentz-model permittivity through an auxiliary-field formulation, and the resulting non-Hermitian spectral problem is solved using Chebyshev spectral discretization.Pseudospectrum analysis is then extended to the optical setting following [2] and used to study the stability of optical resonances under perturbations. The pseudospectrum provides information on the sensitivity of the resonances that is not contained in the QNM spectrum alone. Particular attention is given to the role of the norm used to define the pseudospectrum, by comparing the stability properties obtained with different choices of scalar product. The results show that the assessment of spectral stability is closely related to the functional setting in which perturbations are measured. Combining the hyperboloidal formulation with pseudospectrum analysis therefore makes it possible to compute optical QNMs while also studying their spectral stability. The approach extends techniques developed for resonance problems in gravitational physics to dispersive electromagnetism and provides a basis for studying stability and sensitivity in open optical systems.

</details>


### [117] [A generalized Kirchhoff's law of thermal radiation for Floquet media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24985)
*Sander A. Mann,Dimitrios L. Sounas,Andrea Alù*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文针对线性时变Floquet介质，推导了一个广义基尔霍夫定律（generalized Kirchhoff's law），将给定频率的发射率（emissivity）与伴随系统的谐波分辨吸收率（harmonic-resolved absorptivity）的加权和联系起来。该定律揭示了静态介质中无法实现的热辐射机制，并识别出在相同频率下对所有方向均表现出强发射而吸收可忽略的结构，从而近乎最大程度地违反了传统基尔霍夫定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时变介质中，时间调制破坏了时间平移不变性（time-translation invariance），导致频率转换并与调制驱动发生能量交换，因此吸收率与发射率之间传统的同频关系不再成立。现有基尔霍夫定律仅适用于线性和静态或非互易介质，缺乏针对时变介质的通用理论框架。

Method: 论文通过理论推导，建立了线性时变Floquet介质中发射率与伴随系统（adjoint system）的谐波分辨吸收率之间的加权求和关系。权重项考虑了热占据（thermal occupation）和光子通量转换（photon-flux conversion）因素。

Result: 推导出的广义基尔霍夫定律表明：给定频率下的发射率等于伴随系统各谐波分辨吸收率的加权和。在此基础上，论文识别出某些时变结构在相同频率下对所有方向均表现出强发射而吸收几乎为零，实现了对传统基尔霍夫定律形式的近乎最大违反。

Conclusion: 该广义定律具有实践与理论双重意义：实践上，允许从吸收率计算发射率，简化时变热辐射体的设计；理论上，揭示了静态介质中不可达的热辐射新机制，拓展了对热辐射基本规律的理解。

Abstract: Kirchhoff's law fundamentally relates thermal emission to absorption. For linear, static, reciprocal media, it equates the emissivity and absorptivity for each direction and frequency, while in nonreciprocal systems emission and absorption are equal when the bias is time-reversed. In time-varying media, however, temporal modulation breaks time-translation invariance, converts frequencies, and enables energy exchange with the modulation drive. As a result, a same-frequency relation between absorptivity and emissivity can no longer be expected. Here, we derive a generalized Kirchhoff's law for linear time-varying Floquet media. We show that the emissivity at a given frequency equals a weighted sum of harmonic-resolved absorptivities of the adjoint system, with weights accounting for thermal occupation and photon-flux conversion. This relation has both practical and fundamental consequences. In practical terms, it allows emissivity to be calculated from absorption, simplifying the design of time-varying thermal emitters. More fundamentally, it reveals thermal radiation regimes inaccessible in static media. In particular, we identify time-varying structures that exhibit strong emission with negligible absorption at the same frequency for all directions, yielding a near-maximal violation of the conventional form of Kirchhoff's law.

</details>


### [118] [Time-Reversal Invariant Topological Photonic Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25769)
*Feng Ruili,Qu Tiantao,Wu Xianbin,Shi Xiaoxuan,He Mujun,Zhang Lei,Chen Jun*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文证明全局反演对称性破缺并非拓扑相的必要条件，局部最小对称性破缺即可诱导量子自旋霍尔（QSH）与量子谷霍尔（QVH）效应，并基于光子合金（photonic alloy）平台通过随机掺杂无序实现拓扑带隙的灵活调控，展示了热力学极限下阈值掺杂浓度趋于零的尺寸依赖标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖全局空间反演对称性破缺来生成量子自旋霍尔与量子谷霍尔效应，导致带隙调控需要整体修改晶格结构参数，严重限制了器件的可重构性与后制备适应性。本文旨在探索局部最小对称性破缺是否足以诱导拓扑相，以克服上述瓶颈。

Method: 采用基于平行板波导（parallel-plate waveguide）的光子合金平台，利用随机替代无序（random substitutional disorder）实现局部的z方向镜面对称破缺和面内对称破缺，分别触发QSH与QVH相变。通过反射相位卷绕方法（reflection-phase winding method）进行严格的拓扑表征。

Result: 局部对称性破缺足以打开拓扑带隙并支持稳健的螺旋边缘态（helical edge states）与谷扭结态（valley kink states）。两种构型均呈现显著的尺寸依赖标度行为，在热力学极限下触发拓扑相变的阈值掺杂浓度渐近趋于零。

Conclusion: 该光子合金平台仅通过随机掺杂调控即可实现按需的体带隙工程，无需全局结构重构，为集成拓扑光通信提供了一种可扩展、高效的设计策略，并深化了对无序诱导拓扑物理的理解。

Abstract: Conventional approaches generate quantum spin Hall (QSH) and quantum valley Hall (QVH) effects by breaking spatial inversion symmetry across the entire photonic crystal. Consequently, modulating the bulk transmission gaps within these periodic frameworks typically requires global modification of structural parameters throughout the lattice, severely limiting device reconfigurability and post-fabrication adaptability. In this work, we demonstrate that global inversion symmetry breaking is not a prerequisite for inducing topological phases. Instead, local and minimal symmetry breaking is sufficient. Using a photonic alloy platform based on random substitutional disorder in a parallel-plate waveguide, we show that a disorder-driven local breaking of z-direction mirror symmetry and a local breaking of in-plane symmetry are capable of opening topological gaps to trigger QSH and QVH phase transitions, respectively, thereby supporting robust helical edge states and valley kink states. Rigorous topological characterization via the reflection-phase winding method reveals a striking size-dependent scaling behavior in both configurations, where the threshold doping concentration required to trigger the topological phase transition asymptotically vanishes in the thermodynamic limit. By enabling flexible, on-demand bulk gap engineering simply through random doping tuning rather than global structural reconfigurations, this photonic alloy platform not only deepens understanding of disorder induced topological physics but also offers a scalable, highly efficient design strategy for integrated topological optical communications.

</details>
