### [119] [TempLat: a versatile C++ engine for lattice field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24908)
*Adrien Florio,Franz R. Sattler*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TempLat——一个基于C++的格点场论（lattice field theory）模拟框架，通过符号语言和Kokkos后端实现高性能与跨平台可移植性（支持CPU、NVIDIA/AMD GPU及未来架构），并配套发布并行离散傅里叶变换库ParaFaFT；两者已开源并驱动CosmoLattice的GPU版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有格点场论模拟框架在跨架构（CPU/GPU）性能可移植性上存在不足，且缺乏一种既能保持数学表达式直观性又能编译为高性能内核的符号语言。

Method: 基于表达式模板（expression templates）构建符号语言，将数学表达式近乎逐字翻译为C++代码并编译为优化内核；借助Kokkos实现性能可移植性（performance portability），将硬件抽象为设备概念以支持未来架构扩展。

Result: 在CPU和GPU上均展现出优异的强扩展性（strong scaling）与弱扩展性（weak scaling）；ParaFaFT库在所有支持硬件上实现了良好的并行缩放性能。

Conclusion: TempLat和ParaFaFT均为开源项目，已应用于CosmoLattice的GPU版本，使其能够支持NVIDIA和AMD GPU上的早期宇宙模拟。

Abstract: We present TempLat, a C++ framework for lattice field theory simulations in arbitrary dimensions. Its symbolic language, built on expression templates, translates mathematical expressions almost verbatim into C++ while compiling to highly optimized kernels, in contexts ranging from classical-statistical to Monte Carlo simulations. TempLat is performance-portable: building on Kokkos, it supports large CPU clusters, as well as NVIDIA and AMD GPUs. Its abstraction of hardware into a device concept makes TempLat extensible to future architectures. We demonstrate excellent strong and weak scaling on both CPUs and GPUs. We also present ParaFaFT, a standalone parallel discrete Fourier transform library supporting arbitrary dimensions on all of the above hardware, and benchmark its scaling. Both are open source and available on GitHub, and power a new release of CosmoLattice, a widely used early-universe simulation library now available on GPUs.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Wald-like entropy and Islands in Dimensionally Reduced Einstein-Hilbert Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24883)
*Krishna Jalan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在降维爱因斯坦-希尔伯特（DREH）模型中，利用岛规则研究渐近平直永恒黑洞和准静态蒸发黑洞的岛公式和Page曲线；通过提供广义熵的洛伦兹方案（无需副本技巧），证明了广义熵与DREH-PL作用量的Wald型诺特荷一致，并据此确定了量子极值面、Page时间和Page曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在渐近平直黑洞的降维引力模型中，研究者希望在不依赖非引力浴区（bath region）的情况下，直接在黑洞时空自身中研究霍金辐射的精细熵及其Page曲线，并避免使用欧几里得副本技巧（replica trick）来计算广义熵。

Method: 基于四维爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用的s波近似导出二维DREH模型，并引入Polyakov-Liouville（PL）作用量纳入量子修正；采用岛规则（island rule）和洛伦兹方法计算广义熵，证明其等价于DREH-PL联合作用量的Wald型诺特荷（Wald-like Noether charge），从而确定量子极值面（quantum extremal surface）。

Result: 给出了永恒黑洞和蒸发黑洞的广义熵、Page时间及Page曲线；广义熵不再依赖副本技巧，而是由Wald型诺特荷直接导出。

Conclusion: 本文的洛伦兹方案有效补充了欧几里得路径积分方法；该框架有望扩展至全四维几何，并纳入真空极化修正。

Abstract: We study the island formula and Page curve for the asymptotically flat eternal and quasi-stationary evaporating black hole solutions within the dimensionally reduced Einstein-Hilbert (DREH) model. In this model, the four-dimensional Einstein-Hilbert action reduces to a two-dimensional dilaton gravity, on top of which the quantum corrections can be incorporated via the Polyakov-Liouville (PL) action for a two-dimensional conformal field theory with a large central charge $c$. This gravity model arises from the s-wave approximation of four-dimensional Einstein-Hilbert gravity and provides a fully gravitational setting in which we study the islands, i.e., we will not require a non-gravitational bath region to collect the radiation, instead it lives in the black hole spacetime itself. The fine-grained entropy of Hawking radiation in the eternal and evaporating black holes within the DREH model was derived using the island rule in \cite{djordjevic2022eternal, djordevic2025evaporating}. The island rule is based on the replica method using the Euclidean gravitational path integral. In this work, we complement the Euclidean approach by providing a Lorentzian prescription for the generalized entropy $S_{\text{gen}}$, in the island formula without invoking the replica trick to compute $S_{\text{gen}}$. The generalized entropy is shown to coincide with Wald-like Noether charge of the combined DREH-PL action. Using this generalized entropy, we determine the quantum extremal surface, the Page time, and the Page curve for the eternal and the evaporating black hole. We conclude with a discussion of the possible extension to the full four-dimensional geometry incorporating vacuum polarization corrections.

</details>


### [121] [OPE = QNM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24909)
*Paolo Arnaudo,Cristoforo Iossa,Robin Karlsson,Benjamin Withers*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了大N共形场论（CFT）中热两 point 关联函数的短距离热OPE表示与长距离准正则模（QNM）表示在复时间平面上存在收敛重叠区域，从而建立了OPE与QNM数据之间的映射关系，并提出了热自举（thermal bootstrap）程序框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微观尺度下，大N CFT的热态线性响应由热算子乘积展开（thermal OPE）支配；而在大尺度下，响应则由准正则模（quasinormal modes）这样的集体激发支配。两种描述之间存在鸿沟，本文旨在建立OPE与QNM数据之间的精确对应关系，并探索自洽约束机制。

Method: 在混合时间和空间动量坐标中，分析延迟关联函数（retarded correlator）的OPE表示与QNM表示在复时间平面上的收敛区域。推导Mellin空间中的求和规则（sum rules），并结合光锥OPE（lightcone OPE）分析大空间动量极限下的应力张量关联函数热化行为。

Result: 1. OPE和QNM表示在复时间平面上存在重叠收敛区域，建立了二者之间的映射；2. 大泛音（large-overtone）QNM渐近行为与OPE奇异性相关，低泛音QNM数据则出现在从小时间到大时间的解析延拓中；3. 利用该映射获得了QNM渐近行为的新解析结果，并从OPE数据数值获得了Schwarzschild-AdS$_5$黑膜的低泛音QNM；4. 推导了Mellin空间中的求和规则，建立了OPE谱与QNM数据之间的内在联系；5. 基于光锥OPE分析发现，随着共形对撞机界（conformal collider bounds）趋近饱和，大空间动量下的应力张量关联函数热化变慢。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新的热自举（thermal bootstrap）程序，其中OPE与QNM数据相互约束。该工作为热态共形场论中短距离与长距离描述的统一提供了理论框架。

Abstract: At microscopic scales the linear response of thermal states of large-$N$ CFTs is governed by a thermal operator product expansion (OPE), while at large scales response is governed by collective excitations known as quasinormal modes (QNM). We show that the OPE and QNM representations of the retarded correlator in mixed time and spatial momentum coordinates have an overlapping region of convergence in the complex time plane, giving a map between OPE and QNM data. We show that large-overtone QNM asymptotics are related to OPE singularities, while low-overtone QNM data appear in analytic continuation from short to large times. Using this approach we obtain new analytic results for QNM asymptotics, and numerically obtain low-overtone QNMs from OPE data for the Schwarzschild-AdS$_5$ black brane. We further show that the OPE spectrum is intimately related to QNM data through a set of sum rules which we derive in Mellin space. Finally, using the lightcone OPE, we argue that stress tensor correlators at large spatial momentum thermalise slower as the conformal collider bounds approach saturation. This work points to a new thermal bootstrap programme where OPE and QNM data constrain each other.

</details>


### [122] [Neural Spectral Bias and Conformal Correlators II: Modular and Annulus Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24913)
*Kausik Ghosh,Sidhaarth Kumar,Vasilis Niarchos,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个神经网络自举框架（neural network bootstrap framework），基于模不变性（modular invariance）和Cardy条件（Cardy condition），将其重整为四点关联函数的交叉方程（crossing equations），用于重构二维共形场论（CFT）的配分函数（partition functions）。通过轻量前馈神经网络（feed-forward neural networks）参数化关联函数和配分函数，利用懒训练机制（lazy training regime）中的频谱偏差（spectral bias）选择交叉对称构型，实现了从稀疏数据高精度重构完整配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在从稀疏数据（如能隙和锚点数据）重构二维CFT完整配分函数时面临欠定问题（under-determined problem），缺乏统一且高精度的求解框架。本文旨在将标准模不变性和环形约束（annulus constraints）统一到锚定自举形式（anchored-bootstrap form）中，并借助神经网络解决该重构问题。

Method: 首先将环面（torus）配分函数的模S不变性（modular S-invariance）通过对称轨道描述（symmetric-orbifold description）中的扭场表示（twist-field representation）映射为四点交叉，聚焦于一条线上四个插入点的对角运动学（diagonal kinematics）。对于环形（annulus）配分函数，将开/闭通道对偶性（open/closed channel duality）表述为界面CFT中缺陷变更算子（defect-changing operators）混合四点函数的交叉对称性。重构问题被表述为锚定自举形式，以交叉约束配合最小谱输入（能隙和锚点数据）求解。采用轻量前馈神经网络参数化关联函数和配分函数，利用懒训练机制下的频谱偏差自动选择交叉对称构型。

Result: 该框架统一了二维CFT中标准模约束和环形约束与CFT关联函数的锚定神经方法（anchored neural approach），能够从稀疏数据以显著精度重构完整配分函数。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种新的神经网络自举方法，有效解决了二维CFT配分函数的欠定重构问题，展示了神经网络在共形场论自举问题中的潜力。

Abstract: We develop a neural network bootstrap framework for reconstructing partition functions of two-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs) based on modular invariance and the Cardy condition, which are recast as crossing equations for four-point correlators. For torus partition functions, we use the twist-field representation in the symmetric-orbifold description to map modular S-invariance to four-point crossing and focus on the diagonal kinematics of four insertions on a line. For annulus partition functions, we formulate open/closed channel duality as crossing symmetry for mixed four-point functions of defect-changing operators in interface CFT. In both cases, the reconstruction problem is formulated in the anchored-bootstrap form, where the crossing constraints are supplemented by minimal spectral input (a gap) and anchor data. We solve this under-determined problem by using lightweight feed-forward neural networks to parametrise the correlators and their corresponding partition functions. A key ingredient of this approach is the spectral bias of the neural networks in the lazy training regime, which selects specific crossing-symmetric configurations. This reformulation unifies standard modular and annulus constraints in two dimensions with the anchored neural approach for CFT correlators, providing a new way to reconstruct full partition functions from sparse data with remarkable accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [Kaluza-Klein Perturbation Theory from Exceptional Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24914)
*Camille Eloy,Olaf Hohm,Camilla Lavino,Henning Samtleben,Yehudi Simon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文发展了十维和十一维超引力（supergravity）在广义卡鲁扎-克莱因（Kaluza-Klein）背景（包括AdS黑洞等）上的微扰理论，利用E_{6(6)}例外场论（exceptional field theory）与广义Scherk-Schwarz ansatz，推导了线性化广义微分同胚下规范不变的涨落场方程，并基于同伦转移（homotopy transfer）机制实现了物理与非物理模式的全阶分离，最后以Kerr-Newman AdS$_5$黑洞为例分析了IIB型超引力的卡鲁扎-克莱因谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展高维超引力在复杂卡鲁扎-克莱因背景（包括AdS几何与黑洞时空）上的微扰理论，以系统处理涨落场方程、规范不变性以及希格斯机制（Higgs mechanism）等关键问题，进而为超弦理论中的谱计算与黑洞物理提供严格的微扰框架。

Method: 采用E_{6(6)}例外场论作为统一描述框架，通过广义Scherk-Schwarz ansatz刻画背景流形；利用同伦转移方法将涨落场在微扰论各阶分离为规范不变的物理模式与纯规范的非物理模式，并推导线性化广义微分同胚下规范不变的场方程。

Result: 给出了所有场（包括自旋-2）在希格斯机制下的具体细节；对于高形式规范场（higher-form gauge field）为零的背景实现了模式分离；并以Kerr-Newman AdS$_5$黑洞为例，分析了IIB型超引力在近极端极限下的部分卡鲁扎-克莱因谱。

Conclusion: 该工作为处理包含高形式规范场的一般Kaluza-Klein背景提供了系统化的微扰论工具，并为AdS黑洞等几何中超引力谱的完整计算奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: We develop the perturbation theory of ten and eleven-dimensional supergravities on a large class of Kaluza-Klein backgrounds including familiar AdS examples such as AdS$_5\times S^5$, but also more general manifolds such as black hole geometries. Employing ${\rm E}_{6(6)}$ exceptional field theory with the backgrounds characterized by a generalized Scherk-Schwarz ansatz, we determine the first order field equations for the fluctuations that are gauge invariant under linearized generalized diffeomorphisms. We then present the details of the Higgs mechanism for all fields including spin-2 for the subset of backgrounds in which higher-form gauge fields vanish. We use a recently established machinery based on homotopy transfer that allows one to separate the fields into gauge invariant physical modes and pure gauge unphysical modes to all orders in perturbation theory. Finally, as a first application for backgrounds with higher-form gauge fields switched on, we analyze part of the spectrum of ten-dimensional Kaluza-Klein modes of type IIB supergravity around a Kerr-Newman AdS$_5$ black hole that in the near horizon limit becomes a fibered product of AdS$_2$ and a squashed three-sphere.

</details>


### [124] [The Tachyon Chern-Simons action with a generic tachyon field, and baryons in V-QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24915)
*Jean-Loup Raymond,Matti Järvinen,Elias Kiritsis,Francesco Nitti,Edwan Préau*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息V-QCD模型中，利用超联络（superconnection）形式主义，显式构造了味空间任意复矩阵情况下的Tachyon-Chern-Simons项，并证明重子数等于拓扑瞬子数（topological instanton number），有效四维π介子作用量可还原包含Skyrme项和Wess-Zumino-Witten项的手征拉格朗日量（chiral Lagrangian）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局味反常（global flavor anomalies）的一致性要求QCD全息模型中必须包含Chern-Simons项，因此需要系统分析此类项在全息V-QCD模型中的具体形式与物理后果。

Method: 采用超联络形式主义，在味空间任意复矩阵的一般情形下显式构造Tachyon-Chern-Simons项；利用得到的表达式分析非零夸克质量背景下的重子解结构，并推导有效四维作用量。

Result: 给出了重子数流（baryon number current）和总重子数的表达式，证明重子数等于重子解的拓扑瞬子数；有效四维π介子作用量可重现包含Skyrme项和Wess-Zumino-Witten项的手征拉格朗日量。

Conclusion: 全息V-QCD模型中Tachyon-Chern-Simons项的构造为研究不同味质量夸克背景下的重子结构提供了理论基础，且模型在低能有效描述上与手征微扰论（chiral perturbation theory）自洽。

Abstract: Consistency with global flavor anomalies requires the presence of Chern-Simons terms in holographic models of QCD. Such terms are analyzed in a setup arising in the holographic V-QCD model, where chiral symmetry breaking is implemented through the condensation of a complex scalar field, the tachyon. Using the superconnection formalism, the Tachyon-Chern-Simons terms are constructed explicitly in the general case, where the tachyon is any complex matrix in flavor space. This general case covers, among other things, backgrounds where different quark flavors have different masses. These new results are used to analyze the structure of the baryon solutions in the presence of nonzero quark masses. Expressions for the baryon number current and the total baryon number are found, and the baryon number is shown to be equal to the topological instanton number of the baryon solution. The effective four-dimensional pion action is analyzed and is shown to reproduce the chiral Lagrangian, including the Skyrme and Wess-Zumino-Witten terms.

</details>


### [125] [Carrollian bosonic supergravity at order $α'$ and the universal cancellation of higher-curvature divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24916)
*Eric Lescano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了包含α'修正的四导数项后，玻色子超引力（bosonic supergravity）的卡罗尔极限（Carrollian limit）依然保持有限，并显式构造了有效作用量。同时建立了判断高曲率项贡献有限性的普适判据，并应用于α'²和α'³阶纯引力修正，证明ζ(3)项在α'³阶不贡献于卡罗尔玻色子超引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究卡罗尔极限下高阶导数修正（包括α'修正）的有限性，以理解弦论（string theory）在超光速（ultrarelativistic）极限下的行为。

Method: 通过构造包含四导数项的有效作用量，并建立基于黎曼张量（Riemann tensor）幂次的普适判据，判断高曲率修正的卡罗尔极限是否有限。

Result: 证明了包含α'修正的四导数项后玻色子超引力的卡罗尔极限有限；建立了高曲率项有限性的普适判据；证明α'²和α'³阶纯引力修正存在有限卡罗尔极限，并给出了显式贡献；发现ζ(3)项在α'³阶不贡献于卡罗尔玻色子超引力。

Conclusion: 玻色子超引力在包含α'修正后的卡罗尔极限保持有限，所建立的判据可推广至更高阶曲率修正，为弦论在超光速极限下的研究提供了工具。

Abstract: We prove that the Carrollian limit of bosonic supergravity remains finite after including the four-derivative terms arising from the $α'$-corrections, and we explicitly construct the effective action. We also establish a universal criterion to determine the finiteness of higher-curvature contributions given by powers of the Riemann tensor, ${\rm Riem}_1 \cdots {\rm Riem}_N$, with $N>1$. As applications of this criterion, we prove that the purely gravitational $α'^2$- and $α'^3$-corrections admit a finite Carrollian limit, derive their explicit contributions to the action, and show that the terms proportional to $ζ(3)$ do not contribute to the Carrollian bosonic supergravity at order $α'^3$.

</details>


### [126] [Analytic thermal bootstrap in momentum space: From thermal OPE to QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24919)
*Julien Barrat,Deniz N. Bozkurt,Enrico Marchetto,Alessio Miscioscia,Elli Pomoni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“热启动程”（thermal bootstrap program），通过热OPE数据与红外可观测量（低频行为与准正则模）之间的对偶关系，推导出反演公式（inversion formulae），并利用准正则模频率表达OPE系数，从而获得求和规则（sum rules）以及对准正则谱及其在大空间动量下的渐近行为的约束。在自由理论、二维CFT、大N极限、O(N)模型的ε展开以及强耦合N=4 SYM的R-电流关联函数中验证了该框架，并作为副产品导出了重算子热OPE系数的普适渐近公式，在三维Ising CFT中与蒙特卡洛数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种将紫外数据（由热OPE编码）与红外可观测量（低频行为与准正则模）联系起来的自洽启动程（bootstrap program），从而从热OPE信息中提取准正则模的解析结构和谱约束。

Method: 从满足KMS对称性（KMS symmetry）的单个热OPE块（thermal OPE block）出发，构造热Polyakov块（thermal Polyakov blocks），通过傅里叶变换得到任意空间动量下推迟热关联函数（retarded thermal correlator）的渐近展开；在亚纯性（meromorphicity）假设下，推导反演公式将OPE系数表达为准正则模频率的函数。

Result: 得到了联系热OPE数据与复频率平面上关联函数解析结构的反演公式；推导出非平凡的求和规则及准正则谱的约束条件；在多个理论中验证了框架的有效性；导出了重算子热OPE系数的普适渐近公式，并在三维Ising CFT中与蒙特卡洛数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: 所构造的热启动程成功地将热OPE数据映射为准正则模信息，揭示了紫外与红外之间的深层对偶关系，并为重算子OPE系数提供了普适的渐近行为描述。

Abstract: We initiate a bootstrap program that relates ultraviolet data, encoded in the thermal OPE, to infrared observables, namely, the low-frequency behavior and quasinormal modes. Starting from KMS-symmetric completions of individual thermal OPE blocks, which play the role of thermal Polyakov blocks, we construct their Fourier transform, yielding an asymptotic expansion of retarded thermal correlators valid at any spatial momentum. We use these results to derive inversion formulae and connect thermal OPE data to the analytic structure of retarded correlators in the complex frequency plane. Under the assumption of meromorphicity, the inversion formulae express OPE coefficients in terms of the quasinormal-mode frequencies, leading to nontrivial sum rules, constraints on the quasinormal spectrum, and its asymptotics at large spatial momentum. We illustrate these results in free theories, two-dimensional CFTs, the large-$N$ limit and $\varepsilon$-expansion of the $\mathrm{O}(N)$ model, and the $R$-current correlator of strongly coupled $\mathcal N = 4$ SYM at zero spatial momentum. As a byproduct, we derive universal asymptotic formulae for thermal OPE coefficients of heavy operators, resolving their dependence on spin and extending previous results at zero spatial separation. We test these formulae in the three-dimensional Ising CFT, finding good agreement between the resulting truncated correlators and Monte Carlo data.

</details>


### [127] [Split Heun functions via blown-up surface defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24920)
*Saebyeok Jeong,Tommaso Pedroni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维 $\mathcal{N}=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ 规范理论在 Nekrasov–Shatashvili (NS) 极限下 Heun 方程及其合流极限的共振解，通过推导 blow-up 方程对 NS 函数进行重求和，揭示了共振点附近规范理论解的解析结构，包括附属参数的分支结构和谱隙边缘的周期/反周期解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Nekrasov–Shatashvili (NS) 极限下，四维 $\mathcal{N}=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ 规范理论中的瞬子展开在共振点 $2a/\hbar\in\mathbb{Z}$ 处出现极点，导致规范理论解的解析结构不清晰，特别是附属参数（accessory parameter）的分支结构和 Floquet 解的行为被逐项瞬子展开所掩盖，因此需要发展新的重求和方法来揭示这些结构。

Method: 本文推导了仅涉及 NS 函数（NS functions）的 blow-up 方程（blow-up equations），利用这些方程对体态（bulk）和表面缺陷（surface defect）NS 函数在共振点附近的奇异瞬子展开进行重求和（resummation）。所发展的重求和方法适用于 $N_f=(n_0,n_1)$ 理论，其中 $n_i\leq 2$ $(i=0,1)$，并以 $N_f=(1,1)$ 理论为例进行了显式演示。

Result: 重求和后的体态和表面缺陷 NS 函数揭示了共振点附近规范理论解的解析结构，包括附属参数的分支结构和 Floquet 解的分支结构。在共振点，重求和的附属参数（resummed accessory parameters）和适当归一化的缺陷波函数（defect wavefunctions）存在有限极限，描述了谱隙（spectral gaps）边缘的周期或反周期解，并可构造其对数伴随解（logarithmic companions）。此外，识别了控制能隙闭合和半单共振单值性（semisimple resonant monodromy）的不同嵌套质量轨迹（nested mass loci）：较大轨迹上带边附属参数合并，较小轨迹上两个独立的共振（反）周期 Floquet 解存活。

Conclusion: 本文建立的 blow-up 方程和重求和方法有效解决了 NS 极限下规范理论在共振点处的瞬子展开奇异性问题，完整揭示了共振点附近附属参数的分支结构以及谱隙边缘的周期/反周期解行为。该框架可推广至更广泛的 $N_f=(n_0,n_1)$ 理论族。

Abstract: We study resonant solutions of the Heun equation and its confluent limits that arise in the Nekrasov--Shatashvili (NS) limit of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ gauge theories with fundamental hypermultiplets. At the resonant loci $2a/\hbar\in\mathbb{Z}$ in the Coulomb branch parameter $a$, the Floquet multipliers coalesce and the instanton expansions of the bulk and surface defect NS functions develop poles of increasing order. We derive blow-up equations involving exclusively NS functions and use them to resum these singular expansions. The resulting resummed bulk and surface defect NS functions reveal the analytic structure of the gauge-theoretic solutions near the resonant loci, including the branch structure of the accessory parameter and of the Floquet solutions that is obscured by the term-by-term instanton expansion. At resonance, the resummed accessory parameters and suitably normalized defect wavefunctions admit finite limits that describe periodic or antiperiodic solutions at the edges of spectral gaps and allow us to construct their logarithmic companions. We then identify distinct nested mass loci governing gap closure and semisimple resonant monodromy. On the larger locus the band-edge accessory parameters coalesce, while on the smaller locus two independent resonant (anti)periodic Floquet solutions survive. We develop the general resummation procedure for $N_f=(n_0,n_1)$ theories with $n_i\leq 2$ $(i=0,1)$, and demonstrate it explicitly for the $N_f=(1,1)$ theory.

</details>


### [128] [Unitary Dual-Resonance S-matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24922)
*Claudia de Rham,Andrew J. Tolley,Zhuo-Hui Wang,Shuang-Yong Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构建完全幺正（unitary）的双共振振幅（dual-resonance amplitudes）的新方法，该振幅保留了交叉对称性（crossing symmetry）和Regge行为，但不同于传统弦论世界面构造，通过一种区别于标准eikonal化的机制实现了幺正性（unitarity），且所得S矩阵具有局域性（locality）和解析性（analyticity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的双共振模型在树图层次上未能实现真正的S矩阵幺正性（S-matrix unitarity），即其零宽度共振谱缺乏物理吸收性（absorptive spectrum）。本文旨在克服这一缺陷，构建完全幺正的双共振振幅。

Method: 将零宽度共振塔（zero-width resonance towers）提升为具有物理吸收谱的形式，同时保留其特色的交叉和Regge组织方式；通过一种有别于标准eikonal化（standard eikonalization）的机制实现振幅的幺正化。

Result: 所得到的S矩阵同时满足局域性和解析性，并且能够包含可控的第二片Regge轨迹（second-sheet Regge trajectories）以及显著的非弹性效应（inelasticity）。

Conclusion: 该构造为绘制一致性强耦合S矩阵（consistent strongly coupled S-matrices）的空间开辟了一条新路径。

Abstract: Dual-resonance models realize crossing symmetry, Regge behavior and infinitely many resonances in a compact analytic form but their canonical tree-level realizations fall short of genuine S-matrix unitarity. Here we explicitly construct fully unitary dual-resonance amplitudes distinct from the conventional worldsheet construction. The zero-width resonance towers are promoted into a physical absorptive spectrum that retains their characteristic crossing and Regge organization, while unitarizing the amplitude through a mechanism distinct from standard eikonalization. The resulting S-matrices are also local and analytic, and can contain controllable second-sheet Regge trajectories as well as substantial inelasticity. More broadly, our construction opens up a new route to charting the space of consistent strongly coupled S-matrices.

</details>


### [129] [Feynman Tree Theorem and the Gelfand--Yaglom Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24923)
*Ipak Fadakar,Guilherme L. Pimentel,Behrang Tafreshi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过费曼树定理（Feynman Tree Theorem）提供了Gelfand–Yaglom公式的新图解证明，将一环路行列式（one-loop determinant）重新表达为树图（tree diagrams）之和，并推广至一般边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gelfand–Yaglom公式将薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operator）在一维狄利克雷边界条件（Dirichlet boundary conditions）下的一环路行列式等价于一个初值问题（initial value problem）的解。Polyakov建议以费曼树定理为基础给出该公式的图解证明，本文实现这一思路。

Method: 应用费曼树定理对一环路行列式进行“切割”（cutting），将其重新表达为一系列树图之和。这些树图显式编码了Gelfand–Yaglom初值问题的解。

Result: 建立了Gelfand–Yaglom公式的新的图解证明，将一环路行列式表示为树图的和，并成功将该图解框架（diagrammatic framework）推广至一般边界条件（general boundary conditions）。

Conclusion: 本文为Gelfand–Yaglom公式提供了基于费曼树定理的图解证明，并拓展至一般边界条件；此外，对该方法在量子场论（quantum field theory）中的潜在推广给出了评论。

Abstract: The Gelfand--Yaglom formula equates the one-loop determinant of a Schrödinger operator with Dirichlet boundary conditions to the solution of an initial value problem. Following a suggestion by Polyakov, we provide a new diagrammatic proof of this formula as a direct application of the Feynman Tree Theorem. By cutting the one-loop determinant, we re-express it as a sum of tree diagrams. These trees explicitly encode the solution to the Gelfand--Yaglom initial value problem. We extend this diagrammatic framework to general boundary conditions and comment on a potential generalization to quantum field theory.

</details>


### [130] [Cross-Correlations of Metric and Monopole Perturbations from Holographic Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24989)
*Matheus Cravo,Juliana Z. Finotti,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用三维全息宇宙学（holographic cosmology）玩具模型，通过1-loop计算应力-能量张量与两个SO(3)整体流插入的三点函数⟨TJJ⟩，并结合Sl(2,ℤ)对偶映射，得到了度规扰动与 bulk 磁单极场之间的交叉关联非高斯性，并评估了有效非线性参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全息宇宙学中QFT n点函数与宇宙学关联函数之间的对应关系，尤其是通过Sl(2,ℤ)对偶将电Noether流映射为磁涡旋流（对应宇宙学磁单极），从而研究度规扰动与磁单极场之间的交叉关联非高斯性（non-Gaussianities），为全息宇宙学的实验检验提供新的可观测方向。

Method: 采用三维全息宇宙学玩具模型，进行1-loop计算三点函数⟨TJJ⟩；应用全息词典（holographic dictionary）将QFT n点函数映射为宇宙学关联函数；利用Sl(2,ℤ)对偶将电Noether流映射为磁涡旋流；通过挤压极限（squeezed limit, p₁→0）下的半局域接触项（semi-local contact terms）实现宇宙学关联函数的精确分解；最后评估有效非线性参数fNL。

Result: 标量-单极交叉关联在领头阶消失，即fNL^{ξÃÃ}=0；而张量-单极交叉关联产生非零的fNL^{γÃÃ}。这些结果符合非高斯关联函数预期的振幅层次结构（amplitude hierarchy）。

Conclusion: 研究结果遵循了非高斯关联函数预期的振幅层次，同时为全息宇宙学的实验检验指明了新的方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the 1-loop calculation of the three-point function $\langle TJJ \rangle$ of the stress-energy tensor and two insertions of $SO(3)$ global currents, using a 3d toy model for holographic cosmology. By applying the holographic dictionary that relates these QFT $n$-point functions to cosmological correlators, together with the $Sl(2,\mathbb{Z})$ duality that maps the electric Noether current to a magnetic vortex current dual to cosmological magnetic monopoles, we relate the $\langle TJJ \rangle$ correlator to the cross-correlations between metric perturbations and the bulk magnetic monopole field, specifically mapping to the non-Gaussianities $\langle ξ\tilde{A} \tilde{A} \rangle$ and $\langle γ\tilde{A} \tilde{A} \rangle$. We calculate the semi-local contact terms necessary to achieve the exact factorization of the cosmological correlators in the squeezed limit, $p_1 \to 0$. Finally, we evaluate the effective non-linear parameters, showing that the scalar-monopole cross-correlation vanishes at leading order, $f_{NL}^{ξ\tilde{A} \tilde{A}} = 0$, while the tensor-monopole cross-correlation yields a non-zero $f_{NL}^{γ\tilde A\tilde A}$. These results respect the expected amplitude hierarchy of the non-Gaussian correlators, while pointing at new directions in which holographic cosmology can be tested experimentally.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Gravity Corrections to Giant Graviton Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25054)
*Junding Chen,Xi-Er Du,Hynek Paul,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文计算了最大巨引力子与两个轻超引力子之间关联函数的领先阶量子引力修正，并利用AdS幺正性方法从已知的树级关联函数重构了单圈修正，得到了Mellin空间和位置空间的封闭表达式，同时提取了缺陷通道OPE中领头共形扭结算符的反常维度量子修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超引力极限下的N=4超杨-米尔斯理论（N=4 SYM theory）中，理解巨引力子（giant gravitons）的量子引力修正对于揭示全息对偶（holographic duality）中非微扰物体的量子行为具有重要意义。

Method: 将巨引力子视为零维缺陷（zero-dimensional defect），采用缺陷版本的AdS幺正性方法（AdS unitarity method），从已知的树级关联函数重构单圈修正，并在Mellin空间和位置空间（position space）中得到了封闭形式的结果。

Result: 得到了两个最大巨引力子与两个属于应力张量多重态（stress tensor multiplet）的轻超引力子之间关联函数的领先阶量子引力单圈修正，并在Mellin空间和位置空间中给出了封闭表达式。还提取了缺陷通道OPE中领头共形扭结（leading conformal twist）算符——描述巨引力子与轻超引力子束缚态——的反常维度（anomalous dimension）的量子修正。

Conclusion: 量子引力修正能够通过缺陷视角和AdS幺正性方法有效计算，所得结果为理解AdS/CFT对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）中非微扰物体（如巨引力子）的量子效应提供了新的精确数据。

Abstract: We compute the leading quantum gravity correction to the correlation function of two maximal giant gravitons and two light supergravitons in the stress tensor multiplet in the supergravity limit of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM theory. By viewing the giant gravitons as a zero-dimensional defect, we employ the defect version of the AdS unitarity method to reconstruct the one-loop correction from the known tree-level correlators. We obtain the one-loop result in closed form in both Mellin and position space. We also extract the quantum correction to the anomalous dimension of operators describing a bound state of a giant graviton and a light supergraviton at the leading conformal twist in the defect channel OPE.

</details>


### [132] [A discrete series gauge field at the late-time boundary of $dS_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25067)
*Manizheh Botshekananfard,Elif Büşra Güraksın,Vasileios A. Letsios,Gizem Şengör*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维德西特时空（$dS_4$）的平面片区域中，对自由麦克斯韦场（Maxwell field）进行了正则量子化与表示论分析，识别出两个晚期算符（$\Delta=1$ 和 $\Delta=2$），并展示了它们如何共同构成 $SO(4,1)$ 的酉离散级数表示（unitary discrete series representations），最后探讨了 $\Delta=1$ 算符与三维共形自旋-1规范场之间的可能联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $dS_4$ 中麦克斯韦场的量子化与表示论结构，特别关注其在晚期极限下的算符行为及其与 $SO(4,1)$ 离散级数表示的关系，为进一步理解德西特时空高自旋引力的微观描述提供理论基础。

Method: 通过体正则量子化（bulk canonical quantization）在 Bunch-Davies 真空中对麦克斯韦场进行量子化；采用表示论视角，分析单粒子态在无穷小 dS 变换下的变换性质；在晚期极限下识别晚期算符；引入 CFT 启发的 $SO(4,1)$ 不变内积；利用光子场强自对偶（self-dual）与反自对偶（anti-self-dual）扇区的 dS 不变性来分离螺旋度（helicities）$+1$ 和 $-1$ 的表示。

Result: 识别出两个晚期算符，其标度维度（scaling dimensions）分别为 $\Delta=1$（主导项）和 $\Delta=2$（次主导项）；两个算符共同构成了 $SO(4,1)$ 的光子相关酉离散级数表示，这与 AdS 情形不同（$\Delta=1$ 算符在 AdS 中对应非可归一化模）；该离散级数表示可分解为两个螺旋度 $+1$ 和 $-1$ 的表示的直和。

Conclusion: $dS_4$ 中麦克斯韦场的晚期算符提供了 $SO(4,1)$ 酉离散级数表示的另一种实现方式，其 $\Delta=1$ 晚期算符可能与三维共形自旋-1规范场存在关联，后者在 $dS_4$ 高自旋引力的微观描述中扮演核心角色。

Abstract: We study the free Maxwell field on the planar patch of four-dimensional de Sitter spacetime ($dS_4$). We review its bulk canonical quantization in the Bunch-Davies vacuum, and we give a representation-theoretic viewpoint by studying the transformation properties of single-particle states under infinitesimal dS transformations. By taking the late-time limit, we identify two late-time operators with scaling dimensions $Δ=1$ (leading) and $Δ=2$ (subleading). We introduce CFT-inspired inner products invariant under the dS group ($SO(4,1)$) for states created by late-time operators acting on the Bunch-Davies vacuum. We explain how the unitary discrete series representations of $SO(4,1)$ associated with the photon are furnished by both operators, in contrast with the Maxwell field on $AdS$ where the $Δ=1$ operator corresponds to a non-normalizable mode. We also explain how the corresponding discrete series representations of $SO(4,1)$ split into a direct sum of representations corresponding to two helicities $+1$ and $-1$. This is achieved by taking advantage of the dS invariance of the self-dual and anti-self-dual sectors of the photon field strength. In our outlook, we draw inspiration from a recent proposal for the microscopic description of $dS_4$ higher-spin gravity where conformal gauge fields in 3 dimensions play a central role, and we investigate a possible connection of the $Δ=1$ late-time operator with a conformal spin-1 gauge field in 3 dimensions.

</details>


### [133] [Derivation of the NS5-brane limit of the plane wave matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25116)
*Yuhma Asano,Goro Ishiki,Shinji Shimasaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过规范/引力对偶性（gauge/gravity duality）预测了平面波矩阵模型（BMN矩阵模型）存在非平凡的双重缩放极限（double scaling limit），该极限描述了 $R\times S^5$ 上的 IIA 型小弦理论（little string theory, LST）。作者在规范理论侧证明了配分函数和矩阵模型的某个 1/4 BPS 扇区中确实存在该极限，并推导出一个本征值积分（eigenvalue integral），预计该积分描述了 LST 的 1/4 BPS 扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规范/引力对偶性（gauge/gravity duality）的预测，此前理论推测平面波矩阵模型（plane wave matrix model, BMN矩阵模型）存在一个非平凡的双重缩放极限（double scaling limit），该极限对应 $R\times S^5$ 上的 IIA 型小弦理论（little string theory, LST）。本文旨在从规范理论侧验证该极限的存在性，并推导出相应描述 LST 低能扇区的数学形式。

Method: 作者在规范理论侧（gauge theory side）直接分析 BMN 矩阵模型的配分函数（partition function）以及其中 1/4 BPS 扇区（1/4 BPS sector）的结构，证明了双重缩放极限在该框架下的存在性。随后通过推导得到一个本征值积分（eigenvalue integral），该积分被预期能够描述 LST 的 1/4 BPS 扇区。

Result: 本文成功证明了在 BMN 矩阵模型的配分函数与 1/4 BPS 扇区中，确实存在由规范/引力对偶性所预测的非平凡双重缩放极限，并推导出了相应的本征值积分形式。

Conclusion: 该本征值积分被预期用于描述 $R\times S^5$ 上的 IIA 型小弦理论（LST）的 1/4 BPS 扇区，从而为规范/引力对偶性提供了新的支持证据。

Abstract: From the gauge/gravity duality, it was predicted that there exists a nontrivial double scaling limit of the plane wave matrix model (the BMN matrix model), which describes the type IIA little string theory (LST) on $R\times S^5$. We show on the gauge theory side that such a limit indeed exists for the partition function and in a certain 1/4 BPS sector of the matrix model, and consequently derive an eigenvalue integral, which is expected to describe the 1/4 BPS sector of LST.

</details>


### [134] [Monodromy, Hidden Conformal Symmetry and Soft Hair in Kerr-MOG Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25205)
*Parthapratim Pradhan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）、单值性分析（monodromy analysis）和软毛形式体系（soft-hair formalism）三种独立方法，研究了四维Kerr-MOG黑洞与二维共形场论（2D CFT）之间的对偶对应关系，推导出左右移动温度与中心荷（central charge）的一致表达式，并利用隐藏共形对称性（hidden conformal symmetry）和Cardy公式（Cardy formula）成功复现了该黑洞的Bekenstein-Hawking熵，同时证实了低频率标量吸收截面与对偶CFT有限温度吸收截面的精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kerr/CFT全息对偶（Kerr/CFT holographic duality）推广至修改引力理论（modified gravity theory）框架下的Kerr-MOG黑洞，验证其是否存在类似的二维CFT描述。

Method: 采用三种互补方法——黑洞热力学、单值性分析和软毛形式体系——独立推导对偶CFT的左右温度(T_L, T_R)与中心荷；并通过分析无质量标量场在近区域（near-region）的动力学，揭示非极端（non-extremal）Kerr-MOG黑洞的隐藏共形对称性。

Result: 三种方法给出了对偶CFT的一致表达式；熵乘积（entropy product）既非普适亦非量子化（quantized）；近区域标量波方程展现出(SL(2,ℝ)_L × SL(2,ℝ)_R)共形对称性；Cardy公式计算的微观熵与Bekenstein-Hawking熵精确一致；低频率标量吸收截面（greybody factor）与对偶CFT的有限温度吸收截面精确匹配。

Conclusion: 提供了Kerr-MOG黑洞与二维CFT之间存在Kerr/CFT型全息对偶的有力证据，建立了四维Kerr-MOG时空与二维共形场论的一致性对应关系，且隐藏共形对称性以非平凡作用于黑洞视界上的Vir_L ⊗ Vir_R微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）形式实现。

Abstract: We investigate the two-dimensional conformal field theory (2D CFT) dual of the four-dimensional Kerr-MOG (Kerr-modified gravity) black hole using three complementary approaches: (i) black hole thermodynamics, (ii) monodromy analysis, and (iii) the soft-hair formalism. From these independent methods, we derive consistent expressions for the left- and right-moving temperatures and the central charge of the dual CFT. Finally, we derive the entropy product using these approaches. We show that the product is not universal as well as not quantized. We further explore the hidden conformal symmetry of the non-extremal Kerr-MOG black hole by analyzing the near-region dynamics of a massless scalar field. The resulting Kerr-MOG/CFT correspondence identifies the near-region geometry with a two-dimensional CFT characterized by the temperatures ($T_{L}, T_{R}$) derived in Sec. 3. Moreover, using the Cardy formula, we reproduce the microscopic entropy of the dual CFT and find exact agreement with the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy of the Kerr-MOG black hole. We also show that the low-frequency scalar absorption cross section~(greybody factor) in the near-region Kerr-MOG geometry precisely matches the finite-temperature absorption cross section of the dual 2D CFT. In addition, the near-region scalar wave equation exhibits a hidden $(SL(2,\mathbb{R})_L \times SL(2,\mathbb{R})_R)$ conformal symmetry. These independent results provide strong evidence for the existence of a Kerr/CFT-type holographic duality for the Kerr-MOG black hole, establishing a consistent correspondence between the four-dimensional Kerr-MOG spacetime and a two-dimensional conformal field theory. Furthermore, the hidden conformal symmetry is realized in the form of $\rm {Vir_L} \otimes \rm{ Vir_R}$ diffeomorphisms which act non-trivially on the black hole horizon.

</details>


### [135] [The quantum electrodynamics for dyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25226)
*D. O. R. Azevedo,O. M. Del Cima,T. S. Dias,E. D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于1960年代Cabibbo、Ferrari和Salam的开创性工作，提出了携带电、磁荷的费米子（dyon）的量子电动力学（dQED），其规范群为U(1)×U(1)，对应一个矢量场（光子）和一个赝矢量场（metaphoton）。研究从树图层次分析了模型的谱一致性并建立了所有连续和离散对称性；在量子层次上，利用BRS代数重整化方法（独立于任何正规化方案）并在BPHZL重整化框架下采用Lowenstein-Zimmermann（LZ）减除方案，证明了该模型在所有微扰论阶次下无反常且可乘性重整化，从而验证了其量子一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建携带电荷与磁荷的费米子（dyon）的量子电动力学理论，解决Dirac量子化条件在此类模型中的适用性问题，并验证该理论的量子自洽性。

Method: 采用Becchi-Rouet-Stora (BRS) 代数重整化方法进行量子分析，该方法不依赖任何正规化方案；在Bogoliubov-Parasiuk-Hepp-Zimmermann-Lowenstein (BPHZL) 重整化框架下，因存在无质量规范场，应用Lowenstein-Zimmermann (LZ) 减除方案。

Result: 在树图层次建立了模型的谱一致性，明确了所有连续和离散对称性；证明了模型在所有微扰论阶次下无反常（anomaly）且可乘性重整化（multiplicative renormalizable）。

Conclusion: 所提出的dyon量子电动力学（dQED）模型在量子层次上是自洽的，具有完全的量子一致性。

Abstract: The quantum electrodynamics for massive fermions carrying electric and magnetic charges, the dyons, is proposed based on the 1960's seminal works by Cabibbo, Ferrari, and Salam, with the gauge group being $U(1)\times U(1)$, which is associated to a vector field (photon) and a pseudo-vector field (metaphoton), wherein the Dirac quantization is set aside. At the tree level the spectrum consistency of the model is analyzed, and all continuous and discrete symmetries are established. The quantum analysis is performed by using the Becchi-Rouet-Stora (BRS) algebraic renormalization method, which is independent on any regularization scheme. Moreover, thanks to the presence of massless gauge fields, the Lowenstein-Zimmermann (LZ) subtraction scheme is required within the framework of the Bogoliubov-Parasiuk-Hepp-Zimmermann-Lowenstein (BPHZL) renormalization procedure. Finally, it is verified that the proposed model, the dyon quantum electrodynamics (dQED), is free from any anomaly and is multiplicative renormalizable at all orders in perturbation theory, proving, therefore, its quantum consistency.

</details>


### [136] [String theory mathematics and matrix data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25500)
*Sanjaye Ramgoolam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文综述了置换不变高斯矩阵模型（Permutation Invariant Gaussian Matrix Models, PIGMM），该模型用有限置换对称性 $S_N$ 替代传统随机矩阵理论中的连续对称性，将高维矩阵数据分析简化为仅含13个参数的近对角形式，并成功应用于计算语言学、统计金融学和神经网络权重的数据降维与异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受量子场论和弦理论中矩阵技术的启发，旨在解决传统随机矩阵理论（Random Matrix Theory）中连续对称性假设不适用于某些实际数据的局限，通过引入有限置换对称性 $S_N$ 来减少高维矩阵数据分析的复杂度。

Method: 利用 $S_N$ 的表示论（representation theory）将高度相关的二次矩阵作用量（quadratic matrix action）转化为近对角形式，仅需13个参数；不变可观测量（invariant observables）由图（graph）参数化，并通过Wick收缩（Wick contractions）算法化计算其期望值。

Result: PIGMM在计算语言学、统计金融学和神经网络权重分析中成功实现了数据降维（data reduction）和异常检测（anomaly detection）。

Conclusion: 论文指出PIGMM未来可能应用于强子化算法（hadronization algorithms）和碰撞物理数据的模块化结构（modular structure）等矩阵数据分析任务。

Abstract: Inspired by matrix techniques in quantum field theory and string theory, we review Permutation Invariant Gaussian Matrix Models (PIGMM), which replace the continuous symmetries of traditional Random Matrix Theory, for $ N \times N$ matrices, with finite permutation symmetry, $S_N$. This symmetry-driven approach reduces highly multivariate $N^2$-variable matrix data analysis problems to a rich but tractable space of parameters. The representation theory of $S_N$ brings a highly correlated quadratic matrix action to a near-diagonal form with $13$ parameters. The invariant observables are parameterised by graphs and their expectation values are computed with Wick contractions implemented algorithmically. We review the successful application of PIGMM for data reduction and anomaly detection in computational linguistics, statistical finance and neural network weights. We conclude with a brief discussion of potential future applications to matrix data analysis tasks that exploit hadronization algorithms and the modular structure of collider-physics data.

</details>


### [137] [Toward the Structure Constants of $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25662)
*Beatrix Mühlmann,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文通过镜像对称性（mirror symmetry）将 $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville 理论与 $\mathrm{SL}(2)_k/\mathrm{U}(1)$ 超陪集（supercoset）对偶，进而推导出其二球面（two-sphere）上的结构常数（structure constants），并给出了半经典分析验证及手征环（chiral rings）关联函数计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解析自举方法依赖 $b \leftrightarrow b^{-1}$ 自对偶对称性（self-dual symmetry），而 $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville 理论缺乏该对称性，导致传统方法无法直接适用。因此需要借助镜像对偶关系，从超陪集侧获取结构常数。

Method: 利用 $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville 理论与 $\mathrm{SL}(2)_k/\mathrm{U}(1)$ 超陪集之间的镜像对称性（mirror symmetry），将问题映射到具有 $2$ 维费米子黑洞（$2$d fermionic black hole）靶空间的超陪集侧，推导绕数保持（winding number preserving）与绕数破坏（winding number violating）结构常数的显式表达式。

Result: 在超陪集侧获得了绕数保持与绕数破坏结构常数的显式解析表达式；在 Liouville 侧通过半经典分析（semiclassical analysis）进行检验，发现两者在单圈阶（one-loop order）一致。

Conclusion: 镜像对称性为缺乏自对偶对称性的 $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville 理论提供了可行的求解途径；所得结构常数通过了半经典检验，并且该理论的手征环及 $\frac 12$-BPS 算符（$\frac 12$-BPS operators）关联函数亦可在此框架下得到处理。

Abstract: We discuss the structure constants of spacelike $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville theory on the two-sphere. Due to the absence of a particular $b \leftrightarrow b^{-1}$ self-dual symmetry, where $b$ is the theory's coupling, the standard analytic bootstrap toolkit that was used to solve the bosonic and $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories cannot be implemented in a straightforward way. Our approach, instead, relies on the fact that $\mathcal{N}=2$ Liouville theory is dual by mirror symmetry to the $\mathrm{SL}(2)_k/\mathrm{U}(1)$ supercoset, whose target space is the $2$d fermionic black hole. Leveraging this duality, we obtain explicit expressions for the winding number preserving and violating structure constants on the supercoset side, and test them on the Liouville side through a semiclassical analysis finding agreement up to one-loop order. We also discuss the chiral rings of the theory and evaluate correlators of $\frac 12$-BPS operators.

</details>


### [138] [The coupling flow for supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25714)
*Federico Arrighi,Saurish Khandelwal,Olaf Lechtenfeld*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于超场论中量子关联函数与Nicolai映射的关系，研究四维庞加莱超引力（Poincaré supergravity）中引力常数κ的流方程。通过超共形形式导出流算子（flow operator），但其BRST规范固定引入的乘法项破坏了与Nicolai映射的导数关联。作者论证此类乘法项可能在自由有效理论中借助Wick定理重写为导数形式，并在朗道规范下证明了流方程在κ=0处正则，从而克服了此前构造Nicolai映射的三个障碍中的两个，最后与直接壳上构造的Nicolai映射进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望探索四维庞加莱超引力中Nicolai映射的构造，以理解超对称场论中量子关联函数的编码方式，并克服此前构造尝试中遇到的三个障碍。

Method: 本文采用超共形形式（superconformal formulation）来表达四维庞加莱超引力，将全超共形离壳作用量表示为超变分（supervariation），从而导出有效vierbein理论中的流算子；通过对流方程的BRST规范固定进行分析，借助Wick定理尝试将乘法流贡献重写为导数形式。

Result: 导出了可用于构造全阶Nicolai映射的规范不变流算子；但BRST规范固定引入了破坏导数性质的乘法项；在朗道规范下证明了超引力流方程在κ=0处正则，允许围绕闵可夫斯基几何进行微扰展开；克服了此前三个障碍中的两个；与直接壳上构造的Nicolai映射在κ的领头阶上进行了比较。

Conclusion: 尽管BRST规范固定引入的乘法项目前破坏了与Nicolai映射的关联，但乘法项可能在自由有效理论中借助Wick定理转化为导数形式；在朗道规范下流方程的正则性为后续微扰展开奠定了基础，推进了庞加莱超引力中Nicolai映射的构造研究。

Abstract: Quantum correlation functions in supersymmetric field theories can be encoded in the Nicolai map. The infinitesimal inverse map provides their response to a change in a coupling constant through a ``flow equation''. We investigate the flow in the gravitational constant $κ$ for four-dimensional Poincaré supergravity via its superconformal formulation. By expressing the full superconformal off-shell action as a supervariation we derive a ``flow operator'' in the effective vierbein theory, whose exponentiation yields the gauge-invariant part of an all-order (inverse) Nicolai map for supergravity. Alas, the BRST gauge fixing adds to this functional differential flow operator a multiplicative term, which ruins its derivational property and therefore jeopardizes its connection with the Nicolai map. We argue and present an example, however, that such multiplicative flow contributions might be rewritable as derivational ones with the help of Wick's theorem in the free effective theory where the Nicolai-transformed correlators are ultimately evaluated. At least in the Landau gauge, the supergravity flow equation is shown to be regular at $κ{=}0$, allowing for a perturbative expansion around Minkowski geometry. Therewith, we have overcome two of the three obstacles met in an earlier attempt towards a Nicolai map for Poincaré supergravity. Finally, we compare with the direct on-shell construction of a Nicolai map to leading order in $κ$.

</details>


### [139] [Balanced Metrics Know About SYZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25733)
*Per Berglund,Tristan Hübsch,Vishnu Jejjala,Viktor Mirjanić,Challenger Mishra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Donaldson算法变体，通过在全空间（ambient space）的整体截面（global sections）上操作而非在流形本身上计算，从而获得可解释的平衡度量（balanced metrics），并发现度规参数在大复结构极限（LCSL）附近遵循新的幂律关系，进而将这些幂律与SYZ猜想预测的Gromov-Hausdorff度量坍缩联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calabi-Yau流形上的数值Ricci平坦度量（numerical Ricci-flat metrics）精度日益提高，但往往缺乏可解释性（interpretability），难以提取理论洞见。现有方法无法在较大次数k下利用规范单项基（canonical monomial basis）计算可解释的平衡度量。

Method: 引入Donaldson算法的一种新变体，该变体基于Moore-Penrose伪逆（Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse），作用在背景空间（ambient space）的整体截面上而非流形本身，从而允许使用规范单项基计算可解释的平衡度量，即使在较大次数k下也能实现。

Result: 将该环境算法（ambient algorithm）应用于多个族系，包括Dwork族（Dwork family）和完全交集Calabi-Yau流形（complete intersection Calabi-Yau manifolds），发现度规参数在大复结构极限附近服从新的幂律（power laws）关系。

Conclusion: 所发现的幂律关系可与SYZ猜想（SYZ conjecture）所预测的Gromov-Hausdorff度量坍缩（Gromov-Hausdorff metric collapse）建立联系，从而为理解Calabi-Yau流形的几何结构提供新的理论视角。

Abstract: Numerical Ricci-flat metrics on Calabi-Yau manifolds are becoming increasingly accurate. However, they often lack the interpretability required to extract theoretical insights. In this paper, we introduce a novel variant of Donaldson's algorithm based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse that operates on the global sections of the ambient space rather than the manifold itself. This approach allows us to use the canonical monomial basis to compute interpretable balanced metrics even at large degrees $k$. Applying our ambient algorithm to multiple families, including the Dwork family and complete intersection Calabi-Yau manifolds, we discover that the metric parameters obey novel power laws near the Large Complex Structure Limit (LCSL). We connect these to the Gromov-Hausdorff metric collapse predicted by the SYZ conjecture.

</details>


### [140] [Curved momentum space and finite Landau spectrum in $κ$-Minkowski spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25833)
*Adrián Huamán Vargas,Vladislav Kupriyanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从动量空间的德西特几何（de Sitter geometry）导出κ-庞加莱卡西米尔（κ-Poincare Casimir），并将其作为泊松规范理论（Poisson gauge theory）框架下带电粒子的动力学约束。研究表明，动量空间的曲率导致最大不变动量，进而产生有限朗道能谱（Landau spectrum）并存在最高朗道能级（Highest Landau Level, HLL）；在费米子情形中，能级截断具有自旋依赖性，导致极化最高朗道能级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将κ-庞加莱卡西米尔算子从动量空间的德西特几何导出，并在泊松规范理论的框架下将其用作带电粒子的动力学约束，以探索弯曲动量空间对量子系统（如带电粒子在磁场中的行为）的物理影响。

Method: 采用泊松规范理论框架，以κ-庞加莱卡西米尔作为动力学约束，分别研究标量粒子（scalar particles）和自旋-1/2粒子（spin-1/2 particles）在恒定磁场中的行为，并推导包含全部变形参数1/κ阶数的精确能谱。

Result: 得到了包含全部变形参数（1/κ）阶数的精确能谱；动量空间曲率导致最大不变动量，从而使朗道能谱有限，存在最高朗道能级（HLL）；在费米子情形中，能级截断具有自旋依赖性，产生极化最高朗道能级。

Conclusion: 本文揭示的紫外截断（ultraviolet truncation）及其与反常相关现象（anomaly-related phenomena）的可能关联，为进一步探讨弯曲动量空间在量子场论中的物理后果提供了方向。

Abstract: It is obtained the $κ$-Poincare Casimir from the de Sitter geometry of momentum space and employed as the dynamical constraint governing charged particles within the framework of Poisson gauge theory. The resulting formalism is applied to investigate both scalar and spin-1/2 particles in a constant magnetic field. Exact energy spectra are obtained, including all orders in the deformation parameter $1/κ$. The curvature of momentum space implies a maximal invariant momentum, which in turn leads to a finite Landau spectrum characterized by the existence of a Highest Landau Level (HLL). In the fermionic case, the truncation becomes spin dependent, resulting in a polarized HLL. Possible implications of this ultraviolet truncation and its relation to anomaly-related phenomena are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [141] [Maximal $D=5$ trombone supergravity from M5-branes and $\mathrm{SU}(2)$-flavoured $\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}$ operator spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25952)
*Ritabrata Bhattacharya,Abhay Katyal,Oscar Varela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明，此前发现的包含局部长号缩放对称性（local trombone scaling symmetry）的五维 N=8 规范超引力（gauged supergravity）可通过一致截断（consistent truncation）从 D=11 超引力在 Bah-Beem-Bobev-Wecht 系列解上的紧化得到，并计算了该解上的 Kaluza-Klein 谱，其全局定义的普适部分对偶于一类 N=1 类 S 超共形场论（SCFT）中的轻算符谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已在 M-理论（M-theory）对 Maldacena-Núñez AdS$_5$ 解的一致截断中导出一个包含局部长号缩放对称性（local trombone scaling symmetry）的五维 N=8 规范超引力（gauged supergravity），其满足对偶于 N=2 类 S 超共形场论（SCFT）。本研究旨在将该构造推广至更一般的 N=1 背景族，并提取该背景上全局定义的 Kaluza-Klein 谱（Kaluza-Klein spectrum）的普适部分。

Method: 利用例外广义几何／场论（exceptional generalised geometry/field theory），本文论证同一五维 N=8 超引力也可从 D=11 超引力在 N=1 M5-膜缠绕解家族（包括 N=1 Maldacena-Núñez 构型）上的一致截断得到。随后，运用近期导出的质量矩阵（mass matrices），计算该解上的 Kaluza-Klein 谱，并给出一种提取其中全局定义子扇区的方案。

Result: 本文成功将先前的构造从 N=2 背景推广至包含 N=1 MN1 构型在内的更广泛的 N=1 M5-膜缠绕解家族。计算了 MN1 解上的普适 Kaluza-Klein 谱（universal Kaluza-Klein spectrum），并指出该谱通常仅为局部定义的，进而给出了提取其全局定义子扇区（globally defined subsector）的处方。

Conclusion: 该全局定义的普适 Kaluza-Klein 谱对偶于对偶于 MN1 解的 SU(2)-味化 N=1 类 S 超共形场论（SU(2)-flavoured N=1 class S SCFT）中轻算符谱（light operator spectrum）的一个普适扇区。

Abstract: We recently presented a new $D=5$ $\mathcal{N}=8$ gauged supergravity involving the local trombone scaling symmetry. It arises by consistent truncation of M-theory on the internal space of the Maldacena-Núñez AdS$_5$ solution dual to the $\mathcal{N}=2$ four-dimensional superconformal field theory (SCFT) of class $\mathcal{S}$ associated to M5-branes wrapped on an unpunctured Riemann surface. Using exceptional generalised geometry/field theory, we extend that construction to show that the same $D=5$ $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity also arises by consistent truncation of $D=11$ supergravity on the family of $\mathcal{N}=1$ M5-brane-wrapped solutions of Bah-Beem-Bobev-Wecht, including the $\mathcal{N}=1$ Maldacena-Núñez (MN1) configuration. Then, using recently derived mass matrices, we compute universal sectors of the Kaluza-Klein spectrum on the MN1 solution. In general, this universal spectrum is only locally defined, and we give a prescription for extracting globally defined subsectors thereof. This globally defined universal Kaluza-Klein spectrum is dual to a universal sector of the light operator spectrum of the SU(2)-flavoured $\mathcal{N}=1$ class $\mathcal{S}$ SCFT dual to MN1.

</details>


### [142] [Krylov-Space Memory Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26011)
*Mohsen Alishahiha,Mohammad Javad Vasli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入Krylov空间记忆核（Krylov-space memory cores）作为稳态的深度分辨结构，揭示了在热化非可积系统中，反常初态记忆如何在Krylov空间内组织形成紧凑的低深度记忆核，这些记忆核携带有显著的残余涨落、Gibbs失配和持续流涨落活动，且状态选择性而非可积性的普遍后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热化非可积系统中，反常初态（如弱热化、禁闭诱导反常动力学、多体疤痕态）的记忆是如何在Krylov空间中组织并保持动态编码的，这一问题尚缺乏一个稳态的、深度分辨的分析框架。

Method: 引入Krylov空间记忆核（Krylov-space memory cores）这一稳态概念，通过稳态占据数剖面（stationary occupation profile）识别Krylov链上的晚期概率集中区域，并结合残余平衡涨落（residual equilibration fluctuations）、偏离Gibbs参考（deviation from the Gibbs reference）以及长时间的Krylov流涨落（long-time Krylov-current fluctuations）等互补诊断指标，刻画该区域的物理特征。

Result: 在弱热化、禁闭诱导反常动力学和多体疤痕（many-body scarring）等场景下，反常初态会形成紧凑的低深度记忆核，这些记忆核具有显著的残余涨落、Gibbs失配和持续流涨落活动，并且通常嵌入更宽的稳态占据晕（stationary occupation halos）中。相比之下，通用参考态（generic reference states）不具备同等强度的信号与空间紧凑性。辅助的可积系统比较表明，紧凑的Krylov记忆具有状态选择性（state selective），而非可积性（integrability）的普遍结果。

Conclusion: Krylov空间记忆核为识别结构化量子记忆（structured quantum memory）的驻留位置及其动态编码机制提供了一种稳态框架。

Abstract: We introduce Krylov-space memory cores as stationary, depth-resolved structures that reveal how anomalous initial-state memory is organized inside the Krylov space of otherwise thermalizing nonintegrable systems. The stationary occupation profile identifies where late-time probability is concentrated along the Krylov chain, while complementary diagnostics of residual equilibration fluctuations, deviation from the Gibbs reference, and long-time Krylov-current fluctuations determine the physical character of that region. Across weak thermalization, confinement-induced anomalous dynamics, and many-body scarring, anomalous initial states develop compact low-depth memory cores that carry appreciable residual fluctuations, Gibbs mismatch, and persistent current-fluctuation activity. These cores are often embedded within substantially broader stationary occupation halos. Generic reference states, by contrast, do not exhibit a comparable combination of signal strength and spatial compactness. An auxiliary integrable comparison further shows that compact Krylov memory is state selective rather than a generic consequence of integrability. Krylov-space memory cores therefore provide a stationary framework for identifying where structured quantum memory resides and how it remains dynamically encoded.

</details>

### [143] [Demonstration of 255-kV high-voltage generation with a Cavallo multiplier system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24812)
*S. M. Clayton,T. M. Ito,A. Jacobs,A-T. Le,M. F. Makela,C. M. O'Shaughnessy,N. S. Phan,E. Renner,T. A. Sandborn,T. J. Schaub,I. L. Smythe,J. Surbrook,M. A. Blatnik,B. W. Filippone*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种 Cavallo 静电倍增器（Cavallo electrostatic multiplier），可在传统高压馈通（high-voltage feedthrough）难以应用的低温精密测量环境中，通过原位（in situ）方式产生高电压。室温演示中，系统在 SF₆ 气氛下将 25 kV 直流偏置输入电压提升至约 255 kV，并建立了包含电极错位效应的电容模型描述充电曲线。实验表明，该装置是一种可行的低电流原位高压源，其极限性能受限于电极表面状态和局部场增强引起的瞬态放电，而非气体的本征介质强度（dielectric strength）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多低温精密测量（cryogenic precision measurement）需要在大电场环境中工作，但传统高压馈通在此类条件下不实用。因此，需要开发一种能够在低温等受限环境中实现原位高压产生的装置。

Method: 采用 Cavallo 静电倍增器，通过机械平移的转移电极（transfer electrode）实现电荷转移，并使用定制旋转场强计（rotary field mill）进行非接触式电压测量。实验在约 600 Torr 的 SF₆ 气氛中进行，从 25 kV 直流偏置输入电压出发进行倍增。

Result: 系统输出最高电压约为 255 kV（基于 25 kV 输入）。充电曲线可通过包含实际电极错位（electrode misalignment）的电容模型（capacitance-based model）定量描述。电压保持测量显示长时间尺度下泄漏电流处于皮安（picoampere）量级；接近最大电压时，性能受限于与电极表面状态及局部场增强相关的瞬态放电过程（transient discharge processes）。

Conclusion: Cavallo 倍增器是一种可行的低电流原位高压源。未来低温实现（cryogenic implementation）的主要技术要求包括：电极表面制备（electrode surface preparation）、对齐公差（alignment tolerances）和绝缘性能（insulation performance）。

Abstract: Many cryogenic precision measurements require large electric fields in environments where conventional high-voltage feedthroughs are impractical. To address this, we developed a Cavallo electrostatic multiplier designed for in situ high-voltage generation under such conditions. Here, we report a room-temperature demonstration of this device. Using a mechanically translated transfer electrode and a custom rotary field mill for noncontact voltage measurement, the system reached output voltages up to approximately $255~\mathrm{kV}$ from a $25~\mathrm{kV}$ DC-biased input voltage in approximately $600~\mathrm{Torr}$ of SF$_6$. The charging curves are quantitatively described by a capacitance-based model once realistic electrode misalignment is included. Voltage-hold measurements show picoampere-scale leakage currents on long time scales, whereas operation near the maximum voltage is limited by transient discharge processes associated with electrode surface condition and local field enhancement, rather than by the intrinsic dielectric strength of the gas. These results demonstrate the Cavallo multiplier as a viable low-current, in situ high-voltage source and indicate that electrode surface preparation, alignment tolerances, and insulation performance are the principal requirements for reliable operation in future cryogenic implementations.

</details>


### [144] [Machine-Learning-Based Waveform Discrimination in the Front-End Electronics of the Belle II Central Drift Chamber for Cross-Talk Noise Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25174)
*Yun-Tsung Lai,Taichiro Koga,Yu Nakazawa,Nanae Taniguchi,Keisuke Yoshihara*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文针对Belle II实验中心漂移室（CDC）前端电子学（FEE）中的串扰噪声问题，在Xilinx Virtex-5 FPGA上实现了基于Boosted Decision Tree（BDT）模型的波形判别方法，能够以全流水方式独立处理每个导线通道的波形。离线研究显示串扰噪声可降低约一半，同时信号效率保持在98%以上；固件验证表明在保持触发接受度下降不超过10%的前提下，轨道段和触发率最多降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习（ML）推理已广泛应用于基于FPGA的对撞机实验实时触发系统，但受限于FPGA资源、功耗和探测器覆盖范围，ML在前端电子学（FEE）中的应用尚未被充分探索。Belle II实验CDC的FEE中观测到背景线缆击中事件，导致硬件轨道触发器对串扰噪声敏感，未来随着亮度提升将产生更高的假触发率。

Method: 采用紧凑且快速的Boosted Decision Tree（BDT）模型，在CDC FEE的Xilinx Virtex-5 FPGA上实现波形判别。每个导线通道的波形以全流水线（fully pipelined）方式独立处理，从而在前端层面抑制串扰噪声。

Result: 离线研究表明，串扰噪声可减少约一半（约2倍），同时信号效率保持在98%以上。在Belle II专用标定运行期间的固件验证显示，轨道段和触发率最多降低50%，且对包含径迹的事件的触发接受度下降不超过10%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在探测器FEE中实现紧凑、低延迟的ML推理的技术可行性，凸显了其在未来高能物理实验中智能读出系统中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) inference on FPGAs has been widely adopted in real-time triggering of collider experiments for detector signature identification. In contrast, the ML application in Front-End Electronics (FEE) has not yet been fully explored, primarily due to constraints such as limited FPGA resources, power consumption, and localized detector coverage. In this work, we develop an ML-based waveform discrimination method for the Central Drift Chamber (CDC) of the Belle II experiment to suppress cross-talk noise at the front-end level. The Belle II CDC is a key charged-particle tracking detector for both offline and the real-time hardware trigger. During Belle II operation, background wire hits have been observed in the CDC FEE, where multiple hits occur in neighboring anode wires by large energy deposit. The hardware track trigger employs a Hough transformation based on track segments formed by combining hits from multiple wire layers. Due to the reduced information, the track trigger is sensitive to cross-talk noise, hence resulting in an increased fake trigger rate with higher luminosity in the future. We employ compact and fast Boosted Decision Tree models implemented in a Xilinx Virtex-5 FPGA of the CDC FEE, where waveform is processed independently for each wire channel in a fully pipelined manner. Offline studies show that the cross-talk noise can be reduced by approximately a factor of two while maintaining a signal efficiency above 98%. The firmware validation during dedicated Belle II calibration runs demonstrated reductions of up to 50% in track segment and trigger rates while preserving the trigger acceptance for events containing tracks within 10%. This work demonstrates the technical feasibility of compact and low-latency ML inference in detector FEE and highlights its potential for future intelligent detector readout systems in high-energy physics experiments.

</details>
