# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [A Difference Operator Approach to Quantum Random Walks: Parseval Identity, Krawtchouk Matrices, and Hermite Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24837)
*Chien-Wen Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入离散差分算子D_k研究一维Hadamard量子随机游走（quantum random walk, QRW），推导出概率幅的显式组合表达式，证明其满足Parseval恒等式以保障概率守恒，并揭示该算子与Krawtchouk矩阵及Hermite多项式（Hermite polynomial）之间的深层结构联系，最终从量子干涉角度解释了弹道扩散（ballistic）方差的O(n²)标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子随机游走概率幅与经典组合结构（如Krawtchouk矩阵、Hermite多项式）之间的严格对应关系，从而在离散层面统一描述量子干涉与概率守恒机制，并解释其与经典二项分布（binomial distribution）的差异。

Method: 定义离散差分算子D_k，推导概率幅a(n,k)与b(n,k)的显式组合表达式；利用Krawtchouk矩阵元素间的交叉恒等式（cross identities），通过归纳法证明Parseval恒等式；分析D_k的迭代作用，将其与Hermite多项式在连续极限下建立联系，并基于该算子构造相干性生成元（coherence generator）与升算符（raising operator）。

Result: 概率幅满足Parseval恒等式∑(a²+b²)=2^(n−1)；比值h_m在连续极限下收敛到Hermite多项式；D_k在离散层面连接相邻Krawtchouk矩阵；方差呈弹道O(n²)标度；数值算例n=6与n=10验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 离散差分算子D_k为量子随机游走提供了统一的代数框架，将量子干涉、概率守恒与经典-量子过渡均纳入Krawtchouk- Hermite结构之中，为理解量子游走的相干动力学提供了新的离散分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce a discrete difference operator D_k to study the one-dimensional quantum random walk (QRW) with the Hadamard coin. Explicit combinatorial expressions are obtained for the probability amplitudes a(n,k) and b(n,k), which encode the final step direction and carry alternating signs that reflect the merging of leftward steps. Removing these signs and the coin-state distinction recovers the classical binomial distribution. The symmetric and antisymmetric combinations $a\pm b$ are shown to coincide with diagonal and sub-diagonal entries of the Krawtchouk matrix. Using cross identities among Krawtchouk matrix elements, we prove by induction that the amplitudes satisfy a Parseval identity sum (a^2+b^2)=2^n-1, establishing probability conservation in the Krawtchouk formulation. The operator D_k acts as a discrete Hermite polynomial generator: the ratios h_m = (n choose k)^{-1} D_k^m (n choose k) admit explicit closed forms and converge to Hermite polynomials in the continuous limit. At the discrete level, D_k connects successive Krawtchouk matrices and acts as a coherence generator and a raising operator. The h_m quantify the position-dependent degree of quantum interference on both halves of the distribution, either individually (for x >= 0) or through an inverse-Pascal combination of several h_m (for x < 0), and the ballistic O(n^2) scaling of the variance emerges from the superposition of all excited states. Numerical illustrations for n=6 and n=10 corroborate the analytical results.

</details>


### [146] [An algebraically closed family of informational n-qubit purity invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24987)
*Markus Frembs,Giovanni Natale,Christopher S. P. Wever,Philipp A. Hoehn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族泡利期望值的二次型（quadratics in Pauli expectation values），并证明它们对所有n量子比特纯态构成与态无关的不变量（state-independent invariants）。该族推广了在重构纲领中发现的两量子比特“五边形恒等式”，并揭示了多量子比特情形中不存在于两量子比特情况的新结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在将先前在两量子比特纯态空间中发现的“五边形恒等式”（pentagon identities）推广到任意量子比特数n，并证明这些恒等式构成了与态无关的不变量，从而深化对多体量子系统互补性（complementarity）结构及其与无偏性（mutual unbiasedness）关系的理解。

Method: 通过引入新的数学工具，构建了一族基于泡利期望值（Pauli expectation values）的二次型，并证明其对所有n量子比特纯态均构成与态无关的不变量。该方法推广了重构纲领中的两量子比特五边形恒等式，并依赖于对n量子比特泡利群（Pauli group）中互补性与互无偏性（mutual unbiasedness）的深入分析。

Result: 成功构造出了一族泡利期望值的二次型，并证明了它们对所有n量子比特纯态都是与态无关的不变量。该族推广了两量子比特的五边形恒等式，后者此前被证明可以刻画纯态空间及酉群（unitary group），并在Brukner-Zeilinger信息度量中被解释为互补性等式。推广至多量子比特的情形是非平凡的，且揭示了在两量子比特情形中不存在的新结构性质。

Conclusion: 本文证明了所提出的二次型族构成任意量子比特数纯态空间上的与态无关不变量，为多体量子系统的互补性结构提供了统一框架。该工作的深入分析及其与无偏性和互补性的关系，将在两篇姊妹论文中展开论述。

Abstract: We present a family of quadratics in Pauli expectation values, and prove that they constitute state-independent invariants for all n-qubit pure states. This family generalises the two-qubit `pentagon identities', discovered in the reconstruction programme of [P. A. Höhn, Quantum 1, 38 (2017), P. A. Höhn and C. S. P. Wever, Phys. Rev. A 95, 012102 (2017)], where they characterise the space of pure states, as well as the unitary group, and are interpreted as complementarity equalities in the Brukner-Zeilinger information measure. The generalisation to arbitrarily many qubits is nontrivial as it requires new tools which in turn reveal novel structural properties that are absent in the two-qubit case. A thorough analysis of these properties, and their relation with mutual unbiasedness and complementarity in the n-qubit Pauli group, can be found in two companion papers.

</details>


### [147] [Maximal complementarity in the n-qubit Pauli group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24988)
*Markus Frembs,Giovanni Natale,Christopher S. P. Wever,Philipp A. Hoehn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了n量子比特（n-qubit）泡利群（Pauli group）中的简并（degenerate）可观测量也具有互补性（complementarity），给出了两个可观测量互补的判据，并将最大互补集与信息论纯态互补等式（informational pure state complementarity equalities）相联系，最后证明这些互补集蕴含强熵不确定性关系（entropic uncertainty relations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中的可观测量通常具有互补性，这一性质不仅是量子形式体系的基本原理，也是量子密码协议的关键组成部分。已有的研究主要关注非简并非退化可观测量的最大互补集在素数幂维系统中的存在性，而本文旨在研究简并退化的可观测量之间的互补性，特别是n量子比特泡利群算符在该条件下的互补性质。

Method: 首先推导出了两个简并可观测量互补的判据；其次，将最大互补集与信息论纯态互补等式建立关联；最后，将结果等价地表述为泡利群中（不必是最大的）阿贝尔子群（Abelian subgroups）的最大互补集，并与（可能粗粒化的）互无偏基（mutually unbiased bases）相联系。

Result: 证明了与n量子比特泡利群相关的可观测量在简并条件下同样具有互补性，并建立了最大互补集与信息论纯态互补等式之间的联系；进一步证明这些互补集会导出强熵不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了简并可观测量的互补结构，扩展了互补性理论至泡利群框架，并通过与互无偏基及熵不确定性关系的联系，为量子信息理论提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Observables in quantum mechanics are generally complementary, that is, they reveal mutually incompatible pieces of information about a given system. This property is not only a fundamental tenet of the quantum formalism, but also a key component in quantum cryptographic protocols. As such it has fuelled much research into finding sets of highly complementary observables. In its strongest form -- the one we consider in this work -- the information between complementary observables is not merely incompatible but mutually exclusive: maximal information about one observable implies no information about the other, and vice versa. Maximal sets of non-degenerate complementary observables are known to exist in systems of prime power dimension such as n-qubit systems. Here, we study complementarity of degenerate observables, specifically we prove that observables associated with the n-qubit Pauli group also exhibit complementarity under this restriction: first, we obtain a criterion for two such observables to be complementary and, second, we relate maximal sets of complementary observables with informational pure state complementarity equalities, further studied in two companion papers. Equivalently, these results can be formulated in terms of maximal complementary sets of (not necessarily maximal) Abelian subgroups of the Pauli group linking with (possibly coarse-grained) mutually unbiased bases. Finally, we prove that these complementarity sets entail strong entropic uncertainty relations.

</details>


### [148] [Covariant Quantum Measurements and Stochastic Dynamics on Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25101)
*Naeem Shahid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个群协变量子测量（group-covariant quantum measurements）的框架，将不可约表示扇区间的测量诱导跃迁描述为表示空间上的随机过程，并针对SU(2)情形给出了闭式跃迁核、可逆性、不变测度及连续Fokker-Planck描述，理论结果与数值模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子测量理论对对称性约束下的重复测量过程缺乏统一的随机过程描述，尤其缺乏从群表示论（representation theory）角度理解测量诱导的表示扇区间跃迁机制。

Method: 基于Peter-Weyl分解构造从不可约张量算子（irreducible tensor operators）出发的协变测量算子，证明对对称不变态（symmetry-invariant states），测量通道退化为表示图（representation graph）上的马尔可夫过程（Markov process）；利用Sugiura定理将测量算子解析性约束与探测器谱（detector spectrum）关联，推导出指数衰减探测器模型；对SU(2)特例导出闭式跃迁核，并建立连续Fokker-Planck描述。

Result: 推导出SU(2)群协变测量的闭式跃迁核，证明了测量过程的可逆性（reversibility）及相应不变测度（invariant measure）；得到了漂移系数（drift）与扩散系数（diffusion）的解析预测，并与数值模拟结果吻合。

Conclusion: 该框架为表示空间上的重复协变量子测量提供了随机过程描述，解析结果经数值验证，为理解对称性约束下的量子测量动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We develop a framework for group-covariant quantum measurements in which measurement-induced transitions between irreducible representation sectors are described by a stochastic process on representation space. Starting from the Peter-Weyl decomposition, we construct covariant measurement operators from irreducible tensor operators and show that, for symmetry-invariant states, the measurement channel reduces to a Markov process on the representation graph. We further show that analyticity of the measurement operator constrains the detector spectrum through Sugiura's theorem, motivating a class of exponentially decaying detector models. Specializing to SU(2), we obtain the transition kernel in closed form, establish reversibility and the associated invariant measure, and derive a continuum Fokker-Planck description of the induced dynamics. Analytical predictions for the drift and diffusion coefficients are found to agree with numerical simulations. Our results provide a stochastic description of repeated covariant quantum measurements on representation space.

</details>


### [149] [Can a quantum circuit detect the Unruh effect?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24836)
*Pravin Kumar Dahal,Timothy C. Ralph,William J. Munro,Arkady Fedorov,James Q. Quach*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并分析了利用超导通量量子比特（fluxonium）电路作为Λ型三能级探测器来实验探测类时安鲁效应（timelike Unruh effect）的方案。通过调制激发态跃迁频率，探测器积累与类时安鲁效应相关的几何相位，开放系统模拟预测在530纳秒内基态布居数发生约10%的偏移，灵敏度相比两能级Unruh-DeWitt探测器提升三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始安鲁效应（Unruh effect）（加速观测者将闵可夫斯基真空感知为热浴）的直接探测在实验上不可行；其类时对应物（timelike counterpart）由无质量场在未来与过去光锥之间的纠缠（entanglement）产生，提供了更可行的探测路径，但要求探测器的跃迁频率遵循特定的共形时间（conformal time）标度。因此需要设计一种实际可行的量子电路实现方案。

Method: 采用超导通量量子比特（superconducting fluxonium）电路构建探测器：该电路天然提供两个准简并基态和一个可调激发态，形成有效的Λ型三能级系统（Λ-system）。通过在闵可夫斯基时间（Minkowski time）中调制激发态跃迁频率，使探测器积累与类时安鲁效应相关的几何相位（geometric phase）。采用开放系统模拟（open-system simulation）评估探测器动力学响应。

Result: 开放系统模拟预测：在530纳秒的时间内，探测器基态布居数（ground-state population）发生约10%的偏移。该灵敏度相比传统的两能级Unruh-DeWitt探测器（two-level Unruh-DeWitt detector）提升了三个数量级（three orders of magnitude）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，基于超导通量量子比特的Λ型三能级探测器构成了一个可实际实现的量子电路平台（quantum-circuit platform），能够用于实验探测类时安鲁效应，并更广泛地利用受控量子系统检验量子场论（quantum fields）的基础性质。

Abstract: The Unruh effect predicts that an accelerating observer perceives the Minkowski vacuum as a thermal bath, yet direct detection remains experimentally inaccessible. Its timelike counterpart, arising from the entanglement of massless fields between the future and past light cones, offers a more feasible route but requires a detector whose transition frequency follows a specific conformal-time scaling. We propose and analyze a practical implementation of such a detector using superconducting fluxonium circuits, which naturally provide two quasi-degenerate ground states and a tunable excited state, forming an effective $Λ$-system. By modulating the excited-state transition frequency in Minkowski time, the detector accumulates a geometric phase associated with the timelike Unruh effect. Open-system simulations predict $\sim 10\%$ shift in the ground-state population within $530$ ns, representing a three-order-of-magnitude sensitivity enhancement over two-level Unruh-DeWitt detectors. These results establish a realistic quantum-circuit platform for experimentally probing the timelike Unruh effect and, more broadly, for testing fundamental nature of quantum fields using engineered quantum systems.

</details>


### [150] [Topology-dependent relativistic degradation of multipartite entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24858)
*Shu-Min Wu,Si-Han Shang,Xu Han,Hui-Chen Yang,Qianqian Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，对于非对称多体量子态（asymmetric multipartite states），纠缠拓扑结构（topology of entanglement）对相对论性纠缠退化的影响可与加速度引起的热噪声（thermal noise）相当；在三量子比特Star态的两个不等价加速构型中，外围量子比特的加速会导致中心加速情形中缺失的单纠缠（one-tangle）再生，而真三方纠缠（genuine tripartite entanglement）的衰减鲁棒性也显著不同，揭示了一个由拓扑决定的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论运动对量子纠缠的影响通常归因于加速度诱导的热噪声，但现有研究多集中于对称情形，忽视了不对称多体系统中纠缠拓扑结构的潜在作用。本文旨在探究拓扑因素是否以及在多大程度上影响相对论纠缠退化（relativistic entanglement degradation）。

Method: 在Unruh-DeWitt探测器框架（Unruh-DeWitt detector framework）下，考虑一个三量子比特Star态（three-qubit Star state），比较两种不等价的加速构型：中心量子比特均匀加速（uniform acceleration），以及外围量子比特均匀加速。通过分析加速对单纠缠和真三方纠缠动力学的差异来揭示拓扑效应。

Result: 两种物理上等价的加速会导致定性不同的纠缠动力学：外围量子比特加速时会发生单纠缠的再生（revival of one-tangle），而中心量子比特加速时该现象缺失；真三方纠缠在两种情形下均单调衰减（monotonically decaying），但衰减的鲁棒性（robustness）存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 相对论纠缠退化存在一个拓扑依赖的机制（topology-dependent mechanism），多体量子关联的响应由Unruh热化（Unruh thermalization）与加速子系统在整体结构中的角色共同决定。这项工作将不对称量子网络（asymmetric quantum networks）识别为控制相对论量子资源（relativistic quantum resources）的一个独特平台。

Abstract: The influence of relativistic motion on quantum entanglement is commonly attributed to acceleration-induced thermal noise. Here we show that, for asymmetric multipartite states, the topology of entanglement can become equally important. Considering a three-qubit Star state in the Unruh-DeWitt detector framework, we compare two inequivalent acceleration configurations in which either the central or a peripheral qubit undergoes uniform acceleration. We demonstrate that these physically equivalent accelerations lead to qualitatively different entanglement dynamics: acceleration of a peripheral qubit induces a revival of one-tangle that is absent when the central qubit accelerates, whereas genuine tripartite entanglement decays monotonically but with markedly different robustness. Our results uncover a topology-dependent mechanism for relativistic entanglement degradation, showing that the response of multipartite quantum correlations is determined jointly by Unruh thermalization and the structural role of the accelerated subsystem. This work identifies asymmetric quantum networks as a distinct platform for controlling relativistic quantum resources.

</details>


### [151] [Quantum reference frames beyond subsystems: a reconstruction and generalization of the perspective-neutral framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24976)
*Stefan L. Ludescher,Manuel Mekonnen,Thomas D. Galley,Markus P. Mueller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子参考系（quantum reference frames, QRFs）的概念从张量因子子系统推广到协变量子仪器（covariant quantum instrument），从而覆盖了不可区分粒子的标记框架、关系时钟等新应用，并从资源理论（resource theory）角度为视角中立（perspective-neutral）方法提供了操作基础，同时澄清了约束量子化（constraint quantization）中若干操作含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子参考系框架主要假设参考系对应一个张量因子子系统，这限制了其适用范围，无法处理不可区分粒子、完全相互作用的子系统或频率超选择扇区（frequency superselection sectors）等情形。因此，需要将QRF概念推广到更一般的协变量子仪器，以涵盖这些物理场景。

Method: 通过将量子参考系定义为协变量子仪器，而非局限于张量因子子系统，从而推广了QRFs的概念。作者重构了Höhn等人提出的视角中立方法，并给出了该框架的资源理论基础，利用完全协变操作（completely covariant operations）来解释电荷零扇区（charge-zero sector）和纯态跨视角变换行为的必要性。此外，将关系化映射（relationalization maps）推广到了量子仪器层面。

Result: 该推广可应用于“标记框架”（frames of labeling）以描述不可区分粒子，为对称化公设（symmetrization postulate）和不存在parastatistics提供了可能的解释，并透明地描述了玻色子和费米子的纠缠结构。关系时钟（relational clocks）即使在所有子系统相互作用或存在频率超选择扇区时，也能精确复现薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation）。部分结果（如关系化映射的仪器推广）可适用于其他QRF框架。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了操作量子信息论（operational quantum information theory）与内部量子参考系研究纲领之间的差距，并为约束量子化中约束方程（如C|ψ⟩=0）的含义提供了操作上的澄清。

Abstract: We generalize the notion of quantum reference frames (QRFs) to cases where the frame does not necessarily correspond to a tensor factor subsystem, but to a covariant quantum instrument. This unlocks a variety of physical applications: "frames of labeling" for indistinguishable particles, suggesting explanations for the symmetrization postulate and the absence of parastatistics, and yielding a transparent description of the entanglement of bosons and fermions; and relational clocks reproducing the Schrödinger equation exactly even when all subsystems are interacting or when there are frequency superselection sectors. Our work generalizes the perspective-neutral approach to QRFs pioneered by Höhn and co-authors, which we reconstruct from a simple operational scenario. We give a resource-theoretic grounding of this framework, and show how the notion of completely covariant operations explains the relevance of the charge-zero sector and the pure-state transformation behavior across perspectives. This also suggests operational clarifications of some aspects of constraint quantization, e.g. of the meaning of constraint equations such as $C|ψ\rangle=0$. Some of our results, such as our generalization of relationalization maps to instruments, apply more broadly to other QRF frameworks too, and they contribute to bridging the gap between operational quantum information theory and the internal QRF research program.

</details>


### [152] [Discrete power-law decay of subsystem distance after a quantum quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25661)
*Bin Sui,Jiaju Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对一维无限横场Ising链（transverse-field Ising chain）全局量子淬火（global quantum quench）后的子系统距离衰减进行了数值研究，发现Bures距离（Bures distance）在晚期服从离散幂律衰减，指数取离散值（1, 5/4, 3/2, 7/4, 2, 5/2等），由淬火前后横场及对称激发分数函数（symmetric excitation-fraction function）共同决定，揭示可积量子系统局部热化动力学的离散普适结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全局量子淬火后子系统距离（subsystem distance）随时间的衰减行为，特别是其在无限可积系统中的晚期幂律形式及其普适分类。前人工作仅获得个别指数（如t^{-3/2}），系统分类尚缺乏。

Method: 采用数学严格的Bures距离B_A(t)量化时间演化的约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrix）与其稳态广义吉布斯系综（generalized Gibbs ensemble, GGE）态之间的偏差；在一维无限横场Ising链中对全局量子淬火进行数值研究。

Result: 晚期衰减服从离散幂律B_A(t)∼t^{-λ}，指数λ取离散值：1, 5/4, 3/2, 7/4, 2, 5/2及更多可能值。指数由淬火前后横场及对称激发分数函数m_S(φ)的连续性、边界值、一阶导数边界值等属性共同决定；初态为预淬火哈密顿量基态时的t^{-3/2}衰减被作为特例包含在内。

Conclusion: 可积量子系统中局部平衡化（local equilibration）动力学存在一种离散普适结构（universal discrete structure），Bures距离的幂律指数由淬火参数及预淬火本征态的函数属性完整分类。

Abstract: We present a numerical study of subsystem distance decay following a global quantum quench in the infinite one-dimensional transverse-field Ising chain, using the mathematically rigorous Bures distance $B_A(t)$ to quantify the deviation of the time-evolved reduced density matrix from its stationary generalized Gibbs ensemble state. We show that the late-time decay follows a discrete power law $B_A(t) \sim t^{-λ}$, with the exponent $λ$ confined to discrete values: $1$, $5/4$, $3/2$, $7/4$, $2$, $5/2$, and potentially further values. The specific exponent is jointly determined by the pre- and post-quench transverse fields, as well as by properties of the symmetric excitation-fraction function $m_S(\varphi)$, defined on $\varphi\in[0,π]$ to characterize the pre-quench Hamiltonian eigenstates, including continuity, boundary values, and first-derivative boundary values, among others. The previously established $t^{-3/2}$ decay for the initial ground state of the pre-quench Hamiltonian is naturally recovered as a special case of this general classification. Our results reveal a universal discrete structure governing local equilibration dynamics in integrable quantum systems.

</details>


### [153] [Landau Levels on the Surface of a Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25684)
*Almila Kura,Bayram Tekin,Mehmet Özgür Oktel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了立方体表面上带电粒子在磁单极子场中的量子力学，建立了规范贴片（gauge patches）描述，推导了狄拉克量子化条件（Dirac quantization condition），并通过规范修正旋转算符（gauge-modified rotation operators）对偶数和奇数磁荷下的本征态进行对称性分类，数值计算了朗道能级型（Landau-level-like）谱及其离散对称性分裂，同时分析了离散立方体上的霍夫施塔特（Hofstadter）问题，发现了角态（corner states）等额外结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究立方体表面带电粒子在磁单极子（magnetic monopole）场中的量子力学行为，探究离散立方对称性（cubic symmetry）对能谱的影响，特别是磁荷奇偶性导致的对称性分类差异及相应的朗道能级型谱结构。

Method: 在立方体表面上使用两个规范贴片构建连续模型，通过波函数一致性导出狄拉克量子化条件；构造规范修正的旋转算符（gauge-modified rotation operators）以分类本征态；采用规范协变有限差分离散化（gauge-covariant finite-difference discretization）数值计算能谱；并在离散立方体上研究对应的紧束缚霍夫施塔特问题（tight-binding Hofstadter problem）。

Result: 偶数磁荷的本征态由立方旋转群O（cubic rotation group O）的不可约表示描述，奇数磁荷则需要二元八面体群2O（binary octahedral group 2O）的旋量表示（spinorial representations）。能谱呈现朗道能级型流形（Landau-level-like manifolds），其简并度（degeneracy）被离散立方对称性分裂。在霍夫施塔特问题的谱中发现了通常的磁子带结构（magnetic subband structure）以及位于立方体角附近的局域间隙态（gap states）。

Conclusion: 立方体表面的磁单极子问题可以通过规范贴片和规范修正旋转算符严格构建，其能谱展现出离散对称性调制的朗道能级结构，并且离散化紧束缚模型中存在由立方角点诱导的局域态。

Abstract: We study the quantum mechanics of a charged particle confined to the surface of a cube enclosing a magnetic monopole. The magnetic field is chosen to have a constant magnitude on each face and to point along the outward normal, preserving the rotational symmetry of the cube. We formulate the continuum problem using two gauge patches on the cube surface and show that consistency of the wavefunction gives the Dirac quantization condition. Since an explicit vector potential does not remain invariant under ordinary rotations, we construct gauge-modified rotation operators and use them to classify the eigenstates. Even monopole charges are described by the irreducible representations of the cubic rotation group O, while odd monopole charges require the spinorial representations of the binary octahedral group 2O. We compute the spectrum with a gauge-covariant finite-difference discretization and find Landau-level-like manifolds whose degeneracies are split by the discrete cubic symmetry. We also study the corresponding tight-binding Hofstadter problem on the discretized cube. The resulting spectrum contains the usual magnetic subband structure together with additional gap states localized near the cube corners.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond mean-field dynamics of the Dicke model with non-Markovian dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23028)
*Anqi Mu,Nathan Ng,Andrew J. Millis,David R. Reichman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于密度矩阵（density matrix）的时间相关投影算子（time dependent projection operator）形式化方法，用于计算具有非马尔可夫局域浴（non-Markovian local baths）和一对一全连接相互作用（one-to-all interactions）系统的超越平均场（beyond mean-field）动力学，并结合张量网络影响泛函（tensor network influence functionals）研究了Dicke模型在零温下的超辐射相变（superradiant phase transition）动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均场（mean-field）方法在处理耦合光学腔（optical cavities）的凝聚相系统时存在伪初态依赖（spurious initial state dependence）问题，且无法描述新的时间尺度（time scales），亟需一种能够超越平均场近似、准确处理非马尔可夫浴和长程相互作用的动力学方法。

Method: 采用基于密度矩阵的时间相关投影算子形式化方法，并结合张量网络影响泛函，对耦合至非马尔可夫局域退相位浴（non-Markovian local dephasing baths）的Dicke模型进行零温动力学计算。

Result: 该方法纠正了平均场动力学中存在的伪初态依赖问题，并成功描述了平均场动力学中缺失的新时间尺度的涌现。

Conclusion: 该基于密度矩阵的形式化方法不仅适用于Dicke模型，还可推广至其他具有一对一全连接相互作用（one-to-all interactions）的量子光学系统（quantum optical systems），并适用于有限温度（finite temperatures）情形。

Abstract: We present a density matrix-based time dependent projection operator formalism to calculate the beyond mean-field dynamics of systems with non-Markovian local baths and one-to-all interactions. Such models encapsulate the physics of condensed phase systems immersed in optical cavities. We use this method, combined with tensor network influence functionals, to study the dynamics of the Dicke model coupled to non-Markovian local dephasing baths at zero temperature, which has a superradiant phase transition in the mean-field limit. The method corrects a spurious initial state dependence found in the mean-field dynamics and describes the emergence of new time scales which are absent in the mean-field dynamics. Our formalism, based on density matrices, is applicable to other quantum optical systems with one-to-all interactions at finite temperatures.

</details>


### [155] [Wave-functional formulation of dissipative CSL models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24853)
*Y. M. P. Gomes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在泛函薛定谔表象下构建了非相对论性玻色子场的最小耗散连续自发定域化（CSL）动力学，通过扇区投影得到固定粒子数波函数，并分析了耗散扩展中多体系统的集体动量转移与对混合项，给出了压缩闭包下非广延的静态平均动能标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSL模型通常在粒子数表象或单粒子层次描述自发定域化过程，缺乏在多体泛函框架下系统处理粒子数扇区投影、扇区叠加及耗散扩展的统一形式。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用泛函薛定谔表象编码Fock空间态，通过扇区投影提取固定粒子数波函数。最小CSL耦合含质量密度涂抹；耗散扩展则在坍缩算符中引入涂抹流贡献。通过压缩闭包近似分析多体摩擦效应。

Result: 扇区投影将最小CSL动力学还原为标准非线性随机动力学，坍缩算符作用在构型的涂抹总密度上。耗散扩展中，单粒子扇区复现预期能量平衡；固定多体扇区出现集体动量偏移和对混合项。压缩闭包下，三维弱耗散时静态平均动能呈非广延标度律 T_N≃2T_β/N。

Conclusion: 泛函表象为CSL动力学的扇区投影、放大机制及局域概率平衡提供了透明框架；耗散扩展揭示多体系统无法约化为独立单粒子贡献，其集体摩擦导致非广延静态动能。

Abstract: We formulate minimal and dissipative Continuous Spontaneous Localization (CSL) dynamics in the functional Schrödinger representation for a non-relativistic bosonic field. In this framework, the Fock-space state is encoded in a wave functional, and fixed particle-number wave functions are obtained by sector projection. For the minimal CSL coupling to the smeared mass density, this projection gives the standard nonlinear stochastic dynamics in each \(N\)-particle sector, with the collapse operator acting on the total smeared density of the configuration. This makes the amplification mechanism transparent and allows us to discuss sector superpositions, local probability balance, and the status of Bohmian equivariance at the wave-function level. We then consider a dissipative extension in which the collapse operator includes a smeared current contribution. The one-particle sector reproduces the expected dissipative CSL energy balance, while fixed many-body sectors contain additional collective momentum shifts and pair-mixing terms that are not reducible, in general, to independent one-particle contributions. Within a leading compact closure, the collective pair friction produces a non-extensive stationary mean kinetic energy: in three dimensions and for weak dissipation, $T_N^{\rm comp}\simeq 2T_β/N$, whereas the corresponding dilute energy remains extensive.

</details>


### [156] [Efficient computation of real-time correlators using Pauli Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24924)
*Alexander F. Kemper,Raghav G. Jha,Arnab Bachhar,Goksu C. Toga,Mariano Guerrero Perez,Nicholas J. Mayhall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过结合短时泡利传播（Pauli propagation）数据与基于正定性条件和特征频率的时间延拓方法，克服了泡利算符指数增长的瓶颈，实现了对一维和二维量子系统中实时两点时序关联函数（time-ordered correlator）的经典模拟计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泡利传播方法在计算实时间关联函数时受到泡利算符数量快速增长的严重限制，导致可计算的时窗很短。该工作旨在克服这一指数增长瓶颈，将经典方法可触及的实时动力学范围延拓到更长时间尺度。

Method: 首先通过泡利传播在海森堡绘景（Heisenberg picture）中获得短时间的精确关联函数数据；然后利用正定性条件（positivity condition）以及系统动力学由少数特征频率主导的观测结果，设计时间延拓方法，将短时数据外推至更长时间窗口，从而避免泡利算符的指数增殖。

Result: 所提出的组合方法能够将关联函数延拓至远超直接可计算的时间窗口，同时避免了泡利算符的指数增长；延拓后的关联函数保留了相关的动力学信息和谱信息。

Conclusion: 该工作拓宽了经典方法能够可靠探测相互作用量子多体系统实时动力学的范围，为经典模拟量子动力学提供了新的可行途径。

Abstract: Pauli propagation has shown promise for classically simulating quantum dynamics by evolving observables directly in the Heisenberg picture. In this work, we investigate its use for computing real-time two-point time-ordered correlators in one- and two-dimensional quantum systems. One major limitation of Pauli propagation is the rapid growth in the number of Pauli strings beyond short times. We overcome this limitation by combining accurate short-time Pauli-propagation data with time extension methods based on a positivity condition and the observation that the dynamics are often dominated by a small number of characteristic frequencies. This combined approach extends correlation functions far beyond the directly accessible time window while avoiding the exponential proliferation of Pauli operators. We demonstrate that the resulting correlators retain the relevant dynamical and spectral information. Our results broaden the regime in which classical methods can reliably probe the real-time dynamics of interacting quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [157] [Resolving Structure in Prethermal Floquet Dynamics with Precision Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24937)
*Eyal Leviatan,Tasneem Watad,Roy Perry,Lukas Broers,Mohammed Zuhair Mullath,Ori Alberton,Itai Arad,Yosi Atia,Eyal Bairey,Shaul Barkan,Matan Ben Dov,Asaf Berkovitch,Ewout van den Berg,Itsik Cohen,Omri Golan,Ilya Gurwich,Avieli Haber,Barak A. Katzir,Oded Kenneth,Roei Levi,Yotam Y. Lifshitz,Yaron Lukovsky,Ron Melcer,Adiel Meyer,Boris Muratov,Aviad Panahi,Gili Schul,Tali Shnaider,Maor Shutman,Alireza Seif,Tomonori Shirakawa,Asif Sinay,Vincent P. Su,Hayk Tepanyan,Omri Trebitch,Assaf Zubida,Dorit Aharonov,Hrant Gharibyan,Abhinav Kandala,Seiji Yunoki,Netanel H. Lindner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用IBM Heron r3超导量子处理器和QESEM错误缓解软件，在多达74量子比特的Floquet Ising磁体中实现了百分比精度的磁化动力学测量，观察到了长寿命的次谐波预热振荡（subharmonic prethermal oscillations），并通过有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）发现振荡幅度随系统尺寸下降异常缓慢，为这种振荡响应在热力学极限下的持续存在提供了有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动的相互作用量子多体系统（periodically driven interacting quantum many-body systems）可以表现出长寿命的预热动力学（prethermal dynamics），但经典方法在所需系统尺寸和时间尺度下变得不可靠，而量子硬件中的噪声会降低观测期望值的精度，因此亟需一种方法突破这些限制，以确定预热态的物理性质。

Method: 在重六角格点（heavy-hex lattice）上实现Floquet Ising磁体；使用IBM Heron r3超导量子处理器结合QESEM高级错误缓解软件，测量多达74量子比特的磁化动力学（magnetization dynamics）；采用有限尺寸标度分析；在Quantinuum System Model H2和Quantinuum Helios捕获离子硬件（trapped-ion hardware）上进行跨平台验证。

Result: 以百分比精度解析了长寿命的次谐波预热振荡；实验达到了领先张量网络（tensor-network）模拟无法收敛、稀疏Pauli路径模拟（sparse Pauli-path simulations）存在强截断依赖性的区域；发现振荡幅度随系统尺寸下降异常缓慢，表明该振荡响应在重六角梯子（heavy-hex ladders）的热力学极限下持续存在。

Conclusion: 通过一系列缓解与验证测试，包括无偏错误缓解、独立缓解估计量的一致性、超导硬件上的噪声模型验证以及跨平台验证，证明了研究结果的可靠性。这项工作确立了经过错误缓解的量子处理器（error-mitigated quantum processors）作为定量科学仪器在非平衡量子物质中发现新物理的能力。

Abstract: Periodically driven interacting quantum many-body systems can exhibit long-lived prethermal dynamics, where local observables retain coherent structure even as entanglement and operator complexity grow. Accessing this regime at the system sizes and times needed to determine physical properties of the prethermal state remains a central challenge: state-of-the-art classical methods become unreliable, while noise in quantum hardware degrades observable expectation values. Here we overcome these limitations for a Floquet Ising magnet realized on a heavy-hex lattice. Using the advanced error mitigation software QESEM on an IBM Heron r3 superconducting quantum processor, we measure magnetization dynamics with percent-level precision and resolve long-lived subharmonic prethermal oscillations in systems of up to 74 qubits. These experiments reach regimes for which leading tensor-network simulations fail to converge, while sparse Pauli-path simulations remain strongly truncation dependent despite extensive computations on advanced GPUs and the Fugaku supercomputer. Leveraging this quantum-accessible regime, we extend finite-size scaling to larger systems and find an unexpectedly slow decrease of the oscillation amplitude with system size, providing strong evidence that this oscillatory response persists in the thermodynamic limit of heavy-hex ladders. A hierarchy of mitigation and validation tests, including unbiased error mitigation, agreement between independent mitigation estimators, noise-model validation on the superconducting hardware, and cross-platform corroboration at selected Floquet cycles on Quantinuum System Model H2 and Quantinuum Helios trapped-ion hardware, supports the reliability of these findings. Our work establishes error-mitigated quantum processors as quantitative scientific instruments for discovering new physics in non-equilibrium quantum matter.

</details>


### [158] [Numerical Modeling of Quasiparticle-Induced Dissipation in Fluxonium Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24946)
*Kate Azar,Max Hays,Kyle Serniak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值分析发现，通量量子比特（fluxonium qubit）在原本被认为受到保护免受准粒子（quasiparticle, QP）诱导耗散影响的偏置点（bias points）上，实际上对QP诱导误差仍然敏感；仅当考虑结两侧超导能隙（superconducting gap）的不对称性时，保护才能恢复。此外，对于光子辅助隧穿产生的QP，T₁保护仅对特定能量的配对破缺辐射（pair-breaking radiation）成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡准粒子（nonequilibrium quasiparticles）由杂散红外辐射和电离辐射产生，会限制超导量子处理器的性能并给量子纠错方案带来挑战。现有模型通常假设QP特征能量和量子比特跃迁能量均远小于超导能隙，据此推断通量量子比特（fluxonium）在特定偏置点可免受QP诱导耗散的影响。本文旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 作者通过数值分析，针对不同的QP能量分布以及通过光子辅助隧穿（photon-assisted tunneling）过程产生的QP，计算了通量量子比特中QP诱导耗散的预测速率。

Result: 发现考虑微小数值因子后，现有理论模型在原本被认为受保护的偏置点预测出对QP诱导误差的敏感性；结两侧超导能隙的不对称性（asymmetry）可以恢复对QP诱导弛豫的抑制；对于光子辅助隧穿产生的QP，T₁保护仅对特定能量的配对破缺辐射成立。

Conclusion: 本文揭示的通量量子比特对QP诱导耗散的敏感性认知，为基于通量量子比特的处理器开发以及未来的准粒子缓解策略（QP-mitigation strategies）提供了参考。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium quasiparticles (QPs) generated by stray infrared and ionizing radiation can limit the performance of superconducting quantum processors and present challenges for quantum error correction schemes. Models of QP-induced energy relaxation commonly assume that the characteristic energy of the QPs and the qubit transition energy are both small relative to the superconducting gap. Under these assumptions, certain qubits such as the fluxonium would exhibit protection against QP-induced dissipation at specific bias points. Here, we show that this is not necessarily the case, numerically analyzing the predicted rate of QP-induced dissipation in fluxonium qubits for different QP energy distributions and for QPs created via photon-assisted tunneling processes. We find that accounting for small numerical factors, existing theoretical models predict sensitivity to QP-induced errors at bias points previously thought to be protected. We find that inclusion of asymmetry in the superconducting gap energy across the junction can reintroduce suppression of QP-induced relaxation, as expected. Additionally, for QPs created by photon-assisted tunneling, we predict that $T_1$ protection will only occur for a specific energy of pair-breaking radiation. This understanding of fluxonium sensitivity to QP-induced dissipation informs the development of fluxonium-based processors and future QP-mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [159] [Non-Hermitian entropy production from fluctuation theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24961)
*Vasco Cavina,Frank Ernesto Quintela Rodríguez,Donato Farina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于后选择涨落定理（post-selected fluctuation theorem），建立了一个非厄米动力学（non-Hermitian dynamics）的第一性原理热力学框架。识别出一个在整个非厄米演化过程中保持正定的量，可解释为后选择动力学的熵产生（entropy production）。将该量与已有非厄米熵概念关联，推导出相应的第二定律，并建立与信息论量（特别是Petz-Rényi散度）的联系以给出上下界。进一步将熵产生分解为非相干项与相干项，识别出奇异点（exceptional points）附近相干项的独特特征，并通过两能级系统模型加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米量子系统中热力学行为的严格理论框架尚不完善，尤其是如何在后选择（post-selection）条件下定义熵产生并建立热力学第二定律。

Method: 从后选择版本的涨落定理出发，构建第一性原理热力学框架；识别并定义后选择动力学的熵产生；建立该量与Petz-Rényi散度（Petz-Rényi divergences）等信息论量的联系以推导上下界；将熵产生分解为非相干（incoherent）与相干（coherent）贡献。

Result: 发现一个在后选择非厄米演化中始终保持正定的量，可视为熵产生；该熵产生满足第二定律（second law）；利用与Petz-Rényi散度的关系得到了熵产生的严格上下界；在奇异点（exceptional points）附近，相干项表现出独特的特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为非厄米动力学提供了一个热力学一致的第一性原理框架，揭示了后选择演化中熵产生的物理意义及其信息论基础，并指出了奇异点附近热力学行为的特殊性。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles thermodynamic framework for non-Hermitian dynamics based on a post-selected version of the fluctuation theorem. This allows us to identify a quantity that remains positive throughout the non-Hermitian evolution and can be interpreted as the entropy production of the post-selected dynamics. We relate this quantity to previously proposed notions of non-Hermitian entropy and derive an associated second law. Furthermore, we establish a connection with information-theoretic quantities, in particular the Petz-Rényi divergences, and leverage this connection to derive upper and lower bounds. Finally, we decompose the entropy production into incoherent and coherent contributions, identifying distinctive features of the coherent term in the vicinity of exceptional points. We illustrate our results using a paradigmatic model of non-Hermitian evolution based on a two-level system.

</details>


### [160] [A Kernel-Based Density of States Estimator for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24972)
*Julio Cesar Siqueira Rocha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Rodeo算法作为量子版核多项式方法（KPM），通过Haar随机初态平均获得态密度（DoS）的量子重构方案，仅需单辅助比特电路，且量子典型性保证了随希尔伯特空间维度增大统计误差自动抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的态密度（density of states, DoS）编码了热力学与谱性质，但在希尔伯特空间过大无法对角化时，经典重构变得困难。

Method: 利用Rodeo算法（一种适用于近程量子硬件的最简本征值定位协议），对Haar随机输入态的Rodeo响应取平均，得到与谱核卷积后的DoS。随机态扮演随机迹估计（stochastic trace estimation）角色，时间采样分布则对应阻尼核（damping kernel）。该方法仅需标准单辅助比特（single-ancilla）电路。

Result: 推导了估计量及其不确定性，建立了信号处理窗函数（window functions）与量子重构核之间的显式对应词典，并在二维横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）和自旋-1模型上验证了该方法。

Conclusion: Rodeo算法为经典核多项式方法（KPM）提供了直接的量子类比，量子典型性（quantum typicality）保证统计误差随希尔伯特空间维度增大而抑制。

Abstract: The density of states (DoS) encodes the thermodynamic and spectral properties of quantum many-body systems, yet its reconstruction becomes intractable for Hilbert spaces too large to diagonalize. Classically, the kernel polynomial method (KPM) addresses this by combining stochastic trace estimation with a smoothing kernel. Here we show that the Rodeo algorithm---one of the simplest eigenvalue-location protocols for near-term quantum hardware---provides a direct quantum analogue of this approach. Averaging the Rodeo response over Haar-random input states yields the DoS convolved with a spectral kernel fixed entirely by the distribution of evolution times: the random states play the role of stochastic trace estimation, and the temporal sampling distribution that of the damping kernel. The construction requires only the standard single-ancilla circuit, and quantum typicality suppresses the statistical error as the Hilbert-space dimension grows. We derive the estimator and its uncertainties, establish an explicit dictionary between signal-processing window functions and quantum reconstruction kernels, and validate the method on the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising and spin-1 models.

</details>


### [161] [Invariant-based master equation applied to driven qutrit coupled to a bath and a leaky cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25005)
*Sagarika Basak,A. Javadi,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用基于Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量的主方程推广方法，处理受驱动的开放N能级（N>2）量子系统，克服了传统方法对驱动强度的限制，并展示了该方法作为统一广义框架的能力，能够描述标准近似未能捕捉的耗散新行为，为存在耗散时的量子控制应用铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主方程方法在处理受驱动开放量子系统时存在驱动强度限制，且对多能级（N>2）系统中的新耗散行为描述不足，亟需一个更通用的理论框架。

Method: 采用基于Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量的广义主方程方法（invariant-based master equation），该框架在适当简化下可退化为实验室坐标系主方程（laboratory-frame master equation）和旋转坐标系主方程（rotating-frame master equation）。

Result: 在扩展的二维以上系统中，与基态通过储库诱导退相干（reservoir-induced dephasing）耦合的额外能级会产生标准近似无法捕捉的定性新耗散行为。将该框架应用于受驱动量子点与泄漏腔（leaky cavity）耦合系统，展示了无需额外假设即可描述相关耗散动力学的能力。

Conclusion: 基于不变量的主方程提供了一个统一且广义的理论框架，能够更全面地描述开放量子系统的耗散动力学，为存在耗散条件下的量子控制（quantum control）应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We employ a generalized approach to the master equation for driven open $N$-level ($N>2$) quantum systems using Lewis-Riesenfeld invariants, which avoids the driving-strength restrictions inherent to conventional approaches. We show that the invariant-based master equation provides a unifying generalized framework, which reduces to the frequently employed laboratory-frame master equations and the less frequently employed rotating-frame master equation framework under appropriate simplifications. Extending the prototypical two-level system, we show that the inclusion of another state coupled to the ground state via reservoir-induced dephasing gives rise to qualitatively new dissipative behaviors that are, in general, not captured by standard approximations. We also apply the invariant-based master equation framework to a driven quantum dot coupled to a leaky cavity, demonstrating the framework's ability to capture relevant dissipative dynamics without additional assumptions. Our work paves the way for quantum-control applications in the presence of dissipation.

</details>


### [162] [Detecting quantum phase transitions via shallow variational quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25345)
*Ching-Yu Huang,Min-Fong Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Delta-VQE（delta variational quantum eigensolver）方法研究量子临界性（quantum criticality）探测，发现在使用对偶变分拟设（dual ansätze）时该方法总是检测到自对偶点（self-dual points）而非真实临界点；只有当变分拟设（ansätze）被精心设计以体现竞争相时，才能成功识别真实的伊辛临界点（Ising critical point），且仅存在微小的有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相图（quantum phase diagram）的经典模拟在计算上极其耗费资源，即使远离热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）的小系统也需要巨大的计算代价。Delta-VQE作为一种资源节约型的量子临界性探测方案已被提出，但其更广泛的适用性和内在机制尚未被充分理解。

Method: 研究基于一维横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）并引入三自旋簇相互作用（three-spin cluster interaction），在此设定下伊辛相变通常位于自对偶线（self-dual line）之外。研究比较了两种变分拟设策略：采用对偶变分拟设（dual ansätze）与针对竞争相精心定制的变分拟设。

Result: 当使用对偶变分拟设时，Delta-VQE始终检测到自对偶点而非真实临界点；当变分拟设被精心设计以体现跨边界竞争相时，该方法能成功识别真实伊辛临界点，且仅伴随微小的有限尺寸效应。

Conclusion: Delta-VQE提供了一种资源高效的量子临界性探测方案，无需精确的基态制备（ground-state preparation），但其诊断能力根本上依赖于对物理上具有代表性的变分拟设进行审慎选择。

Abstract: Mapping quantum phase diagrams through classical simulation is notoriously resource-intensive, as even small systems far from the thermodynamic limit demand prohibitive computational effort. The variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) offers a compelling alternative, exploiting approximate ground states to distinguish phases. An appealing proposal, dubbed as Delta-VQE, determines critical points by contrasting variational energies optimized from reference states of distinct phases. Intriguingly, the diagnostic sharpens as circuit depth decreases, highlighting its promise as a resource-conscious probe of quantum criticality. To probe the broader applicability and underlying mechanisms of this approach, we investigate the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model with a three-spin cluster interaction, a setting in which the Ising transitions are generally situated beyond the self-dual line. We demonstrate that, whenever dual ansätze are employed, Delta-VQE invariably detects the self-dual points rather than the true criticality. In contrast, when ansätze are carefully tailored to embody the competing phases across the boundary, the genuine Ising critical point can be successfully identified with only minor finite-size effects. Our results establish that, while Delta-VQE provides a resource-efficient probe of quantum criticality without requiring precise ground-state preparation, its diagnostic power is fundamentally contingent upon the judicious selection of physically representative ansätze.

</details>


### [163] [Interaction-Endowed PT-Symmetry and its Effects on Decoherence, Einselection, and Non-Markovianity in a Central Spin Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25561)
*Leoj Phoebe M. Esquierdo,Lemuel John F. Sese,Rayda P. Gammag*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将PT对称性（PT-symmetry）引入中心自旋模型（central spin model），通过可调厄米性参数γ的非厄米相互作用，利用伪厄米形式论（pseudo-Hermitian formalism）和Dyson映射将非厄米哈密顿量转化为厄米表示。研究发现：退相干（decoherence）随γ增大而减慢，并在γ=1时完全停止；定义了与度规算符（metric operator）对易的伪厄米可观测量作为自哈密顿量（self-Hamiltonian），系统在γ→1时逐渐选择其本征态；强环境区域中中心自旋稳态纯度（steady-state purity）出现反常下降，但存在对应最大信息耗散（information dissipation）的转折点（最小值）；Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) 非马尔可夫性度量（non-Markovianity measure）在强环境区域展现类似转折行为，信息回流（information backflow）在达到饱和点后下降，标志着PT对称性开始屏蔽中心自旋免受环境影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索PT对称性在中心自旋模型中的引入对量子退相干（quantum decoherence）和环境诱导的量子达尔文主义（environment-induced quantum Darwinism）等基本量子过程的影响机制，揭示非厄米哈密顿量（non-Hermitian Hamiltonian）与开放量子系统动力学之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过添加含可调厄米性参数γ的PT对称相互作用，将PT对称性引入中心自旋模型；采用伪厄米形式论，借助Dyson映射（Dyson map）将非厄米哈密顿量变换为其厄米表示；定义了与度规算符对易的伪厄米可观测量作为自哈密顿量；使用BLP度量评估有限时间内的非马尔可夫性。

Result: 退相干速率随γ增大而减慢，并在γ=1时完全停止；在γ→1极限下，环境诱导的超选择（einselection）迫使系统选择自哈密顿量的本征态；强环境区域中中心自旋的稳态纯度随γ增大出现反常下降，但存在对应最大信息耗散至自旋浴（spin bath）的转折点（最小值）；BLP度量在强环境区域中展现出相似的转折点行为，即信息回流先达到饱和点后下降。

Conclusion: PT对称性可以调控中心自旋模型的退相干动力学，γ参数的增加能有效抑制退相干；当γ足够大时，PT对称性开始屏蔽（shield）中心自旋免受环境干扰，这一屏蔽效应体现在BLP度量转折点之后信息回流的衰减上。

Abstract: We have introduced PT-symmetry to a central spin model by adding a PT-symmetric interaction with a tunable hermiticity parameter $γ$. Using the pseudo-Hermitian formalism, we applied a Dyson map to transform the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian to its Hermitian representation. We have found that the decoherence slows down as $γ$ increases, eventually ceasing at $γ=1$. We define a pseudo-Hermitian observable that commutes with the metric operator to add as the self-Hamiltonian. Einselection gradually forced the system to select the eigenstates of the self-Hamiltonian as $γ\rightarrow1$. The steady-state purity of the central spin states in the strong environment regime exhibits a paradoxical decrease as $γ$ increases. However, a turning point (minimum) corresponding to a maximum information dissipation to the spin bath is found. Finally, the Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) measure, which is used to quantify the non-Markovianity of a quantum system, was evaluated for a finite time. The BLP measure in the strong environment regime exhibited similar turning point behavior, which means that the information backflow reaches a saturation point before declining. This decline signifies the point where PT-symmetry starts shielding the central spin from the environment.

</details>


### [164] [Lowering the implementation barrier of neutral-atom quantum computing with agentic workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25834)
*Constantin Dalyac,Alexandre Dauphin,Loïc Henriet,Christophe Jurczak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于智能体（agentic）的工作流，能够自动化地将理论量子协议转化为中性原子量子处理器（neutral-atom quantum processor）上的硬件实验，同时保留研究人员对关键环节的验证。通过多体物理与优化三个案例验证，该工作流可在无人值守下完成从论文/专利到云量子处理单元（QPU）运行的流程，并利用第二个智能体对633篇Rydberg阵列论文进行分类，发现近半数论文可在当前量子处理单元上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将理论量子协议转化为硬件实验需要跨领域专长（协议设计、编译、模拟、云执行），这一过程当前构成量子计算发展的主要瓶颈。

Method: 构建一种智能体工作流（agentic workflow），自动执行从量子协议设计到编译、模拟、云量子处理单元执行的全流程，同时保留人为干预进行关键验证。另使用独立的第二个智能体对633篇Rydberg阵列arXiv论文进行可实施性分类。

Result: 在三个多体物理与优化案例中，智能体可在过夜时间内自主完成从已发表论文或专利到量子处理单元运行的全流程；人类专家的关键干预发现了智能体选择的不可靠可观测量（observable）以及看似合理但错误的硬件诊断（hardware diagnosis）；对633篇论文的分类显示近半数可在当前量子处理单元上实现，其余需特定硬件升级。

Conclusion: 智能体工作流为理论思想与物理硬件之间提供了实用桥梁，可大幅降低量子实验门槛，使更广泛的科学社群得以参与量子计算实验。

Abstract: Quantum computers are moving from research laboratories to industrial machines accessible via the cloud and integrated into high-performance computing facilities. However, translating theoretical quantum protocols into hardware experiments remains a major bottleneck, requiring expertise across protocol design, compilation, simulation, and cloud execution. Here, we introduce an agentic workflow that automates this pipeline on neutral-atom quantum processors (here two Pasqal QPUs available on the cloud) while keeping the researcher in the loop for critical validation. In three case studies from many-body physics and optimization, the agent went from published paper or patent to a QPU campaign run overnight. In particular, human intervention was crucial to ensure scientific validity: the agent selected an inadequate observable in one experiment and constructed a plausible but incorrect hardware diagnosis in another, with both failures detected only through domain-expert review. Finally, we use a second agent to classify a corpus of 633 Rydberg-array arXiv papers and show that nearly half are implementable on present-day QPUs while identifying specific hardware upgrades needed for the rest. Together, these results demonstrate that agentic workflows provide a practical bridge between theoretical ideas and physical hardware, opening quantum experimentation to a much broader scientific community.

</details>


### [165] [Path integral approach to the truncated Wigner approximation of driven-dissipative spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26029)
*Viktoria Noel,Michael Fleischhauer,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了开放自旋-1/2系统中截断维格纳近似（truncated Wigner approximation, TWA）的Keldysh路径积分形式，证明其与连续SU(2)相空间方法等价，为耗散自旋动力学提供了统一的场论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相空间方法（如TWA）为超出平均场水平的开放量子多体系统动力学提供了半经典模拟框架，但针对自旋系统的严格路径积分对应关系尚未建立，尤其是耗散过程的正确映射缺乏统一场论基础。

Method: 利用连续SU(2)相空间，作者为相互作用的开放自旋-1/2系统建立了Keldysh路径积分表述；特别地，通过正确地将算符乘积映射到弯曲的自旋相空间上，推导出与连续TWA一致的随机运动方程。

Result: 所建立的路径积分形式与从连续TWA得到的随机方程一致，并能够精确再现单个耗散自旋的精确动力学（exact dynamics）。该方法为TWA提供了统一的场论基础，并可作为向超越半经典近似方向扩展的系统起点。

Conclusion: 该工作为开放自旋系统的半经典模拟提供了严格的场论支撑，统一了TWA与路径积分方法，并为未来超越半经典近似的扩展奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Phase-space approaches such as the truncated Wigner approximation (TWA) provide an efficient semiclassical framework for performing approximate simulations of the dynamics of open quantum many-body systems outside the reach of exact numerical methods but beyond the mean-field level. For bosonic systems, TWA is known to be equivalent to a Keldysh path-integral formulation truncated at second order in the so-called quantum fluctuations. This semiclassical approach provides an alternative transparent route towards approximate stochastic equations of motion, which can be efficiently solved. Here we establish the corresponding path-integral formulation for interacting open spin-$1/2$ systems using the continuous $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ phase space. We show, in particular, that a consistent treatment of dissipation requires correctly mapping operator products onto the curved spin phase space, leading to stochastic equations that coincide with those obtained from the continuous TWA formulation and thus reproduce the exact dynamics of a single dissipative spin. Our results provide a unified field-theoretic foundation for the TWA to dissipative spin dynamics and offer a systematic starting point for extensions beyond the semiclassical approximation.

</details>
