### [166] [Dual-Level Atomic and Coordination Geometry Learning for Crystal Property Prediction Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24818)
*Sanjay Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CPGN（Coordination Polyhedron Graph Network），一种多尺度图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN），通过联合学习原子、键和配位多面体（coordination polyhedron）三种尺度的表示，显式建模晶体中的配位多面体结构，从而提升材料性质的预测精度与物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN（如CGCNN、MEGNet、ALIGNN、SchNet）主要在原子级别表示晶体，通过消息传递（message passing）隐式学习局部化学环境，但许多材料性质实际上由配位多面体决定。因此，需要一种能够显式建模配位多面体的方法。

Method: CPGN构建了三类耦合图：原子图（atom graph）编码元素与键合信息；线图（line graph）捕捉角度相互作用；配位多面体图（coordination polyhedron graph）通过Voronoi方法导出局部环境，描述多面体之间的角共享、边共享和面共享关系。每个多面体引入物理上有意义的几何描述符，并通过交错消息传递（interleaved message-passing）与双向交叉注意力（bidirectional cross-attention）实现跨尺度信息交换。

Result: 在Materials Project、JARVIS-DFT和QM9基准数据集上，CPGN优于现有最先进GNN模型。在Materials Project上，形成能MAE为0.060 eV/atom，带隙MAE为0.292 eV；在JARVIS-DFT上提供具有竞争力的多性质预测，在QM9上实现更优的HOMO预测。

Conclusion: 显式建模配位多面体能够改善晶体表示学习，实现准确且物理可解释的材料性质预测。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of crystal properties remains a key challenge in computational materials science. While graph neural networks (GNNs) such as CGCNN, MEGNet, ALIGNN, and SchNet have shown strong performance, they primarily represent crystals at the atomic level and implicitly learn local chemical environments through message passing. However, many material properties are governed by coordination polyhedra, the fundamental structural units formed by atoms and their neighboring atoms. To address this limitation, we propose the Coordination Polyhedron Graph Network (CPGN), a multi-scale GNN that jointly learns atomic, bond, and coordination-polyhedron representations. CPGN constructs three coupled graphs: an atom graph encoding elemental and bonding information, a line graph capturing angular interactions, and a coordination polyhedron graph describing Voronoi-derived local environments through corner-, edge-, and face-sharing relationships. Physically meaningful geometric descriptors are incorporated for each polyhedron, while an interleaved message-passing mechanism with bidirectional cross-attention enables effective information exchange across structural levels. Extensive evaluations on the Materials Project, JARVIS-DFT, and QM9 benchmark datasets demonstrate that CPGN outperforms existing state-of-the-art GNN models. It achieves a formation-energy MAE of 0.060 eV/atom and a band-gap MAE of 0.292 eV on the Materials Project, while providing competitive multi-property prediction on JARVIS-DFT and superior HOMO prediction on QM9. The results highlight that explicit modeling of coordination polyhedra improves crystal representation learning and enables accurate, physically interpretable prediction of material properties.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [An adaptive phase field framework for large-scale interface evolution problems using a strong-form gradient smoothing approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25142)
*Zirui Mao,Alice Xu,Shenyang Hu,Panos Stinis,Yuyan Shao,Ang Li,Yulan Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合梯度平滑方法（Gradient Smoothing Method, GSM）与分层自适应移动结构化网格的强形式相场求解器，能够在保持二阶精度的同时实现近线性计算复杂度 O(N)，显著优于均匀网格方法的二次复杂度 O(N²)，适用于大规模薄界面演化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型（phase-field model）在计算力学和材料建模中模拟界面主导现象时，由于需在全域解析薄扩散界面（thin diffuse interface），导致计算成本极高，难以应用于大规模问题。

Method: 将梯度平滑方法（GSM）与分层自适应移动结构化网格（hierarchical adaptive and moving structured mesh）耦合，在界面区域自动局部加密，在体区域保持粗网格；引入分层细化设计（layered refinement design）以保持界面附近局部均匀分辨率，使 GSM 离散化在网格非均匀条件下仍维持整体二阶精度。

Result: 基于 Allen-Cahn 和 Cahn-Hilliard 方程的数值算例表明，该自适应 GSM 求解器在达到所需界面演化精度的同时，计算复杂度为 O(N)（近线性），而均匀网格方法为 O(N²)（二次），对于薄界面或界面面积分数较小的大规模问题效率显著提升。

Conclusion: 尽管 GSM 每自由度成本高于标准有限差分格式，但自适应框架大幅降低了总自由度数量，使该方法在大规模相场模拟中兼具精度与效率优势，优于现有弱形式（weak-form）和强形式（strong-form）求解器。

Abstract: Multiscale problems with evolving interfaces are ubiquitous in science and engineering. Phase-field models are a powerful tool for simulating interface-dominated phenomena in computational mechanics and materials modeling, but their application to large-scale problems is often constrained by the high computational cost of resolving thin diffuse interfaces over the entire domain. This paper presents an efficient strong-form phase-field solver that couples the Gradient Smoothing Method (GSM) with a hierarchical adaptive and moving structured mesh, enabling automatic localization of resolution within a narrow interfacial region while retaining coarse discretization in bulk domains. A layered refinement design is introduced to preserve locally uniform resolution across the interface, allowing the GSM discretization to maintain overall second-order accuracy despite strong mesh non-uniformity away from the interface. Although GSM incurs a higher per-degree-of-freedom cost than standard finite-difference schemes, the adaptive framework substantially reduces the total number of degrees of freedom, resulting in near-linear computational scaling compared with the quadratic scaling of uniform-grid approaches. Numerical examples based on the Allen-Cahn and Cahn-Hilliard equations demonstrate that the proposed adaptive GSM solver delivers desired accuracy for interface evolution while attaining more favorable computational complexity, O(N), than existing weak-form and strong-form solvers, becoming significantly more efficient for large-scale problems with thin interfaces or a small interfacial area fraction relative to the whole domain.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [No Gelation and Global Existence for a Boltzmann Equation with Regularly Varying Mass-Exchange Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25112)
*Siwei Luo,Jian-Guo Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对Grad截断硬势（Grad-cutoff hard potentials）下带有质量交换（mass exchange）的空间均匀玻尔兹曼方程（spatially homogeneous Boltzmann equation），提出了一种无凝胶化机制（no-gelation mechanism），证明了在有限物理矩（finite physical moments）条件下全局积分弱解（global integral weak solution）的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有质量交换的玻尔兹曼方程在Grad截断硬势条件下可能出现的有限时间质量逃逸（finite-time mass escape to infinity）或凝胶化（gelation）问题，即在无需假设更高阶质量矩（higher mass moment）的前提下建立全局解。

Method: 构造了一个由二项铰链（dyadic hinges）组成的凸超线性权重（convex superlinear weight），利用正则变化（regular variation）使两种碰撞构型下的符号增量（signed increments）可比，并通过质量守恒（mass conservation）及条件γ<1获得消失因子L^{γ-1}，从而在质量截断近似（mass-cutoff approximations）下获得一致矩有界性（uniform moment bound）。

Result: 对于任意具有有限物理矩的非负初始密度（nonnegative initial density），在每一有限时间区间上获得了质量截断近似的一致矩有界性，排除了有限时间质量逃逸，最终得到了全局积分弱解。

Conclusion: 所提出的无凝胶化机制适用于Grad截断硬势与正则变化质量交换率的一般情形，无需高阶质量矩假设即可保证全局弱解的存在性，为带质量交换的玻尔兹曼方程理论提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: We develop a no-gelation mechanism that yields global solutions to the spatially homogeneous Boltzmann equation with mass exchange for Grad-cutoff hard potentials $B=E^γb(ξ), 0<γ<1$ and regularly varying mass-exchange rates. Without assuming any higher mass moment, we construct a convex superlinear weight assembled from dyadic hinges. Production of the weighted moment by collisions between large particles of comparable mass is absorbed by dissipation through collisions with a uniformly populated reservoir of bounded-mass particles. Regular variation makes the signed increments in these two collision configurations comparable, while mass conservation and $γ<1$ yield the vanishing factor $L^{γ-1}$. This yields a uniform moment bound of the mass-cutoff approximations on every finite time interval and rules out finite-time mass escape to infinity. Consequently, for every nonnegative initial density with finite physical moments, we obtain a global integral weak solution.

</details>


### [169] [Homogenization of Time-Discrete Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25206)
*Yunhong Deng,Yuan Gao,Li Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究平环上Fokker–Planck方程在时间离散情形下，采用输运型度量和加权L2度量两种极小化移动方案（minimizing movement scheme）的均匀化极限（homogenization limit）。结果表明，度量选择至关重要：两种方案在尺度参数趋零时导致不同的均匀化极限，进而当时间步长趋于零时收敛到不同的演化方程。其中，仅加权L2度量方案能够恢复正确的有效动力学（effective dynamics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时间离散化中度量选择对Fokker–Planck方程在快速振荡扩散系数下的均匀化极限的影响，并验证哪种离散方案能够正确恢复连续极限下的有效动力学。

Method: 在平环（flat torus）上对具有快速振荡扩散系数的Fokker–Planck方程进行时间离散化，采用极小化移动方案，分别比较输运型度量（transport-type metric）与加权L2度量（weighted L2 metric）两种选择；然后分析尺度参数趋于零时的均匀化极限，再令时间步长趋于零得到演化方程。

Result: 两种度量方案在均匀化极限下给出不同的结果，进而导出不同的演化方程；仅加权L2度量方案能够恢复正确的有效动力学。

Conclusion: 在时间离散框架下，极小化移动方案中度量选择的差异会实质性地改变Fokker–Planck方程的均匀化极限，只有加权L2度量方案与连续极限的正确行为一致。

Abstract: In this work, we study the homogenization limit for Fokker--Planck equations on the flat torus with rapidly oscillating diffusion coefficients at the time-discrete level. We discretize the equation in time using a minimizing movement scheme and compare two choices of metric: a transport-type metric and a weighted L2 metric. We prove that this choice is crucial: the two schemes lead to different homogenization limits as the scaling parameter tends to zero, and consequently to different evolution equations as the time step is sent to zero. In particular, only the scheme based on the weighted L2 metric recovers the correct effective dynamics.

</details>


### [170] [Well-posed homogenized strain-gradient models for linear elastodynamics and elastostatics in arbitrary periodic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25602)
*Rémi Cornaggia,Marc Bonnet,Giuseppe Rosi,Saad El Ouafa,Nicolas Auffray*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对周期介质中的线弹性静力学与动力学问题，发展了适定的均质化应变梯度模型（strain-gradient model），重点解决弹性波传播问题。通过二阶周期均质化结合Boussinesq技巧，将原本病态的四阶偏微分方程转化为具有对称性、正定性和强制性的应变梯度弹性（strain-gradient elasticity, SGE）模型，并利用Hille-Yosida定理建立了瞬态问题的适定性。数值算例验证了模型捕捉各向异性与色散效应的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典均质化（homogenization）方法在超越主导阶（leading order）时通常产生病态（ill-posed）的有效算子，导致模型在数学上不适定，无法可靠用于弹性波传播模拟。本文旨在发展一种既能够捕捉高阶效应（如色散和各向异性）又同时保证数学适定性的均质化应变梯度模型。

Method: 采用经典的两尺度渐近展开方法（two-scale asymptotic expansion method）对$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d=2,3$）中的周期介质进行二阶周期均质化，对周期胞元几何或材料分布无限制。利用互易恒等式（reciprocity identities）减少需要求解的胞元问题数量。引入Boussinesq技巧（Boussinesq-trick procedure），通过一个可调标量权重将四阶偏微分方程重新表述为具有所需对称性、正定性（sign-definiteness）和强制性（coercivity）的有效应变梯度弹性模型，并借助Hille-Yosida定理证明瞬态初值问题与受迫响应问题的适定性（well-posedness）。

Result: 互易恒等式显著削减了胞元问题的求解数量。得到的SGE模型满足对称性、正定性和强制性要求。针对三种二维周期胞元（正方形、六边形及非中心对称手性晶格）的数值算例表明，该模型能准确捕捉超越经典弹性的各向异性与色散效应，色散关系与Floquet-Bloch参考计算结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 本文建立的均质化应变梯度模型在保证数学适定性的前提下，成功将高阶周期性均质化理论拓展至弹性波传播问题，为周期介质中的色散与各向异性分析提供了可靠的理论框架。该方法的通用性（适用于任意胞元几何与材料分布）以及计算效率的提升使其具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This work develops well-posed homogenized strain-gradient models for linear elastostatics and elastodynamics in periodic media, with a primary focus on elastic wave propagation. Using the classical two-scale asymptotic expansion method, we carry out second-order periodic homogenization for media in $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($d = 2, 3$), with no restriction on the periodicity cell geometry or material distribution. Reciprocity identities applied to suitably chosen pairs of cell solutions provide alternative expressions for the effective stiffness and inertia tensors arising at the leading, first and second orders, substantially reducing the number of cell problems that must actually be solved. Since direct two-scale homogenization beyond leading order generically yields ill-posed effective operators, a Boussinesq-trick procedure is introduced, involving a tunable scalar weight, to recast the resulting fourth-order partial differential equation as a valid strain-gradient elasticity (SGE) model possessing the requisite symmetry, sign-definiteness and coercivity properties. These properties are then used, via the Hille-Yosida theorem, to establish the well-posedness of the corresponding transient initial-value and forced-response problems. Several practically relevant special cases are examined, including centrosymmetric cells, homogeneous mass density and homogeneous elasticity, each yielding simplified model structures. Numerical illustrations on three two-dimensional periodicity cells (square, hexagonal and a non-centrosymmetric chiral lattice) compare the resulting dispersion relations against reference Floquet-Bloch computations and assess transient wave propagation, demonstrating the model's capacity to capture anisotropic and dispersive effects beyond classical elasticity while preserving mathematical well-posedness.

</details>


### [171] [Long-wave instability of periodic shear flows with constant magnetic field for the 2D resistive MHD equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25836)
*Roberto Feola,Luca Franzoi,Riccardo Montalto,Claudia Peña*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维粘性、电阻性磁流体动力学（Magnetohydrodynamic, MHD）方程在周期域上围绕剪切流与恒定背景磁场的长波线性稳定性与不稳定性，给出了显式条件并证明了解在Sobolev范数下的指数增长或衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Colombo等人针对Navier-Stokes方程的长波稳定性分析非平凡地推广到磁流体动力学（MHD）方程，延续Kolmogorov、Meshalkin、Sinai及Yudovich等经典工作的思路。

Method: 采用非微扰归一化变换（non-perturbative normal form transformation）将零傅里叶模式与非零模式解耦，并结合针对参数α下从未扰动零特征值分岔出的特征值的渐近展开（asymptotic expansions）。

Result: 建立了涉及粘度ν、电阻率η及背景磁场分量b1,b2的剪切流形貌U(y)显式条件，在α≪1参数区域内获得了长波线性稳定与不稳定的判别准则；相空间分裂为不稳定子空间与稳定子空间。

Conclusion: 在Sobolev范数下，解在稳定子空间上指数衰减，在不稳定子空间上指数增长，揭示了背景磁场与耗散参数对长波动力学行为的关键影响。

Abstract: We investigate the long-wave linear stability and instability of the two-dimensional viscous, resistive Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) equations, in vorticity-current formulation, on the periodic domain ${\mathbb T}_α\times {\mathbb T} = \Big( {\mathbb R}/(\frac{2 π}α {\mathbb Z}) \times {\mathbb R}/(2 π{\mathbb Z}) \Big)$, around a periodic shear flow $(U(y),0)$ coupled with a constant background magnetic field ${\bf b}=({\rm b}_1,{\rm b}_2)$. It is a non-trivial extension of a recent paper for the Navier-Stokes equations by Colombo, Dolce, Montalto & Ventura to the MHD setting in the spirit of the classical works of Kolmogorov, Meshalkin, Sinai and Yudovich. We establish explicit conditions on the shear flow profile $U(y)$ involving the viscosity $ν$, the resistivity $η$ and the components of the background magnetic field ${\bf b}$ to obtain linear long-wave stability and instability in the regime $α\ll 1$. The proof combines a non-perturbative normal form transformation decoupling the zero Fourier mode from the non-zero modes with sharp asymptotic expansions of the eigenvalues bifurcating from the zero unperturbed eigenvalue with respect to the parameter $α$. As a dynamical consequence, we obtain a splitting of the phase space into unstable and stable subspaces, on which solutions grow or decay exponentially in Sobolev norm.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Many-body Tipping Dynamics of ChatGPT-like AIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25279)
*Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示，ChatGPT类AI在确定性贪心解码（deterministic greedy decoding）下产生有害、误导或重复等不良输出，其根本原因是token（自旋）在有限层系统中跨越时的多体相互作用（many-body interactions）。这种倾倒行为表现为输出盆地间的动态首过过程（first passage process），注意力无序（attention disorder）控制着盆地边界附近的输运方向。通过少数盆地约化（few-basin reduction）得到封闭的有限层阈值，其粗粒化预测与多个ChatGPT类模型吻合良好。研究结论认为，此类AI故障是可预测的工程风险，而非本质上不可预测的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT类AI在架构和训练方式存在显著差异的情况下，仍会在确定性解码条件下意外产生有害、误导或重复等不良内容。该现象背后的统一机制尚不清楚，需要从物理视角进行解释。

Method: 将token类比为自旋（spin），将有限层Transformer建模为多体相互作用系统；将倾倒行为建模为输出盆地间的动态首过过程；引入注意力无序（attention disorder）概念描述输运方向的调控机制；通过少数盆地约化（few-basin reduction）得到封闭形式的有限层阈值，并进行粗粒化预测。

Result: 粗粒化预测结果在多个ChatGPT类模型家族中均表现出良好的一致性。注意力无序可以调控系统向盆地边界靠近、远离或沿边界运动。

Conclusion: 一类广泛的AI故障是“可预见的工程风险”（foreseeable engineering risk），而非本质上不可预测的行为。该发现对AI危害的法律和社会评估具有重要启示。

Abstract: Why do ChatGPT-like AIs, despite major architectural and training differences, unexpectedly tip to undesirable content (e.g. harmful, misleading, repetitive) even under deterministic greedy decoding? We show that a broad class of such tippings is caused by the many-body interactions between tokens (spins) as they cross the finite-layer system. Tipping emerges as a dynamical first passage process between competing output basins. Attention disorder controls the transport toward, away from, or along the basins' boundary. A few-basin reduction yields a closed finite-layer threshold, whose coarse-grained predictions show good agreement across ChatGPT-like families. These results suggest that a broad class of AI failures represents 'foreseeable engineering risk' rather than inherently unpredictable behavior, with important implications for legal and societal assessments of AI harm.

</details>


### [173] [Steering topology distributions for unified generative design of architected metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24777)
*Haolin Li,Yuyang Miao,Menglei Li,Jinshuai Bai,Liyuan Wang,Xin Liu,Bo Gao,Jiantao Liu,Danilo Mandic,Zahra Sharif Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi,Weiqiu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式拓扑优化（Generative Topology Optimization, GenTO）框架，通过在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型（diffusion model）获得可复用的拓扑先验知识（topology prior），并利用用户定义的物理目标与约束迭代引导拓扑分布朝向任务特定的高性能区域，从而将优化对象从单一结构转变为任务适配的拓扑分布。在热极值、多目标形态控制、属性定向负泊松比设计及振动传输设计等多个问题上，GenTO 复用了预训练先验，保持了结构多样性，并达到了经数值基准与实验验证的高性能解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超材料设计方法通常针对单一设计问题定制，缺乏对拓扑知识的有效重用，难以在目标、约束和物理功能变化时实现广泛适用的设计。

Method: GenTO 首先在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型，学习通用的拓扑先验分布（topology prior distribution）；然后在推理阶段，通过用户定义的物理目标函数与约束条件，迭代地对拓扑分布进行引导（steering），使其向任务特定的高性能区域偏移，最终实现从单一结构优化到任务适配拓扑分布优化的转变。

Result: 在热极值、多目标形态控制、属性定向负泊松比设计与振动传输设计等异构拓扑优化任务中，GenTO 复用了预训练的拓扑先验，在保持结构多样性的同时获得了高性能解，并通过数值基准和实验验证证实了有效性。

Conclusion: 可复用的拓扑知识（reusable topology knowledge）可作为实现高效、可扩展的构型超材料设计的统一原则，从而建立起通用化设计的理论基础。

Abstract: Architected metamaterials derive their functions from structure, creating vast opportunities to program physical responses through topology design. However, existing design methods are often tailored to individual design problems, making limited use of topology knowledge for effective and broadly applicable design as objectives, constraints, and physical functions change. Here we introduce Generative Topology Optimization (GenTO), a unified framework that turns a learned topology prior into a reusable design engine. GenTO trains a diffusion model on a large full-order topology dataset and then iteratively steers the resulting topology distribution toward task-specific high-performing regions using user-defined physical objectives and constraints. This shifts the object of optimization from a single structure to a task-adapted topology distribution. Across topology design problems spanning thermal extremization, multi-objective morphology control, property-targeted auxetic design, and vibration transmission design, GenTO reuses pretrained topology priors for heterogeneous tasks, preserves structural diversity, and reaches high-performing solutions supported by numerical benchmarks and experimental validation. These results establish reusable topology knowledge as a unified principle for effective and scalable architected metamaterial design.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [The Ultimate Ishii-Pastur Theorem for Whole-Line Ergodic Block Jacobi Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25808)
*Zhenfu Wang,Bei Zhang,Qi Zhou*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文建立了整条实直线（whole-line）上遍历块雅可比算子（ergodic block Jacobi operators）的终极 Ishii-Pastur 定理，证明在最小非负李雅普诺夫指数严格为正的区域上，最大谱测度（maximal spectral measure）的支集包含于一个零容度（zero capacity）的 Borel 集，并在标量情形下解决了 Damanik 与 Fillman 提出的开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为整条实直线上的遍历块雅可比算子建立一种终极形式的 Ishii-Pastur 定理，并在标量情形下解决 Damanik 与 Fillman 所提的整条实直线开问题（Problem 4.7.18）。

Method: 采用遍历块雅可比算子（ergodic block Jacobi operators）框架，结合最大谱测度（maximal spectral measure）与李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）理论，通过几乎处处的实现（almost every realization）进行分析，将谱测度的支集约束在零容度（zero capacity）Borel 集上。

Result: 对于几乎所有的实现，在最小非负李雅普诺夫指数严格为正的区域上，最大谱测度的支集包含于一个零容度的 Borel 集。该结果在标量（scalar）情形下完整解决了整条实直线上的相关开问题。

Conclusion: 建立的终极 Ishii-Pastur 定理为遍历块雅可比算子的谱分析提供了更紧的测度支集刻画，并解决了标量情形下长期悬而未决的整条实直线问题。

Abstract: We establish an ultimate Ishii-Pastur theorem for whole-line ergodic block Jacobi operators. We prove that, for almost every realization, the restriction of a maximal spectral measure to the region where the smallest nonnegative Lyapunov exponent is strictly positive is carried by a Borel set of zero capacity. In the scalar case, this settles the whole-line problem formulated by Damanik and Fillman \cite[Problem~4.7.18]{Damanik-Fillman-book}.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Tidal deformation of an accreting compact object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24938)
*Avijit Chowdhury,Chiranjeeb Singha,Kazuharu Bamba,Sumanta Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究嵌入在准静态自引力薄吸积盘中的类史瓦西奇异紧凑天体（ECOs）的静态可变形性。通过相对论性薄盘解建模外部时空，发现完美反射ECOs的标量和自旋-1响应对致密性的对数依赖关系在吸积盘存在下保持不变，但响应的整体幅度被盘显著放大。这表明环境效应可严重影响潮汐信号，但在适当条件下仍允许在引力波观测中区分无视界紧凑天体与黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑天体通常被物质丰富的环境所包围，此类环境可能显著改变天体的潮汐响应。理解环境效应对潮汐特征的影响，对于在引力波观测中区分无视界紧凑天体（ECOs）与黑洞至关重要。

Method: 采用相对论性薄盘解（relativistic thin-disk solution）对外部时空进行建模，在保持解析可控性的前提下隔离环境对标量和自旋-1（spin-1）响应的贡献。研究中考虑嵌入在准静态自引力薄吸积盘中的类史瓦西（Schwarzschild-like）奇异紧凑天体，并假设ECO边界为完美反射。

Result: 对于完美反射ECOs，近地平线物理决定的标量和自旋-1响应对致密性（compactness）的特征对数依赖关系在吸积盘存在下保持不变。吸积盘主要对响应的整体幅度产生显著放大效应。

Conclusion: 环境效应会严重影响潮汐特征，但在适当条件下仍有可能通过引力波观测区分无地平线紧凑天体与黑洞。该结果强调了在解释引力波潮汐信号时需考虑天体周围环境的影响。

Abstract: Tidal deformation of a compact object serves as a sensitive probe of the strong-gravity regime and nature of the compact object. It captures how a compact object responds to the external perturbing field of a companion. In realistic astrophysical settings, compact objects are typically immersed in matter-rich environments, which can significantly alter the response. In this work, we investigate the static deformability of Schwarzschild-like exotic compact objects (ECOs) embedded in a quasi-stationary, self-gravitating thin accretion disk. By modelling the external spacetime with a relativistic thin-disk solution, we isolate environmental contributions to the scalar and spin-1 response while maintaining analytical control. We show that, for perfectly reflecting ECOs, the characteristic logarithmic dependence of the scalar and spin-1 response on compactness, set by near-horizon physics, remains intact even in the presence of accretion. The disk primarily amplifies the overall magnitude of the response significantly. These findings highlight that environmental effects can seriously impact tidal signatures, while still permitting, under suitable conditions, the distinguishability of horizonless compact objects from black holes in gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [176] [Fast, accurate, and differentiable: a neural-network surrogate for NRSur7dq4 precessing binary black hole waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24960)
*Michael Pürrer,Ashwin Girish,Lucy M. Thomas,Scott E. Field,Vijay Varma*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经网络代理模型（neural network surrogate model），用于模拟NRSur7dq4进动双黑洞并合的引力波波形。该代理将波形分解为构成量，并为每个量训练独立的多层感知机（MLP）。经验证，在10,000个波形上，中位天空平均频域失配度（median sky-averaged frequency-domain mismatch）在8.0×10⁻⁵到1.7×10⁻⁴之间，且在NVIDIA L40S GPU上端到端评估单个波形约需1毫秒，速度约为LALSimulation C实现的10倍，高吞吐量下可达约140倍。该工作首次将经验证的数值相对论精度、全可微分及GPU加速的推理流水线结合于进动数值相对论波形的神经网络代理中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值相对论波形模型（如NRSur7dq4）计算成本高，难以同时满足低延迟参数估计和大规模波形生成的需求。本文旨在构建一个兼具数值相对论级精度、可微分性和GPU加速的神经网络代理模型，以支持基于自动微分的梯度推断方法（如Fisher信息矩阵、梯度MCMC等）。

Method: 该代理模型将NRSur7dq4波形分解为若干构成量（constituent quantities），并为每个构成量训练一个独立的多层感知机（multilayer perceptron, MLP）。整个波形到似然（waveform-to-likelihood）流水线在JAX框架中实现，完全可微分（differentiable）。

Result: 在总质量60到300 M⊙范围内，中位天空平均频域失配度（median sky-averaged frequency-domain mismatch）为8.0×10⁻⁵至1.7×10⁻⁴，95百分位低于10⁻³。在NVIDIA L40S GPU上，端到端评估单个波形约需1毫秒，约为LALSimulation C版本的10倍；批量大小64时吞吐量约为LALSimulation的140倍。

Conclusion: 该神经网络代理是首个将数值相对论验证的精度、全可微分性和GPU加速推理流水线结合于进动数值相对论波形模型的方案。其速度和可微分性使其适用于梯度MCMC、GPU加速嵌套采样（nested sampling）及重要性采样（importance sampling）等梯度推断方法，以及低延迟参数估计和大规模波形生成任务。

Abstract: We present a neural network surrogate model that emulates the NRSur7dq4 gravitational waveform model for precessing binary black hole mergers. The surrogate decomposes the waveform into constituent quantities and trains an independent multilayer perceptron (MLP) for each. We validate the surrogate against NRSur7dq4 on 10,000 waveforms spanning its full parameter space ($1 \leq q \leq 4$, $|χ_{A,B}| \leq 0.8$). For representative total masses between 60 and 300 $M_\odot$, median sky-averaged frequency-domain mismatches range from $8.0 \times 10^{-5}$ to $1.7 \times 10^{-4}$, with 95th percentiles below $10^{-3}$. On an NVIDIA L40S GPU the JAX surrogate evaluates a single waveform in about 1 ms end-to-end, roughly 10 times faster than the LALSimulation C implementation of NRSur7dq4, and sustains about 140 times the LALSimulation throughput at batch size 64, making it well suited for both low-latency parameter-estimation samplers and large-scale waveform generation. The full NRSur7dq4 NN waveform-to-likelihood pipeline is implemented in JAX and is differentiable. This is the first neural-network surrogate of a precessing numerical-relativity waveform model to combine validated NR-faithful accuracy with a fully differentiable, GPU-accelerated inference pipeline, enabling gradient-based inference approaches via automatic differentiation including Fisher information matrices, GPU-accelerated nested sampling, gradient-based MCMC and importance sampling.

</details>


### [177] [Coupled Gravitoelectromagnetic Response of a Magnetically Supported Generalized Hayward Black Hole in Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24982)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,M. Zeyauddin,A. Gulhane*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了磁化广义海亚德黑洞（magnetically supported generalized Hayward black hole）在非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamic）框架下的外部耦合引力与电磁响应，计算了准正则模（quasinormal spectrum）、度量与光学特性、极喉权重（extremal throat weights）、四极波转换（quadrupolar wave conversion）、有限带宽传输（finite bandwidth transfer）及相干吸收本征通道（coherent absorption eigenchannels），并验证了外部双曲性与通量守恒。结果表明非线性电动力学算子在电磁与引力分支中分别产生三次和四次谱修正，且在高χ值下呈现显著的反射转换与吸收特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在探索非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）框架下磁化广义海亚德黑洞（magnetically supported generalized Hayward black hole）的外部耦合引力与电磁响应特性，以理解其准正则谱、波转换及吸收行为。

Method: 作者构建了非线性电动力学完备下的外部耦合引力与电磁响应系统，将奇偶性扇区（parity sectors）简化为显式势函数的两通道波系统（two channel wave systems），并通过有效带电算子（effective charged operator）提取弱核心几何与模式混合效应，随后进行减法处理以分析残余谱层次。

Result: 电磁分支（primarily electromagnetic branch）呈现三次谱修正（cubic spectral corrections），引力分支（primarily gravitational branch）呈现四次修正（quartic corrections）；基态四极分支（fundamental quadrupolar branches）在χ≈0.70附近 intrinsic 线宽分离，奇偶性分裂（parity splitting）为次主导效应。在χ=0.9时，正宇称反射转换（positive parity reflected conversion）达45.8%，带宽平均后仍为40.8%；相干入射组合产生98.4%亮吸收与1.3%暗吸收。

Conclusion: 独立评估的可观测量为同一非线性电动力学算子（nonlinear electrodynamic operator）提供了相互一致的诊断依据，验证了模型的内部自洽性。

Abstract: We construct the exterior coupled gravitational and electromagnetic response of a magnetically supported generalized Hayward black hole within a specified nonlinear electrodynamic completion. Both parity sectors reduce to canonical two channel wave systems with explicit potentials and the correct Schwarzschild and effective Reissner--Nordström limits. We compute the quasinormal spectrum, metric and optical characteristics, extremal throat weights, quadrupolar wave conversion, finite bandwidth transfer, and coherent absorption eigenchannels while verifying exterior hyperbolicity and flux conservation. An effective charged operator captures the leading weak core geometry and mode mixing; after its subtraction, the residual hierarchy is consistent with cubic spectral corrections in the primarily electromagnetic branch and quartic corrections in the primarily gravitational branch and principal scattering observables. The fundamental quadrupolar branches become intrinsically linewidth separated near $χ\simeq0.70$, whereas parity splitting remains subleading. Metric and optical characteristic families separate monotonically toward extremality. At $χ=0.9$, positive parity reflected conversion reaches $45.8\%$ and remains $40.8\%$ after moderate bandwidth averaging, while coherent incident combinations yield $98.4\%$ bright and $1.3\%$ dark absorption. These independently evaluated observables provide mutually consistent diagnostics of the same nonlinear electrodynamic operator.

</details>


### [178] [Fixing IR tail of gravitational waves from domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25073)
*Ivan Dankovsky,Dmitry Gorbunov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出早期宇宙物质不均匀性剧烈演化产生的引力波（GW）数值模拟中，红外谱段存在非物理的寄生振荡（wiggles），这些振荡源于双频项且应被消除。作者提出一种数值平滑方法来恢复真实谱，并强调该方法的通用性，同时指出PRS标度法可能导致错误的GW谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值模拟早期宇宙引力波（gravitational waves, GW）产生时，功率谱的红外（infrared）部分出现高度振荡的非物理特征，这些寄生振荡（wiggles）源于对振荡幅度的平方进行朴素平均时产生的双频项（double frequency terms）。实际观测（如NANOGrav）关注的是谱最大值附近的红外斜率，这反映了GW产生高峰期的动力学过程，因此必须准确预测，但寄生振荡干扰了这一目标。

Method: 利用CosmoLattice代码对畴壁网络（Domain Wall network）进行数值模拟。提出一种数值平滑程序：在GW产生结束后，将模拟额外延长数个哈勃时间（Hubble times），以消除非物理的寄生振荡。对比了该程序与PRS标度方案（PRS prescription）下延长模拟的结果，后者对标量场方程不同部分进行人工标度（artificial scaling）。

Result: 所提出的数值平滑程序能够有效消除寄生振荡，恢复真实的GW谱。验证了PRS标度方案下技术自然的长时间模拟延长会导致错误的GW谱，即使源项（source terms）被适当重标度（rescaled）依然如此。

Conclusion: 该数值平滑程序具有通用性，可应用于其他早期宇宙中可能存在的GW源的数值模拟。PRS标度方案不适用于延长模拟以恢复真实谱，因此建议采用直接延长模拟时间的方法来消除寄生振荡。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of gravitational waves (GW) production during violent evolution of matter inhomogeneities in the early Universe yield highly wiggle infrared parts of the spectra. Obtained directly from the two-point correlation function, these wiggles are nonphysical, corresponding to the parasitic, double frequency terms in naively averaged squared oscillation amplitudes of GW, and hence must be washed out. The deep infrared behavior can be predicted on general grounds, e.g. fixed by causality considerations. However, what matters for real observations, e.g. like that of NANOGrav, and what can be only inferred from numerical simulations, is the infrared slope near the maximum of the spectrum. It reflects the dynamics responsible for the GW production in its heyday, and hence must be accurately predicted. We illustrate the problem with numerical simulations of the Domain Wall network performed with the help of code CosmoLattice. We suggest a numerical procedure to smooth out these parasitic wiggles, which allows us to recover the true spectrum. Being quite generic, it may be applied to numerical simulations of other hypothetical sources of GW possibly operating in the early Universe. The procedure requires to extend the simulation by a few Hubble times after termination of the GW production. We checked with numerical simulations, that the technically natural long-time extension of simulations, which becomes available via artificial scaling of different parts in the scalar sector equations provided by PRS prescription, gives wrong GW spectra even if the source terms are properly rescaled.

</details>


### [179] [GSpyNetTree-O4: an event validation tool used in the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25208)
*Sofia Alvarez-Lopez,Man Leong Chan,Franz S. Herbst,Dhatri Raghunathan,Airene Ahuja,Annudesh Liyanage,Julian Ding,Alejandro Garcia-Varela,Raymond Ng,Jess McIver*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GSpyNetTree-O4，这是LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA合作组在第四轮观测运行中部署的引力波数据瞬态噪声（glitch）分类与事件验证工具。其主要改进在于采用新架构，能够同时识别同一输入中的瞬态噪声与引力波信号，并在训练集中加入真实噪声叠加模拟引力波信号的样本。在测试中，低质量、高质量和极高质量三类分类器分别识别了97.9%、97.7%和95.4%的瞬态噪声，并在无噪声样本中正确报告无数据质量问题的比例分别为97.1%、96.6%和96.0%。该工具已成功部署，提升了引力波事件验证流程的自动化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器数据中频繁出现的非高斯瞬态噪声（glitches）会影响引力波搜索、参数估计及下游分析。为了及时识别和减轻引力波候选体附近的瞬态噪声干扰，LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA合作组采用数据质量报告（Data Quality Report）框架，并在第四轮观测运行中部署了GSpyNetTree-O4作为相应的瞬态噪声分类与事件验证工具。

Method: GSpyNetTree-O4采用了新架构，能够同时识别同一输入中的瞬态噪声和引力波信号。研究团队扩展并增强训练集，加入了模拟引力波信号与真实瞬态噪声重叠的样本，并应用60 Hz校准校正以匹配第四轮观测运行的预期数据。该工具基于时间-频率输入（采用不同的Q值构建）进行分类，包含低质量、高质量和极高质量三类分类器。

Result: 在测试数据上，低质量、高质量和极高质量分类器分别识别了97.9%、97.7%和95.4%的瞬态噪声。在无噪声样本（包括仅含引力波信号样本和无噪声样本）中，三组分类器正确报告无数据质量问题的比例分别为97.1%、96.6%和96.0%。此外，研究还评估了模型在未见过的瞬态噪声形态、第四轮观测运行中少量Virgo探测器瞬态噪声以及不同Q值选择下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GSpyNetTree-O4已作为数据质量报告工具成功部署，提升了引力波事件验证工作流的自动化水平。这一工具能够有效分类瞬态噪声并同时识别引力波信号，对保障第四轮观测运行中的引力波数据分析质量具有重要意义。

Abstract: The frequent presence of non-Gaussian transient noise, or glitches, in gravitational-wave detector data can affect gravitational-wave searches, parameter estimation, and downstream analyses. To identify and mitigate transient noise near gravitational-wave candidates in a timely manner, the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration employs the Data Quality Report. In the fourth observing run, GSpyNetTree-O4 was deployed within this framework as a tool for glitch classification and event validation. We describe GSpyNetTree-O4 and the main developments relative to its predecessor, GSpyNetTree. The most important update was a new architecture that allowed the simultaneous identification of glitches and gravitational-wave signals when both were present in the same input. We also expanded and augmented the training set with examples in which simulated gravitational-wave signals overlapped with real glitches, and applied $60\,\mathrm{Hz}$ calibration corrections to better match the data expected during the fourth observing run. On test data, the low-mass, high-mass, and extremely high-mass classifiers identified $97.9\%$, $97.7\%$, and $95.4\%$ of glitches, respectively. Among samples without a glitch, including gravitational-wave-only and No Glitch samples, the classifiers correctly reported no data-quality issues in $97.1\%$, $96.6\%$, and $96.0\%$ of cases, respectively. We further assessed the robustness of GSpyNetTree-O4 on unseen glitch morphologies, a small set of Virgo glitches from the fourth observing run, and different choices of the $Q$-value used to construct the time-frequency inputs. GSpyNetTree-O4 was successfully deployed as a Data Quality Report tool and increased automation in gravitational-wave event validation workflows.

</details>


### [180] [Parametrized beyond-Teukolsky framework in the time domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25311)
*Ciro De Simone,Sebastian H. Völkel,Kostas D. Kokkotas,Salvatore Capozziello*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了参数化超越Teukolsky框架的时域求解，通过(2+1)维高斯波包散射实验，完整模拟了从初始响应到振铃（ringdown）直至晚期行为的线性摄动演化，并利用时域波形提取了修正引力理论中的模型参数，为检验广义相对论的参数化偏离提供了时域互补框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论（General Relativity）的修正会显著改变黑洞的微扰响应，但现有研究仅将振铃阶段作为本征值问题（eigenvalue problem）处理，缺乏对完整线性演化过程的时域分析，无法全面捕捉参数化偏离对波形幅值、相位和晚期衰减行为的影响。

Method: 采用参数化超越Teukolsky框架（beyond-Teukolsky framework），实现其首次时域实现，进行(2+1)维高斯波包（Gaussian wave packets）散射实验，获得从初始瞬态响应到振铃及晚期行为的完整线性演化。

Result: 在低方位角数（azimuthal numbers）下与频域本征值基准结果高度一致；高方位角数受模式混合（mode mixing）影响，导致提取模式精度受限。变形参数（deformation parameters）可显著影响振铃幅值和相位，趋势可由近地平线修正势结构解释；晚期行为中，近地平线变形不改变尾部指数（tail exponent），但可显著改变幂律衰减（power-law decay）的起始时间。

Conclusion: 时域演化方法为检验黑洞摄动理论中广义相对论的参数化偏离提供了互补且灵活的框架，能够提取频域方法无法直接获得的完整波形信息，包括幅值、相位和晚期行为特征。

Abstract: Modifications to General Relativity can significantly alter the perturbative response of black holes, leaving imprints on quasinormal-mode spectra, waveform amplitudes and phases, and late-time tails. The parametrized beyond-Teukolsky framework was introduced to capture possible deviations from Kerr dynamics, but the ringdown has so far only been explored as an eigenvalue problem. We present the first time-domain implementation of this framework and perform (2+1)-dimensional scattering experiments with Gaussian wave packets. This approach provides the full linear evolution of the perturbation, from the initial prompt response through the ringdown and into the late-time regime. Using frequency-domain eigenvalue results as benchmarks, we find excellent agreement for low azimuthal numbers, while higher azimuthal numbers are affected by mode mixing, which limits the precision of the extracted modes. We further use the time-domain waveforms to estimate quadratic and mixed coefficients within the linearized modified-potential model, providing a diagnostic for the regime of validity of the linear approximation. Beyond mode frequencies, we show that the deformation parameters can strongly affect the ringdown amplitude and phase, with trends that can be understood from the near-horizon structure of the modified potential. We also analyze the late- time behavior, finding that near-horizon deformations leave the tail exponent unchanged but can substantially shift the onset of the power-law decay. These results demonstrate that time-domain evolutions provide a complementary and flexible framework for testing parametrized deviations from General Relativity in black-hole perturbation theory.

</details>


### [181] [Extracting Energy from a Non-Kerr Rotating Spacetime with an Anomalous Quadrupole Moment via Magnetic Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25315)
*Ke Wang,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过磁重联（magnetic reconnection）机制从具有反常四极矩（anomalous quadrupole moment）的非克尔旋转时空（non-Kerr rotating spacetime）中提取能量。结果表明，正且小的反常四极矩对应更高的能量提取功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非克尔旋转时空这一具有闭合类时曲线（closed timelike curves）的特殊结构是否以及如何通过磁重联机制实现能量提取，并分析反常四极矩对提取过程的影响。

Method: 在引入该时空的事件视界（event horizon）、能层（ergosphere）和闭合类时曲线后，分析了加速与减速等离子体在无穷远处的单位焓能量（energy per unit enthalpy）、能量提取的允许区域，以及能量提取的功率与效率。

Result: 无论反常四极矩为正还是为负，能量提取均有可能实现；但正且小的反常四极矩对应更高的能量提取功率与效率。

Conclusion: 磁重联机制可在具有反常四极矩的非克尔旋转时空中提取能量，且反常四极矩的符号与大小显著影响能量提取的功率和效率。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to extract energy from a non-Kerr rotating spacetime with an anomalous quadrupole moment via the magnetic reconnection mechanism. Unlike many other rotating spacetimes, this spacetime possesses closed timelike curves, and the corresponding spacetime regions must be excluded when extracting energy. After introducing the event horizon, ergosphere, and closed timelike curves of this spacetime, we deeply analyze the energy per unit enthalpy at infinity for accelerated and decelerated plasmas, the allowed region for energy extraction, and the power and efficiency of energy extraction. The results show that energy extraction is possible for both positive and negative anomalous quadrupole moments, but a positive and small anomalous quadrupole moment corresponds to higher power and efficiency of energy extraction.

</details>


### [182] [Memory Effect for deformed gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25452)
*P. -M. Zhang,M. Elbistan,P. A. Horvathy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了夹层引力波（sandwich gravitational waves）的形变过程，发现将纯位移参数（DM）波压缩为脉冲波时会丢失位移属性，仅保留速度效应（VM），并且无法通过无限增大形变参数获得真正的脉冲波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索夹层引力波在形变过程中的属性变化，特别是纯位移波（DM）向脉冲波（impulsive wave）转变时位移效应与速度效应的演化行为。

Method: 通过形变参数（deformation parameter）$p$ 对夹层引力波进行挤压（squeezing）操作，分析位移参数与速度效应的变化趋势。

Result: 将纯位移波压缩为脉冲波时，位移属性（DM）丧失，仅保留速度效应（VM）。对于每个固定的 $p$，可通过增大振幅恢复 DM，但振幅在 $p\to\infty$ 时发散，无法获得脉冲波。

Conclusion: 纯位移波无法通过连续形变转化为脉冲波，表明两类波之间存在本质上的不可逾越的界限。

Abstract: Deformations of sandwich gravitational waves are considered. Squeezing a wave with pure displacement (DM) parameters to an impulsive wave, the DM property is lost, leaving us with the velocity effect (VM). For each fixed value of the deformation parameter $p$, DM can be restored by further increasing the amplitude which however diverge when $p\to\infty$, and no impulsive wave is obtained.

</details>


### [183] [Gravitational Baryogenesis in Rainbow Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25491)
*P. Goodarzi,H. Mohseni Sadjadi,R. Darabi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究在正则化四维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内（Einstein Gauss Bonnet）模型中引入彩虹引力（rainbow gravity）修正，研究引力重子生成（gravitational baryogenesis）。该联合框架在辐射主导时期产生了非零的里奇标量（Ricci scalar）时间导数，从而在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特（Einstein Hilbert）贡献为零的辐射时期，由高斯-博内项产生非零重子不对称性。通过解析推导和数值分析，确定了与观测重子熵比兼容的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力重子生成机制通常需要宇宙膨胀期间里奇标量的时间导数非零，但标准辐射主导时期该导数为零。本研究旨在通过引入正则化四维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内模型与彩虹引力修正，在辐射时期也能产生非零里奇标量时间导数，从而解释观测到的重子不对称性。

Method: 采用正则化四维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内模型，并叠加彩虹引力修正；推导了高曲率（high curvature）和微扰（perturbative）两种区域下重子熵比（baryon to entropy ratio）的解析表达式；通过数值分析扫描参数空间，并与观测值进行对比。

Result: 高斯-博内项在辐射时期产生了非零重子不对称性，而爱因斯坦-希尔伯特贡献在该时期为零；数值分析识别出了能够复现观测重子熵比的可行参数空间。

Conclusion: 正则化四维爱因斯坦-高斯-博内模型与彩虹引力修正的组合框架，为辐射时期产生重子不对称性提供了有效机制，所得结果与观测数据一致。

Abstract: The gravitational baryogenesis is studied in regularized four dimensional Einstein Gauss Bonnet model with rainbow gravity corrections. This combined framework generates a non vanishing time derivative of the Ricci scalar even during radiation domination era. We derive analytical expressions for the baryon to entropy ratio in both the high curvature and perturbative regimes. The Gauss Bonnet contribution produces nonzero asymmetry precisely at radiation era, where the Einstein Hilbert contribution vanishes. Numerical analysis identifies viable parameter spaces reproducing the observed baryon to entropy ratio, compatible with observations.

</details>


### [184] [Gravitational wave constraints on corrections to Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula in classical F(R) gravity and quantum GR : implications for theory parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25513)
*Parthasarathi Majumdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用双黑洞并合引力波数据对贝肯斯坦-霍金面积公式的修正施加观测约束，结合霍金面积定理的验证与瓦尔德熵形式，限制经典F(R)引力中的逆面积修正，并通过绝对一致性准则约束圈量子引力与纠缠熵方法中的对数修正，进而限制超出标准模型粒子的自旋与种类数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测为检验黑洞热力学理论提供了前所未有的精度。本文旨在利用LVK合作组对霍金面积定理（Hawking Area Theorem）的高置信度观测验证（信噪比约80，置信度5σ），对黑洞熵的贝肯斯坦-霍金面积公式（Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula）可能存在的修正施加观测约束，从而探索经典引力理论与量子引力理论的可行性。

Method: 本文将引力波观测结果与贝肯斯坦（Bekenstein）的黑洞熵思想及广义第二定律（generalized second law of thermodynamics）相结合，利用瓦尔德熵泛函形式（Wald entropy function formalism），对经典F(R)引力中大视界面积球面对称黑洞解的领先逆面积修正（leading inverse area corrections）施加约束。研究中引入了“绝对一致性”（absolute consistency）概念，并将其与“相对一致性”（relative consistency）对比。此外，本文进一步考虑了来自非微扰无物质圈量子引力（Loop Quantum Gravity）和依赖于物质的微扰纠缠熵（Entanglement entropy）两种途径的量子广义相对论修正，将两者领先的对数面积修正（leading logarithmic corrections）结合，并施加绝对一致性条件。

Result: 对经典F(R)引力，绝对一致性准则将某些F(R)引力参数关联起来。对于量子修正，结合来自圈量子引力与纠缠熵方法的领先对数修正并施加绝对一致性条件后，显著限制了超出标准模型（Beyond Standard Model）基本粒子的自旋（spin）与种类数（number of species），其中部分粒子常被认为是暗物质候选者（Dark Matter candidates）。

Conclusion: 引力波数据对黑洞熵修正的观测约束能够有效缩小经典引力理论和量子引力理论的参数空间，并为超出标准模型的新物理（特别是暗物质候选粒子）提供来自黑洞热力学的独立限制。

Abstract: This contribution considers constraints from analyses of gravitational wave data from binary black hole coalescence, on possible corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking Area Formula for black hole entropy. Most recent analyses of gravitational wave data from the LVK Consortium appear to confirm the Hawking Area Theorem for black holes at a $5 σ$ accuracy, for the `loudest' signal (SNR of the order of $80$) of binary black hole merger inherent in the recent observation GW250114. Amalgamating this result with Bekenstein's ideas of black hole entropy and the generalized second law of thermodynamics, we constrain leading inverse area corrections for large horizon area spherical black hole solutions of classical $F(R)$ gravity, using the Wald entropy function formalism. The implementation of the observational constraints entails the notion of `absolute consistency' which we introduce and contrast with `relative consistency'. This absolute consistency criterion is shown to relate some of the parameters of $F(R)$ gravity. Next we consider leading quantum general relativistic corrections to the Area formula, arising both from the non-perturbative matter-free Loop Quantum Gravity and matter-dependent, perturbative Entanglement entropy approaches. Combining the leading logarithmic corrections in horizon area (for large areas) from both approaches, and imposing absolute consistency with the observational validation of the Area Theorem, is shown to lead to significant restrictions on the spin and number of species of Beyond Standard Model spectrum of elementary particles, some of which are often assumed to be Dark Matter candidates.

</details>


### [185] [Spin precession in the strong deflection limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25617)
*Jiafei Geng,Yunchuan Xiang,Qingquan Jiang,Xiankai Pang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对静态球对称且渐近平直时空，导出了粒子的自旋进动角（spin precession angle）在强偏转极限（strong deflection limit, SDL）下的展开式，得到了SDL系数，并建立了自旋进动角与偏转角之间的简单关系。该工作填补了SDL框架下自旋进动系统研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然一般球对称时空的偏转角（deflection angle）在强偏转极限（SDL）下的处理已经成熟，但自旋进动（spin precession）的SDL系统研究尚属空白。本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 在静态、球对称、渐近平直时空中，沿测地线传播的粒子其自旋进动角被推导为SDL展开式。通过解析计算获得SDL系数，并与偏转角建立简单关系。该形式被应用于Schwarzschild时空（得到全解析SDL系数）和Reissner-Nordström（RN）时空（得到至$\mathcal{O}(Q^2)$阶的微扰电荷修正）。

Result: 在Schwarzschild时空中获得了完全解析的SDL系数；在RN时空中得到了至$\mathcal{O}(Q^2)$阶的微扰电荷修正。结果明确验证了无质量粒子在后向散射（backward scattering）中的自旋翻转（spin-flip）普遍性，这是导致glory spot（光环斑）消失的潜在机制。无质量粒子的自旋进动与电荷无关，而有质量粒子则展现出非平凡的电荷依赖性，从而区分了RN时空与Schwarzschild时空。

Conclusion: 本文建立了SDL下自旋进动角的系统展开框架，发现了其与偏转角之间的简洁关系，并通过具体时空的解析计算验证了无质量粒子自旋翻转的普适性以及有质量粒子的电荷依赖性。

Abstract: The strong deflection limit (SDL) of the deflection angle is well established for general spherically symmetric spacetimes, but a systematic SDL treatment of spin precession has been lacking. We derive the SDL expansion for the spin precession angle of particles propagating along geodesics in static, spherically symmetric, and asymptotically flat spacetimes. The SDL coefficients are obtained, and a simple relation linking the precession angle to the deflection one is established. Applying the formalism to Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström (RN) spacetimes, we obtain fully analytic SDL coefficients for the former and perturbative charge corrections to $\mathcal{O}(Q^2)$ for the latter. These results explicitly verify the universal spin-flip of massless particles in backward scattering, which is the underlying mechanism governing the absence of the glory spot. While the spin precession of massless particles is charge-independent, massive particles acquire a non-trivial charge dependence that distinguishes the RN case from Schwarzschild.

</details>


### [186] [Conserved Gravitational Charges as Curvature Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25646)
*Emel Altas,Bayram Tekin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在渐近AdS和渐近平坦时空中，标准的守恒引力荷（如ADM能动量、角动量等）可以等价地表示为线性化曲率（linearized curvature）的通量形式，通过引入一个无散度的四阶张量和反对称庞加莱 Killing势，实现了电荷的曲率通量表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 守恒引力荷通常表示为度规扰动及其一阶导数的面积分，文章旨在探索能否将这些电荷等价地重写为线性化曲率的通量形式，以揭示其几何本质并统一不同背景下的构造。

Method: 构造一个无散度的四阶张量，其迹正比于宇宙学爱因斯坦张量（cosmological Einstein tensor）。在渐近AdS背景下，该方法重现了已知的Abbott-Deser电荷的曲率表示。对于渐近平坦背景，引入一个反对称庞加莱 Killing势（antisymmetric Poincaré Killing potential），该势适配代数Bianchi恒等式，将线性化爱因斯坦流（linearized Einstein current）转化为全散度，从而导出单一的曲率通量公式。

Result: 渐近平坦构造不能通过简单的Λ→0极限从AdS构造得到，因为平移群的Killing二形式（Killing two-form）为零。新公式的平移部分重现了ADM能动量；在四维时空下，其Lorentz部分在标准Regge-Teitelboim衰减和宇称条件下重现了角动量和boost/质心荷。在Schwarzschild、boosted Schwarzschild和Kerr数据上验证了归一化。在非最大对称爱因斯坦背景下，背景Weyl曲率会产生额外项，最大对称构造不能直接扩展为纯余维二曲率通量公式。

Conclusion: 守恒引力荷可以等价地表示为线性化曲率的通量形式，该构造在最大对称背景（AdS和渐近平坦）下有效，但无法直接推广到非最大对称的爱因斯坦背景。

Abstract: Conserved gravitational charges are commonly expressed as surface integrals of the metric perturbation and its first derivatives. We show that, in the usual asymptotically AdS and asymptotically flat settings, the standard charges admit equivalent representatives as fluxes of linearized curvature. The construction uses a divergence-free rank-four tensor whose trace is proportional to the cosmological Einstein tensor. On a maximally symmetric AdS background, it reproduces the known curvature representation of the Abbott-Deser charges. The asymptotically flat construction is not obtained by taking a naive $Λ\rightarrow 0$ limit, since the Killing two-form vanishes for translations. We instead introduce an antisymmetric Poincaré Killing potential. A representative Killing potential adapted to the algebraic Bianchi identity converts the linearized Einstein current into a total divergence and yields a single curvature-flux formula. Its translation sector reproduces the ADM energy--momentum, while in four dimensions its Lorentz sector reproduces the angular momentum and boost/center-of-mass charges under the standard Regge-Teitelboim falloff and parity conditions. The normalization is checked explicitly for Schwarzschild, boosted Schwarzschild, and Kerr data. On non-maximally symmetric Einstein backgrounds, background Weyl curvature generates additional terms, so the maximally symmetric construction does not directly extend to a pure codimension-two curvature-flux formula.

</details>


### [187] [Taub-NUT as gravitational dyon with torsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25654)
*Dmitri Gal'tsov,Rostom Karsanov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出Taub-NUT度规的Levi-Civita联络在Misner弦上存在奇异性，并产生含delta函数平方的爱因斯坦张量；通过引入挠率（torsion）可改进该问题，此时爱因斯坦张量可解释为具有负可变张力的宇宙弦（cosmic string），而Misner弦中的Komar流由挠率生成，Taub-NUT度规表现为由两个自旋流体束支持的孤子（soliton）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Taub-NUT度规中Levi-Civita联络在Misner弦上出现的奇异性问题，以及由此导致的爱因斯坦张量含有delta函数平方的病态行为。

Method: 通过引入挠率来修正联络结构，将奇异行为重新解释为负可变张力的宇宙弦，并利用挠率生成Komar流来解释渐近Komar荷（Komar charges）。

Result: 引入挠率后，爱因斯坦张量可解释为具有负可变张力的宇宙弦；Misner弦中的Komar流由挠率生成，从而解释了渐近Komar荷；Taub-NUT度规被重构为两个自旋流体束支持的孤子。

Conclusion: 挠率的引入可以消除Taub-NUT度规中Misner弦的奇异性，并提供一个自洽的几何解释，将Taub-NUT度规视为由自旋流体支撑的孤子解。

Abstract: We show that the Levi-Civita connection for the Taub-NUT metric is singular on Misner strings and generates an Einstein tensor containing the squares of the delta function. This can be improved by introducing torsion, in which case the Einstein tensor may be interpreted as that of a cosmic string with negative variable tension. Komar flows in Misner strings are generated by torsion, which is therefore responsible for the asymptotic Komar charges. The Taub-NUT metric then appears as a soliton supported by two spin-fluid beams.

</details>


### [188] [Echoes and quasinormal modes for static loop quantum black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25738)
*Huajie Gong,Guoyang Fu,Shulan Li,Jian-Pin Wu,Qin Tan,Qiyuan Pan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了多极指数 l=1 的静态圈量子黑跳（loop quantum black bounce, LQBB）时空中的标量扰动，聚焦于时域信号和基本准正则频率（quasinormal frequencies, QNFs）。通过有限差分法发现，在单势垒有效势的正则黑洞（regular black hole, RBH）构型中无回波（echoes），而在势阱结构的可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）构型中则产生清晰回波。QNFs 在两种方法下结果一致，且回波的出现与有效势剖面密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解圈量子黑跳（LQBB）模型中正则黑洞（RBH）与可穿越虫洞的波动力学特征，特别是标量扰动下的时域信号与准正则频率（QNFs）行为，以澄清视界与喉结构如何影响振铃（ringdown）和回波（echoes）。

Method: 采用有限差分法（finite difference method）计算时域信号；利用 Prony 方法和直接积分法（direct integration method）提取准正则频率（QNFs）。

Result: 在正则黑洞（RBH）情形下，增大反弹参数 r_b 或量子参数 α 均导致衰减变慢；在虫洞情形下，准正则频率（QNFs）对模型参数呈非单调依赖。单势垒有效势的 RBH 构型无回波，而势阱结构的虫洞构型产生清晰回波。

Conclusion: LQBB 时空为研究正则黑洞（RBH）和可穿越虫洞中的波动力学提供了一个有用框架，有助于阐明视界与喉结构如何影响振铃（ringdown）和回波（echoes）现象。

Abstract: We investigate scalar perturbations of the static loop quantum black bounce (LQBB) spacetime with multipole index $l=1$, focusing on time-domain signals and fundamental quasinormal frequencies (QNFs). The LQBB model provides a unified description of regular black holes (RBHs) and traversable wormholes, governed by the quantum parameter $α$ and the bounce parameter $r_b$. Using the finite difference method, we find no echoes for the displayed RBH configurations with a single-barrier effective potential, whereas clear echoes are produced by the potential well structure in selected traversable wormhole configurations. The QNFs obtained from the Prony method and the direct integration method are in good agreement. In the RBH case, increasing $r_b$ or $α$ leads to a slower decay. In the wormhole case, the QNFs depend non-monotonically on the model parameters, and the emergence of echoes is closely tied to the effective potential profile. These results show that the LQBB spacetime provides a useful framework for studying wave dynamics in RBHs and traversable wormholes, and for clarifying how horizon and throat structures affect ringdown and echoes.

</details>


### [189] [Late-Time Cosmological Tests of a Minisuperspace Generalized-Uncertainty-Principle Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25745)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由二次广义不确定性原理（GUP）形变的FLRW微超空间泊松代数，在宇宙学背景下的可观测效应。利用宇宙学时计、Pantheon+超新星样本和DESI DR2重子声学振荡数据，在背景层面约束形变参数β*，结果以95%置信度给出-0.142<β*<0.005的范围，与平坦ΛCDM模型部分兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙学中可能的量子引力效应，特别是这些效应能否通过晚期宇宙膨胀的观测加以检验，是本文的核心动机。已有研究对广义不确定性原理（GUP）的宇宙学后果关注不足，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用二次广义不确定性原理（GUP）对FLRW微超空间泊松代数进行形变，在一阶形变参数下修改弗里德曼方程（Friedmann equation）从而改变均匀膨胀历史。由于该方法不唯一确定微扰扇区，分析被限制在背景可观测量。使用31个宇宙学时计（cosmic-chronometer）测量、未校准的1580个天体的Pantheon+哈勃流样本，以及13个分量的DESI DR2重子声振荡（baryon-acoustic-oscillation, BAO）似然函数，将声视界（sound horizon）视为冗余参数。所得宇宙学与平坦ΛCDM和Chevallier–Polarski–Linder（CPL）参数化进行比较。

Result: 在基线标度下，形变参数β*在68%置信度为-0.086^{+0.040}_{-0.032}，在95%置信度为-0.142<β*<0.005。

Conclusion: GUP形变效应可以在背景层面与当前观测数据进行比较，所得约束范围对平坦ΛCDM模型未显示出显著的偏离信号，且窄于CPL参数化给出的暗能量（dark energy）演化范围。这表明当前的观测精度尚不能排除该GUP模型，但对其参数空间已形成有效限制。

Abstract: The investigation of possible quantum-gravity effects in cosmology is of considerable interest, especially when such effects can be tested against observations of the late-time expansion. In the present work we study a quadratic generalized-uncertainty-principle (GUP) deformation of the FLRW minisuperspace Poisson algebra. At first order in the deformation parameter, the construction modifies the Friedmann equation and hence the homogeneous expansion history. Since it does not determine a unique perturbation sector, we restrict the analysis to background observables, using 31 cosmic-chronometer measurements, the uncalibrated 1580-object Pantheon$+$ Hubble-flow sample, and the 13-component DESI DR2 baryon-acoustic-oscillation likelihood, while treating the sound horizon as a nuisance parameter. We compare the resulting cosmology with flat \LCDM{} and with the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder parametrization. For the baseline normalization we obtain $\betastar=-0.086^{+0.040}_{-0.032}$ at $68\%$ credibility and $-0.142<\betastar<0.005$ at $95\%$ credibility.

</details>


### [190] [General Relativistic Entropic Acceleration at the perturbation level: a CLASS implementation and first Boltzmann-code constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25841)
*Simone D'Onofrio,Dong Ha Lee,Eleonora Di Valentino,Juan García-Bellido*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次将广义相对论熵加速（GREA）模型嵌入爱因斯坦-玻尔兹曼求解器（CLASS），通过有效流体与参数化后弗里德曼方案（parametrized-post-Friedmann scheme）计算完整CMB与物质功率谱，并结合COBAYA与DESI DR2 BAO等数据进行MCMC分析，发现耦合参数α~1且拟合优度与ΛCDM相当（|Δχ²|≲6），同时模型预言了红移z~2处的幻像分界线（phantom divide）二次穿越，为热力学动力学所独有。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GREA模型此前仅在背景（background）层面与数据进行比较，缺乏对完整宇宙微波背景（CMB）与物质功率谱的检验。本文旨在将GREA模型首次实现于爱因斯坦-玻尔兹曼（Einstein-Boltzmann）求解器中，以利用高精度CMB与重子声学振荡（BAO）数据对其进行严格约束。

Method: 将GREA背景直接积分到CLASS代码中，熵成分（entropic component）则作为受参数化后弗里德曼方案（parametrized-post-Friedmann scheme）调控的有效流体（effective fluid）进行演化，从而获得完整的CMB与物质功率谱；采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法（Markov-chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）结合COBAYA工具、主CMB似然函数、DESI DR2 BAO及Ia型超新星数据进行参数约束。

Result: 约束得到的耦合参数α~1，与理论预期高度一致；尽管增加了一个自由参数，模型的拟合优度与ΛCDM模型相当（|Δχ²|≲6）；从数据中推断的状态方程（equation of state）与分箱的模型无关重建结果一致，且在红移z≃2处呈现幻像分界线的第二次穿越。

Conclusion: GREA模型在完整扰动层次的检验中表现良好，其热力学动力学预言的幻像分界线二次穿越是超越ΛCDM的一个独特特征，值得进一步关注。

Abstract: General Relativistic Entropic Acceleration (GREA) attributes the late-time acceleration of the Universe to the entropy growth of the causal cosmological horizon, without a cosmological constant, with a phenomenology fixed by the single $\mathcal{O}(1)$ parameter $α$. The model has so far been confronted with data only at the background level. We present its first implementation within an Einstein-Boltzmann solver: the GREA background is integrated directly into CLASS, while the entropic component is evolved as an effective fluid regulated by the parametrized-post-Friedmann scheme, giving access to the full CMB and matter power spectra. A Markov-chain Monte Carlo analysis with COBAYA against the full primary-CMB likelihoods, DESI DR2 BAO and Type Ia supernovae constrains the coupling $α\sim 1$, in excellent agreement with the theoretical prediction, with a fit matching $Λ$CDM to within $|Δχ^2| \lesssim 6$ despite the addition of a single free parameter. The equation of state inferred from the data agrees with binned, model-independent reconstructions and exhibits a second crossing of the phantom divide at $z \simeq 2$, a distinctive prediction of the thermodynamic dynamics rather than of an imposed parametrization.

</details>


### [191] [Analytic backreaction of a scalar wig on a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25932)
*Marco de Cesare,Manuel Del Piano,Carlos A. R. Herdeiro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文解析研究了球对称大质量复标量准束缚态对史瓦西黑洞的反作用，在小耦合近似下通过应力-能量张量求解爱因斯坦方程至振幅二次阶，并利用匹配渐近展开解析得到了准共振频率，揭示了黑洞质量随指数衰减的标量云吸收而单调增长直至饱和的动力学过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球对称大质量复标量准束缚态（quasi-bound state）对史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）的反作用（backreaction），以理解标量场与黑洞引力之间的非线性耦合效应。

Method: 在小耦合条件 $r_0 μ \ll 1$ 下，计算基态标量 $s$ 波的应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor），并在内行埃丁顿-芬克尔斯坦坐标（ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates）中通过振幅的二次阶求解爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）；利用匹配渐近展开（matched asymptotic expansions）解析确定基态 $s$ 波的小质量准共振频率（quasi-resonant frequency），并用Leaver方法（Leaver's method）进行数值验证。

Result: 得到了度规扰动（metric perturbations）和Misner-Sharp质量（Misner-Sharp mass）的显式表达式，推导了未来外陷捕视界（future outer trapping horizon）的演化方程；黑洞质量随衰减的水平通量单调增长，并在有限标量云被完全吸收后达到饱和，云质量的减少在微扰阶上恰好平衡了视界的增长；同时确定了标量小耦合近似与引力微扰展开同时有效的参数区域。

Conclusion: 与非稳态处理不同，该计算保留了准束缚态的指数衰减特性，并揭示了黑洞质量增长与标量云吸收之间的精确平衡关系，为研究标量场-黑洞系统的动力学演化提供了解析框架。

Abstract: We analytically determine the leading backreaction of a spherically symmetric massive complex scalar quasi-bound state (with mass $μ$) on a Schwarzschild black hole with (initial) gravitational radius $r_0$. Working in the small-coupling regime, $r_0 μ\ll 1$, we evaluate the stress-energy tensor of the fundamental scalar $s$-wave and solve the Einstein equations through quadratic order in its amplitude in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates. We also determine the small-mass quasi-resonant frequency of the fundamental $s$-wave analytically by matched asymptotic expansions and validate it numerically using Leaver's method. Unlike steady-state treatments, the calculation retains the exponential decay of the quasi-bound state. We obtain explicit expressions for the metric perturbations and Misner-Sharp mass and derive the evolution of the future outer trapping horizon. The black-hole mass grows monotonically with the decaying horizon flux and saturates when the finite scalar cloud has been absorbed, with the decrease of the cloud mass exactly balancing the horizon growth at the perturbative order considered. We also determine the domain in which the scalar small-coupling approximation and the gravitational perturbative expansion are simultaneously valid.

</details>


### [192] [Causal Green function decomposition for quantum black hole seismology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26010)
*Xi-Li Zhang,Jing Ren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了量子黑洞（quantum black hole）时域格林函数（time-domain Green function），通过将格林函数分解为因果不同的分量并选择一致的反拉普拉斯（inverse-Laplace）围道，阐明了时域响应如何由不同准正则模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）集合构建而成，为黑洞光谱学（BH spectroscopy）与量子黑洞地震学（quantum BH seismology）提供了统一的因果框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器灵敏度的提升使得黑洞振铃光谱（ringdown spectroscopy）的检验日益精确，但黑洞准正则模（QNMs）存在谱不稳定性（spectral instability）：小幅的近视界修正可导致截然不同的QNM谱。量子黑洞因其内部存在显著反射而成为这一张力的自然场景，但其时域波形与不同QNM谱之间的关系缺乏一致的理论图像。

Method: 系统考察了量子黑洞的时域格林函数（time-domain Green function），考虑了位于光环势垒（light-ring potential barrier）内外两侧的源。通过将格林函数分解为因果上不同的分量，并选择相应一致的反拉普拉斯围道（inverse-Laplace contours），阐明了响应如何由不同QNM集合构建而成。

Result: 量子黑洞QNM重建在探测到弯曲时空后总能忠实描述信号，但其实际效率高度依赖演化阶段（evolutionary stage）。在内部反射变得相关之前，该基与BH QNM和尾巴展开（tail expansions）形式上等价，收敛性质（convergence properties）受源位置影响。在晚期，长寿命模（long-lived modes）提供了高效基。时域模拟证实了这些结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为黑洞光谱学与量子黑洞地震学（quantum BH seismology）提供了一个统一的因果框架（unified causal framework），澄清了量子黑洞时域波形与不同QNM谱之间的关系及其适用条件。

Abstract: The growing sensitivity of gravitational-wave detectors enables increasingly precise tests of black hole (BH) ringdown spectroscopy. BH quasinormal modes (QNMs) are, however, spectrally unstable: small near-horizon modifications can produce a drastically different QNM spectrum, while causality requires the prompt ringdown to remain BH-like until the reflected signal returns. Quantum BHs with substantial interior reflection provide a natural setting for this tension, yet the relation between their time-domain waveform and different QNM spectra still lacks a consistent picture. In this work, we systematically examine the time-domain Green function for quantum BHs, considering sources located outside and inside the light-ring potential barrier. By decomposing the Green function into causally distinct components and choosing the corresponding inverse-Laplace contours consistently, we clarify how the response is built from different sets of QNMs. We find that the quantum BH QNM reconstruction always faithfully describes the signal once the curved spacetime is probed, but its practical efficiency depends strongly on the evolutionary stage. Before interior reflection becomes relevant, we prove that this basis is formally equivalent to the BH QNM and tail expansions, with convergence properties sensitive to source location. At late times, the long-lived modes provide an efficient basis. Time-domain simulations confirm these results, providing a unified causal framework for BH spectroscopy and quantum BH seismology.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum Gravity from Fractal Entanglement Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26035)
*Jaume Gine*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出时空是由底层量子信息网络的纠缠结构（entanglement structure）涌现产生的分形几何（fractal geometry），并基于此发展出一个称为“分形纠缠量子引力”（FEQG）的统一框架，从信息论距离定义出发，推导出薛定谔方程和爱因斯坦引力，预测在普朗克尺度附近时空有效维数降至2维、引力势修正以及超短尺度上量子力学的可能偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子引力和时空本质问题提供一个统一的信息论起源，即从纠缠结构（entanglement structure）中同时涌现出时空、量子力学和引力，从而弥合广义相对论与量子理论之间的概念鸿沟。

Method: 在纠缠图上定义信息论距离$d_{ij}=-\ell_0\log(I_{ij}/I_0)$，产生尺度依赖的分形时空；利用不可微轨迹导致随机测地线和复协变导数，进而涌现出薛定谔方程；从纠缠诱导度规的时间依赖性导出爱因斯坦引力，并引入分形修正项得到广义场方程$G_{μν}=8πG(T_{μν}+αE_{μν}+βF_{μν})$。

Result: 框架预测普朗克尺度附近时空有效维数流向$D\to 2$（维数约化，dimensional reduction）；引力势被修正；超短尺度上量子力学可能出现偏离标准形式的偏差。

Conclusion: 分形纠缠量子引力框架表明，量子理论和引力均可追溯至同一信息论源头——纠缠结构，为统一描述微观量子与宏观时空提供了新路径。

Abstract: In this paper we propose that spacetime is an emergent fractal geometry generated by the entanglement structure of an underlying quantum information network. Indeed, it is developed a framework in which spacetime, quantum mechanics, and gravity emerge from the entanglement structure of a universal quantum state. Geometry is defined by an information-theoretic distance $d_{ij}=-\ell_0\log(I_{ij}/I_0)$ on an entanglement graph, producing a scale-dependent, fractal spacetime whose effective dimension flows toward $D\to 2$ near the Planck scale. In this fractal geometry, nondifferentiable trajectories lead to stochastic geodesics and a complex covariant derivative, from which the Schrödinger equation follows as an emergent dynamical law. Gravity arises from the time dependence of the entanglement-induced metric, yielding Einstein gravity in the macroscopic limit and fractal corrections encoded in a generalized field equation $G_{μν}=8πG(T_{μν}+αE_{μν}+βF_{μν})$. The resulting \emph{Fractal Entanglement Quantum Gravity} (FEQG) framework predicts dimensional reduction, modified gravitational potentials, and possible deviations from standard quantum mechanics at ultrashort scales, offering a unified informational origin for quantum theory and gravitation.

</details>
