### [194] [Experiments with interacting ultracold polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25519)
*Philip D. Gregory,Jonathan M. Mortlock,Daniel K. Ruttley,Simon L. Cornish*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了过去二十年里超冷极性分子（ultracold polar molecules）实现完全量子控制（full quantum control）的实验进展，重点涵盖超冷分子碰撞控制、偶极相互作用（dipolar interactions）工程以及光晶格（optical lattices）中的量子模拟三个关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷极性分子凭借长程偶极相互作用和丰富的长寿命内部结构，为量子科学实验提供了激动人心的平台，但分子的复杂性也带来了诸多实验挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述形式，系统梳理了过去二十年的实验进展，聚焦于超冷分子碰撞控制、偶极相互作用调控以及光晶格中的量子模拟三大主题。

Result: 回顾了将超冷极性分子置于完全量子控制之下的关键实验突破，包括对分子碰撞的主动控制、偶极相互作用的工程化利用，以及在光晶格中实现初步的量子模拟。

Conclusion: 超冷极性分子平台经过多年发展已具备量子控制能力，未来有望在量子模拟等应用中发挥更大作用。

Abstract: Ultracold polar molecules have emerged as an exciting platform for experiments in quantum science, with many proposed applications seeking to leverage the existence of long-range dipolar interactions and the rich internal structure of long-lived states. However, the complexity of molecules also presents many experimental challenges. In this chapter, we review the experimental advances over the past two decades that have brought ultracold molecules under full quantum control. We focus on three key topics: control of ultracold molecular collisions, engineering dipolar interactions and nascent quantum simulations in optical lattices.

</details>


### [195] [Deterministic loading of molecular arrays by microwave-assisted collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25783)
*Etienne F. Walraven,Kang Feng,Jonas Rodewald,Michael R. Tarbutt,Tijs Karman*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用微波辅助碰撞（microwave assisted collisions）与分子激发态搁置（shelving）技术，抑制分子光镊阵列（molecular tweezer arrays）装载过程中的碰撞损失，并通过受控碰撞实现确定性分子弹出，预测填充率可达96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子光镊阵列在量子模拟（quantum simulation）、量子传感（quantum sensing）和量子计算领域潜力巨大，但现有基于光辅助碰撞（light-assisted collisions）的增强装载方法无法直接推广到分子系统，因为分子间的碰撞损失问题严重。

Method: 将分子搁置（shelving）在转动或振动激发态（rotationally or vibrationally excited states）上，使已搁置分子与新装载分子之间产生排斥性范德华相互作用（repulsive van der Waals interaction），从而抑制碰撞损失；进一步引入微波辅助碰撞以控制碰撞后分子的末态和释放的能量，并探索多种确保确定性分子弹出（deterministic ejection）的策略。

Result: 通过上述方案，对于可激光冷却的分子（laser-coolable molecules），预测可实现高达96%的填充率（filling fraction），为构建可扩展的分子阵列（scalable molecular arrays）提供了可行途径。

Conclusion: 利用现有可激光冷却分子的实验技术，结合激发态搁置与微波辅助碰撞，可有效克服分子阵列装载中的碰撞损失瓶颈，实现高填充率的可扩展分子光镊阵列。

Abstract: Molecular tweezer arrays offer great prospects for quantum simulation, sensing, and computing, and would benefit from methods that enhance loading efficiency. Whereas light-assisted collisions underpin enhanced loading methods for atomic tweezer arrays, this approach cannot be directly extended to molecular arrays due to collisional loss. We show how this collisional loss can be suppressed by shelving molecules in rotationally or vibrationally excited states, so that a shelved molecule interacts with a newly loaded molecule through a repulsive van der Waals interaction. By introducing microwave assisted collisions, we show how to control the final states and the energy released in a collision between a pair of molecules. Following this controlled collision, one of the two molecules can be ejected, and we explore several strategies for ensuring deterministic ejection. Our schemes rely on currently available techniques for laser-coolable molecules, and we predict achievable filling fractions up to 96%, paving the way for scalable molecular arrays.

</details>


<div id='math.RA'></div>

# math.RA [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Iterate Wronskians over $\mathbb{R}^d$ as $N$-ary brackets on $\mathbb{R}[x^1,\ldots,x^d]$: the $N$-bonacci numbers bound the highest total degrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26039)
*Markuss G. Kenins,Arthemy V. Kiselev*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $\mathbb{R}[x^1,\ldots,x^d]$ 上完全广义 Wronskian（complete generalised Wronskian）$W_d^k$ 作为 $N$ 元 Lie 括号（Lie bracket）的迭代增长问题，证明了多项式最高全次数（maximal total degree）的渐近增长速率不超过 $N$ 阶 $N$-bonacci 数（$N$-bonacci numbers），并且在 $d=1$ 且 $k$ 为奇数的情形下可达该上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究将完全广义 Wronskian 视为 $N$ 元 Lie 括号后，反复迭代该括号操作所产生的多项式序列的最高全次数随迭代次数 $n$ 的增长速率问题。

Method: 将 $d$ 元多项式代数上的完全广义 Wronskian $W_d^k$ 定义为微分阶数为 $k$ 的 $N=\binom{d+k}{d}$ 元 Lie 括号；通过 $N$-bonacci 数列递推 $F^{(N)}_n=F^{(N)}_{n-1}+\cdots+F^{(N)}_{n-N}$ 刻画多项式最高全次数的增长上界，并针对 $d=1$ 且 $k$ 为奇数的特殊情形给出可达性证明。

Result: 对于任意初始参数选择，多项式最高全次数序列 $d^{(N)}_n \geqslant 0$ 的渐近增长（若存在增长）不超过第 $n$ 个 $N$-bonacci 数，即 $\lim_{n\to+\infty} (d^{(N)}_n/F^{(N)}_n )<\infty$。同时，当 $d=1$ 且 $k$ 为奇数时，最高多项式次数确实可以达到 $N$-bonacci 上界。

Conclusion: 完全广义 Wronskian 迭代生成的多元多项式序列的最高全次数受 $N$-bonacci 数列控制，且该上界在特定条件下是紧的。

Abstract: For the algebra $\mathbb{R}[x^1,\ldots,x^d]$ of polynomials in $d\geqslant 1$ variables, regard the complete generalised Wronskian $W_d^k$ of differential order $k\geqslant 1$ over $\mathbb{R}^d$ as the $N=\tbinom{d+k}{d}$-ary Lie bracket. Take an $N$-tuple of polynomials, calculate their Wronskian, and keep re-using the newly-created polynomials to produce more of them. The problem is: how fast do their maximal total degrees grow with the number $n$ of iterations of the bracket? Here enter the $N$-bonacci numbers defined by the recurrence $F^{(N)}_n=F^{(N)}_{n-1}+\cdots+F^{(N)}_{n-N}\in \mathbb{N}$. We prove that for any choice of the initial arguments, the sequence of highest total degrees $d^{(N)}_n \geqslant 0$ grows (if at all) asymptotically no faster than the $n$th $N$-bonacci number: $\lim_{n\to+\infty} (d^{(N)}_n/F^{(N)}_n )<\infty$. We show that for $d=1$ and $k$ odd, the highest polynomial degrees do attain the $N$-bonacci bound.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Controlling Turbulent Flows in Compressible Active Nematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24927)
*Dimitrios Krommydas,Paarth Gulati,Aparna Baskaran,M. Cristina Marchetti*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文针对光图案化准二维活性悬浮液实验中观察到的大密度涨落现象，发展了一套可压缩活性向列相（compressible active nematics）连续介质理论。研究发现，在空间 patterning 的活性驱动下，可压缩性可作为调节密度变化和湍流流动的关键控制参数，并在尖锐活性界面处稳定了一种一维涡旋链的动态定态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光图案化准二维活性悬浮液实验显示体系存在显著的密度变化，现有理论尚未充分解释可压缩性在其中的作用。作者由此出发，旨在建立可压缩活性向列相的连续理论框架，并探究可压缩性如何调控密度对比与活性湍流。

Method: 研究采用连续介质理论建模，针对由apolar杆状单元组成的活性向列相悬浮液，其取向在π旋转下不变。理论考虑了空间图案化的活性（spatially patterned activity），并通过精确可解的一维约化模型，导出了控制密度对比的无量纲参数。利用可压缩性作为调节手段，分析活性湍流从高活性区向低活性区的转变，以及尖锐活性界面处的动力学定态。

Result: 在伸展型（extensile）各向同性活性压力下，活性物质从高活性区域被排出，在低活性区域累积；密度对比由单一无量纲参数决定，该参数与可压缩性（compressibility）成线性关系。可压缩性可调控活性湍流从高活性区转向低活性区。在尖锐活性界面处，体系稳定形成一维涡旋链，该链由软性、活性诱导的约束（activity-induced confinement）维持。

Conclusion: 可压缩性被确立为调控活性向列相悬浮液中密度变化与湍流流动的关键控制参数（control parameter）。这一发现为理解和操控活性物质中的密度不均匀性与复杂流动提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Motivated by experiments on light-patterned, quasi-2D active suspensions that exhibit large density variations, we develop a continuum theory of compressible active nematics--suspensions of apolar rods whose orientation is invariant under pi rotations. Under spatially patterned activity, the extensile isotropic active pressure expels material from high-activity regions and accumulates it in low-activity ones; an exactly solvable 1D reduction shows that the resulting density contrast is governed by a single dimensionless parameter, linear in the compressibility. Using compressibility as a tuning knob, we then steer active turbulence from high- to low-activity regions and, at sharp activity interfaces, stabilize an analytically tractable dynamical steady state: a one-dimensional chain of vortices held by soft, activity-induced confinement. Our results establish compressibility as a control parameter for density variations and turbulent flow in active nematic suspensions.

</details>


### [198] [Transition between ground states in square anisotropic artificial colloidal ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25050)
*Leonardo G. Alanis-Cantú,Antonio Ortiz-Ambriz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过在旋转外磁场下动态引入各向异性相互作用，人工胶体冰（ACI）系统可以从无电荷的“二进二出”冰规则态转变为带电的“四进四出”态。最终构型强烈依赖磁场旋转速率：高速旋转时系统通过无扩散转变达到无缺陷终态；低速旋转时遍历性破缺，系统被困在部分有序的亚稳态中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工胶体冰（Artificial Colloidal Ice, ACI）中，通过旋转外磁场动态引入各向异性相互作用，探索系统从无电荷的“二进二出”（2-in, 2-out）冰规则态向带电的“四进四出”（4-in, 4-out）态转变的过程及其动力学机制。

Method: 在已由各向同性磁偶极相互作用控制的ACI系统中，通过旋转外磁场动态引入各向异性相互作用，并调节磁场的旋转速率 $\omega$，观察不同速率下系统最终构型的变化。

Result: 最终构型强烈依赖磁场旋转速率：高角速度下，系统通过无扩散转变（diffusionless transformation）从初始基态达到无缺陷的终态；低旋转速率下，系统出现遍历性破缺（ergodicity breakdown），被困在部分有序的亚稳态（metastable state）中。

Conclusion: 旋转磁场速率是控制ACI系统从冰规则态向带电态转变及最终有序程度的关键参数，低速旋转下遍历性破缺导致系统无法达到完全有序的平衡态。

Abstract: In Artificial Colloidal Ice (ACI), paramagnetic colloidal particles are confined in double-well traps and interact via repulsive, isotropic, magnetic dipole-dipole interactions that can be controlled by an external magnetic field. In this paper, we dynamically introduce anisotropic interactions to ACI by rotating the external magnetic field, which, in equilibrium, makes the system go from a charge-free 2-in, 2-out ice rule state, to a charged 4-in, 4-out state. We observe a strong dependence of the final configuration on the field's rotation rate $ω$: at high angular velocity, the system achieves a defect free final state via a difussionless transformation from the initial ground state. However, counterintuitively, at slow rotation rates, ergodicity breaks down, trapping the system in a partially ordered metastable state.

</details>


### [199] [The effect of side chain length on the mesomorphic properties of ferroelectric nematogens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25087)
*Natalia Podoliak,Martin Cigl,Pavlo Golub,Anej Sterle,Marta Lavrič,Nerea Sebastian,Aitor Erkoreka,Josu Martinez-Perdiguero,Alenka Mertelj,George Cordoyiannis,Vladimíra Novotná*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过对已知铁电向列相（ferroelectric nematic phase, NF）液晶分子进行结构修饰，引入二甲基氨基末端并延长侧链，制备了两种新同系物。研究发现，侧链延长后，体系由原先的各向同性（Iso）直接转变为NF相，变为出现一个狭窄的向列相（N）中间相。通过极化、二次谐波（SHG）信号和介电常数测量确认了NF相的铁电性，并系统比较了侧链长度对液晶性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电向列相的发现为软物质化学与物理学开辟了新方向。本研究旨在通过分子结构改性，系统探究侧链长度对铁电向列相介晶性质（mesomorphic properties）的影响，特别是中间相行为的变化规律。

Method: 基于已知铁电向列相液晶分子（ferroelectric nematogen），将二甲基氨基（dimethylamino）封端部分作为结构修饰单元，通过延长侧链合成了两种新同系物。采用极化测量、二次谐波（SHG，second harmonic generation）信号检测和介电常数（permittivity）测量等手段确认NF相的铁电性（ferroelectricity）。

Result: 与先前报道的同系物直接由各向同性（Iso）相转变为NF相不同，延长烷基链后体系在Iso相与NF相之间出现了一个狭窄的向列相（N，nematic phase）中间相。实验确认了新化合物的铁电性，并建立了其介晶性质与材料性质参数。

Conclusion: 侧链长度对铁电向列相的相变行为具有显著影响：延长侧链可引入额外的向列相中间相，并带来新的效应。通过对比不同侧链长度下的极化、SHG信号和介电参数，揭示了侧链长度与铁电性能之间的关联规律。

Abstract: The discovery of ferroelectric nematic phase opened new directions in the field of soft matter chemistry and physics. In this paper, we have modified a previously reported ferroelectric nematogen based on molecular structure with dimethylamino-terminated part. We have prepared two new homologues by prolonging a side chain and investigated the effect of its length on the mesomorphic properties. While previously reported homologues exhibited a direct phase transition from the isotropic (Iso) to the ferroelectric nematic phase (NF), for prolonged alkyl chain there is a narrow nematic phase (N) in between. For new compounds, we have established their mesomorphic and material properties. We have confirmed ferroelectricity of the NF phase by the polarization, SHG signal and permittivity. Finally, we have compared these parameters with respect to the side-chain length and discussed new effects, discovered for prolonged homologues due to the presence of an additional N phase.

</details>


### [200] [Fast Stokesian Dynamics for Rigid Aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25161)
*Deepak Mangal,Avinesh Ojha,Wanjiao Liu,Ronald G. Larson,Jesse Capecelatro*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速斯托克斯动力学（fast Stokesian dynamics, FSD）框架，用于刚性聚集体（rigid aggregates）悬浮液的动力学与流变学模拟。该框架将Fiore和Swan（2019）的球体级公式扩展到多珠刚性体，通过几何约束隐式施加刚性约束，实现稳定高效的时间积分，并开发了块三角分解预条件子（block-triangular factorization preconditioner）用于鞍点系统求解。方法作为HOOMD-blue软件套件的开源插件实现，在基准问题及碳黑浆料实验流变学中得到了验证，准确捕捉了剪切稀化及高剪切粘性行为，并展示了良好的GPU可扩展性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性聚集体悬浮液在自然界和工业过程中广泛存在，但其动力学与流变学的精确模拟面临计算效率低和刚性约束处理困难的挑战。现有方法在可扩展性和准确性方面存在不足，亟需一种既能处理多体刚性约束又能高效并行计算的数值框架。

Method: 方法基于快速斯托克斯动力学（fast Stokesian dynamics, FSD），将多珠刚性体视为通过几何约束连接的球体集合，采用隐式刚性约束实现稳定时间积分。为求解鞍点系统，开发了块三角分解预条件子，结合远场迁移率近似逆与Schur补的块对角近似，通过LU分解独立求解每个聚集体子块。远场流体相互作用采用Ewald分裂方法处理，并引入了依赖于系统尺寸的Ewald分裂参数以加速低体积分数下的模拟。

Result: 在基准测试中，方法准确捕捉了双球在剪切流中的动力学、颗粒对沉降、布朗扩散及稀相至结构化悬浮液的流变行为。验证结果准确复现了碳黑浆料实验流变学中的剪切稀化与高剪切粘性特征。GPU执行在系统规模较小时表现出有利的扩展性，每个珠子的运行时间随规模增加先下降后趋于饱和。尺寸依赖的Ewald分裂参数在低体积分数下实现了最高一个数量级的加速，而恒定Ewald分裂在大系统中呈现线性扩展，尺寸依赖选择因远场计算增加导致二次扩展。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够对斯托克斯流中的刚性聚集体悬浮液进行准确且可扩展的模拟，为处理复杂工业材料如碳黑浆料的流变行为提供了有效工具。方法在GPU加速和Ewald分裂参数选择方面的优化策略，为不同规模下实现高效计算提供了灵活途径。

Abstract: We present a fast Stokesian dynamics (FSD) framework for the dynamics and rheology of suspensions of rigid aggregates. The method extends the sphere-level formulation of Fiore and Swan (2019) to multi-bead rigid bodies. Rigidity is enforced implicitly through geometric constraints, enabling stable and efficient time integration. We develop a block-triangular factorization preconditioner for the resulting saddle-point system. The approach combines an approximate inverse of the far-field mobility with a block-diagonal approximation of the Schur complement, enabling independent inversion of each aggregate sub-block via LU decomposition. The method is implemented as an open-source plugin for the HOOMD-blue software suite, and validated against benchmark problems, including doublet dynamics in shear flow, pair sedimentation, Brownian diffusion, and suspension rheology across dilute and structured regimes, accurately capturing both deterministic and stochastic behavior. The framework is further validated against experimental rheology of carbon black slurries, explicitly accounting for van der Waals cohesion, Hertzian contact, and tangential friction via enhanced lubrication. The simulations accurately reproduce the shear-thinning and high-shear viscous regimes. The method exhibits favorable GPU scaling for small system sizes, with decreasing runtime per bead prior saturation. A size-dependent Ewald splitting parameter accelerates simulations at low volume fractions, yielding up to an order-of-magnitude speedup compared to constant Ewald splitting. For larger systems, a constant Ewald splitting produces linear scaling with particle number, whereas the size-dependent choice leads to quadratic scaling due to increased far-field cost. Overall, the proposed framework enables accurate and scalable simulation of rigid aggregate suspensions in Stokes flow.

</details>


### [201] [Reciprocal theorem for ion-releasing colloidal particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25385)
*Evgeny S. Asmolov,Olga I. Vinogradova*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文推广了电解质溶液中悬浮颗粒的倒易定理（reciprocal theorem），特别针对催化胶体（catalytic colloids）释放离子的情形。推广的核心在于捕捉催化颗粒周围二次离子云（secondary cloud）的形成效应，这等价于计入系统的一个额外电荷Q。结果表明，催化颗粒的推进速度存在一个正比于Q和外加电场E∞的额外贡献，其中Q的符号由离子扩散率差异决定，大小由离子平均表面通量控制。该定理适用于均质释放离子的被动颗粒的电泳（electrophoresis）和扩散泳（diffusiophoresis），以及非均质活性颗粒（微游泳者，microswimmers）的自推进，且该额外项在某些情形下消失或影响甚微，但在许多情形下会显著改变颗粒迁移率的大小甚至符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有倒易定理无法有效描述电解质溶液中催化颗粒释放离子时形成的二次离子云效应，而这一效应对颗粒推进速度有重要影响。因此需要推广倒易定理，引入额外电荷Q来统一刻画该效应。

Method: 通过理论推导，将倒易定理推广到施加或自发涌现电场的电解质溶液中的悬浮颗粒，并引入一个额外电荷Q来等效描述催化颗粒周围二次离子云的形成。基于该推广导出颗粒推进速度中正比于Q和外部电场E∞的额外贡献，并给出Q的符号和大小由离子扩散率差及平均离子表面通量决定的表达式。

Result: 催化颗粒的推进速度存在一个额外贡献，正比于额外电荷Q和外加电场E∞；Q的符号由离子扩散率差异决定，大小由离子平均表面通量控制。该推广定理适用于均质释放离子的被动颗粒的电泳和扩散泳，以及非均质活性颗粒的自推进。在某些情形下额外项消失或影响甚微，但在许多情形下会显著改变颗粒迁移率的大小甚至符号。

Conclusion: 推广的倒易定理能够统一描述电解质溶液中催化颗粒的推进行为，并捕捉二次离子云效应。该结果对理解微游泳者相互作用具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We describe a generalization of the reciprocal theorem for particles suspended in electrolyte solutions and subjected to an electric field that could be either applied or emerged spontaneously. Attention is focused on catalytic colloids that release ions. The power of the generalization is to capture the effect of formation of a secondary cloud around a catalytic particle, which is equivalent to accounting for an excess charge $Q$ of a system. Our results show that the propulsion speed of catalytic particles has an extra contribution proportional to $Q$ and an external field $E_{\infty}$. The derived equation for $Q$ reveals that its sign is defined by the difference in the ion diffusivity and the magnitude is controlled by the average flux of ions from the surface. We demonstrate the application of the generalized theorem to electro- and diffusiophoresis of homogeneously releasing ions passive particles, as well as to a self-propulsion of inhomogeneous active particles (microswimmers). It is shown that whilst in some situations the extra term in the reciprocal theorem vanishes or has a little effect on the particle mobility, in many others it may dramatically change its magnitude, and even sign. In addition, the relevance of our results for microswimmer interactions is discussed briefly.

</details>


### [202] [Ultra-broadband and time-resolved depolarized dynamic light scattering for probing molecular dynamics in supercooled liquids and glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25506)
*Till Böhmer,Rolf Zeißler,Robin Schwäch,Jan P. Gabriel,Florian Pabst,Thomas Blochowicz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去偏振动态光散射（depolarized dynamic light scattering, DDLS）实验装置的搭建方案，通过结合光纤光子相关光谱、多散斑光子相关成像与高频DDLS，实现了对过冷分子液体与玻璃中转动动力学超过20个时间数量级的连续、超宽带测量，并建立了适用于平衡态与非平衡态体系的统一实验框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态光散射（dynamic light scattering, DLS）虽为软凝聚态物质微观动力学探测的通用技术，但应用于过冷分子液体和玻璃时，面临弱去偏振散射、玻璃化转变附近的缓慢弛豫以及与互补谱学技术进行定量对比等挑战，亟需高性能实验方案以解决这些难题。

Method: 该装置结合了常规光纤光子相关光谱（fiber-optical photon correlation spectroscopy）、多散斑光子相关成像（multispeckle photon correlation imaging）与高频DDLS，通过优化信噪比和长期光学稳定性，并处理相干性（coherence）与部分外差效应（partial heterodyning effects），实现了多检测方案下电场自相关函数（electric-field autocorrelation function）的一致性。

Result: 不同检测方案经适当处理后能得到相同的电场自相关函数，从而构建连续超宽带DDLS数据集。多散斑检测无需时间平均即可进行时间分辨相关测量，将DDLS的应用范围扩展至非平衡态体系，例如老化分子玻璃（aging molecular glasses）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的实验框架，可用于在极宽的时间范围内对平衡态与非平衡态分子重定向动力学（molecular reorientation dynamics）进行定量研究。

Abstract: Dynamic light scattering (DLS) is a versatile technique for probing microscopic dynamics in soft condensed matter. However, applying DLS to supercooled molecular liquids and glasses demands exceptional experimental performance due to weak depolarized scattering, slow relaxation near the glass transition, and the need for quantitative comparison with complementary spectroscopic techniques. In this tutorial we discuss, how a depolarized dynamic light scattering (DDLS) setup can be tailored to meet these challenges. By combining conventional fiber-optical photon correlation spectroscopy, and multispeckle photon correlation imaging with high-frequency DDLS, such a setup allows to capture rotational dynamics across more than 20 orders of magnitude in time. We detail the experimental design required for high signal-to-noise ratios and long-term optical stability, alongside the treatment of coherence and partial heterodyning effects. As we demonstrate, after proper treatment the different detection schemes yield the same electric-field autocorrelation function, enabling the construction of continuous, ultra-broadband DDLS datasets. Furthermore, multispeckle detection enables time-resolved correlation measurements without temporal averaging, extending DDLS to non-equilibrium systems such as aging molecular glasses. This methodology establishes a unified experimental framework for quantitative investigations of equilibrium and non-equilibrium molecular reorientation dynamics over an exceptionally broad time range.

</details>


### [203] [From real polymers to random graphs: percolation thresholds in associative polymer solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25534)
*Xinxiang Chen,Lennart Hebestreit,Friederike Schmid*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过分子动力学模拟与随机图模型（random graph）和随机几何图（random geometric graph）模型相结合，揭示了可逆缔合聚合物溶液（reversible associative polymer solutions）中溶胶-凝胶转变（sol-gel transitions）的阈值偏离经典Flory-Stockmayer理论的原因。结果表明，无坐标的随机图恢复了平均场极限，而随机几何图在恰当的检测半径下能定量再现分子动力学模拟中的渗流阈值偏移；预凝胶区形成的主环（primary loops）是偏离平均场预测的主要微观来源，且凝胶点附近的团簇尺寸统计符合三维渗流（three-dimensional percolation）普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶胶-凝胶转变在软物质和生物体系中广泛存在，但经典Flory-Stockmayer理论因忽略空间组织（spatial organization）和环状结构（loop formation）而无法准确预测转变阈值，亟需建立更精确的理论框架。

Method: 将分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）与随机图（random graph）和随机几何图（random geometric graph）模型相结合，系统分析了拓扑（topology）和几何（geometry）在可逆缔合聚合物溶液中各自的作用。

Result: 无坐标随机图恢复了Flory-Stockmayer平均场极限；随机几何图在检测半径根据聚合物构象尺寸（conformational size）选取时，定量再现了分子动力学模拟中的渗流阈值偏移；该几何映射对具有规则间隔结合位点的线性链在宽链刚度范围内均定量成立。预凝胶区形成的主环是偏离平均场预测的主导微观来源，凝胶点附近的团簇尺寸统计与三维渗流普适类一致。

Conclusion: 随机几何图建立了描述可逆缔合聚合物溶液中拓扑转变的极小预测框架（minimal predictive framework），表明凝胶化和网络形成可直接从单链构象信息（single-chain conformational information）推断得出。

Abstract: Sol-gel transitions are ubiquitous in soft matter and biological systems, yet their thresholds are often poorly captured by classical Flory-Stockmayer theory because spatial organization and loop formation are neglected. Here, we combine molecular dynamics simulations with random graph and random geometric graph models to determine the respective roles of topology and geometry in reversible associative polymer solutions. We show that a coordinate-free random graph recovers the mean-field Flory-Stockmayer limit, whereas a random geometric graph quantitatively reproduces the shifted percolation thresholds observed in molecular dynamics simulations when the detection radius is chosen according to the polymer conformational size. This geometric mapping remains quantitatively valid for linear chains with regularly spaced binding sites over a broad range of chain stiffness. At the microscopic level, we identify primary loops formed already in the pre-gel regime as the dominant source of the deviation from mean-field predictions. Near the gel point, the cluster-size statistics obtained from simulations and random geometric graphs are consistent with the universality class of three-dimensional percolation. These results establish random geometric graphs as a minimal predictive framework for describing topological transitions in reversible associative polymer solutions and show that gelation and network formation can be inferred directly from single-chain conformational information.

</details>


### [204] [Lipid-Mediated Control of Thermally Induced Shape Transformations in Liquid Crystal Droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25559)
*Mengwei Li,Lisa Tran*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验揭示了单油酸甘油酯掺杂的8CB液晶液滴在升温过程中的结构演变机制，发现其形状变化与体相介晶相转变（特别是近晶相-向列相转变）存在不连续耦合，且内部单油酸浓度与外部磷脂环境共同调控相变、细丝形成及形变路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分子尺度组织如何转化为介观尺度形状变化是软物质物理的核心问题。脂质和液晶为探讨这一跨尺度耦合提供了理想平台，但碳氢链堆积变化如何通过界面有序性、锚定条件和弹性应力影响液滴形态，尚需系统研究。

Method: 采用偏振光学显微镜（polarized optical microscopy）观测单油酸甘油酯掺杂的4-辛基-4'-氰基联苯（8CB）液滴在水性磷脂溶液中的升温过程，并结合界面张力（interfacial tension）测量分析其结构演变与形变行为。

Result: 液滴经历耦合的近晶相-向列相-各向同性相（smectic-nematic-isotropic）转变，细长丝状结构仅出现在近晶相区间；在近晶相-向列相转变处观察到液滴形状变形的不连续（discontinuous）突变，首次报告了与近晶相-向列相转变耦合的不连续液滴形状变化转变；内部和外部脂质含量改变可调控形变路径（丝状主导vs.无定形向列相形变）；界面张力测量表明，更强的界面张力降低并不必然导致丝状化或变形。

Conclusion: 油脂组成和碳氢链结构（hydrocarbon-chain architecture）是调控液晶液滴热致形状变化的关键参数，液滴形态动力学源于体相弹性（bulk elasticity）、介晶相结构（mesophase structure）与脂质介导的界面组织之间的耦合。

Abstract: Lipids and liquid crystals provide a useful platform for addressing how molecular-scale organization is translated into mesoscale shape transformation. Variations in hydrocarbon-chain packing can modify interfacial order, anchoring conditions, and elastic stresses, potentially coupling molecular organization to droplet morphology. Here, we investigate the temperature-dependent structural evolution of monoolein-doped 4-octyl-4'-cyanobiphenyl (8CB) droplets dispersed in aqueous phospholipid solutions. Using polarized optical microscopy, we show that the internal monoolein concentration and the external lipid environment jointly regulate phase transitions, thin filamentation, and larger deformations during heating. The droplets undergo coupled smectic-nematic-isotropic transitions, with extended thin filaments observed exclusively in the smectic regime. At the smectic-to-nematic transition, we observe an abrupt and discontinuous onset of droplet shape deformation, revealing a shape-change transition coupled to the bulk mesophase transition. To our knowledge, this is the first report of a discontinuous droplet-shape-change transition coincident with a smectic-to-nematic phase transition. Varying the internal and external lipid contents redirects the reconfiguration pathway between filament-dominated smectic responses and more amorphous nematic shape changes. Interfacial tension measurements further show that stronger reductions in liquid-crystal-aqueous interfacial tension do not necessarily produce filamentation or deformation. Instead, the observed morphodynamics arise from the coupling between bulk elasticity, mesophase structure, and lipid-mediated interfacial organization. These findings establish lipid composition and hydrocarbon-chain architecture as key parameters governing thermally induced shape transformations in liquid crystal droplets.

</details>


### [205] [Stress Drops Associated with Surface Crack Formation in Photo-aged Polypropylene during Three-Point Bending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25582)
*Kazuya Haremaki,Yusuke Koide,Takashi Uneyama,Yuichi Masubuchi,Takato Ishida*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过三点弯曲（three-point bending）实验，揭示了光老化聚丙烯（PP）中沿厚度方向存在的脆化梯度（embrittlement gradient）会直接反映在弯曲应力-应变曲线中：当老化面位于拉伸侧时，曲线出现离散应力降（stress drop），对应表面裂纹的萌生；而反向加载则无此现象，表明三点弯曲比单轴拉伸（uniaxial tensile testing）更适合检测光老化聚合物的表面脆化（surface embrittlement）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚丙烯（PP）在紫外（UV）辐照下会发生由表及里的非均匀光氧化（photo-oxidation），导致沿厚度方向形成脆化梯度。传统单轴拉伸测试假定截面均匀变形，只能获得空间平均（spatially averaged）响应，难以探测表面脆化层。因此需要一种能选择性检测表面脆化层力学响应的实验方法。

Method: 将轻微光老化（尚未出现可见表面裂纹）的PP试件以两种构型进行三点弯曲：老化面位于拉伸侧，以及相反面（reverse surface）位于拉伸侧。同时进行原位侧面观察（in-situ side-view observation），并对比单轴拉伸测试。

Result: 当老化面位于拉伸侧时，应力-应变曲线出现多个离散应力降，且每个新表面裂纹的形成与一次应力降在时间上同步。当相反面位于拉伸侧时，曲线未观察到明显的应力降。单轴拉伸则只能给出空间平均的响应。

Conclusion: 三点弯曲在试件表面施加最大拉伸应变，从而选择性地探测脆化表面层，其应力-应变曲线中的离散应力降直接反映了沿厚度方向的脆化梯度。三点弯曲是检测光老化聚合物表面脆化的有效方法。

Abstract: Using three-point bending, this study investigates surface-crack formation in photo-aged polypropylene (PP) that has a depth-dependent aging gradient. PP undergoes embrittlement under ultraviolet (UV) irradiation, and because the photo-oxidation proceeds inward from the irradiated surface, the embrittlement develops non-uniformly across the specimen thickness. PP specimens were mildly photo-aged by UV irradiation and had not yet developed visible surface cracks. Each specimen was bent in two configurations: with the UV-irradiated ("aged") surface on the tensile side, and with the opposite ("reverse") surface on the tensile side. When the aged surface was on the tensile side, the stress-strain curves exhibited several discrete stress drops, and in-situ side-view observation confirmed that the formation of each new surface crack coincided with a stress drop. In contrast, no clear stress drops were observed when the reverse surface was on the tensile side. These results show that the through-thickness gradient of embrittlement is directly reflected in the bending stress-strain response. Uniaxial tensile testing, the standard method for evaluating mechanical properties, formally assumes a nominally uniform deformation across the cross-section and therefore reflects the spatially averaged response. Three-point bending, by contrast, imposes the largest tensile strain at the specimen surface and thus selectively probes the embrittled surface layer, making it an effective method for detecting the surface embrittlement of photo-aged polymers.

</details>


### [206] [Physical Mechanism of Vacuole Formation in Liquid Droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25743)
*Pranay Jaiswal,Ivar S. Haugerud,William Verstraeten,Kerstin Göpfrich,Job Boekhoven,Christoph A. Weber*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析，提出液滴中形成的长寿命真空泡（vacuoles）是由液滴内部的局部旋节线失稳（local spinodal instability）这一通用机制驱动的；借助非平衡热力学（non-equilibrium thermodynamics）框架，揭示了控制真空泡形成与大小的物理化学条件，并为多隔室功能化设计提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上在多种液滴体系（如生物分子凝聚体（biomolecular condensates）和合成凝聚物（synthetic coacervates））中均观察到真空泡现象，但其形成在热力学上不利（增加界面面积），因此其背后机制尚不明晰。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，利用非平衡热力学建立了一个识别真空泡形成及其尺寸控制条件的理论框架。通过温度淬火（temperature quenches）以及与液滴内部或外部化学过程耦合的场景来验证机制。 

Result: 发现真空泡形成是一种由液滴内部局部旋节线失稳驱动的通用机制。理论框架成功指出了影响真空泡形成和大小（size）的物理化学条件。

Conclusion: 研究结果不仅解释了真空泡的形成机理，还提出了促进真空泡化（vacuolation）的分子设计与化学路径，从而可用于构建具备增强表面催化（enhanced surface catalysis）和隔室分裂（compartment fission）等工程化功能的多隔室结构。

Abstract: Vacuoles have been observed in liquid droplets across variety of experimental systems, ranging from biomolecular condensates composed of proteins and RNA, to synthetic coacervates formed by charged polymers or synthetic nanostars. These vacuoles are long-lived domains depleted of droplet material, and their formation is puzzling because the associated increase in interfacial area is thermodynamically unfavorable. Using theory, we show that vacuoles form through a generic mechanism: a local spinodal instability within the droplet. We demonstrate this mechanism in several experimentally relevant scenarios, including temperature quenches and droplets coupled to chemical processes occurring either inside or outside the droplet. Using non-equilibrium thermodynamics, we develop a theoretical framework that identifies the physicochemical conditions controlling whether vacuoles form and how big vacuoles can become. Our results suggest molecular designs and chemical pathways that promote vacuolation, enabling multi-compartment formation with engineered functions such as enhanced surface catalysis and compartment fission.

</details>


### [207] [Crack-Tip Opening as a Probe for Length-Scale Separation in Geometrically Nonlinear Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25771)
*Raúl Lazo-Molina,Mokhtar Adda-Bedia,Mohit Pundir,Rodrigo Arias,David S. Kammer*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过可压缩 St. Venant-Kirchhoff 材料模型，在无材料非线性的条件下，单独研究几何非线性（GNL）对脆性软固体静态裂纹近尖端场的影响。提出基于裂纹张开位移（CTOD）的后处理方法与渐近解析解，发现几何非线性存在一个由泊松比决定的特征非线性长度尺度，该尺度与远场应力强度因子呈二次方关系，并起到应变硬化与裂纹屏蔽作用，可作为非线性弹性断裂力学的基础框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软弹性固体中，断裂由几何非线性和材料非线性的复杂耦合驱动。几何非线性是所有高变形固体的普遍特征，但以往研究常将两者混淆。因此，有必要在排除材料非线性的情况下，单独建立几何非线性的基准影响，为后续整合材料非线性的研究提供正则化基线。

Method: 采用可压缩 St. Venant-Kirchhoff 材料模型（仅含几何非线性、不含材料非线性），在 I 型平面应变条件下分析静态裂纹的大变形行为。提出基于裂纹张开位移（crack-tip opening displacement, CTOD）轮廓的后处理方法，并推导渐近解析解；进一步利用匹配渐近展开（matched-asymptotics）方法定义非线性长度尺度。

Result: 发现一个独特的近尖端区域，其中 CTOD 偏离经典线弹性预测，进入由泊松比（Poisson's ratio）决定的非线性区间；定义的非线性长度尺度 λₙₗ 与该区域边界对应，且与远场应力强度因子 K_I 呈二次方关系。几何非线性作为一种内在的应变硬化（strain-stiffening）机制，足以触发能量分配，有效屏蔽裂纹尖端，产生表观增韧效应。

Conclusion: 几何非线性材料模型可作为更广泛的非线性弹性断裂力学研究的基础框架，为后续整合材料非线性效应提供正则化基准。

Abstract: Soft elastic solids are highly deformable materials where fracture is driven by the complex coupling of geometric and material nonlinearities. While geometric nonlinearity (GNL) arises kinematically from the intrinsic capacity of solids to undergo large deformations, material nonlinearity stems from the constitutive behavior unique to each class of materials. Because GNL is a universal feature of all highly deformable solids, establishing its standalone impact is a prerequisite for understanding nonlinear fracture. Here, we focus on brittle soft solids to study the role of GNL alone on the near-tip fields of a static crack under mode I plane-strain conditions, providing a canonical baseline for integrating material nonlinearities in future investigations. By utilizing a compressible St. Venant-Kirchhoff material model, we analyze crack behavior under large deformations in the absence of material nonlinearity. We propose a robust postprocessing methodology based on the crack-tip opening displacement (CTOD) profile and derive asymptotic analytical solutions. Our results reveal a distinct near-tip region where the CTOD departs from classical linear elastic predictions, transitioning into a nonlinear regime dictated by Poisson's ratio. Using a matched-asymptotics approach, we define a physical nonlinear length scale $λ_\mathrm{nl}$ that bounds this region and scales quadratically with the far-field stress intensity factor $K_I$. We show that GNL acts as an intrinsic strain-stiffening mechanism sufficient to trigger energy partitioning, effectively shielding the crack tip and imparting an apparent toughening. Ultimately, we conclude that the geometrically nonlinear material model serves as a foundational framework for the broader study of nonlinear elastic fracture mechanics.

</details>


### [208] [Physics-Guided Interpretable Machine Learning Framework for Anomalous Transport in Crowded Media with Tunable Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25827)
*Zakiya Shireen,Sujin B. Babu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个物理引导的可解释机器学习框架（physics-guided interpretable machine-learning framework），耦合布朗团簇动力学模拟（Brownian Cluster Dynamics simulations）、替代机器学习模型（surrogate machine-learning models）和基于SHAP的解释方法，定量解耦了总体积分数、探索粒子分数和模板键柔性（template bond flexibility）对探索粒子（explorer particle）在拥挤介质中输运的独立与耦合效应。研究以二元胶体系统（binary colloidal systems）为模型体系，发现键柔性通过促进局域键重组（local bond rearrangements）而不改变不可逆网络拓扑，独立调控网络形成后的弛豫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥挤介质（crowded media）中的输运受多种物理机制共同调控，但由于微结构（microstructure）的持续演化会不断改变各机制的相对影响，定量解耦它们的独立与耦合贡献一直是一个长期挑战。

Method: 开发了一个物理引导的可解释机器学习框架：将布朗团簇动力学模拟与替代机器学习模型及基于SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）的解释方法相结合，定量分析总体积分数、探索粒子分数和模板键柔性三个因素对探索粒子输运的独立与耦合效应。

Result: 结构描述符（structural descriptors）、均方位移（mean-squared displacement）、中间散射函数（intermediate scattering functions）和位移分布（displacement distributions）表明，网络形成通过增强瞬态笼蔽效应（transient caging）定位探索粒子。键柔性通过促进局域键重组以释放瞬态笼蔽粒子，独立调控网络形成后的弛豫过程，且不改变不可逆网络拓扑。拥堵和组成主导整体输运响应，但定量归因揭示了拥堵、组成和模板键柔性的相对贡献随约束环境演化而变化的规律。

Conclusion: 键柔性被确立为异质胶体网络（heterogeneous colloidal networks）中弛豫的独立控制参数。该工作提供了一个通用的物理引导可解释机器学习框架，可用于定量解耦复杂输运现象中的耦合物理机制。

Abstract: Transport in crowded media is governed by the interplay of multiple physical mechanisms. Quantitatively disentangling their individual and coupled contributions remains a longstanding challenge because the evolving microstructure continuously modifies their relative influence. Here, we develop a physics-guided interpretable machine-learning framework that couples Brownian Cluster Dynamics simulations with surrogate machine-learning models and SHAP-based interpretation to quantitatively disentangle the individual and coupled effects of total volume fraction, explorer fraction, and template bond flexibility on explorer-particle transport. We demonstrate the framework using binary colloidal systems in which explorer particles diffuse through a template network formed by irreversible bonds with tunable flexibility. Structural descriptors, mean-squared displacement, intermediate scattering functions, and displacement distributions reveal that network formation localizes explorer particles through enhanced transient caging. Bond flexibility emerges as an independent regulator of post-network relaxation by promoting local bond rearrangements that facilitate the release of transiently caged particles without altering the irreversible network topology. Although crowding and composition dominate the overall transport response, quantitative attribution reveals how the relative contributions of crowding, composition, and template bond flexibility evolve as the confining environment develops. Beyond establishing bond flexibility as a distinct control parameter for relaxation in heterogeneous colloidal networks, this work provides a general physics-guided interpretable machine-learning framework for quantitatively disentangling coupled physical mechanisms in complex transport phenomena.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Tuning Density and Spin Ordering of Degenerate Fermi Gases in an Optical Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25267)
*Wei Qin,Yuan-Hong Chen,Renyuan Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合到高精细度光学腔的自旋简并费米气体（spin-degenerate Fermi gas），揭示了标量-矢量耦合竞争（scalar-vectorial coupling competition）与泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）协同决定超辐射（superradiance）相变阈值，并发现两分量费米气体的布居数比（population ratio）可驱动连续或不连续的实空间相分离（real-space phase separation）相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋简并费米气体在光学腔中标量耦合与矢量耦合的竞争机制，以及该竞争如何与泡利阻塞共同影响系统超辐射相变的阈值和类型。

Method: 通过调节泵浦场与腔场的相对偏振角（relative polarization angle）来控制标量与矢量耦合的权重；基于两分量费米气体模型，分析不同布居数比下的相变行为（连续与不连续相变）。

Result: 相变阈值由标量-矢量耦合权重与泡利阻塞协同决定，后者决定了超辐射所需临界泵浦晶格深度（critical pump lattice depth）；两分量费米气体的布居数比可导致连续或不连续两种实空间相分离相变；相对偏振角对相变有显著影响，且结论适用于玻色系统。

Conclusion: 标量-矢量耦合竞争是系统相变边界（phase transition boundary）的根本控制因素；研究结果为未来的实验实现提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: We investigate a spin-degenerate Fermi gas coupled to a high-finesse optical cavity, where the competition between scalar and vectorial couplings is controlled by the relative polarization angle of the pump and cavity fields. We find that the phase transition threshold is synergistically determined by the scalar-vectorial coupling weight and Pauli blocking, with the latter dictating the critical pump lattice depth required for the onset of superradiance. For a two-component Fermi gas with opposite spins, the population ratio drives two distinct types of phase transitions corresponding to real-space phase separation: continuous and discontinuous. Nevertheless, the boundary of the phase transition remains fundamentally governed by the scalar-vectorial coupling competition. We clarify the impact of the relative polarization angle on phase transitions of the system; these results also apply to bosonic systems. Our results provide valuable theoretical insights for future experimental realizations.

</details>


### [210] [From weakly to strongly-interacting driven-dissipative bosons in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25508)
*Martina Zündel,Loïc Herviou,Léonie Canet,Anna Minguzzi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过数值方法和微扰理论分析了一维受驱动-耗散玻色-哈伯德模型（driven-dissipative Bose-Hubbard model）在非相干泵浦及单体和双体损耗下的两点时空关联函数，揭示了两种互补参数区域：大填充弱相互作用下的Kardar-Parisi-Zhang标度行为，以及弱填充强相互作用下平均场相变的残余与激发性质的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维受驱动-耗散玻色-哈伯德模型在不同填充和相互作用强度下的非平衡态特性，特别是其时空关联行为及标度性质。

Method: 结合随机半经典模拟（stochastic semi-classical simulations）和张量网络方法（tensor network methods）等数值方法，以及基于Keldysh形式论（Keldysh formalism）的微扰理论，分析系统的两点时空关联函数。

Result: 在大填充弱相互作用区域，谱函数（spectral function）线宽中观测到Kardar-Parisi-Zhang标度（Kardar-Parisi-Zhang scaling）；在弱填充强相互作用区域，分析了平均场相变（mean field transition）的残余特征，并识别出激发性质的转变。

Conclusion: 研究覆盖了该受驱动-耗散玻色-哈伯德模型相图的两个重要区域，为理解非平衡驱动-耗散系统中的标度行为和相变提供了一维模型的分析结果。

Abstract: We consider a one-dimensional driven-dissipative Bose-Hubbard model subjected to incoherent pump and one- and two-body losses, and analyze it by studying its two-point space-time correlations. By employing a combination of numerical methods, such as stochastic semi-classical simulations and tensor network methods, as well as perturbation theory within the Keldysh formalism, we characterize the system at varying filling and interaction strength. We present results for two complementary regimes: i) at large filling and weak interactions, where we show that Kardar-Parisi-Zhang scaling is visible in the linewidth of the spectral function and ii) at weak filling and strong interactions, where we analyze what remains of the mean field transition and identify a change of nature of the excitations. Our study covers two important regions of the phase diagram of such a system.

</details>


### [211] [Ultradilute quasi-two-dimensional Bose-Bose liquid mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25558)
*Leandra Vranješ Markić,Ivan Poparić,Krešimir Dželalija,Petar Stipanović,Jordi Boronat*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法研究超稀释³⁹K玻色-玻色（Bose-Bose）体混合物在二维（2D）极限方向谐振约束下的液滴态，发现仅在最强约束下二维平均场（MF）+李-黄-杨（LHY）泛函才适用，且适用范围极窄，对涡旋研究具有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超稀释³⁹K玻色-玻色（Bose-Bose）混合物在外部谐振势沿一维方向压缩至二维（2D）极限下的液滴行为，并检验二维平均场（MF）+李-黄-杨（LHY）泛函的适用范围。

Method: 采用两种不同相互作用势模型，在T=0时通过量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法计算不同约束强度下的状态方程（equation of state），状态方程基于s波散射长度（s-wave scattering length）a和有效力程（effective range）r_eff刻画。基于QMC状态方程构建二维QMC密度泛函（density functional），并结合局域密度近似（local density approximation, LDA）确定自束缚液滴（self-bound drop）性质。

Result: 在中等压缩下，二维QMC泛函与三维QMC泛函预测的液滴能量和轮廓一致，且计算成本显著降低。仅在最强约束且满足a和r_eff普适性的体系中，结果趋近于二维MF+LHY预测，表明二维LHY泛函的适用约束范围极窄。

Conclusion: 二维LHY泛函仅在非常窄的约束范围内适用，这一结论对涡旋（vortices）的研究具有重要影响。

Abstract: We study ultradilute $^{39}$K Bose-Bose bulk mixtures and droplets in an external harmonic potential that confines them in one spatial direction towards the two-dimensional (2D) limit. Equations of state for several confinements are obtained with quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) at $T=0$, using interaction potentials that include information on the $s$-wave scattering length $a$ and the effective range $r_{\rm eff}$. Performing the calculations using two different interaction potential models we have determined the range of confinements for which equations of state are universal in terms of $a$ and $r_{\rm eff}$. Based on the QMC equation of state, we develop a 2D QMC density functional for each confinement strength and use it together with the local density approximation to determine properties of the self-bound drops. For moderate squeezing, energies and droplet profiles obtained using the 2D QMC functional agree well with those obtained using 3D functionals, while offering a substantial reduction in computational cost, and a consistent approach in crossover to 2D. Noticeably, our results approach 2D mean-field (MF) + Lee-Huang-Yang (LHY) predictions only for the most strongly confined systems for which universality in terms of $a$ and $r_{\rm eff}$ is observed. This implies a very narrow range of confinements for which 2D LHY functionals are applicable, which has important consequences for the study of vortices.

</details>


### [212] [Ro-vibrational van der Waals interaction between ultracold polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25774)
*Kang Feng,Hanwei Yang,Hubert J. Jóźwiak,Tijs Karman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文描述了超冷极性分子之间的振动-转动范德瓦尔斯相互作用，该相互作用强且能有效抑制碰撞损失，从而无需主动屏蔽即可实现不同振动-转动态分子费米混合物的蒸发冷却，并为量子模拟、杂质物理、光晶格稳定化及合成维度中的相互作用控制提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷极性分子在低温下的碰撞损失一直是实验中的一个关键难题，制约着蒸发冷却和量子模拟等应用的实现。本文旨在探索一种无需主动外场屏蔽即可抑制碰撞损失并实现高效冷却的相互作用机制。

Method: 本文描述了超冷极性分子之间存在的振动-转动（ro-vibrational）范德瓦尔斯（van der Waals）相互作用，并分析该相互作用对弹性碰撞和碰撞损失的影响，进而提出了将其用于不同振动-转动态分子费米混合物（Fermi mixtures）的蒸发冷却方案。

Result: 该振动-转动范德瓦尔斯相互作用强度大，可导致快速弹性碰撞，并将碰撞损失抑制数个数量级。该方案无需主动屏蔽，且与微波屏蔽（microwave shielding）兼容，能实现受控的态依赖相互作用。

Conclusion: 该相互作用为量子模拟（quantum simulation）和杂质物理（impurity physics）提供了新机会，并可拓展至稳定光晶格（optical lattices）中的费米分子、控制合成维度（synthetic dimensions）中的相互作用、增强光镊装载以及实现直接红外屏蔽（infrared shielding）。

Abstract: We describe the ro-vibrational van der Waals interaction between ultracold polar molecules. This interaction is strong, leading to fast elastic collisions and orders of magnitude suppression of collisional loss. This enables evaporative cooling of Fermi mixtures of molecules in different ro-vibrational states, without active shielding by applying external fields. The scheme is compatible with microwave shielding, where it enables controlled state dependent interactions, opening up new opportunities for quantum simulation and impurity physics. The interaction can also be used to stabilize fermionic molecules in optical lattices, to control interactions in synthetic dimensions, for enhanced tweezer loading, and direct infrared shielding.

</details>


### [213] [Tunable state-dependent interactions in collisionally stable mixtures of polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25777)
*Hubert J. Jóźwiak,Hanwei Yang,Eugen Dizer,Arthur Christianen,Tijs Karman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出将极性分子的基态(v=0)与第一激发态(v=1)编码为赝自旋-1/2系统，通过双重微波屏蔽技术大幅抑制双体损耗与三体重组，实现长程偶极伊辛交换、密度-密度和自旋-密度相互作用，从而在块体气体和光学晶格中分别实现巡回量子磁性、量子液滴以及扩展哈伯德模型与t-J_z模型。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性分子（polar molecules）的集体量子态调控面临两体损耗（two-body losses）与三体重组（three-body recombination）的严重限制，现有方案难以同时实现低损耗和强相互作用耦合。

Method: 将基态(v=0)和第一激发态(v=1)振动能级编码为赝自旋-1/2系统（pseudo-spin-1/2 system），采用双重微波屏蔽（double microwave shielding）同时保护两个态，利用与自旋态相关的微波缀饰（state-dependent microwave dressing）产生高度可调的长程偶极伊辛交换（dipolar Ising exchange, J_z）、密度-密度（density-density, V）和自旋-密度（spin-density, W）相互作用。

Result: 双重微波屏蔽将双体损耗压制数个数量级，同时严格避免三体重组；相互作用程长（interaction length scale）明显超过典型粒子间距，使系统进入强相互作用区（strongly interacting regime）。

Conclusion: 在块体气体中可实现巡回量子磁性（itinerant quantum magnetism）与具有新型各向异性自旋织构（anisotropic spin textures）的量子液滴（quantum droplets）；在光学晶格中可自然实现扩展哈伯德模型（extended Hubbard model）与t-J_z模型，为量子模拟（quantum simulation）开辟新方向。

Abstract: We propose encoding a pseudo-spin-$1/2$ system in the ground ($v=0$) and first excited ($v=1$) vibrational states of polar molecules. Double microwave shielding simultaneously shields molecules in both states, suppressing two-body losses by orders of magnitude while strictly avoiding three-body recombination. The microwave dressing is state-dependent and results in highly tunable, long-range dipolar Ising exchange ($J_z$), density-density ($V$), and spin-density ($W$) interactions. These interaction length scales readily exceed the typical interparticle spacing, pushing the molecules deep into the strongly interacting regime. In bulk gases, this enables the exploration of itinerant quantum magnetism and quantum droplets with novel anisotropic spin textures; in optical lattices, it naturally realizes extended Hubbard and $t$-$J_z$ models, opening new directions in quantum simulation.

</details>

### [214] [The geometry-first formulation of gauge theory is not equivalent to the symmetry-first one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24901)
*Henrique Gomes*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了规范理论的“几何优先”与“对称优先”两种表述不等价，并从理论可表达性、生成结构的不确定性以及范畴函子的忠实性、满性与本质满性三个层面加以证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清规范理论（gauge theory）中“几何优先”表述（gauge group 作为结构化向量丛的自同构群）与“对称优先”表述之间的逻辑关系，检验两者是否等价。

Method: 通过对比两种表述在三个方面的差异进行论证：1) 对有限张量数据的生成结构限制下，几何优先表述能容纳的理论更少；2) 即使对称优先理论存在几何优先表述，主丛与物质丛也无法确定生成该规范群的结构化向量丛；3) 从几何优先生成对象到带物质的主丛的范畴函子忠实但非本质满也非满。

Result: 几何优先表述排除了加法结构群 ℝ 的带荷理论；标准模型规范群的对角商在“经典菜单”上不满足本质满性，加法 ℝ 例在任何有限张量菜单上也不满足；实定向纤维 ℝ^(2m) 情形下函子不满，因为主丛范畴允许 SO(2m) 由非正常正交映射诱导的外自同构，但没有保结构的纤维映射能实现该自同构。

Conclusion: 几何优先与对称优先这两种规范理论表述在逻辑上不等价，几何优先表述在理论涵盖范围和范畴结构上均弱于对称优先表述。

Abstract: This paper argues that the geometry-first and symmetry-first formulations of gauge theory are not equivalent. They differ in three respects. First, the geometry-first formulation---in which gauge groups arise as automorphism groups of structured fundamental vector bundles---admits fewer theories when its generating structures are restricted to finite tensorial data. Charged theories with additive structure group $\mathbb{R}$ have no such presentation. Second, even when a symmetry-first theory has a geometry-first presentation, its principal bundle and matter bundles do not determine which structured vector bundles generated the gauge group. Third, the natural functor from geometry-first generating objects to principal bundles with matter is faithful but neither essentially surjective nor full. Essential surjectivity fails for the diagonal quotient of the Standard Model gauge group on what I call `the classical menu', and for the additive-$\mathbb{R}$ examples on every finite tensorial menu. Fullness fails for a real oriented fibre $\mathbb{R}^{2m}$: the principal-bundle category admits the outer automorphism of $SO(2m)$ induced by an improper orthogonal map, but no structure-preserving fibre map induces it.

</details>
